Star Wars Characters Timeline

Click on any of the Star Wars characters below to see it in the timline!

Huyang, born 25020 BBY

Huyang is the Jedi Order's lightsaber teacher. The ancient droid spent a thousand generations aboard the training ship Crucible, helping younglings turn the kyber crystals they found during the Gathering into their first blades. His students included Yoda, Mace Windu, Bell Zettifar and Kanan Jarrus. His memory banks hold a record of every lightsaber and the Jedi who built it. His past is so old that younglings joked he had arrived at the Temple in a big blue box. During the Clone Wars he took Ahsoka Tano's group of initiates to Ilum. When Hondo Ohnaka's pirates raided the Crucible, he hid the students' crystals inside his own chest and kept fighting after losing his head and arms. He lived through the Jedi Purge. Years later he helped Ahsoka train Sabine Wren and flew with her as she searched for Ezra Bridger and Grand Admiral Thrawn. That search ended in another galaxy, where he gave Ezra a spare emitter he had made for Kanan and stayed on Peridea with Ahsoka and Sabine.

~25,020 BBY - Built

The Mark IV architect droid is built. Stories say he reached the Jedi Temple in a big blue box. He goes on to serve the Jedi Order as a lightsaber architect.

Before 800 BBY - Teaching a young Yoda

He helps the youngling Yoda build his lightsaber. Huyang later calls Yoda's hunt for his kyber crystal an excellent story.

High Republic Era - Bell Zettifar's first saber

Aboard the Crucible, he oversees Bell Zettifar as he builds his first lightsaber and trains against remotes.

232 BBY - Repairing Barabo's lightsaber

Nubs, Nash Durango, RJ-83 and Yoda bring him the broken lightsaber of Jedi Master Barabo. He recognizes it at once and repairs it.

Before 19 BBY - Mace Windu and Kanan Jarrus

He guides Mace Windu through building his lightsaber. Later he helps the young Caleb Dume, who becomes Kanan Jarrus, and gives him one of two matching emitters he owns.

22 BBY - Legends among the initiates

Early in the Clone Wars, initiates getting ready for the Gathering trade rumors about how old he is and where he came from.

20 BBY - The Gathering on Ilum

He goes with Ahsoka Tano and a clan of younglings to Ilum. Back on the Crucible, he coaches them as they build and personalize their lightsabers.

20 BBY - Raid on the Crucible

Hondo Ohnaka's pirates board the ship. Huyang hides the initiates' crystals in his chest. He loses his head and arms to blaster fire but still beats a pirate.

20 BBY - Rescue on Florrum

Ganodi reattaches his head, and he helps fly the Crucible to pick up the others before it crashes. The Jedi and the pirates team up against General Grievous and escape to Obi-Wan Kenobi's fleet, where Huyang is fully repaired.

19 BBY - Surviving the Purge

He outlives the Jedi Order's destruction and survives the years of the Empire.

Before 9 ABY - Training Sabine Wren

He joins Ahsoka Tano while she trains Sabine Wren, who finds the fussy droid irritating.

~9 ABY - Mission to Arcana

While Ahsoka looks for the star map to Thrawn and Ezra Bridger, he keeps her T-6 shuttle at a distance. He picks her up just before the HK droids explode.

~9 ABY - Investigation on Lothal

He scans the mercenaries' lightsabers and identifies Baylan Skoll as a Jedi-trained fighter. He stays with Sabine in the hospital after Shin Hati wounds her and helps pull data from a captured HK droid's head.

~9 ABY - Mentor to master and apprentice

He oversees Sabine's training aboard the shuttle and urges Ahsoka and Sabine to stay together.

~9 ABY - Another galaxy

After reaching Peridea, he gives Ezra Bridger the spare emitter matching Kanan's and praises Ezra's new blue lightsaber.

~9 ABY - Left on Peridea

When Thrawn gets away and Ezra goes home, Huyang stays behind on Peridea with Ahsoka and Sabine.

Huyang's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (24998 years old)
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY (25029 years old)

Maz Kanata, born 973 BBY

Maz Kanata is the ancient, goggle-wearing pirate queen whose lakeside castle on Takodana served as a neutral haven for smugglers, pirates and travelling musicians for about a thousand years. Her house had a single rule: no fighting, and no politics. She is Force-sensitive, but she never became a Jedi. Over the centuries she travelled the galaxy collecting relics, trusting the ones that spoke to her through the Force. During the High Republic she took in the infant Qort, who later became a Jedi, and stood beside her friend Sav Malagán when the Nihil attacked Takodana. She kept her abilities hidden during the Empire's reign. Even so, she helped Leia Organa plan her disguise as Boushh to rescue Han Solo, and in the years after Endor she left home again, sensing that the Force was out of balance. At some point she came into possession of Anakin and Luke Skywalker's lightsaber. Thirty years after Endor, she offered it to Rey and pushed her to accept her calling. When the First Order destroyed her castle, she put the saber in Finn's hands instead. Later she pointed Finn and Rose Tico toward a codebreaker on Canto Bight, and in the end she joined the Resistance on Ajan Kloss as an adviser to Leia. She mourned Leia's death and awarded Chewbacca a medal after the victory at Exegol.

973 BBY - Birth

Maz Kanata is born, more than a thousand years before the war between the First Order and the Resistance.

~966 BBY - Pirate Queen of Takodana

Living in an ancient castle beside Nymeve Lake, Maz becomes known as a pirate queen. She gives young smugglers money, gear and contacts and eventually controls more than a thousand ports while the Republic is still young.

~966 BBY - Centuries of Collecting

Maz travels the galaxy for centuries, gathering relics and treasures. She stores them in her castle's vaults and in hidden caches that she logs in her Smuggler's Guide.

~240 BBY - Taking in Qort

Criminals leave a baby Aloxian at her castle. Maz calms him with a vonduun crab skull helmet and asks Jedi Master Sav Malagán for help, and the child, Qort, is taken into the Jedi Order.

232 BBY - Jet Pack Trouble on Tenoo

Maz visits her friend Marlaa Jinara on Tenoo to get her jet pack repaired. The pack malfunctions and flies off, and the youngling Kai Brightstar helps get it back.

~232 BBY - Nihil Attack on Takodana

Krix Kamerat's Nihil cell attacks Takodana's Jedi Temple. Maz and Sav Malagán watch Qort, freed from his mask, drive the raiders off.

19 BBY - Hiding Her Gifts

After the Galactic Empire rises and the Jedi are purged, Maz keeps her Force abilities secret.

1 BBY - A Voice in the World Between Worlds

Ezra Bridger hears Maz's voice while he is inside the World Between Worlds.

0 ABY - Striking Back at the Empire

Shortly after the Battle of Yavin, Maz learns that the Empire has been gathering information on her. She hires a mercenary to hit Imperial targets on six worlds.

4 ABY - Planning Han Solo's Rescue

On Ord Mantell, Maz meets her "boyfriend" Chewbacca again and suggests that Leia Organa disguise herself as the bounty hunter Boushh to get into Jabba's palace.

~4 ABY - Han Solo Brings Trouble

After Endor, Han Solo comes to her castle to meet an Imperial defector with information about Kashyyyk. When Imperial forces follow him, Maz orders him to clean up the mess.

5 ABY - Mercy for Romwell Krass

Maz frees Imperial agent Romwell Krass and a New Republic pilot from her dungeon after their brawl and tells Krass to find peace. Sensing that the Force is out of balance, she sets off to explore the galaxy aboard the Tua-Lu.

9 ABY - Galactic Legend

Maz is now counted among the most famous pirates in the galaxy, and competitors at the Hunters tournament on Vespaara trade stories about her.

34 ABY - Counsel for Rey and Finn

Han Solo brings Rey, Finn and BB-8 to her castle. Maz challenges Finn's urge to run, and when the Skywalker lightsaber calls to Rey, she urges her to take it.

34 ABY - Battle of Takodana

The First Order destroys her castle. Maz gives the lightsaber to Finn, who fights beside Han and Chewbacca until the Resistance arrives.

34 ABY - A Union Dispute

While caught in a firefight, Maz sends Finn and Rose Tico to Canto Bight to find a master codebreaker.

35 ABY - Joining the Resistance

After helping Poe Dameron's Resistance with intelligence, Maz joins them on Ajan Kloss as an adviser. She urges them to find Exegol, is present at Leia's death, and gives Chewbacca the Medal of Bravery after the victory.

Maz Kanata's Timeline

  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (1007 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (1008 years old)

Yoda, born 896 BBY

For more than eight centuries, Yoda shaped the Jedi Order. He was its long-serving Grand Master, a teacher of younglings and Padawans, and the Jedi who outlived the Order's fall long enough to hand its legacy to Luke Skywalker. He was already a Grand Master in the High Republic era. In 382 BBY he helped end the Night of Sorrow on Dalna, and afterwards he chose to keep what the Jedi had learned about the Nameless creatures out of the Archives. Nearly 150 years later he stepped away from the Council to teach aboard the Star Hopper and fought the Nihil at Trymant IV and beyond. His apprentices over the centuries included Kantam Sy and Dooku, who later became a Sith Lord. In the last days of the Republic he doubted that Anakin Skywalker should be trained, and he sensed that a hidden Sith Master was still at work. He brought the clone army to Geonosis and commanded Republic forces through the Clone Wars. During the war the spirit of Qui-Gon Jinn taught him how to keep his identity after death. When Order 66 wiped out the Jedi, Yoda fought Darth Sidious in the Senate chamber, lost, and went into exile on Dagobah. Years later, after facing his own failures, he trained Luke Skywalker. He died of old age in 4 ABY and became one with the Force. Decades later he appeared to Luke on Ahch-To and helped his old student forgive himself.

896 BBY - Birth

Yoda is born. His species is ancient and mysterious, and he never reveals where he comes from.

~890s BBY - Youngling of the Jedi Order

Yoda joins the Order as a youngling. He finds his kyber crystal at the Gathering and builds his first lightsaber with Professor Huyang aboard the Crucible.

~880s BBY - Padawan years

He is paired with a Master younger than usual and travels to many worlds as a Padawan.

796 BBY - Becomes a Jedi Master

At about one hundred years old, Yoda begins the eight centuries he will spend training generations of Jedi.

~382 BBY - Grand Master of the High Republic

Yoda sits on the Jedi High Council and shares the title of Grand Master. He advises Chancellor Kyong Greylark during the crisis involving the Path of the Open Hand.

382 BBY - The Night of Sorrow

Yoda travels to Dalna with Greylark and joins the battle against the Path. With Yaddle, he rescues Gella Nattai and Axel Greylark from a collapsing sinkhole.

382 BBY - Keeping the Nameless secret

After the funeral for the fallen Jedi, Yoda and Creighton Sun agree to keep what they know about the Nameless creatures out of the Jedi Archives.

~230s BBY - Master of Kantam Sy

At the Endovar temple, Yoda lets his Padawan Kantam Sy leave the Order. When Sy returns, Yoda knights them so they can train Lula Talisola.

233 BBY - Assigning initiates

At the Grand Temple on Coruscant, Yoda sends younglings to outposts across the galaxy. He gives Kai Brightstar his own training saber.

232 BBY - Sabbatical aboard the Star Hopper

Yoda leaves the Council to teach Padawans aboard the Star Hopper and helps Avar Kriss's Force network during the Great Hyperspace Disaster.

232 BBY - Trymant IV

Yoda fights the Nihil during the Emergence at Trymant IV and finds a strange artifact aboard Marchion Ro's ship.

232 BBY - Starlight Beacon and the Nihil

Yoda attends the dedication of Starlight Beacon and takes Zeen Mrala in. He then hunts the Nihil, starting in the Bright Jewel system.

230 BBY - Return at Corellia

Yoda reappears after disappearing for more than a year. With Azlin Rell, he helps Kantam Sy and other Jedi fight the Nihil at the Corellian Shipyards.

~Late centuries BBY - Teacher of future Masters

Yoda trains Dooku and Cin Drallig as Padawans, and he also mentors Mace Windu, Ki-Adi-Mundi and Obi-Wan Kenobi.

32 BBY - The Chosen One

The Council turns down Qui-Gon Jinn's request to train Anakin Skywalker. After Qui-Gon dies on Naboo, the Council lets Anakin train anyway, over Yoda's objections, and Yoda senses that a Sith Master remains.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

Yoda brings the new clone army to Geonosis and rescues the surviving Jedi. He then duels Count Dooku, his former Padawan.

22–19 BBY - Clone Wars general

Yoda commands Republic forces and serves as a senior strategist throughout the war.

19 BBY - Lessons from Qui-Gon Jinn

Qui-Gon's spirit guides Yoda on a journey that teaches him how to keep his identity after death.

19 BBY - Order 66 and the duel with Sidious

Yoda survives the clone betrayal on Kashyyyk and faces Emperor Palpatine in the Senate chamber. He fails to defeat him.

19 BBY - Exile on Dagobah

Yoda helps hide the Skywalker twins and goes into hiding on Dagobah.

~2 BBY - Guiding Kanan and Ezra

From afar, Yoda speaks to Kanan Jarrus and Ezra Bridger through the Force and guides them at the Lothal Jedi Temple.

1 BBY - Kenobi becomes one with the Force

After Obi-Wan's death, Yoda talks with his spirit. He faces his past in the Cave of Evil and agrees to train Luke.

3 ABY - Training Luke Skywalker

Yoda trains Luke on Dagobah until Luke leaves to rescue his friends at Cloud City.

4 ABY - Death on Dagobah

Weak with age, Yoda confirms that Vader is Luke's father and tells him about another Skywalker. He then dies and becomes one with the Force.

4 ABY - Spirit at Endor

Yoda's spirit appears with those of Obi-Wan and Anakin at the victory celebration.

34 ABY - Lesson on Ahch-To

Yoda appears to the exiled Luke, burns the ancient tree with lightning, and teaches him that failure is the greatest teacher.

Yoda's Timeline

  • Star Wars: Young Jedi Adventures, 232 BBY (664 years old)
  • The Acolyte, 132 BBY (764 years old)
  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY (864 years old)

  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY (895 years old)
  • Episode V: The Empire Strikes Back, 3 ABY (899 years old)
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY (900 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (930 years old)

Jabba Desilijic Tiure, born 600 BBY

Jabba is the most notorious gangster of the Outer Rim. For centuries he ran smuggling, spice, slavery, gambling and piracy from his desert palace on Tatooine, and he became one of the most powerful members of the Grand Hutt Council. During the High Republic he took part in a Hutt plot to wreck a treaty on Hynestia, and was captured and held for ransom for it. By the fall of the Republic he was hosting the Boonta Eve Classic, where a young slave named Anakin Skywalker won. When the Clone Wars began, Count Dooku and his uncle Ziro had his infant son Rotta kidnapped, hoping to turn the Hutts against the Republic. The plan failed when Anakin and Ahsoka Tano brought the child home, and Jabba opened his hyperspace lanes to the Republic. He later had Ziro killed, and Maul's Shadow Collective forced him into a short alliance. Under the Empire he kept his power by making deals with Darth Vader, and after Vader wiped out the rest of the Hutt Council he was left in sole control of the Hutt Clans. His long feud with the smuggler Han Solo ended with Solo frozen in carbonite and hung in his throne room. That trophy brought Luke Skywalker and his friends to Tatooine, and in 4 ABY, above the Great Pit of Carkoon, Leia Organa strangled Jabba with the chain he had used to keep her captive.

600 BBY - Birth on Nal Hutta

Jabba is born into the Desilijic clan. His grandparents are Mama and Papa the Hutts, and his uncles are Ziro and Ebor.

Centuries before the Galactic Civil War - Rise to crime lord

Jabba builds a criminal empire from his palace in Tatooine's Northern Dune Sea. Working with the Crymorah Syndicate wins him a seat on the Grand Hutt Council.

Before 232 BBY - The Hynestian treaty plot

Jabba and other Hutts try to spark a diplomatic incident at a treaty banquet on Hynestia. Jedi stop them, and Queen Nyla takes the Hutts captive and holds them for ransom.

32 BBY - Boonta Eve Classic

Jabba hosts the Boonta Eve Classic podrace in Mos Espa and dozes through the race that Anakin Skywalker wins.

22 BBY - Rotta's kidnapping

Jabba's son Rotta is kidnapped in a plot run by Count Dooku and Ziro. Anakin Skywalker and Ahsoka Tano bring the Huttlet back. After Padmé Amidala exposes Ziro, Jabba signs a treaty that opens Hutt hyperspace lanes to the Republic.

~21 BBY - The Papanoida affair

Jabba lets Baron Papanoida test the bounty hunter Greedo, which proves Greedo kidnapped the Pantoran chairman's daughters.

~21 BBY - Ziro eliminated

The Hutt Council has Cad Bane free Ziro from Republic custody. Jabba then hires Sy Snootles, who kills Ziro and brings Jabba his incriminating journal.

~20 BBY - Forced into the Shadow Collective

Maul's forces beat the Hutt Council and corner Jabba in his palace. Jabba is forced to supply the takeover of Mandalore.

Early Imperial Era - Water tax and Kenobi

During a drought, Jabba's thugs collect water taxes until a hidden Obi-Wan Kenobi stops them. Jabba hires Black Krrsantan to track down whoever interfered.

~10 BBY - Han Solo joins his payroll

Han Solo becomes Jabba's best smuggler. Later Solo dumps a cargo to escape Imperial inspectors and ends up deep in debt to Jabba.

1 BBY - Docking Bay 94

Han Solo kills Greedo. Jabba confronts Solo in Mos Eisley and gives him more time to pay, at fifteen percent interest. Solo joins the Rebellion instead, and Jabba puts a large bounty on him.

0 ABY - Deal with Darth Vader

After the Death Star is destroyed, Vader force-chokes Jabba into lending him Boba Fett and Black Krrsantan. Jabba then agrees to supply the Imperial war machine in return for being left to run his empire.

~3 ABY - Sole master of the Hutt Clans

Vader kills the rest of the Grand Hutt Council, which leaves Jabba the only surviving member and the most powerful Hutt in the galaxy.

~3 ABY - Solo delivered in carbonite

Boba Fett delivers the frozen Han Solo, and Jabba hangs him in his throne room as his favorite decoration. Around the same time he sends Kay Vess to find out who Zerek Besh has planted as a spy in his palace.

4 ABY - Death at the Great Pit of Carkoon

Jabba captures Luke Skywalker's rescue party and tries to feed them to the Sarlacc. During the fight, Leia Organa strangles him with her slave chain aboard his sail barge, the Khetanna, which is then destroyed.

Jabba Desilijic Tiure's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY (568 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY (578 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (578 years old)
  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY (599 years old)
  • Episode V: The Empire Strikes Back, 3 ABY (603 years old)
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY (604 years old)

Chewbacca, born 200 BBY

Han Solo's loyal co-pilot and best friend, Chewbacca spent more than two centuries fighting alongside the heroes who shaped the galaxy. A veteran of the Clone Wars, he escaped Trandoshan hunters on Wasskah with Ahsoka Tano and helped Yoda flee Kashyyyk when Order 66 was issued. The Empire enslaved him, and he was rescued from a Mimban prison by a young Han Solo, to whom he swore a life debt. The two pulled off the Kessel Run, won the Millennium Falcon from Lando Calrissian and became notorious smugglers, until a charter to Alderaan pulled them into the Rebellion. Chewbacca helped rescue Princess Leia from the Death Star, covered Luke Skywalker at Yavin, survived Hoth and Cloud City, freed Han from Jabba's palace and helped bring down the shield generator on Endor. After the war he helped liberate his enslaved homeworld of Kashyyyk and went back to his family, then returned to smuggling with Han after Ben Solo fell to the dark side. He was at Han's side when his friend was killed on Starkiller Base. He then flew Rey to Ahch-To, piloted the Falcon at Crait, survived his apparent death on Pasaana and fought at Exegol, where the Sith were finally defeated.

200 BBY - Birth on Kashyyyk

Born in the city of Rwookrrorro on Kashyyyk to Attichitcuk during the High Republic Era. He later marries Mallatobuck and has a son, Lumpawaroo.

20 BBY - Hunted on Wasskah

Captured by Garnac's Trandoshan hunting guild and taken to Wasskah as prey. He joins Ahsoka Tano, Jinx and O-Mer, builds a transmitter that calls in Tarfful's Wookiee relief force, and helps defeat the hunters.

19 BBY - Battle of Kashyyyk

Fights beside Yoda, Luminara Unduli and Tarfful against the Separatist invasion. When Order 66 is issued, he helps Yoda escape the clone troopers in an escape pod.

~19 BBY–10 BBY - Enslaved by the Empire

Captured with other Wookiees by Trandoshan slavers and made to cut wroshyr trees for the Empire. He is handed over to the Empire and moved between Imperial facilities before ending up on Mimban as "the Beast."

10 BBY - Meeting Han Solo

Han Solo is thrown into his cell on Mimban and the two escape together. Chewbacca swears a life debt to Han and joins Tobias Beckett's gang.

10 BBY - Vandor Heist and the Kessel Run

Takes part in the failed conveyex heist on Vandor and the raid on Kessel, where he frees enslaved Wookiees. He flies as the Falcon's co-pilot for the first time on the record-setting Kessel Run.

10 BBY - Showdown on Savareen and Winning the Falcon

Survives Beckett's betrayal on Savareen and helps hand the coaxium over to Enfys Nest. Han then wins the Millennium Falcon from Lando on Numidian Prime.

9 BBY - Hunted by Zuckuss and 4-LOM

Rescues Han from the bounty hunters Zuckuss and 4-LOM at Valtos after crime lord Nodo puts a bounty on the pair.

1 BBY - Hired at Mos Eisley

Chewbacca and Han agree to fly Obi-Wan Kenobi, Luke Skywalker and the droids to Alderaan. They find the planet destroyed and help rescue Princess Leia from the Death Star.

0 BBY - Battle of Yavin

He urges Han to turn back, and the Falcon's return drives off Darth Vader so Luke can destroy the Death Star. Chewbacca is later honored with a medal.

0 ABY - Rescue on Cyrkon

Rescues Rebel lieutenant Caluan Ematt from Cyrkon and escapes ISB Commander Alecia Beck.

3 ABY - Hoth and Cloud City

Escapes Echo Base with Han, Leia and C-3PO. At Cloud City, Lando betrays them to Vader and Han is frozen in carbonite. Chewbacca escapes with Leia and Lando.

4 ABY - Rescue of Han Solo

Gets into Jabba's palace on Tatooine as Leia's "prisoner" and helps free Han and defeat Jabba.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Fights alongside the Ewoks and helps destroy the Death Star II's shield generator, which lets the Alliance fleet destroy the station.

5 ABY - Liberation of Kashyyyk

With Han and New Republic allies, he frees Kashyyyk and its enslaved Wookiees from Imperial control. He then returns home to his family.

~28 ABY - Back to Smuggling

After Ben Solo turns to the dark side and destroys Luke's new Jedi, Chewbacca rejoins Han as a smuggler. The pair eventually lose the Falcon and fly the Eravana instead.

34 ABY - Recovering the Falcon

They get the Falcon back over Jakku, along with Rey, Finn and BB-8, and help bring the map to Luke Skywalker to the Resistance.

34 ABY - Battle of Starkiller Base

Plants explosives on Starkiller Base and watches Kylo Ren kill Han. He wounds Kylo, then flies the Falcon to rescue Finn and Rey.

34 ABY - Ahch-To and Crait

Flies Rey to Ahch-To to find Luke Skywalker, then pilots the Falcon to save the Resistance survivors at Crait.

35 ABY - Quest for Exegol

Travels to Pasaana and Kijimi searching for Palpatine's hidden world. He is captured by the First Order and seemingly killed when Rey destroys a transport, but he is actually held on the Steadfast and rescued.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Flies the Falcon at the head of the Citizens' Fleet against the Final Order and sees the Sith finally defeated. Maz Kanata gives him a medal.

Chewbacca's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (178 years old)
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY (181 years old)
  • Solo: A Star Wars Story, 10 BBY (190 years old)
  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY (199 years old)
  • Episode V: The Empire Strikes Back, 3 ABY (203 years old)
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY (204 years old)
  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (234 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (234 years old)
  • Galaxy's Edge, 34 ABY (234 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (235 years old)

Ki-Adi-Mundi, born 172 BBY

Ki-Adi-Mundi is best remembered as one of the steady, sometimes skeptical voices of the Jedi High Council in the Republic's last years, and as a Jedi whose career spanned more than a century. In 132 BBY, during the High Republic, he was a newly made Master who sat on the tactical council Vernestra Rwoh called to discuss the assassin Mae. Decades later he held a Council seat of his own. There he taught younglings alongside Yarael Poof, pushed back on Qui-Gon Jinn's calls for more outreach, and in 32 BBY famously insisted that the Sith had been extinct for a millennium. He survived the arena battle on Geonosis that began the Clone Wars and became a Jedi General. He took scars from an early clash with General Grievous and led the northern assault in the Second Battle of Geonosis, where he topped Anakin Skywalker and Ahsoka Tano with 65 kills. On the Council he took a hard line, arguing against intervening on neutral Mandalore and personally telling Ahsoka Tano she had been expelled after the Temple bombing. In 19 BBY, while commanding the 21st Nova Corps in the Mygeeto campaign, he was gunned down by his own clone troopers when Order 66 was issued. His lightsaber survived him and ended up with the collector Dok-Ondar.

~172 BBY - Birth on Cerea

Ki-Adi-Mundi is born on Cerea. Canon gives no birth year, so this is an estimate based on his being a newly raised Jedi Master by 132 BBY. He trains in the Order under Yoda.

132 BBY - Council on the Mae-ho Aniseya threat

As a newly elevated Jedi Master, he joins Vernestra Rwoh's tactical council, reviews footage of Mae fighting Master Sol, and warns that she could become a major threat.

Unknown - Funeral of Master Katri

He gives the eulogy at Master Katri's funeral at the Jedi Temple after she is killed on Raxus Secundus.

33 BBY - Teaching with Yarael Poof and the Kwenn outreach

Now a Council member, he teaches younglings alongside Yarael Poof, hears Qui-Gon Jinn's proposal to engage more with the galaxy, dismisses it as extreme, and later takes part in the outreach mission to Kwenn.

32 BBY - Return of the Sith

He tells Qui-Gon Jinn that the Sith have been extinct for a millennium, sits on the Council that evaluates young Anakin Skywalker, and attends Qui-Gon's funeral on Naboo.

29 BBY - Rescue at Carnelion IV

He travels with a Republic task force that rescues Obi-Wan Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker and ends the Open-Closed War.

22 BBY - Separatist Crisis

He meets with Chancellor Palpatine about the Military Creation Act and dismisses Count Dooku as a political idealist rather than a murderer.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

He joins Mace Windu's strike team in the Petranaki Arena, is one of the few Jedi to survive the fighting, and escapes on Yoda's gunships as the Clone Wars begin.

22 BBY - Early war and the trial of Prosset Dibs

He shares a toast with his old friend Mace Windu, sits in judgment on the traitor Prosset Dibs, and is scarred by General Grievous in an early encounter.

~22 BBY - Second Battle of Geonosis

He leads the northern prong of the assault on the ray shield generator, survives a gunship crash, fights his way through a cave full of Geonosians, and finishes with 65 kills before being treated in a bacta tank.

~22 BBY - Poggle the Lesser and the brain worms

He helps hunt Poggle, then escorts him to Coruscant as the brain worm crisis unfolds on Ahsoka Tano and Barriss Offee's ship.

~21 BBY - Council crises

He watches Grievous's transmission showing the captured Eeth Koth, Maul's massacre on Raydonia, and other emergencies, and attends Obi-Wan Kenobi's staged funeral.

19 BBY - Mandalore refused

He argues that the Jedi cannot help Duchess Satine Kryze, because intervening on neutral Mandalore would be treated as an act of war.

19 BBY - Temple bombing and Ahsoka Tano's expulsion

After the Jedi Temple hangar bombing, he votes against protecting Tano and formally tells her she has been expelled. He later invites her back after she is cleared, but she refuses.

19 BBY - Doubting Yoda's visions

He is skeptical when Yoda claims Qui-Gon Jinn's spirit has contacted him, and suspects a Sith attack instead.

19 BBY - Quinlan Vos affair

He takes part in the Council's decision to rescue Quinlan Vos, who had turned to the dark side under Dooku as "Admiral Enigma."

19 BBY - Death on Mygeeto

While leading the 21st Nova Corps in the Mygeeto campaign during the Outer Rim Sieges, he is shot down by his own clone troopers when Order 66 is issued.

~34 ABY - Lightsaber in Dok-Ondar's collection

His lightsaber ends up with the collector Dok-Ondar on Batuu, who tells part of its history to a First Order sergeant.

Ki-Adi-Mundi's Timeline

  • The Acolyte, 132 BBY
  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY
  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY

Count Dooku, born 102 BBY

Dooku was one of the finest duelists the Jedi Order ever produced. He spent most of his long life as a Jedi, then gave it up and became Darth Tyranus, the Sith apprentice who led the galaxy into the Clone Wars. His father, the Count of Serenno, abandoned him as a baby, and Jedi seekers took him in. He grew up in the Temple as Yoda's Padawan, and his curiosity about the dark side worried his teachers even then. As a Jedi Master he trained Rael Averross and Qui-Gon Jinn and later sat on the High Council. Over the decades, a corrupt Senate and a Council he saw as too cautious wore down his faith. In 42 BBY he left the Order, overthrew his brother Ramil and took back the title of Count. Qui-Gon's death at Naboo pushed him further, and after he killed Master Yaddle he became Darth Sidious's apprentice. He then helped set up the secret clone army and gathered thousands of star systems into the Confederacy of Independent Systems. As the Separatists' Head of State he opened the war at Geonosis in 22 BBY. He trained General Grievous and Asajj Ventress, betrayed Ventress on his master's orders, and later tried to groom Savage Opress and Quinlan Vos to replace her. In 19 BBY he took Chancellor Palpatine hostage during the Battle of Coruscant. There Anakin Skywalker beat him in a duel aboard the Invisible Hand and executed him at Palpatine's urging. Dooku never learned that he had only ever been a placeholder until Sidious found a new apprentice.

102 BBY - Birth on Serenno

Born to Count Gora and Countess Anya of House Serenno. His Force powers show early, and his frightened father leaves the baby outside the palace. A Jedi seeker rescues him and brings him to the Jedi Temple on Coruscant.

~101 BBY - Youngling of the Hawk-Bat Clan

Raised in the Temple under Master Tera Sinube, alongside his close friend Sifo-Dyas and his rival Arath Tarrex.

90 BBY - Return to Serenno

At 12, visits Serenno for a galactic festival with Yoda. He saves his siblings Jenza and Ramil from kidnappers, is trapped with Jenza when a building collapses, and learns that Count Gora is his father. He keeps in touch with Jenza for years afterward.

88 BBY - Break-in at the Bogan Collection

Sneaks into the Temple's vault of dark side artifacts with Sifo-Dyas and accidentally hurts his friend through the Force. The incident leads to a duel with Lene Kostana, who later becomes a mentor to him.

86 BBY - Padawan of Yoda

Passes the Initiate Trials at 16. He beats Sifo-Dyas in the Padawan tournament, but Kostana picks Sifo-Dyas, and Grand Master Yoda takes Dooku as his apprentice instead.

~85 BBY - Protobranch Disaster

Defies the Council to warn the Republic about Sifo-Dyas's vision of a solar storm. The storm kills much of Protobranch's population, and Dooku's trust in the Council and the Senate is badly shaken.

~82 BBY - Mother's Funeral and Asusto

His father attacks him at Countess Anya's funeral. Soon afterward, cultists capture him on Asusto, where he sees visions of his future and uses Force lightning for the first time.

~80 BBY - Knight, Master and Teacher

Finishes his training and rises to Jedi Master, becoming known as one of the best lightsaber duelists of his generation. He takes Rael Averross as his first Padawan and trains him for about ten years.

68 BBY - Takes Qui-Gon Jinn as Padawan

At 34, chooses 12-year-old Qui-Gon Jinn as his apprentice. On Numidian Prime he saves Qui-Gon from the bounty hunter Shenda Mol with Force lightning.

~64–60 BBY - Senator Dagonet's Son

While helping recover a senator's kidnapped son, he watches the senator's troops massacre villagers. In his anger he reaches for the dark side and nearly chokes the senator to death before Qui-Gon stops him.

~58 BBY - Joins the Jedi High Council

Uncovers Master Yula Braylon as a leak on the Council after Ramil's airspeeder is sabotaged at the Dragonfire Air Rally. He accepts a Council seat, and Qui-Gon is knighted around the same time.

~50–48 BBY - Investigation on Raxus Secundus

With Mace Windu, looks into Master Katri's murder and finds corruption in the Senate. Windu is given the Council seat Dooku hoped for, which leaves Dooku resentful.

42 BBY - Leaves the Jedi Order

Becomes one of the Lost Twenty, returns to Serenno, overthrows his corrupt brother Ramil and takes the title of Count. He begins talking in secret with Darth Sidious.

32 BBY - Death of Qui-Gon and Fall to the Dark Side

Qui-Gon Jinn is killed at the Battle of Naboo. Dooku then kills Master Yaddle in a duel in the Works and becomes Sidious's apprentice under the name Darth Tyranus.

~32 BBY - Seizing the Clone Army

Has Sifo-Dyas killed, takes over the clone army Sifo-Dyas had ordered on Kamino, and recruits Jango Fett as the clones' template. Around the same time he kills his sister Jenza to prove his loyalty to Sidious.

~32–22 BBY - Architect of the Separatist Crisis

Uses his wealth and charm to draw thousands of star systems into a secessionist movement while the droid army is built up in secret.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

As Head of State of the Confederacy of Independent Systems, sees the Clone Wars break out. He cuts off Anakin Skywalker's arm, duels Yoda and escapes with the Death Star plans.

22–20 BBY - Master of the Separatist War

Commands the war through General Grievous and his dark apprentice Asajj Ventress. He is captured more than once, including by the pirate Hondo Ohnaka, and each time gets away.

~20 BBY - Betrayal of Ventress

On Sidious's orders, tries to have Ventress killed. He then survives a Nightsister plot against him involving Savage Opress, and he and Grievous later attack Dathomir.

19 BBY - The Quinlan Vos Affair

Takes the Jedi Quinlan Vos as a secret apprentice. Vos turns out to be loyal to the Jedi after all, and Dooku narrowly escapes.

19 BBY - Death at the Battle of Coruscant

Helps take Chancellor Palpatine hostage. Aboard the Invisible Hand, Anakin Skywalker defeats him and beheads him at Palpatine's urging.

Count Dooku's Timeline

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY (80 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY (80 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (80 years old)
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY (83 years old)

Plo Koon, born 100 BBY

Plo Koon was a Jedi Council master and one of the Republic's best starfighter pilots during the Clone Wars. He was known above all for how much he cared about the clone troopers under his command. As a young master he found a three-year-old Ahsoka Tano on Shili and brought her to the Jedi Temple, and he stayed a protective mentor to her for the rest of his life. He trained Bultar Swan to knighthood and sat on the Council that met Anakin Skywalker. He fought in the arena at Geonosis and survived, then took command of the 104th "Wolfpack" Battalion alongside Commander Wolffe. After his fleet was wiped out by the Malevolence's ion cannon, he flew escort for the attack that crippled the warship. He also escorted the evacuation of Felucia, hunted down a young Boba Fett, led the fleet that pulled the strike team out of the Citadel, and helped retake Felucia. He was part of the Council that expelled Ahsoka after the Temple bombing and later invited her to return. In the last days of the war he won the Battle of Cato Neimoidia. When Order 66 was issued, his own ARC-170 escort shot his fighter down over the planet's bridge cities, and he died in the crash.

Unknown - Birth on Dorin

Born on the Kel Dor homeworld. Canon gives no year. He was later taken into the Jedi Order and became the Padawan of Master Tyvokka.

34 BBY - Rescue on Oosalon

Arrives on Oosalon with Depa Billaba and Qui-Gon Jinn to back up Mace Windu after Windu defeats Warlord Guattako's army of kidnapped children.

33 BBY - Discovers Ahsoka Tano

On Shili he finds the three-year-old Togruta Ahsoka Tano, who had just escaped a false Jedi, and brings her to the Jedi Temple. The two stay close for the rest of his life.

~33 BBY - Council mission to Kwenn

Now a Council master, he travels to Kwenn with Saesee Tiin. They settle a labor dispute at Kwikhaul, help rescue a beached oroko, and he coordinates the taxi fleet that evacuates civilians during the pirate attack on the Bicentennial Celebration.

32 BBY - Naboo Crisis

Sits on the Council that doubts the Sith have returned and refuses to let Qui-Gon Jinn train Anakin Skywalker. After Jinn's death he attends the funeral on Naboo.

~22 BBY - Bultar Swan knighted

His Padawan Bultar Swan becomes a Jedi Knight shortly before the war.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

Joins the 212-member Jedi assault team in the Petranaki Arena, is among the survivors rescued by Yoda and the clone army, and fights in the first battle of the Clone Wars.

22 BBY - Hunting the Malevolence

Leads the 104th Battalion from the Triumphant. The Malevolence's ion cannon disables his fleet at Abregado. He holds out in an escape pod with Wolffe, Sinker and Boost until Anakin and Ahsoka rescue them.

22 BBY - Battle of the Kaliida Nebula

Flies the Blade of Dorin as escort for Shadow Squadron through a neebray herd. The squadron wrecks the Malevolence's ion cannon, and he then helps command the pursuit that ends with the ship's destruction.

21 BBY - Evacuation of Felucia

Leads fighters that break through the Separatist blockade to cover the gunships evacuating Kenobi, Skywalker and Tano's troops.

~21 BBY - Hunt for Boba Fett

Rescues Mace Windu and Anakin from the wreck of the Endurance on Vanqor. With Ahsoka he tracks Aurra Sing to Florrum, captures Boba Fett, and gets the hostages freed.

~21 BBY - Rescue from the Citadel

Briefs the strike team sent to free Even Piell, then leads the fleet to Lola Sayu that extracts the survivors. Afterward he covers for Ahsoka's unauthorized part in the mission.

~20 BBY - Second Battle of Felucia

Leads an assault on a droid outpost with Anakin and Ahsoka. When Trandoshan hunters kidnap Ahsoka, he alerts Republic forces to search for her and later welcomes her home.

~20 BBY - Rescue of Adi Gallia

Leads the Wolfpack aboard General Grievous's ship to free Adi Gallia.

19 BBY - Ahsoka's trial and departure

Sits on the Council that expels Ahsoka after the Temple hangar bombing. Once Barriss Offee is exposed, he and the Council offer to take her back, and she declines.

19 BBY - Battle of Cato Neimoidia

Commands the 442nd Siege Battalion to victory in the last days of the war.

19 BBY - Death during Order 66

While he flies over the bridge cities of Cato Neimoidia, his own ARC-170 wingmen open fire when Order 66 is issued. His starfighter is shot down and he dies in the crash.

Plo Koon's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY
  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY

Sheev Palpatine, born 84 BBY

The Sith Lord who brought down the Galactic Republic and ruled the galaxy as its Emperor, Sheev Palpatine led a double life for decades. In public he was a mild senator. In secret he was Darth Sidious, apprentice to Darth Plagueis. He killed his master and took the Zabrak child Maul from Dathomir to train as his own apprentice. Then he engineered the Trade Federation's invasion of his own homeworld, and the crisis it caused won him the office of Supreme Chancellor in 32 BBY. Working with his second apprentice, Count Dooku, he ran both sides of the Clone Wars. That let him gather emergency powers, turn Anakin Skywalker into Darth Vader and wipe out the Jedi with Order 66. In 19 BBY he declared himself Emperor. His Empire held the galaxy for a generation until the Rebellion rose against it. At the Battle of Endor in 4 ABY, Vader turned on him to save Luke Skywalker and threw him down a reactor shaft. Palpatine had planned for this. Using dark side transference, his spirit moved into a failing clone body on the hidden world of Exegol. From there he set the Contingency in motion to destroy what was left of the Empire, created Snoke to steer the First Order, and built the Final Order. He announced his return in 35 ABY but was finally destroyed at the Battle of Exegol by his granddaughter Rey, who turned his own lightning back on him with the strength of the Jedi behind her.

84 BBY - Birth on Naboo

Sheev Palpatine is born into the noble Palpatine family and grows up in Theed, where he learns politics and manipulation early.

65 BBY - Theed Power Generator Ceremony

As a rising figure in Naboo public life, Palpatine attends the opening of the Theed power generator.

52 BBY - Sith Apprentice and Senator

By now he is Darth Plagueis's apprentice, under the name Darth Sidious. With his master's help he is elected Senator of Naboo, which also makes him senator for the Chommell sector. Around this time he begins mentoring the young Wilhuff Tarkin.

42 BBY - Meeting Dooku

Rael Averross introduces Palpatine to Jedi Master Dooku. Later, after Dooku leaves the Order, Sidious draws him toward the Sith.

~40 BBY - Claiming Maul

On Dathomir, Sidious takes Mother Talzin's son Maul as his apprentice, breaking his promise to make Talzin his right hand.

Before 32 BBY - Murder of Darth Plagueis

Following the Rule of Two, Sidious kills his master in his sleep and becomes Sith Master. He trains Maul harshly and takes him to Malachor.

33 BBY - Testing Maul

Sidious rebukes Maul for his impatience and then tests his loyalty, setting him up to kill a Jedi Padawan in the Kellux system. He also has Maul kill the Bimm Voh, a former Jedi seneschal who had been passing him Jedi texts.

32 BBY - Invasion of Naboo

Sidious orders the Trade Federation to blockade and invade Naboo, and sends Maul after Queen Amidala. As Palpatine, he gets the Queen to call a vote of no confidence in Chancellor Valorum and is elected Supreme Chancellor. Maul is cut down by Obi-Wan Kenobi and presumed dead.

~32 BBY - The Clone Army

When he learns that Sifo-Dyas has ordered a clone army from Kamino, Sidious has Dooku kill him and take over the order.

22 BBY - Emergency Powers and the Clone Wars

During the Separatist Crisis, the Senate gives Palpatine emergency powers and he approves the Grand Army of the Republic. The Clone Wars begin at Geonosis, with Dooku, now Darth Tyranus, leading the Separatists.

22–19 BBY - Controlling Both Sides

Palpatine directs the war on both sides, piles up executive power, grooms Anakin Skywalker, and deals with the return of Maul and the Nightsisters.

19 BBY - Order 66 and the Empire

Palpatine lets Anakin kill Dooku, then reveals himself as a Sith. Mace Windu tries to arrest him and is killed, and Anakin becomes Darth Vader. Palpatine issues Order 66, declares himself Emperor, and fights Yoda to a standstill in the Senate chamber.

19 BBY–1 BBY - Imperial Reign

The Emperor rules largely unchallenged. He uses the Inquisitorius to hunt surviving Jedi and builds the Death Star.

1 BBY - Dissolution of the Senate

With the Death Star complete, the Emperor dissolves the Imperial Senate. The station is destroyed at the Battle of Yavin.

3 ABY - A New Target

The Emperor senses Luke Skywalker's power and orders Vader to turn the boy to the dark side or destroy him.

4 ABY - Death at Endor

On the second Death Star, the Emperor tries to replace Vader with Luke. Vader turns on him and throws him down a reactor shaft. His spirit passes into a clone body on Exegol.

5 ABY - The Contingency

His posthumous Contingency plan leads to the Battle of Jakku and the end of the Galactic Empire. Loyalists retreat to the Unknown Regions.

5 ABY–35 ABY - Rebuilding from Exegol

Kept alive by the Sith Eternal, Palpatine creates Snoke to lead the First Order and turn Ben Solo. He also builds the Final Order fleet of Xyston-class Star Destroyers.

35 ABY - Exegol Broadcast

Palpatine announces his return to the galaxy and destroys Kijimi to show the Final Order's power.

35 ABY - Final Death at Exegol

During the Battle of Exegol, he drains the life force of the dyad formed by Rey and Ben Solo to restore himself. Rey, backed by the voices of past Jedi, turns his lightning against him and destroys him for good.

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY (62 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY (62 years old)

  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY (88 years old)

Qui-Gon Jinn, born 80 BBY

Qui-Gon Jinn is best known as the Jedi who found Anakin Skywalker, the boy he believed was the Chosen One, and as the mentor whose death set Obi-Wan Kenobi's path. He was a maverick Jedi Master who followed the Living Force even when that meant defying the High Council. He grew up in the Jedi Temple and became Count Dooku's Padawan at twelve. He was knighted around 58 BBY, and in 44 BBY he took Kenobi as his own apprentice. Together they spent a year protecting Duchess Satine Kryze on Mandalore and uncovered a royal plot on Pijal. After Pijal he turned down a seat on the Jedi Council so he could keep training Kenobi. Qui-Gon also learned in secret how to keep his consciousness after death. In 32 BBY he was sent to end the Trade Federation's blockade of Naboo and rescued Queen Amidala. On Tatooine he met the enslaved boy Anakin, whose midi-chlorian count was higher than any on record, and fought off Darth Maul, which proved to him that the Sith had returned. The Council refused to train Anakin. Qui-Gon then went back to Naboo, where Maul mortally wounded him during the battle for Theed. With his last breath he made Kenobi promise to train the boy. Even in death he stayed part of the story: he returned as a voice and then as a Force spirit to guide Yoda and, years later, Kenobi.

~80 BBY - Birth on Coruscant

Qui-Gon Jinn is born on the galactic capital and later taken to the Jedi Temple, where he joins the Heliost Clan and learns lightsaber combat from Tera Sinube.

~68 BBY - Padawan of Dooku

At twelve he becomes Master Dooku's apprentice. He first sees the holocron of Jedi prophecies, which will fascinate him for the rest of his life.

~68 BBY - First battle on Shurrupak

Four months into his apprenticeship he goes into battle for the first time and meets Rael Averross, Dooku's former Padawan, who becomes a lifelong friend.

Hunting Shenda Mol

On a strike team to Numidian Prime, Qui-Gon is captured by the bounty hunter Shenda Mol. Dooku saves him with Force lightning, which troubles the young Padawan.

~64–60 BBY - Rescue of Senator Dagonet's son

Qui-Gon calms a deadly standoff in which Dooku nearly kills a corrupt senator, and Dooku praises him as the wiser man.

60 BBY - Trials on Felucia

During his trials he falls in love and has a relationship that tests his commitment to the Code. He stays in the Order but keeps a Mustafar fire diamond as a memento.

~58 BBY - Dragonfire Air Rally and knighthood

He and Dooku expose a spy on the Council. Dooku takes a Council seat, and Qui-Gon is made a Jedi Knight around the same time.

44 BBY - Takes Obi-Wan Kenobi as Padawan

At thirty-six, Qui-Gon takes on Obi-Wan Kenobi as his apprentice.

42 BBY - Dooku leaves the Order

His former master's departure shakes him deeply, but he regains his resolve.

~41–40 BBY - Protecting Satine Kryze

He and Kenobi spend a year on the run on Mandalore protecting Duchess Satine Kryze from bounty hunters until the civil war ends.

~41 BBY - Life Day on Kashyyyk

Visiting his Wookiee friend Brennonn, Qui-Gon is captured by Trandoshan hunters. Kenobi rescues him.

40 BBY - Missions to Teth and Pijal

After a mission against Wanbo the Hutt, he is offered a seat on the Jedi Council. On Pijal he uncovers Princess Fanry's plot and saves Czerka's slaves, then turns down the Council seat so he can keep training Kenobi.

~40 BBY - Escorting Lo for Yoda

He and Kenobi help Yoda after a raid on the Flesh Mongers and take the Force-sensitive child Lo to the Temple.

Secrets of eternal life

Taught by the Force Priestesses and a Shaman of the Whills, he secretly begins learning how to keep his consciousness after death.

37 BBY - Crisis of purpose on Bri'n

Called a warrior by Priestess Th'er, he goes looking for answers. A dark Force vision teaches him that balance must come without unchecked violence.

34 BBY - Mission to Oosalon

With Mace Windu, Plo Koon and Depa Billaba, he helps defeat the warlord Guattako, who had kidnapped children to fight for him.

32 BBY - Blockade and invasion of Naboo

He is sent to negotiate with the Trade Federation, survives their attempt to kill him, and rescues Queen Amidala from occupied Theed.

32 BBY - Discovery of Anakin Skywalker

Stranded on Tatooine, he meets young Anakin, wins his freedom in the Boonta Eve race, and fights Darth Maul briefly.

32 BBY - Before the Jedi Council

He insists that Anakin is the Chosen One. The Council refuses to train the boy.

32 BBY - Death on Naboo

In the Battle of Naboo, Darth Maul mortally wounds him. Dying, he makes Kenobi promise to train Anakin and becomes one with the Force.

19 BBY - Voice guiding Yoda

As a disembodied voice, he leads Yoda on a quest to learn how to become a Force spirit.

9 BBY - Appears to Kenobi

After Kenobi's confrontation with Darth Vader, Qui-Gon finally appears to his old apprentice as a Force spirit.

Qui-Gon Jinn's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY (48 years old)

Mace Windu, born 72 BBY

Mace Windu was the Jedi Order's greatest champion in the last years of the Republic. He sat on the Jedi High Council and served as Master of the Order, and he was one of the few Jedi ever to stand toe to toe with a Sith Lord and win. As a baby he lost his parents and was taken to the Temple. He trained as a Padawan under Cyslin Myr and later under Yoda, and became a Knight around 50 BBY. He took Katri's seat on the Council after the deadly mission to Raxus Secundus, which he carried out alongside his friend Dooku. He trained Depa Billaba and invented Vaapad, a fighting style that channels his own inner darkness into controlled aggression, and his amethyst lightsaber became a symbol of his authority. He doubted Qui-Gon Jinn's claim that the Sith had returned, and he never fully trusted Anakin Skywalker. Even so, he led the Jedi rescue at Geonosis, where he beheaded Jango Fett. In the Clone Wars that followed he served as a general, a diplomat and an adviser to Chancellor Palpatine. Freeing Ryloth earned him the name "Hammer of Ryloth." In 19 BBY Anakin revealed that Palpatine was Darth Sidious. Windu went to arrest him and defeated him, but then Anakin turned on him, and Sidious killed him. The Empire used his attempt to justify Order 66 and later painted him in its propaganda as a traitor and a gang leader.

72 BBY - Birth on Haruun Kal

Born to the most powerful members of his tribe. Like other toddlers on Haruun Kal, he gets an inoculation that leaves a small scar.

~71 BBY - Orphaned

Garu-bears kill his parents, but his mother hides him in a tree first. The tribe notices how strangely calm he is, and a visiting Jedi recognizes that he is Force-sensitive.

~71 BBY - Taken to the Jedi Order

In a tribal ritual he picks the shell of a lightsaber over a lushfruit and a puzzle pyramid. With the tribe's blessing, he is sent to the Jedi Temple to train.

~65 BBY - Youngling Trials

He struggles to build his first lightsaber around an amethyst kyber crystal until Professor Huyang teaches him patience. He spars with T'ra Saa and becomes friends with Sora Bulq.

60 BBY - Mission to Mathas

As Cyslin Myr's Padawan he helps capture Drooz, a con man posing as a Jedi prophet. Myr teaches him to rein in his anger and leave Drooz's punishment to the courts.

~50 BBY - Knighted

Finishes his training under Yoda and becomes a Jedi Knight. His early missions include protecting a kidnapped alien king, investigating Warden Mattis Coto at Devshi, and recovering the coaxium ultra formula on Ro Mira.

~50 BBY - Rescue of Depa Billaba

Rescues Depa Billaba and Sar Labooda from pirates and brings them to the Temple. He later trains Billaba all the way to knighthood.

~49 BBY - Jedi Master

Reaches the rank of Master in his early twenties, somewhere between 50 and 48 BBY. He creates the Vaapad fighting style with help from Sora Bulq.

~49 BBY - Incident on Raxus Secundus

He and Dooku recover the body of Master Katri and discover that her own Raxian guards killed her. After her funeral, Windu is given her seat on the High Council.

~45 BBY - Master of the Order

Becomes an elected leader of the Jedi, serving alongside Yoda, and carries a new electrum-finished lightsaber that marks his rank.

41 BBY - Ontotho Uprising

Helps end the conflict between the Daa Corporation and the Fylari, then knights Cere Junda for her heroism.

34 BBY - Mission to Oosalon

Lets himself be captured so he can find the warlord Guattako's hidden base. He puts his broken lightsaber back together with the Force and helps rescue 26 kidnapped children.

32 BBY - The Return of the Sith

Doubts Qui-Gon Jinn's report of a Sith warrior and turns down the request to train Anakin Skywalker. After Jinn's funeral on Naboo, he accepts that the Sith have survived.

~29 BBY - Watching Over Skywalker

Keeps a close eye on Anakin's training and reluctantly agrees to let Chancellor Palpatine mentor the boy.

26 BBY - Testing Kenobi and Skywalker

Helps stage Yoda's fake kidnapping to test Obi-Wan and Anakin as a team.

22 BBY - Separatist Crisis

Warns Palpatine that there aren't enough Jedi to protect the Republic. He assigns Kenobi and Skywalker to guard Padmé Amidala and follows Kenobi's discovery of the clone army.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

Leads a Jedi strike team into the Geonosian arena and beheads Jango Fett. This battle starts the Clone Wars.

22 BBY - Hammer of Ryloth

As a Jedi General, he frees Ryloth from Separatist occupation alongside Cham Syndulla.

~21 BBY - General of the Clone Wars

Leads clone troopers on the front lines, oversees the war effort from Coruscant, and serves as a diplomat and adviser to the Chancellor.

19 BBY - Suspicion of the Chancellor

Grows wary of Palpatine's increasing power and of Anakin's closeness to him, and senses the dark side gaining strength.

19 BBY - Death on Coruscant

After Anakin reveals that Palpatine is Darth Sidious, Windu leads Kit Fisto, Saesee Tiin and Agen Kolar to arrest him. Windu alone survives the fight and overpowers Sidious, but Anakin cuts off his hand and Sidious throws him to his death.

19 BBY - Legacy

Palpatine uses Windu's attempt to justify Order 66 and the Jedi Purge. Imperial propaganda brands him a criminal, but people like Cham Syndulla still remember him with respect.

Mace Windu's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY (40 years old)
  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY (50 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY (50 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (50 years old)
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY (53 years old)

Mas Amedda, born 70 BBY

Mas Amedda was the bureaucrat who stood at Palpatine's shoulder through the Republic's final years and then helped run the Empire that replaced it. As Vice Chair and Speaker of the Senate under Finis Valorum, he slowed relief for Naboo during the 32 BBY blockade and steered the chancellor into the missteps that led to his ouster. He then pledged himself to Palpatine, who later told him he was secretly the Sith Lord Darth Sidious. In 22 BBY Amedda nudged Jar Jar Binks into proposing the emergency powers that created the Grand Army of the Republic. During the Clone Wars he ran the secret Strategic Advisory Cell, which oversaw the early Death Star project and Orson Krennic's rise. He also managed the Senate floor for Palpatine, pushing the attempted occupation of Mandalore and the bank deregulation and clone funding bills. When the Empire was declared he stayed at Palpatine's side and presided over the burning of fallen Jedi's lightsabers, then served as Grand Vizier and ran the huge Imperial bureaucracy. After Palpatine died at Endor, Amedda was left as a powerless figurehead under Gallius Rax. Once the Empire collapsed at Jakku, he signed its surrender to the New Republic and stayed on as the nominal head of Coruscant's provisional government. History remembers him as a spineless sycophant.

32 BBY - Vice Chair Under Valorum

As Vice Chair and Speaker of the Senate, Amedda uses his bureaucratic power to slow relief for blockaded Naboo. Rumors say he is on the Trade Federation's payroll.

32 BBY - Valorum's Downfall

During Queen Amidala's appeal to the Senate, Amedda tells Valorum to sustain Lott Dod's objection. The advice helps provoke the Vote of No Confidence that removes Valorum from office.

32 BBY - Palpatine's Vice Chair

Amedda keeps his post after Palpatine is elected Supreme Chancellor and swears loyalty to him. Palpatine later reveals that he is Darth Sidious, and Amedda willingly joins his scheme.

~24 BBY - Strength and Unity Controversy

Amedda comes up with the Loyalist Committee's "Strength and Unity" propaganda poster. Because it includes his own image, the press accuses him of narcissism.

22 BBY - Emergency Powers

After the Separatists' droid army is revealed, Amedda talks Jar Jar Binks into proposing emergency powers for Palpatine, which lets the chancellor activate the clone army.

22 BBY - Launch of the Grand Army

Amedda stands with Palpatine and the Loyalist Committee to watch the clone army deploy after the First Battle of Geonosis.

22 BBY - Strategic Advisory Cell

Palpatine puts Amedda in charge of the Strategic Advisory Cell, the council that oversees the Republic's most secret military projects. One of his recruits is Orson Krennic.

~22 BBY - Zillo Beast Incident

Amedda backs Palpatine's decision to kill the Zillo Beast so it can be studied. The creature breaks loose and rampages across Coruscant.

21 BBY - Death Star Project

Amedda and Palpatine show the Cell the captured battle station plans. Amedda coordinates the project's early stages and reluctantly approves Krennic's plan to recruit Galen Erso.

~21 BBY - Mandalore Resolution

Amedda pushes the Senate to authorize an occupation of Mandalore over Duchess Satine Kryze's objections. The resolution is rescinded once Deputy Minister Jerec's undoctored testimony comes out.

~21 BBY - Pantora Blockade Hearings

Amedda presides over the Senate sessions on the Trade Federation's blockade of Pantora.

~20 BBY - Banking Deregulation and Clone Funding

Amedda helps defeat the Separatist peace initiative. He pushes through bank deregulation and Halle Burtoni's bill to buy five million more clones.

~20 BBY - Death of Onaconda Farr

Amedda attends Senator Farr's funeral and follows Inspector Tan Divo's investigation, which ends with Lolo Purs arrested for the murders.

20 BBY - Festival of Light

Amedda goes with Palpatine to Naboo, where Moralo Eval's bounty hunters try to kidnap the chancellor.

19 BBY - Rise of the Empire

Amedda is at Palpatine's side when the Empire is declared. He is present when Yoda confronts Sidious in the Senate chamber.

19 BBY - Burning of the Lightsabers

Amedda presides over a ceremony outside the burned Jedi Temple in which the lightsabers of fallen Jedi are destroyed.

19 BBY - Grand Vizier

Rewarded for his loyalty, Amedda becomes Grand Vizier and sits on the Imperial Ruling Council. He runs the Empire's bureaucracy from Coruscant and sometimes Byss.

4 ABY - Death of the Emperor

After Palpatine dies at Endor, Amedda is left as a powerless mouthpiece for the civilian government, controlled by Fleet Admiral Gallius Rax.

5 ABY - Surrender of the Empire

After the Battle of Jakku, Amedda signs the Galactic Concordance on Chandrila, surrendering the Empire to the New Republic.

5 ABY - Provisional Government

Amedda stays on as the nominal, powerless head of Coruscant's provisional government while the New Republic restores democracy.

Mas Amedda's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY
  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY

Nute Gunray, born 69 BBY

Nute Gunray is best remembered as the Trade Federation viceroy who blockaded and invaded Naboo, and whose greed made him a willing tool of Darth Sidious. He had already become viceroy when the Eriadu Conference emptied out the Federation's leadership, and with Sidious secretly behind him he took full control of the conglomerate. In 32 BBY he carried out the Sith Lord's plan: he blockaded Naboo, tried to kill the Jedi envoys Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi, and occupied the planet to force Queen Amidala to sign a treaty. The crisis ended when Amidala captured him in her own throne room. He then went through four Supreme Court trials without being convicted, kept his wealth and his titles, and came out of it with a lasting grudge against Amidala. He put the Federation's droid armies behind Count Dooku's Separatists in exchange for her assassination, which failed. From Geonosis he watched the Clone Wars begin. During the war he schemed against Amidala on Rodia, was captured by the Republic, and escaped with Asajj Ventress's help. When Dooku and Grievous were dead, he fled with the Separatist Council to Mustafar expecting the peace Sidious had promised. Instead Darth Vader killed the whole council, and Gunray was the last to die, begging for his life. Imperial records later claimed he had taken his own life.

34 BBY - Viceroy of the Trade Federation

Gunray has become Viceroy of the Trade Federation, with Darth Sidious quietly steering his rise.

33 BBY - Seizing control after Eriadu

The disastrous Eriadu Conference leaves a power vacuum at the top of the Federation. Gunray takes total control of the conglomerate, and Lott Dod takes its Senate seat.

32 BBY - Blockade of Naboo

After a trade-route taxation dispute, Gunray's fleet blockades Naboo from his flagship, the Saak'ak. On Sidious's orders he tries to kill the Jedi envoys Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi.

32 BBY - Occupation of Naboo

His droid army takes Theed and forces the population into camps. Gunray sits on Amidala's throne, holds Governor Sio Bibble prisoner, and keeps demanding that the queen sign his treaty.

32 BBY - Captured at the Battle of Naboo

Amidala tricks his droids with a decoy and takes Gunray and Rune Haako prisoner in the throne room. When the Droid Control Ship is destroyed, his army shuts down, and he is sent to Coruscant for trial.

32 BBY–28 BBY - Four Supreme Court trials

For four years expensive lawyers keep him out of trouble. The third trial ends in a hung jury and the fourth finds him not guilty, so he keeps his assets and titles. He blames Amidala and has the Federation spread rumors against her through the press.

24 BBY - Joining the Separatist movement

After Dooku's Raxus Address, Gunray allies with the new Confederacy of Independent Systems. His condition is that Senator Amidala be killed.

22 BBY - Plot against Amidala

Dooku hires Jango Fett and Zam Wesell to kill Amidala, and both attempts fail. On Geonosis, Gunray refuses to sign the Separatist treaty until he has her head on his desk.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

He watches the execution arena and the Jedi rescue, then flees as the clone army arrives. The Federation core ships are evacuated as the Clone Wars break out.

22 BBY - Mission on Nooroyo

Early in the war, Gunray goes to Nooroyo in person to negotiate a spice deal. Amidala and Anakin Skywalker are on the planet at the same time and have to leave in a hurry.

22 BBY - Trap on Rodia

He promises relief supplies to Rodia in return for Senator Onaconda Farr handing over Amidala. The plan backfires, and Gunray is captured by Republic forces.

22 BBY - Escape from the Tranquility

While he is being taken to Coruscant, the traitorous Senate Guard captain Faro Argyus and Asajj Ventress free him. He flees with Ventress, who then kills Argyus.

22 BBY - Bait on Vassek

A hologram of Gunray lures Kit Fisto and Nahdar Vebb into General Grievous's fortress, where Grievous is waiting for them.

~21 BBY - Lending the fleet to Cad Bane

On Sidious's orders, Gunray sends Federation warships to support Cad Bane at Devaron. He is furious when Bane loses ship after ship to the Republic.

19 BBY - Council on Utapau

After Dooku's death, Gunray openly challenges Grievous's ability to protect the Separatist Council. The council is then sent to Mustafar just before the Republic attacks Utapau.

19 BBY - Death on Mustafar

Sidious sends Darth Vader, supposedly to make peace. Vader kills the entire Separatist Council, and Gunray is the last to die, begging for his life. The Empire later records his death as a suicide and dissolves the Trade Federation.

~3 ABY - Remains found by Vader

Sidious leaves Vader stranded on Mustafar as punishment. There Vader finds Gunray's decaying body in the ruined Separatist complex and relives the massacre.

Nute Gunray's Timeline

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY

Bail Organa, born 67 BBY

Bail Organa led the loyal opposition in the Senate for decades and quietly helped found the Rebel Alliance. He was born Bail Prestor and gave up his family name to marry Breha Organa, heir to Alderaan's throne. After the Invasion of Naboo in 32 BBY he replaced Bail Antilles in the Galactic Senate, where he became known for his honesty and his strong defense of democracy. He worked alongside Mon Mothma, Onaconda Farr and Padmé Amidala, and was one of the Jedi Order's closest political friends. During the Clone Wars he pushed for relief missions, refugee aid and peace efforts, but hawkish rivals kept blocking him. He signed the Petition of 2,000 against Palpatine's growing power, only to watch the Republic become the Empire. In the chaos after Order 66 he helped Yoda and Obi-Wan Kenobi escape, and he and Breha adopted Padmé's infant daughter Leia, hiding her from her father, Darth Vader. Publicly he stayed in the Imperial Senate. In secret he used Alderaan's wealth to fund resistance cells, recruited Ahsoka Tano to coordinate them, and helped merge them into the Alliance to Restore the Republic in 2 BBY. On the eve of Scarif he sent Leia to find Kenobi and went home to warn his people that war was coming. He died days later when the Death Star destroyed Alderaan, and the rebellion he helped build remembered him as one of its great martyrs.

Date unknown - Birth as Bail Prestor

Born on Alderaan into House Prestor, one of the Elder Houses. As a boy he dreamed of chasing purrgil beyond Kathou, and he looked up to the Jedi Thraymus Argent, who investigated an incident in his family.

Date unknown - Marriage to Breha Organa

Married Alderaan's crown princess and took the Organa name. When Breha became queen he took on the consort's titles, including Viceroy, along with the roles of First Chairman and Governor of Alderaan.

~40–34 BBY - Rescue on Planet WG4587

While serving as junior senator, his corvette crashed on a world full of kaiju-like creatures called kaijura. Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi came to his rescue.

32 BBY - Senator of Alderaan

After the Invasion of Naboo he succeeded Bail Antilles in the Galactic Senate and resigned as governor. He befriended the newly elected Mon Mothma and advised Chancellor Palpatine.

28 BBY - Allying with Padmé Amidala

Led a progressive faction with Mothma and Onaconda Farr. He saved Amidala's double from a plot set in a condemned part of the Senate building, brought Amidala into his faction, and hosted her on Alderaan during the Senate recess.

28 BBY - The Bromlarch crisis

Urged Amidala to join the survey team sent to Bromlarch. The relief package that followed, the Mid Rim Cooperation Motion, was named after one of his own remarks.

24 BBY - Loyalist Committee

Joined Palpatine's Loyalist Committee during the Separatist Crisis while opposing both the Emergency Powers Act and the Military Creation Act.

22 BBY - Start of the Clone Wars

Heard Kenobi's report from Geonosis and argued that the Senate would not approve the clone army before a Separatist attack. He then watched the Grand Army of the Republic launch.

22–20 BBY - Humanitarian efforts

Led relief efforts for Christophsis and Ryloth, organized the Alderaan Refugee Conference, and pushed for peace. His faction's attempt to cut military spending ended with Onaconda Farr's murder.

19 BBY - Petition of 2,000

Joined the Delegation of 2,000 in calling on Palpatine to give up his emergency powers, then witnessed the Jedi being declared traitors and the Galactic Empire proclaimed.

19 BBY - Adoption of Leia

Rescued Yoda after Order 66, sheltered Kenobi, and adopted Padmé's daughter Leia with Breha. He also took Kenobi's newborn nephew Luke's safety into his circle of secrets.

~18 BBY - Recruiting Ahsoka Tano

After a run-in with the Inquisitorius, he brought Ahsoka Tano into his growing resistance movement.

9 BBY - Leia's kidnapping

When Leia was kidnapped, he called Obi-Wan Kenobi out of exile to bring her home.

~5–3 BBY - Coordinating rebel cells

Worked secretly with Ahsoka Tano and the Phoenix cell, including the Lothal ship deal that exposed Leia to the rebellion, while carefully keeping up his public role in the Imperial Senate.

2 BBY - Birth of the Rebel Alliance

After Mon Mothma denounced the Empire and fled the Senate, the scattered rebel cells joined together as the Alliance to Restore the Republic. Bail stayed in the Senate and later handed his seat to Leia.

1 BBY - Scarif and the Death Star

Sat on Alliance High Command during the Scarif crisis. He sent Leia to Tatooine to find Kenobi and went home to warn Alderaan that there would be no peace.

1 BBY - Death on Alderaan

Killed along with Breha and the rest of Alderaan's people when the Death Star destroyed the planet.

Bail Organa's Timeline
