Jango Fett, born 66 BBY

Jango Fett was the most feared bounty hunter of the Republic's last years, and the man whose DNA became the Grand Army of the Republic. After his parents were murdered, the Mandalorians took him in as a foundling. He trained under a mentor named Jaste, received his own armor and fought in the Mandalorian Civil Wars. He then left to work as a bounty hunter. Over many years he built his reputation with jobs like the Phaedra hunt for Jabba the Hutt and the search for the stolen Hope of Glee Anselm. He also stole the Firespray patrol craft that became Slave I and mentored the young Cad Bane. Soon after the Invasion of Naboo, Darth Tyranus chose him as the clone template on Kamino. His price was twenty million credits and one unaltered clone, whom he raised as his son Boba and taught the family trade. Ten years later he hired Zam Wesell for the plot to kill Senator Padmé Amidala. When she failed, he silenced her with a saberdart, and that dart led Obi-Wan Kenobi to Kamino and then to Geonosis. Jango died in the First Battle of Geonosis in 22 BBY. He shot down Jedi Master Coleman Trebor, but Mace Windu beheaded him. His legacy lived on in the millions of clones who carried out Order 66 and in Boba, who took over his ship and armor and became the galaxy's top bounty hunter in his turn.

66 BBY - Birth

Jango Fett is born. He is later tied to the Mandalorian colony world of Concord Dawn, though he likes to keep his true origins vague.

~55 BBY - Mandalorian Foundling

After his parents are murdered, the Mandalorians find the boy on Concord Dawn and take him in as a foundling. They raise him as a warrior, and he trains under a mentor known as Jaste.

~50 BBY - Mandalorian Civil Wars

Jango fights in the Mandalorian Civil Wars and receives his own set of Mandalorian armor.

~45 BBY - Turns to Bounty Hunting

He leaves the Mandalorians to become a bounty hunter. It takes him years of work to build a reputation that lets him pick his own jobs.

~40 BBY - Slave I

On a job at the prison moon Oovo 4, Jango steals a Firespray-31 patrol craft. He names it Slave I and fits it with heavy weapons, including seismic charges.

~40 BBY - Underworld Connections

He works often with the Clawdite Zam Wesell, becomes friends with the pirate Hondo Ohnaka, knows Aurra Sing and mentors the young Cad Bane.

Between 40 and 34 BBY - The Phaedra Job

Jabba the Hutt hires Jango to recover the thief Phaedra and the money she took from the Galactic Bank of Coruscant. On Malastare he makes a deal with her instead, helping her escape Marshal Buck Vancto for three times Jabba's fee.

~33 BBY - The Hope of Glee Anselm

Jango is hired to track the thieves who stole the Hope of Glee Anselm. On Nar Shaddaa he fights off a k'lor'slug, and the case brings him to Count Dooku's attention.

~32 BBY - Recruited by Tyranus

Soon after the Invasion of Naboo, Jango wins a competition set up by Dooku. On the moons of Bogden, Darth Tyranus hires him as the genetic template for the clone army being grown on Kamino.

~32 BBY - Boba Fett

On top of his twenty-million-credit fee, Jango asks for one unaltered clone. He names the boy Boba and raises him as his son in Tipoca City.

32–22 BBY - Overseeing the Clones

Jango helps design the clones' training programs and oversees their flash training. He takes no pride in the troopers and calls them livestock bred as cannon fodder.

32–22 BBY - Training Boba

Jango keeps taking bounties and uses them to teach Boba to pilot, survive, shoot and fight.

~22 BBY - Mission to Ord Mantell

On Boba's first mission, father and son join three hunters to bring back a runaway Twi'lek girl. When Rinn and Tiver betray them, Boba kills both and chooses to spare Neelda.

~22 BBY - The Wesell Run

Jango helps Zam Wesell capture the indebted collector Sinvatt Bovic on Fytoun. During the job he kills a Clawdite imposter and survives a bluebarb wasp dart.

22 BBY - Plot Against Amidala

Working for Dooku, Jango hires Wesell to kill Senator Padmé Amidala. After her attempts fail and the Jedi catch her, he kills her with a Kamino saberdart so she cannot name him.

22 BBY - Confronted on Kamino

Obi-Wan Kenobi questions Jango in Tipoca City and then fights him on the landing platform. Jango escapes to Geonosis with Boba, not knowing Kenobi has put a homing beacon on Slave I.

22 BBY - Chase Through the Asteroids

Jango hunts Kenobi through the Geonosian asteroid field and wrongly believes he has destroyed him.

22 BBY - Death on Geonosis

Jango captures Anakin Skywalker and Padmé Amidala in the droid factory and guards Dooku in the arena. He kills Jedi Master Coleman Trebor, but after a reek tramples him and damages his jetpack, Mace Windu beheads him. Boba mourns over his father's helmet.

Jango Fett's Timeline

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY (44 years old)

General Grievous, born 65 BBY

General Grievous was the Confederacy's most feared battlefield commander during the Clone Wars. He was a four-armed cyborg who hunted Jedi and kept their lightsabers as trophies. Born Qymaen jai Sheelal, he was a sniper by the age of eight. He rose to become a legendary warlord of the Kaleesh and took the name Grievous during his people's brutal war against the Yam'rii of Huk. When the Republic and the Jedi stepped in on the Yam'rii side, the sanctions that followed starved his people, and he swore revenge. He went on to serve the InterGalactic Banking Clan as an enforcer and slowly traded his body for machine parts. Then a shuttle crash, secretly arranged by Count Dooku, left him barely alive, and Separatist surgeons rebuilt him almost entirely. Only his brain, eyes, spinal cord and a few vital organs survived. Dooku trained him in lightsaber combat, and he became Supreme Martial Commander of the Separatist Droid Armies. Across the war he led countless campaigns, killed many Jedi and fought repeated duels with his nemesis, Obi-Wan Kenobi. In the war's final days in 19 BBY he led the attack on Coruscant and captured Chancellor Palpatine. After Dooku's death he became acting leader of the Separatists and fled to Utapau. There Kenobi tracked him down and killed him, and the Confederacy soon collapsed.

Before the Clone Wars - Birth on Kalee

Qymaen jai Sheelal is born in the wilds of Kalee while the Kaleesh are fighting a war against the insectoid Yam'rii of Huk.

Before the Clone Wars - Child sniper

His father teaches him to shoot an Outland rifle. By the age of eight he is serving as a sniper and has already killed forty enemies.

Before the Clone Wars - Warlord "Grievous"

He rises quickly to become a feared warlord and takes the name Grievous. He gathers Kalee's elite Izvoshra warriors as his personal bodyguard.

Before the Clone Wars - Campaigns against Huk

Grievous drives the Yam'rii off Kalee and takes the war to the Huk worlds. His successes catch the attention of Dooku and Darth Sidious.

Before the Clone Wars - Republic intervention

The Republic and the Jedi take the Yam'rii side. The sanctions that follow starve hundreds of thousands of Kaleesh, and Grievous swears vengeance.

Before the Clone Wars - Enforcer for the Banking Clan

Grievous becomes an enforcer for the InterGalactic Banking Clan and commands its droid army. He is forbidden to keep the Izvoshra, so he has the IG-100 MagnaGuards built and trains them himself.

Before the Clone Wars - Cybernetic transformation

With the Sith's encouragement, Grievous begins replacing parts of his body with cybernetic upgrades.

Before the Clone Wars - Shuttle crash and rebuilding

Dooku secretly sabotages his shuttle and blames the Jedi for the crash. Separatist and Geonosian surgeons rebuild the dying warlord as an almost entirely mechanical four-armed cyborg and alter his mind without his knowledge.

Before the Clone Wars - Lightsaber training

Dooku trains Grievous in lightsaber combat and gives him his first captured Jedi lightsaber. Grievous passes his skills on to his MagnaGuards.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

Grievous fights in secret in the Geonosian catacombs. He kills the Jedi who stumble on him so that the Separatist Council can escape, and none survive to report his existence.

22 BBY - Supreme Martial Commander

He is made a general and Supreme Martial Commander of the Separatist Droid Armies, second only to Dooku. Early in the war he scars Ki-Adi-Mundi.

~22 BBY - The Malevolence campaign

His flagship, the Malevolence, wipes out Republic fleets with its ion cannon until Anakin Skywalker's forces destroy it.

~22 BBY - Vassek 3

At his fortress on Vassek 3, Grievous fights Kit Fisto and kills Fisto's former Padawan, Nahdar Vebb.

~22 BBY - Hunting Jedi

Grievous captures Jedi Master Eeth Koth to lure Kenobi into a trap. The Republic rescues Koth, and Grievous escapes.

~21 BBY - Captured by pirates

Hondo Ohnaka's pirates briefly hold Grievous prisoner. Kenobi and Anakin nearly capture him before he gets away.

~20 BBY - Assault on Dathomir

On Dooku's orders, Grievous leads a droid army that massacres the Nightsisters of Dathomir.

~20 BBY - Return to Kalee

On his homeworld, Grievous clashes with Anakin Skywalker during a mission to recover the kidnapped Jedi A'Sharad Hett.

19 BBY - Clash with Maul and Mother Talzin

Grievous leads Separatist forces against Maul's Shadow Collective. Dooku and Grievous kill Mother Talzin.

19 BBY - Battle of Coruscant

Grievous leads the raid on the Republic capital and captures Chancellor Palpatine. Kenobi and Skywalker rescue him from the Invisible Hand, Dooku is killed, and Grievous escapes in a pod.

19 BBY - Leader of the Confederacy

As acting head of the Separatists, Grievous retreats to Utapau. On Sidious's orders he sends the Separatist Council to Mustafar.

19 BBY - Death on Utapau

Kenobi confronts him, cuts off two of his arms and chases him across the sinkhole city. He kills Grievous by firing a blaster into his organic gut-sack, and the Clone Wars end soon after.

General Grievous's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY

Wilhuff Tarkin, born 64 BBY

Wilhuff Tarkin was the cold architect of the Empire's rule by terror and the man who turned the Death Star on Alderaan. He came from a hard-edged Eriaduan dynasty that trained its heirs as hunters, and he made his name early by brutally crushing pirates in the Seswenna. After service with the Republic's Judicial Forces he became governor of Eriadu under Sheev Palpatine's patronage. He joined the Republic Navy when the Clone Wars broke out. As a captain he survived capture and torture in the Citadel on Lola Sayu, and the ordeal left him with a lasting regard for Anakin Skywalker and deep contempt for Jedi generalship. Promoted to admiral and then Adjutant General, he prosecuted Ahsoka Tano for the Jedi Temple bombing and pushed for her execution. Under the Empire he helped replace the clone army with conscripts, became the first Grand Moff with authority over the Outer Rim, and hunted rebels on worlds like Lothal. He also took control of the Death Star project from Orson Krennic. Convinced that fear alone could hold the galaxy together, he destroyed Jedha City, Scarif and Alderaan. He was still sure of victory when Luke Skywalker destroyed the station over Yavin, killing him with it.

~64 BBY - Birth on Eriadu

Born into the wealthy and demanding Tarkin family, and raised in the family compound to value discipline and obedience.

48 BBY - Trials on the Carrion Plateau

At sixteen, he finishes his wilderness training under his grand-uncle Jova by outwitting a pack of predators at the Carrion Spike. His training then moves on to space combat and piloting.

46 BBY - Crushing Q'anah's Marauders

Serving with the Outland Regions Security Force, he works out the pattern of the pirate Q'anah's raids, captures her ship and sends her crew slowly into a sun.

~45 BBY - Judicial training and meeting Palpatine

He attends the Sullust Sector Spacefarers Academy, where he meets Senator Palpatine, who helps him get into the Judicial Academy. He is later credited with saving his team during a mission to Halcyon.

36 BBY - Leaves the Judicial Forces

He resigns his rank to go into politics, as Palpatine had once suggested.

~34 BBY - Governor of Eriadu

With Palpatine's backing he becomes governor, drives immigrant communities off Eriadu, and helps undermine Chancellor Valorum around the Eriadu Conference.

~24 BBY - Rejecting Dooku

Over a series of dinners he turns down Count Dooku's attempts to bring Eriadu into the Separatist cause, which was secretly a loyalty test set by Sidious. He then decides not to seek reelection.

22 BBY - Joins the Republic Navy

When the Clone Wars begin he enlists as a captain and carries out covert missions for Chancellor Palpatine.

21 BBY - Murkhana and Kamino

He commands the assault on Murkhana and is decorated after the Battle of Kamino.

20 BBY - Captured and rescued from the Citadel

He is captured with Even Piell while carrying half of the Nexus Route in his memory, and is tortured at the Citadel on Lola Sayu. A Jedi team rescues him, and he forms a mutual respect with Anakin Skywalker.

20 BBY - Admiral at Carida

Now an admiral, he survives the Separatist rhydonium attack on the station Valor and writes a scathing report on Jedi command.

20–19 BBY - Special Weapons Group

He joins the Strategic Advisory Cell's group overseeing the battle station project, which puts him in rivalry with Krennic, and he questions Galen Erso.

19 BBY - Prosecutor of Ahsoka Tano

He leads the military's investigation of the Jedi Temple bombing and is made Adjutant General. He prosecutes Ahsoka Tano and asks for the death penalty until Barriss Offee confesses.

19 BBY - Rise of the Empire

He stands with Palpatine at the Empire's founding and becomes a regional governor.

19–18 BBY - Project War-Mantle

With Vice Admiral Rampart he oversees the replacement of clones with conscripted stormtroopers, including the operation that destroys Tipoca City on Kamino.

~14 BBY - Grand Moff of the Outer Rim

He becomes the Empire's first Grand Moff and forms an unofficial triumvirate with the Emperor and Darth Vader.

5 BBY - Arrival on Lothal

He arrives on Lothal to deal with the Spectres, executes the officers who failed to stop them, and captures Kanan Jarrus.

1 BBY - Seizing the Death Star

He tests the superlaser on Jedha City, takes command of the station from Krennic, and destroys the Scarif base during the rebel raid.

1 BBY - Destruction of Alderaan

To make an example of the planet and pressure Princess Leia, he orders Alderaan destroyed.

1 BBY - Death at the Battle of Yavin

He refuses to evacuate and is killed when Luke Skywalker destroys the Death Star.

Wilhuff Tarkin's Timeline

Cad Bane, born 62 BBY

Feared as the deadliest bounty hunter of the Clone Wars, Cad Bane was a cold, wide-brimmed gunslinger who would take any job for the right price and made a specialty of beating Jedi. He started out as a street orphan named Colby who was pulled into the gangster Lazlo's crew. After Lazlo was killed, he went after revenge, gunned down a marshal, served time on Torano and later killed his childhood friend Niro. Mentored for a short time by Jango Fett, he built his reputation working for Darth Maul before the Battle of Naboo and became the top hunter in the galaxy after Jango died. For Darth Sidious he stole a holocron from the Jedi Temple, forced Anakin Skywalker to unlock a kyber memory crystal and kidnapped Force-sensitive children. For the Hutts he took the Senate hostage to free Ziro, and later fought Obi-Wan Kenobi and Quinlan Vos to a standstill on Teth. He came through Count Dooku's deadly tournament in the Box and led the plot to kidnap Chancellor Palpatine, but was arrested once Kenobi's cover as Rako Hardeen was blown. He kept working under the Empire and hunted the clone girl Omega, but Fennec Shand and Clone Force 99 took her back from him. In 9 ABY he was an enforcer for the Pyke Syndicate on Tatooine, where his former protégé Boba Fett beat him in a duel and killed him in the streets of Mos Espa.

~62 BBY - Birth on Duro

Born as Colby. He never knew his parents and grew up an orphan on the streets of a crime-ridden city alongside his friend Niro.

Early life - Recruited by Lazlo

After watching Colby and Niro con a droid, the gangster Lazlo takes them on for a raid on a shop's casino. Niro is caught by police, and Colby escapes with Lazlo's gang, who give him his friend's share.

Early life - Becomes Cad Bane

Now calling himself Cad Bane, he is a member of Lazlo's gang and in a relationship with Arin, another gang member.

Early life - Revenge for Lazlo

When a marshal kills Lazlo, Bane takes his hat and hunts the lawman down. He kills the marshal in a showdown, but Arin is wounded, and Niro, now a deputy, has him arrested.

Early life - Prison and the killing of Niro

Bane serves time on Torano and is freed five years early on a technicality. He comes home, discovers that Arin married Niro, kills Niro in a gunfight and leaves town after meeting the boy Isaac.

Before 32 BBY - Mentored by Jango Fett

At the start of his bounty hunting career, Jango Fett mentors Bane for a short time. Bane ends up owing him favors he never repays.

Before 32 BBY - Hired by Darth Maul

On Nar Shaddaa, Bane, Aurra Sing and other hunters help Maul capture the Padawan Eldra Kaitis from Xev Xrexus's auction. They survive a crash on the Moon of Drazkel and the hunters sent after them.

22 BBY - Top of the trade

With Jango Fett dead at Geonosis, Bane is regarded as the best bounty hunter in the galaxy. He works for the Separatists and the Hutts.

~21 BBY - Raid on the Jedi Temple

Darth Sidious hires Bane to steal a holocron from the Jedi Temple's vault. With help from Cato Parasitti and Todo 360, he gets away with it.

~21 BBY - Battle of Devaron

Bane captures Jedi Master Bolla Ropal and his kyber memory crystal, and Ropal dies under torture. Bane then holds Ahsoka Tano hostage to make Anakin Skywalker open the holocron, fakes his own death and escapes.

~21 BBY - Kidnapping Force-sensitive children

For Sidious he abducts Wee Dunn and Zinn Toa. He is caught on Naboo while trying to take Roo-Roo Page, leads the Jedi into a trap at Black Stall Station and escapes.

~21 BBY - Senate hostage crisis

Working for Jabba and the Hutt Council, Bane's crew takes senators hostage in the Senate Building and trades them for Ziro the Hutt's release.

~21 BBY - Hunt for Ziro

Hired again to find Ziro after he escapes, Bane tracks him to Teth and finds him already dead. He fights Obi-Wan Kenobi and Quinlan Vos there and gets away.

~20 BBY - Prison break with Moralo Eval

Bane lets himself be locked up so he can break Moralo Eval out of the Republic's Central Detention Center. With help from Boba Fett and Bossk they escape, taking "Rako Hardeen" with them.

~20 BBY - The Box and the plot against Palpatine

Bane kills Bulduga for his hat on Serenno, survives Count Dooku's tournament in the Box and oversees the plot to kidnap Chancellor Palpatine. The plan fails because Hardeen is Kenobi in disguise, and Bane is arrested.

19 BBY - The hunt for Omega

Now working under the new Empire, Bane is hired by Lama Su to bring back the clone Omega. He catches her, but Fennec Shand and Clone Force 99 take her from him.

9 ABY - Enforcer for the Pykes

Bane works as the Pyke Syndicate's enforcer on Tatooine while they push the spice trade into Mos Espa. He shoots down Cobb Vanth and taunts his old protégé Boba Fett.

9 ABY - Death in Mos Espa

During the fight for Mos Espa, Bane duels Boba Fett in the street. Fett beats him and runs him through with his gaffi stick, killing him.

Jar Jar Binks, born 60 BBY

Jar Jar Binks is best remembered as the clumsy, good-hearted outcast whose actions helped save his world and then, without his meaning to, helped end the Republic. Boss Nass had banished him from Otoh Gunga for wrecking a heyblibber submarine. During the Trade Federation's invasion of Naboo in 32 BBY, Qui-Gon Jinn saved his life and he attached himself to the Jedi. He guided them through the planet's core, travelled with Queen Amidala to Tatooine and Coruscant, and then pointed her to the Gungan Grand Army. That tip led to the alliance between Gungans and Naboo that won the Battle of Naboo, and Jar Jar himself fought in it as a newly promoted Bombad General. He later became a Junior Representative in the Galactic Senate. In 22 BBY, while filling in for Senator Amidala, he proposed that Chancellor Palpatine be given emergency powers to raise a clone army. That motion started the Clone Wars. During the war he flew diplomatic and field missions: he helped secure supplies from Toydaria, saved Padmé on Rodia, took command after a shuttle crash on Florrum, brought the Gungan army to Mon Cala, and posed as Boss Lyonie to capture General Grievous. He also rescued Queen Julia of Bardotta alongside Mace Windu. After Padmé's funeral and the rise of the Empire, his own people shunned him for his part in Palpatine's rise. By 5 ABY he was a street clown in Theed, disliked by adults but loved by children. There he befriended the burned refugee orphan Mapo and took him on as his clowning apprentice.

32 BBY - Banished from Otoh Gunga

Boss Nass exiles Jar Jar from the underwater city for crashing his personal heyblibber submarine.

32 BBY - Meeting Qui-Gon Jinn

While Jar Jar is foraging, he runs into the Trade Federation's invasion force. Qui-Gon Jinn saves his life, and Jar Jar declares a life debt and leads the Jedi to Otoh Gunga.

32 BBY - Through the Planet Core

Jar Jar guides Qui-Gon and Obi-Wan Kenobi by bongo submarine to Theed. They survive an opee sea killer and a colo claw fish on the way.

32 BBY - Journey to Tatooine

Jar Jar escapes Naboo with Queen Amidala, the first time he has left his world. In Mos Espa he gets into trouble with Sebulba and meets Anakin Skywalker.

32 BBY - The Gungan Grand Army

The Senate fails to help Naboo on Coruscant. Jar Jar tells Amidala about the Gungan Grand Army and leads her to the Gungans' Sacred Place, where the two peoples agree to an alliance.

32 BBY - Battle of Naboo

Boss Nass promotes Jar Jar to Bombad General, and he leads Gungan forces on the Great Grass Plains. Anakin then destroys the Droid Control Ship. Jar Jar attends Qui-Gon's funeral and the victory celebrations.

~32 BBY - Hunt for the Blobball

Jar Jar and Queen Amidala follow a map from Boss Nass to a supposed Gungan relic. It turns out to be an old delicacy.

~32–22 BBY - Junior Representative

Padmé brings Jar Jar into the Naboo delegation to the Galactic Senate as a Junior Representative.

22 BBY - Emergency Powers

While standing in for Senator Amidala, who has gone into hiding, Jar Jar proposes that Chancellor Palpatine receive emergency powers. The motion passes and the Grand Army of the Republic is created.

22 BBY - Mission to Toydaria

Jar Jar distracts Senator Lott Dod at a banquet so that Bail Organa can load relief supplies for Ryloth.

22 BBY - Mission to Rodia

Separatist droids take Jar Jar for a Jedi. He befriends the Kwazel Maw Bogey and helps rescue Padmé and C-3PO from Nute Gunray.

22 BBY - Ransom on Florrum

Senator Kharrus dies in a shuttle crash, leaving Jar Jar in charge of the surviving clones. He defeats Turk Falso's pirates, and the power blackout he causes helps free Kenobi and Anakin.

22 BBY - Blue Shadow Virus

Jar Jar helps expose Dr. Nuvo Vindi's secret laboratory on Naboo and helps keep the virus from escaping.

~21 BBY - Battle of Mimban

After Jedi Master Laan Tik is killed, Jar Jar takes command and uses Tik's lightsaber to save Captain Rex.

~21 BBY - War on Mon Cala

Jar Jar persuades the Gungan High Council to send the Grand Army to Mon Cala. When Riff Tamson cracks Padmé's helmet, Jar Jar seals it with his spit.

~20 BBY - Capturing Grievous

Jar Jar impersonates the mind-controlled Boss Lyonie to call off a Gungan attack on Theed, and helps capture General Grievous. Roos Tarpals is killed in the fight, and Grievous is later traded for Anakin.

~20 BBY - Mission to Bardotta

Jar Jar and Mace Windu rescue Queen Julia from the Frangawl Cult and Mother Talzin on Zardossa Stix.

19 BBY - Birth of the Empire

Jar Jar welcomes Palpatine back after the Battle of Coruscant and is in the Senate chamber when the Republic becomes the Galactic Empire.

19 BBY - Padmé's Funeral

Jar Jar walks with Boss Nass among the chief mourners at Padmé Amidala's funeral on Naboo.

~19 BBY–4 ABY - Second Exile

Gungans and Naboo shun Jar Jar again for his part in Palpatine's rise to power.

5 ABY - Street Clown of Theed

Jar Jar now performs in Theed's Plaza of the Catalan. There he befriends Mapo, a burned refugee orphan, and agrees to teach him to be a clown.

Jar Jar Binks's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY
  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY

Thrawn, born 59 BBY

Grand Admiral Thrawn is the Empire's most feared strategist, a commander who beat his enemies by studying their art and culture until he could predict how they would act. He was born into the small, poor Kivu family, and his talent got him adopted into the powerful Mitth family as a merit adoptive. At the Taharim Academy he was accused of cheating after posting a record score on a combat simulation, then cleared himself by repeating the tactic in a live exercise. He rose through the Chiss Expansionary Defense Fleet, hunting pirates and fighting the Vagaari, a campaign that cost him his adoptive brother Thrass. Near the end of the Clone Wars he fought alongside Anakin Skywalker at Mokivj. He then broke the Nikardun Destiny at Primea, defused a Grysk-backed plot to start a Chiss civil war, and led an alliance of neighboring nations to victory at Sunrise. That victory made him too controversial to keep, so he went into exile in the service of both the Ascendancy and the Empire. With cadet Eli Vanto as his translator and protégé, he graduated from the Royal Imperial Academy in three months and climbed to Grand Admiral in command of the Seventh Fleet. From his flagship Chimaera he hunted the Lothal rebels, crushed them at Atollon, and worked with Darth Vader against the Grysks. During the liberation of Lothal, purrgil dragged the Chimaera through hyperspace, and he was stranded on the distant world of Peridea alongside Ezra Bridger. Years later his protégée Morgan Elsbeth and the Great Mothers brought him back, and he returned to the galaxy to plan a revival of Imperial power.

~59 BBY - Birth on Rentor

Born Kivu'raw'nuru, core name Vurawn, into the small and poor Kivu family.

~56 BBY - Loss of His Sister

His older sister Vurika is taken to serve as a sky-walker navigator. Only three years old, he believes she has died.

~39 BBY - Adopted into the Mitth Family

Thurfian interviews him, and Patriarch Thooraki quietly arranges his immediate adoption as a merit adoptive. He becomes Mitth'raw'nuru, "Thrawn." On his first spaceflight, to the Taharim Academy on Naporar, he meets Al'iastov, and at his rematching dinner on Avidich he meets Thrass.

~39 BBY - Cheating Accusation at Taharim

Accused of cheating on a combat simulation after a record score, he proves his innocence in a live exercise arranged by General Ba'kif at the urging of Senior Cadet Ziara (Ar'alani).

~37 BBY - Commissioned as Lieutenant

Graduates from the Taharim Academy. Thrass declares his friendship, which secretly settles a Stybla offer to rematch him into their family.

Before 19 BBY - Pirate Hunts and the Vagaari

As a Junior Commander and later senior officer, he fights pirates on the Ascendancy's frontier and leads operations against the Vagaari, during which Thrass is killed.

~19 BBY - Mission to Mokivj

Travels into known space looking for allies against the Nikardun and fights alongside Jedi Anakin Skywalker at Mokivj during the Clone Wars.

~19 BBY - Battle over Primea

Defeats General Yiv's fleet, bringing about the collapse of the Nikardun Destiny.

~18 BBY - Hoxim Incident

Defuses the Agbui scheme, backed by the Grysk agent Jixtus, to set three Chiss families against each other in civil war.

~18 BBY - Last Stand at Sunrise

Unites several allied nations to defeat Jixtus's Grysk fleet above Sunrise.

~18 BBY - Exile

Under political pressure over his methods, he is exiled with Supreme General Ba'kif's quiet blessing, so that he can study the Galactic Empire as a possible ally against the Grysks.

~14 BBY - Discovered by the Empire

Captain Voss Parck of the Strikefast finds him in Wild Space. He is presented to Emperor Palpatine and pledges his service.

~14 BBY - Royal Imperial Academy

With cadet Eli Vanto as his translator and aide, he learns Basic and graduates from the Coruscant academy in three months.

2 BBY - Promoted to Grand Admiral

Defeats the insurgent Nightswan at Batonn, is promoted to Grand Admiral by the Emperor, and sends Eli Vanto to the Chiss Ascendancy.

2 BBY - Lothal Campaign Begins

Takes command of the Seventh Fleet from the Chimaera at Governor Pryce's request to destroy the Lothal rebels, and champions the TIE Defender program.

1 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Finds and crushes Phoenix Cell's base on Atollon, though Chopper Base's survivors escape.

1 BBY - Mission with Darth Vader

Sent by the Emperor with Vader to Batuu. At Mokivj they defeat the Grysks and rescue captive Chiss children.

1 BBY - Project Stardust Supply Lines

Exposes Grand Admiral Savit's treason and defeats a Grysk force threatening Death Star supply lines.

~0 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

During the rebels' final assault, purrgil wrap around the Chimaera and jump it into hyperspace, stranding Thrawn and Ezra Bridger far beyond the galaxy.

0 BBY–9 ABY - Stranded on Peridea

Remains stranded on Peridea while the Empire falls. Remnant loyalists rely on his knowledge of the Unknown Regions.

9 ABY - Return from Peridea

With help from Morgan Elsbeth and the Great Mothers, escapes Peridea aboard the Chimaera, leaving Ahsoka Tano and Sabine Wren behind, and sets course for Dathomir.

Thrawn's Timeline

  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (54 years old)
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY (68 years old)

Luthen Rael, born 58 BBY

The hidden architect of the early Rebellion, Luthen Rael spent nearly two decades running a secret spy network against the Empire while posing as a refined antiques dealer on Coruscant. Born under the name Lear, he served as an Imperial Army sergeant until he was ordered to take part in a massacre around 18 BBY. Sickened by it, he deserted and took with him a young survivor he named Kleya Marki, who grew up to be his second-in-command. From about 15 BBY he built the web of operatives, informants and financiers that the ISB would come to call "Axis." Mon Mothma's money funded it, he armed Saw Gerrera's Partisans, and he ran a mole, Lonni Jung, inside the ISB itself. In 5 BBY he recruited Cassian Andor from Ferrix and sent him on the Aldhani heist, a raid meant to provoke the Empire into a crackdown that would turn people against it. Rael was ruthless. He let Anto Kreegyr's fighters walk into a trap to protect his source, had Tay Kolma eliminated, and shot Jung once his cover was blown. When Dedra Meero finally identified him in 1 BBY, he stabbed himself rather than be captured. Imperial medics kept him alive for interrogation, but Kleya slipped into the hospital and let him die. Before his fall he had passed Jung's warning about Krennic's secret weapon to the Alliance, and the Rebellion he had sacrificed everything for went on to win without him.

~18 BBY - Massacre and Desertion

Lear, an Imperial Army sergeant from Fondor serving as a swamp trooper, has a crisis of conscience during a massacre. He deserts and takes with him a young survivor he later names Kleya Marki.

~16 BBY - Bombing on Naboo

Now calling himself Luthen Rael, he stages one of his first attacks, bombing an Imperial convoy on Naboo. Kleya is with him, but at the last moment he presses the detonator himself.

15 BBY - Foundations of a Movement

Rael starts building a counter-Imperial movement, knowing it will cost him any ordinary life.

~10 BBY - Galactic Antiquities

He opens Galactic Antiquities and Objects of Interest on Coruscant as a cover. The gallery gives him access to the elite, and he cultivates allies such as Mon Mothma, Saw Gerrera and the ISB mole Lonni Jung.

5 BBY - Recruiting Cassian Andor

Rael goes to Ferrix to buy a stolen Starpath unit and instead recruits Cassian Andor. The two fight their way past Preox-Morlana security to escape.

5 BBY - The Aldhani Heist

He places Andor on Vel Sartha's team, which robs the Aldhani Garrison of a sector's payroll. Afterward he tells Mon Mothma the point was to provoke the Empire into cracking down harder.

5 BBY - The Spellhaus Sacrifice

Jung warns him that the ISB has set a trap for Anto Kreegyr's attack on Spellhaus. Rael refuses to let Jung quit, persuades Saw Gerrera to let Kreegyr's group be wiped out so the mole stays hidden, and escapes an Imperial Arrestor cruiser on the way out.

5 BBY - Ferrix Uprising

He returns to Ferrix intending to kill Andor at Maarva Andor's funeral. Instead, after the riot, he accepts Andor into the rebellion.

4 BBY - Mothma Family Wedding

Rael attends Leida Mothma's wedding on Chandrila as the curator of a gift. While there he has Cinta Kaz eliminate Tay Kolma, who had become a security risk.

3 BBY - Ghorman Intrigue

Rael sends Andor to assess the Ghorman Front. When Andor turns the job down, Rael sends Vel Sartha and Cinta Kaz in his place. With Kleya's help he also removes the listening device from Davo Sculdun's collection during the Senate Investiture party.

2 BBY - Ghorman Massacre and Mothma's Escape

Rael sends Andor to kill Dedra Meero on Ghorman, but the attempt fails amid the massacre. He then warns Mothma that Bail Organa's extraction team has been compromised and arranges for Andor to get her off Coruscant.

1 BBY - Killing Lonni Jung

Jung tells him about Krennic's secret weapon project and Galen Erso. Rael then shoots Jung and passes the intelligence to Kleya to send on to the Alliance.

1 BBY - Death on Coruscant

Cornered by Dedra Meero in his gallery, Rael stabs himself. Imperial medics keep him alive at Lina Soh Hospital, but Kleya infiltrates the building and disconnects his life support, and he dies.

Luthen Rael's Timeline

  • Andor, 5 BBY

Obi-Wan Kenobi, born 57 BBY

Obi-Wan Kenobi is remembered as the Jedi who trained two generations of Skywalkers. He was the father Anakin rebelled against and the mentor who later set Luke on his path. As a rule-breaking youngling he was apprenticed to the unorthodox Qui-Gon Jinn, and together they took missions to Mandalore, where he fell for Duchess Satine Kryze, and to Teth and Pijal. At Naboo he watched Darth Maul kill his master, then cut the Sith in half, which made him a Jedi Knight. Keeping his promise to the dying Qui-Gon, he took the young Anakin as his Padawan. In the Clone Wars he served as a Jedi General and Council member, earned the nickname "the Negotiator," fought beside Commander Cody, and lost Satine to Maul's revenge. He ended the war by killing General Grievous on Utapau. After Order 66 he faced Anakin, by then Darth Vader, on Mustafar and left him broken. He then went into exile on Tatooine as the hermit Ben, watching over the infant Luke. He left the desert once to rescue a kidnapped young Leia, a mission that brought him face to face with Vader again. Years later he killed Maul for good. In 1 BBY he gave Luke his father's lightsaber and let Vader strike him down on the Death Star so the others could escape. After becoming one with the Force, he kept guiding Luke through the Galactic Civil War, and his voice was among those that helped Rey on Exegol.

57 BBY - Birth on Stewjon

Obi-Wan is born on Stewjon. Later in life he keeps only faint memories of his parents and of a baby he believes was his brother.

57 BBY - Taken to the Jedi Temple

Because he is Force-sensitive, he is brought to the Jedi Temple on Coruscant before he is six months old and raised in the Kybuck Clan of younglings.

54 BBY - First Force training

At three he begins learning basic Force techniques. Yoda teaches him, and Cin Drallig trains him in lightsaber forms. As a youngling he gets a reputation for breaking rules.

~44 BBY - Padawan of Qui-Gon Jinn

At thirteen he is chosen by Qui-Gon Jinn. The two find it hard to work together, and he builds his first lightsaber.

~41 BBY - Mission to Lenahra

Angry that his master has not shown up, the sixteen-year-old leaves for Lenahra alone. There he meets Dexter Jettster, who becomes a lifelong friend.

~41 BBY - Protecting Satine Kryze

He spends a year on Mandalore with Qui-Gon guarding Duchess Satine Kryze from insurgents and bounty hunters. He falls in love with her but stays loyal to the Jedi Code.

40 BBY - Incident on Teth

A mission to Teth goes wrong, and he is briefly taken hostage by Wanbo the Hutt's majordomo. Afterward he feels he has let his master down.

40 BBY - Crisis on Pijal

He and Qui-Gon uncover a plot by Princess Fanry. Standing in as the Republic's representative, he helps Pijal set up a democratic Assembly.

32 BBY - Invasion of Naboo

He survives the Trade Federation's attempt to kill him and Qui-Gon, helps rescue Queen Amidala, and first meets the young Anakin Skywalker on Tatooine.

32 BBY - Duel with Darth Maul

After Maul kills Qui-Gon in Theed, Obi-Wan cuts the Sith Lord in half and promises his dying master that he will train Anakin.

32 BBY - Knighted and takes Anakin as Padawan

The Jedi Council makes him a Knight and, with reluctance, lets him train Anakin Skywalker.

~30 BBY - Trials of a young master

He takes Anakin on missions such as the one to Dallenor and brings him to Ilum to build his first lightsaber.

22 BBY - Kamino and Geonosis

He finds the secret clone army on Kamino, is captured on Geonosis, and fights Count Dooku as the Clone Wars break out.

22 BBY - High Jedi General

He is given command of the Open Circle Fleet, the Third Systems Army, and the 7th Sky Corps, with Commander Cody as his second, and becomes a Jedi Master on the High Council.

~20 BBY - Death of Satine

Maul returns seeking revenge, takes over Mandalore, and kills Satine in front of Obi-Wan.

19 BBY - Battle of Utapau

He kills General Grievous, which effectively ends the Clone Wars.

19 BBY - Order 66 and Mustafar

He survives the clones' betrayal and learns that Anakin has become Darth Vader. On Mustafar he defeats his former apprentice and leaves him maimed.

19 BBY - Exile on Tatooine

He hands the infant Luke to the Lars family and settles in the desert as the hermit Ben Kenobi.

9 BBY - Rescue of Leia Organa

He comes out of exile to rescue the kidnapped young Leia. Inquisitors hunt him, and in two duels he confronts Vader.

~2 BBY - Final duel with Maul

When Maul tracks him down on Tatooine, Obi-Wan kills him to keep Luke safe.

1 BBY - Meeting Luke

He rescues Luke from Tusken Raiders, gives him Anakin's lightsaber, and hires Han Solo and Chewbacca to fly them to Alderaan.

1 BBY - Death aboard the Death Star

He switches off the tractor beam, then lets Vader strike him down so Luke and his friends can escape. He becomes one with the Force.

0 BBY - Voice at Yavin

His voice tells Luke to use the Force during the run on the Death Star's trench.

3 ABY - Guiding Luke to Dagobah

His Force spirit appears to a freezing Luke on Hoth and sends him to Yoda.

4 ABY - Reunion with Anakin

He tells Luke the truth about his family, and after Endor he appears beside Yoda and the redeemed Anakin.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

His voice joins the voices of the Jedi who give Rey strength against the returned Palpatine.
