Bib Fortuna, born 57 BBY

For decades Bib Fortuna was the pale, whispering gatekeeper at Jabba the Hutt's side. As Jabba's majordomo he ran the daily business of the Tatooine palace and decided who got to see the crime lord, while secretly hating his master and waiting for a chance to kill him and take over his empire. He had grown rich selling fellow Twi'leks into slavery, and he beat his rival Bidlo Kwerve for the majordomo job. He held the post by the time of the 32 BBY Boonta Eve Classic, where he woke a dozing Jabba for the end of Anakin Skywalker's winning race. In the early days of the Empire he paid Cid to recover the young rancor Muchi, a job Clone Force 99 carried out. He also handed out work to Han Solo and Chewbacca, stood beside Jabba when Darth Vader came to hire bounty hunters, and waited impatiently for Boba Fett to deliver the frozen Han Solo. Luke Skywalker got past him with a Jedi mind trick, and he survived the destruction of the Khetanna at the Great Pit of Carkoon despite being presumed dead. He then took over Jabba's palace as a bloated crime lord in his own right. His rule ended in 9 ABY, when Boba Fett and Fennec Shand stormed the palace and Fett shot him on his throne and took it for himself.

Unknown - Early life on Ryloth

Born into the Fortuna clan, a prominent family with close ties to the criminal underworld, he gets rich as a slave trader selling fellow Twi'leks.

Before 32 BBY - Becomes Jabba's majordomo

Enters Jabba the Hutt's service and beats Bidlo Kwerve for the post of majordomo. He gives Jabba the rancor Pateesa as a birthday present, and Kwerve ends up as one of the rancor's early victims.

32 BBY - Boonta Eve Classic

Watches the podrace from Jabba's private balcony and wakes the sleeping Hutt to announce young Anakin Skywalker's victory over Sebulba.

19 BBY - Recovering Muchi

Hires Cid on Ord Mantell to rescue the young rancor Muchi from Zygerrian slavers. He threatens her over the delays, then arrives at her parlor in person just as Clone Force 99 returns with the rancor.

~8–2 BBY - Dealing with Han Solo

Hires Han Solo and Chewbacca, along with Greedo, to steal Krestrel D'Naran's urn from Corellia. He then gives up Solo's location to Marshal Buck Vancto, on the condition that Vancto waits until the job is finished.

~0 ABY - Darth Vader at the palace

Is held at lightsaber point by Darth Vader, who comes to hire bounty hunters. He is present for the Empire's negotiations with Jabba the next day, and he later threatens the mercenary Saponza on Jabba's behalf.

3 ABY - The hunt for Han Solo

Chases Boba Fett over his late delivery of the frozen Solo. He runs the palace while Jabba is away at Crimson Dawn's gathering on Jekara, and he gives in when Fett storms the palace looking for answers.

~4 ABY - Kijimi power play

Helps Zeva Bliss take control of the Spice Runners of Kijimi from her mother, Zarah.

4 ABY - Rescue of Han Solo

Brings C-3PO and R2-D2 before Jabba and falls for Luke Skywalker's mind trick. He plots to kill Jabba during the execution trip to the Great Pit of Carkoon. Jabba dies there and the Khetanna is destroyed, but Fortuna survives even though he is presumed dead.

4–9 ABY - Crime lord of Tatooine

Takes over Jabba's palace with servants and slaves of his own, and grows obese from years of overindulgence.

9 ABY - Death

After the second rescue of Grogu, Boba Fett and Fennec Shand storm the palace. Fortuna greets Fett nervously, and Fett shoots him dead and takes his throne.

Bib Fortuna's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY
  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY
  • The Book of Boba Fett, 9 ABY

Tobias Beckett, born 55 BBY

Tobias Beckett was the veteran thief and gunslinger who took a young Han Solo under his wing, then betrayed him, and taught him the hard way that nobody in the underworld can be trusted. A career criminal working for the Crimson Dawn syndicate in the early years of the Empire, Beckett ran a small crew with his lover Val and the pilot Rio Durant. He was notorious for the death of the bounty hunter Aurra Sing, though he claimed he only pushed her and the fall did the rest. While posing as an Imperial captain during the Mimban campaign, he crossed paths with Solo, first jailing him and then taking him and Chewbacca aboard. The botched coaxium heist on Vandor cost Beckett both Val and Rio. To make up the loss to Dryden Vos, he led a daring raid on the Kessel spice mines aboard the Millennium Falcon. On Savareen he sold out Solo's plan to Vos, then turned on Vos too and tried to take the coaxium for himself. Solo shot him before he could draw. Dying in his protégé's arms, Beckett told him he had finally made the smart move, and admitted that he had meant to go home to Glee Anselm and learn to play the valachord.

Date unknown - Birth on Glee Anselm

Tobias Beckett is born on Glee Anselm. Canon gives no birth year.

~13 BBY - Death of Aurra Sing

Beckett pushes the feared bounty hunter Aurra Sing to her death, and the kill makes his name across the underworld.

~10 BBY - Cato Neimoidia Job

Beckett's crew steals a crate from a freight platform in Zarra. Val finds Qi'ra's old logbook inside, and the gang plans to hit a conveyex carrying Crimson Dawn cargo.

~10 BBY - Heist on Hovun IV

Beckett poses as a death priest and steals identichips from Dvorad for Crimson Dawn. Enfys Nest ambushes him on Munt Ontdal, and he blows up the chips and his ship, the Rampart, so the Cloud-Riders can't have them.

10 BBY - Undercover on Mimban

Disguised as an Imperial Army captain, Beckett ends up leading mudtroopers in battle. He has the deserter Han Solo jailed, then takes Solo and Chewbacca aboard a stolen AT-hauler.

10 BBY - Heist on Vandor

The plan to steal coaxium from an Imperial conveyex falls apart when the Cloud-Riders attack. Val and Rio Durant die, the cargo is lost, and Beckett builds grave markers for his fallen crew.

10 BBY - Deal with Dryden Vos

Beckett avoids execution by promising Vos unrefined coaxium from Kessel. As part of the deal, he has to take Qi'ra onto his crew.

10 BBY - Raid on Kessel

Posing as a guard named Tuul, Beckett helps steal coaxium from the Pyke mines. The crew escapes on Lando Calrissian's Millennium Falcon.

10 BBY - Death on Savareen

Beckett tells Vos about Solo's plan to give the coaxium to the Cloud-Riders, then abandons Vos and tries to take the coaxium himself. Solo shoots him, and Beckett dies admitting that Solo made the smart move.

Tobias Beckett's Timeline

  • Solo: A Star Wars Story, 10 BBY

Gial Ackbar, born 55 BBY

Gial Ackbar was the admiral who commanded the rebel fleet at Endor, the man who saw the Emperor's trap for what it was, and one of the few commanders to serve through every major war from the Clone Wars to the First Order's rise. He started out as Coral City's representative on the Calamari Council and rose to captain of the Mon Calamari Guard. In the Clone Wars he protected the young Prince Lee-Char and guided him through the Separatist-backed civil war on his homeworld, which ended with Lee-Char crowned king. When the Empire invaded Mon Cala, Ackbar led his people's defense, then fled and joined the young Rebel Alliance. After Admiral Raddus died at Scarif, Ackbar became supreme commander of the Alliance Fleet. He later led the Mon Calamari mutiny that brought the Mercantile Fleet's cruisers over to the rebels, and directed the fleet from Home One at the Battle of Endor. As a fleet admiral of the New Republic he won the decisive Battle of Jakku, then retired to Mon Cala. Decades later Leia Organa talked him out of retirement to help the Resistance. He oversaw the attack on Starkiller Base and was killed on the bridge of the Raddus when First Order fighters attacked the fleeing Resistance fleet.

~55 BBY - Birth on Mon Cala

Born on Mon Cala in the last years of the Galactic Republic. Canon only places his birth between about 61 and 52 BBY.

Before 20 BBY - Council Seat and Rise in the Guard

Represents Coral City on the Calamari Council, then joins the Mon Calamari Guard and rises to captain, becoming chief military advisor to the throne.

~20 BBY - Battle of Mon Cala

After King Yos Kolina is assassinated, Ackbar protects Prince Lee-Char as Quarren separatists and Riff Tamson's droid forces attack Coral City. He fights beside Anakin Skywalker, Kit Fisto and Ahsoka Tano and is taken prisoner.

~20 BBY - Liberation and Coronation

Leads the prisoners' uprising once Lee-Char wins the Quarren over. Tamson is defeated, and Ackbar stands beside the throne at Lee-Char's coronation.

18 BBY - Imperial Invasion of Mon Cala

As Commander and Chief of Security, he confronts Darth Vader and the Inquisitors, defends the northern hemisphere and destroys an Imperial sky-base. The Empire still takes the planet.

After 18 BBY - Joins the Rebellion

Flees Mon Cala and joins the nascent Alliance to Restore the Republic. At one point he is enslaved by Tarkin until rebels rescue him.

1 BBY - Supreme Commander of the Fleet

After Admiral Raddus dies at Scarif, Ackbar takes command of the Alliance Fleet. He sends ships to protect Alderaanian refugees at Fostar Haven.

0 ABY - Evacuation of Yavin 4

Helps organize the rebel evacuation of the Great Temple after the Battle of Yavin.

0 ABY - Missions with Luke Skywalker

Sends Luke to Rodia, to scout the moon Fex and to extract Drusil Bephorin from Denon. He also backs the plan to hijack the Star Destroyer Harbinger to relieve Tureen VII.

~1 ABY - Mutiny at Mon Cala

Answers King Lee-Char's dying call and leads the Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet in a mutiny against the Empire. Captain Pysk Lila sacrifices the Aurora Flare to open an escape route.

~1 ABY - Assault on Mako-Ta Space Docks

Survives Death Squadron's ambush on the sabotaged new fleet and gets Home One away into hyperspace.

3 ABY - Hoth and the Road to Endor

Keeps the Alliance Fleet together after the Empire's attack on Echo Base.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Commands the rebel fleet from Home One, realizes the Imperial trap and holds the line until the second Death Star is destroyed.

4–5 ABY - New Republic Fleet Admiral

Becomes a fleet admiral of the New Republic Defense Fleet and leads the campaign against the Imperial remnants.

5 ABY - Battle of Jakku

Leads the New Republic to its decisive victory over the Empire, then retires to Mon Cala.

~9 ABY - New Republic Council

Appears as a senior New Republic figure during the era of Ahsoka.

34 ABY - Returns to Serve the Resistance

Leia Organa persuades him out of retirement. He takes back his admiral's commission at the D'Qar base and helps oversee the destruction of Starkiller Base.

34 ABY - Death aboard the Raddus

Killed on the Raddus's bridge when Kylo Ren and his TIE escorts attack the fleet during the evacuation of D'Qar.

Gial Ackbar's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY
  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY

Maul, born 54 BBY

Maul was the Sith assassin who announced the return of the Sith to the Jedi after a thousand years of hiding. He then spent more than three decades feeding on his hatred for the Jedi, for Obi-Wan Kenobi and for the master who threw him away. Darth Sidious took him from his mother, Talzin, as a child and made him into a weapon. Maul trained in secret, hunting beasts and even a lone Padawan, until the Invasion of Naboo in 32 BBY finally let him loose. He duelled Qui-Gon Jinn on Tatooine and later killed him on Naboo, but Kenobi cut him in half and left him for dead. He survived, broken and half-mad, in the junk pits of Lotho Minor until his brother Savage Opress found him during the Clone Wars. Talzin's magick restored his mind and gave him new legs. Maul then became a crime lord. He took over Mandalore through Death Watch and forced Black Sun, the Pykes and the Hutts into his Shadow Collective. Sidious struck back by killing Savage, and Talzin was killed soon after, but Maul survived both blows and a final clash with Ahsoka Tano during the Siege of Mandalore. Under the Empire he ruled the underworld from the shadows as the head of Crimson Dawn, working through Dryden Vos and later Qi'ra. His hunt for Sith secrets on Malachor pulled him into the lives of Ezra Bridger and Kanan Jarrus. His obsession finally led him to Kenobi's hiding place on Tatooine, where their last duel ended with a single stroke. Maul died believing the boy Kenobi was protecting would avenge them both.

54 BBY - Birth on Dathomir

Born to Mother Talzin, leader of the Nightsisters. He grows up as a Nightbrother alongside his brothers Feral and Savage Opress.

~40 BBY - Taken by Darth Sidious

Sidious, once Talzin's ally, takes the angry young Zabrak from Dathomir to become his secret Sith apprentice. Talzin swears she will have her revenge.

~40 BBY - Training as a Sith

Maul is trained as a Sith warrior and assassin and builds his own double-bladed lightsaber. On Malachor, Sidious makes him breathe the ashes of fallen Sith, and the visions leave him hungry for revenge on the Jedi.

~33 BBY - Hunting on Twon Ketee

While restless for action, Maul hunts rathtars and stalks Jedi on Coruscant. Sidious warns him to stay hidden until the time is right.

~33 BBY - The Padawan Eldra Kaitis

With a crew of bounty hunters led by Cad Bane, Maul tracks down a captured Padawan who is up for auction. He kills her on Drazkel's moon, then kills the crime boss Xev Xrexus to protect the Sith's secret.

33 BBY - Kwenn

Sidious sends Maul to Kwenn while the pirate Zilastra plots against members of the Jedi Council. There he confronts the Jedi outpost's seneschal, Voh.

32 BBY - Duel on Tatooine

Maul tracks Queen Amidala to Tatooine with his probe droids and attacks Qui-Gon Jinn. It is the first time in a millennium that the Jedi have faced a Sith, and Jinn barely escapes.

32 BBY - Battle of Naboo

In the Theed power generator, Maul fights Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi and kills Jinn. Kenobi then cuts him in half and he falls down a reactor shaft, presumed dead. Sidious replaces him with Count Dooku.

32 BBY - Exile on Lotho Minor

Kept alive by his hatred, Maul ends up on a junk world. He builds himself a spider-like six-legged body and slowly loses his mind.

~20 BBY - Found by Savage Opress

During the Clone Wars, Savage Opress finds his brother with a talisman from Talzin. Talzin's magick restores Maul's mind and gives him cybernetic legs.

~20 BBY - Revenge on Kenobi

Maul massacres villagers on Raydonia to draw Kenobi out, then duels him aboard a tanker. He takes Savage as his apprentice.

~20 BBY - Skirmish on Florrum

The brothers kill Adi Gallia but are beaten by Kenobi and Hondo Ohnaka's pirates. They barely escape the crash of their ship.

~19 BBY - The Shadow Collective

Maul brings Death Watch, Black Sun, the Pyke Syndicate and the Hutts together into a criminal alliance. He uses it to seize Mandalore and makes Almec his puppet prime minister.

~19 BBY - Battle of Mandalore

Darth Sidious arrives and kills Savage Opress. He defeats and captures Maul to use him against his own allies.

19 BBY - Second Battle of Dathomir

Maul escapes Sidious and regroups his syndicates. In retaliation, Sidious sends Grievous to Dathomir, and Talzin dies protecting her son.

19 BBY - Siege of Mandalore

Maul sets a trap to lure Anakin Skywalker and Kenobi to Mandalore, but Ahsoka Tano leads the Republic forces instead. She captures him as the Jedi Purge begins.

19 BBY - Escape During Order 66

Maul breaks free when the clones turn on Ahsoka aboard their Venator. He flees as the ship crashes.

~18 BBY - The Shadow Lord

Now hunted by the Inquisitors, who call him "the Shadow," Maul works to rebuild his criminal empire in secret, away from the new Empire's eyes.

10 BBY - Master of Crimson Dawn

From behind the scenes, Maul controls the Crimson Dawn syndicate through Dryden Vos. After Vos is killed, he summons Qi'ra to take her place at his side.

3 BBY - Mission to Malachor

Maul reaches the Malachor Sith Temple, forms an alliance with Ezra Bridger and blinds Kanan Jarrus. He escapes after Darth Vader arrives.

2 BBY - Holocrons and Visions

Maul takes the Ghost crew hostage to force Ezra to join a holocron fusion. Through their shared vision, he learns that Obi-Wan Kenobi is alive.

2 BBY - Death on Tatooine

Maul follows Ezra to Tatooine and faces Kenobi one last time. Kenobi cuts him down in a single exchange, and Maul dies in Kenobi's arms, hoping the Chosen One will avenge them.

Maul's Timeline

  • Episode I: The Phantom Menace, 32 BBY (22 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (32 years old)
  • Star Wars: Maul - Shadow Lord, 17 BBY (37 years old)
  • Solo: A Star Wars Story, 10 BBY (44 years old)
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (49 years old)

Owen Lars, born 52 BBY

Owen Lars is best known as the stern uncle who raised Luke Skywalker in hiding on a remote moisture farm. The son of Cliegg Lars, he became Anakin Skywalker's stepbrother when his father freed and married Shmi Skywalker. In 22 BBY he watched that family fall apart: Shmi was taken and killed by Tusken Raiders, Anakin came back in a rage to recover her body, and a grieving Cliegg died soon afterward. Owen then took over the homestead and married Beru Whitesun. In 19 BBY, after some reluctance, he agreed to adopt Anakin's infant son. He believed the Jedi had destroyed his stepbrother, so he spent years keeping Obi-Wan Kenobi away from Luke, hiding the boy's heritage and keeping him busy with farm work. He stood up to the Inquisitor Reva when she came for Luke, and he survived a beating by the bounty hunter Black Krrsantan. His caution could not keep the galaxy out forever. In 1 BBY he bought the droids C-3PO and R2-D2, and stormtroopers hunting the stolen Death Star plans tracked R2-D2 to the farm and killed Owen and Beru. Their deaths sent Luke off with Kenobi toward the Rebellion and the Battle of Yavin.

Date unknown - Birth

Owen is born to the moisture farmer Cliegg Lars and his wife Aika, who met on a Core World. After Aika dies, father and son move to the Lars homestead near Anchorhead on Tatooine.

Date unknown - Meeting Beru Whitesun

On a trip to Anchorhead, Owen meets Beru Whitesun, a third-generation moisture farmer, and the two start dating.

Date unknown - A New Stepmother

With the family's help, Cliegg wins Shmi Skywalker from Watto, frees her and marries her. Owen grows to love his stepmother, and he resents the son who left her to join the Jedi.

22 BBY - Shmi's Abduction

Tusken Raiders take Shmi. Cliegg leads a rescue party that goes badly, loses a leg, and comes back with only three other survivors.

22 BBY - Anakin Returns

Anakin Skywalker and Padmé Amidala arrive at the homestead. Anakin borrows Owen's swoop to track the Tuskens, brings back Shmi's body, and the family buries her. Owen comes away seeing his stepbrother as violent and full of anger.

22 BBY - Inheriting the Farm and Marriage

Cliegg dies soon after, heartbroken, and leaves Owen the farm. Owen marries Beru and the couple work the homestead together. They struggle with infertility.

19 BBY - Adopting Luke

Obi-Wan Kenobi gives Owen and Beru Anakin's infant son, Luke. Owen is reluctant, but Beru convinces him, and he forbids Kenobi to visit or train the boy.

~12 BBY - Blaster Lessons

Owen finds seven-year-old Luke outside alone waiting for a meteor shower. He decides the boy needs to learn farm safety and teaches him to use a blaster rifle.

9 BBY - Confronting the Inquisitors

Owen confronts Kenobi in Anchorhead, and then the Third Sister holds him at lightsaber-point in the town square while hunting a Jedi. He gives nothing away.

9 BBY - Defending the Homestead

Warned that the Third Sister is hunting Luke, Owen and Beru fight her at the farm. She eventually spares Luke and brings him back. Afterward Owen makes peace with Kenobi and offers to let him meet the boy someday.

Date unknown - The Great Drought

During a severe drought, Kenobi secretly shields the farm from Jabba's water-tax collectors and rescues Luke after the boy sneaks out to steal water.

Date unknown - Krrsantan's Captive

A year after the drought, Owen angrily tells Kenobi to stop interfering. The Wookiee bounty hunter Krrsantan then kidnaps and tortures Owen. Kenobi and Luke, flying the repaired skyhopper, save him.

~7 BBY - Proud Uncle

Owen tells Beru that twelve-year-old Luke is like a son to him and says he will not let him join the Imperial Academy. After Kenobi rescues Luke from a krayt dragon, Owen thanks him but warns him never to come back.

1 BBY - Buying the Droids

Owen buys C-3PO and R2-D2 from Jawas. He tells Luke that Obi-Wan died around the same time as his father and makes him wait another year to enroll at the Academy.

1 BBY - Death

Stormtroopers searching for the stolen Death Star plans track R2-D2 to the homestead. They interrogate and kill Owen and Beru and burn the farm. The loss pushes Luke to leave Tatooine with Kenobi.

Owen Lars's Timeline

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY (30 years old)
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY (33 years old)
  • Obi-Wan Kenobi, 9 BBY (43 years old)
  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY (51 years old)

Hondo Ohnaka, born 52 BBY

Hondo Ohnaka is the galaxy's most charming opportunist, a pirate whose career lasted decades and who kept turning up, for a price, on the edges of its biggest wars. Sold into slavery by his con-artist parents, he escaped, learned the criminal trade as an advisor to Porla the Hutt, then betrayed his master and fled with a band of fellow servants. He conned a collector out of a fleet of ships and built the Ohnaka Gang into a feared crew based on Florrum. During the Clone Wars he captured Count Dooku and then Obi-Wan Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker, hoping to ransom all three. The plan failed, and it made him a lasting enemy in Dooku, who later sent General Grievous to destroy his base. Hondo swung between foe and ally of the Jedi: he raided Felucian villages, kidnapped Ahsoka Tano, and then helped her and a group of younglings escape Grievous. He armed the Onderon rebels and fought off Maul and Savage Opress. After the Empire broke up his gang he worked alone as a smuggler and bounty chaser, became an unlikely friend to Ezra Bridger and the Spectres, and helped free Lothal. By the time of the First Order he had rebuilt his fortunes as the head of Ohnaka Transport Solutions on Batuu, a front for smuggling, and he quietly helped the Resistance after Crait.

Unknown - Birth on Sriluur

Born to a pair of con artists. His mother teaches him to pick pockets instead of singing him lullabies.

Unknown - Sold into slavery

His parents sell him, claiming it is an offering to the Weequay god Quay. He escapes by disguising himself as a policeman aboard a ship bound for Boonta.

Unknown - Advisor to Porla the Hutt

Starts as Porla's cupbearer and rises to become the Hutt's trusted advisor, learning the criminal trade along the way.

Unknown - Betrayal and flight

Plots with fellow servants to rob Porla of his skiff, vehicles and weapons, then leads the runaways into a life of piracy.

Unknown - Founding the Ohnaka Gang

Pressures a collector on Farana into handing over a Corona-class frigate, which becomes his flagship Acushnet, along with six Flarestar attack shuttles. He sets up a sprawling base on Florrum and becomes its pirate king.

Unknown - Mercenary days with Aurra Sing

Joins a mercenary crew with Aurra Sing, starts an on-and-off romance with her, and gets to know Jango Fett and Cad Bane.

~32 BBY - Auction of Eldra Kaitis

Bids at Xev Xrexus's auction for a captured Jedi Padawan, then pays to join the hunt for her. Aurra Sing's crew kills his men and steals one of his shuttles.

22 BBY - Capturing Count Dooku

Takes Dooku prisoner after the Count crashes on Vanqor and demands a spice ransom from the Republic. He then drugs and imprisons Obi-Wan Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker too. All three escape, and the Jedi spare him.

~21 BBY - Raids on Felucia

Extorts nysillin from a Felucian village until Jedi and hired bounty hunters drive him off. He duels Anakin on a cliff edge before retreating.

~21 BBY - Sheltering Aurra Sing and Boba Fett

Gives refuge to Sing and young Boba Fett on Florrum, then persuades Boba to reveal where the Republic hostages are held. He secretly rescues Sing and keeps Slave I.

~20 BBY - Arms for Onderon

Delivers rocket launchers to the Onderon rebels at Anakin Skywalker's request.

~20 BBY - Raid on the Crucible and fall of Florrum

Captures Ahsoka Tano while trying to steal the younglings' kyber crystals. Dooku sends Grievous to seize Florrum and jail Hondo. The younglings free him, and he flies Slave I to rescue them.

~20 BBY - Defeating Maul and Savage Opress

Fights off Maul and Savage Opress after they turn part of his crew against him, fighting beside Obi-Wan Kenobi. He then loots the brothers' wrecked ship.

~20 BBY - Clash with the Blood Bone Order

Loses the plundered cargo of the Steady On when Lassa Rhayme and Asajj Ventress raid his base.

~19 BBY - Gang dissolved

The new Empire breaks up the Ohnaka Gang, and Hondo carries on alone as a freelance outlaw.

Imperial Era - Heist on Jedha

Helps Dok-Ondar take a kyber statue from the Temple of the Kyber and fights Chirrut Îmwe along the way.

~11 BBY - Hunting Qi'ra

Teams up with IG-88 to capture Crimson Dawn's Qi'ra on Ord Mantell. She turns the two against each other and collects bounties on both of them.

Imperial Era - Forged journal auction

Tries to sell forged copies of "Maz Kanata's Journal." The Rang Clan exposes the forgery, and the auction ends in a firefight.

~3 BBY - Brothers of the Broken Horn

Gets Ezra Bridger and Chopper involved in stealing Azmorigan's ship, which begins his friendship with the Spectres.

~2 BBY - The Wynkahthu Job

Leads the Spectres on a salvage job aboard a sunken Imperial transport and is arrested by the Empire.

~1 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

Helps Ezra Bridger and the rebels drive the Empire off Lothal.

~3 ABY - A Pirate's Fortune

Works with Kay Vess against the Rokana Raiders.

~34 ABY - Ohnaka Transport Solutions on Batuu

Runs a shipping front for smuggling at Black Spire Outpost, leasing the Millennium Falcon from Chewbacca for smuggling runs. He also helps the Resistance after the Battle of Crait.

Hondo Ohnaka's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY
  • Galaxy's Edge, 34 ABY

Orson Krennic, born 51 BBY

Orson Krennic was the ambitious Imperial director who pushed the Death Star from a stalled Separatist blueprint to a working planet-killer, and died under its fire on Scarif. A gifted, charming architect, he met the scientist Galen Erso in the Republic Futures Program and later used that friendship for his own ends. During the Clone Wars he joined the secret Special Weapons Group, got the Erso family out of Separatist captivity on Vallt and brought the Geonosians in to build the station. Under the Empire he drew Galen into Project Celestial Power, claiming it was about clean energy while secretly turning his kyber research into a weapon. His career rose and fell with every setback and with his running feud with Wilhuff Tarkin. After the Ersos fled, Krennic tracked them to Lah'mu, where his death troopers killed Lyra and he took Galen back to finish the superlaser. In the Empire's last years before the Galactic Civil War, he set up the Ghorman project to seize kalkite, fought Grand Admiral Thrawn for funding and kept the project secret. In the end it got away from him. After he ordered the test that wiped out Jedha City, rebels stole the station's plans from Scarif. Tarkin took command of the Death Star and fired on the base, and Krennic died in the blast of the weapon he had spent his life building.

~51 BBY - Birth on Lexrul

Born in Sativran City on Lexrul in the final decades of the Galactic Republic.

36 BBY - Republic Futures Program

At fifteen, earns a place in the Futures Program on Brentaal, where he befriends fellow student Galen Erso. He graduates with high honors after becoming famous for his late nights out.

~30s BBY - Republic Corps of Engineers

Joins the design regiment of the Corps of Engineers and supervises large construction projects. He uses his influence to get Galen a visiting professorship at the Institute of Applied Science.

23 BBY - Kartoosh Facility

Helps engineer the huge construction and overhaul facility above Kartoosh.

~22 BBY - Strategic Advisory Cell

During the Clone Wars, secretly joins the Republic Special Weapons Group and argues that the Republic should build the captured battle station design itself, using Galen Erso's crystal research.

~22 BBY - Rescue on Vallt

Posing as a Zerpen Industries employee, trades captured Separatist scientists for the imprisoned Erso family. He then calls in a Republic cruiser, which destroys the Keep where they were held.

~22 BBY - The Geonosian Hive

Persuades the captive Archduke Poggle the Lesser to have his hive build the Death Star. Months later, Poggle orders his drones to tear down their work and escapes, badly damaging Krennic's reputation.

~19 BBY - Project Celestial Power

After the Empire replaces the Republic, recruits Galen for a supposed clean-energy program on Coruscant while secretly turning his kyber crystal research into a weapon.

~19 BBY - Malpaz Disaster

A weapons research facility on Malpaz explodes. Krennic covers it up as an attack to keep Galen working, and receives only a modest promotion to commander.

~18 BBY - Summit at Raven's Peak

Briefs Tarkin's summit on Eriadu about Project Stardust. Partisans and Clone Force 99 attack the compound during the meeting.

~18 BBY - Superlaser Prototype

While Tarkin is busy with an uprising in the Salient system, he test-fires a prototype weapon into the Hero Twins black holes.

~18 BBY - The Ersos Escape

With Saw Gerrera's help, the Erso family slips away from Coruscant. Krennic is demoted to lieutenant commander and vows to find them.

14 BBY - Tarkin Becomes Grand Moff

Tarkin gains authority over the Death Star but keeps his distance, blaming Stardust's setbacks on Krennic.

13 BBY - Lah'mu

Now Director of Advanced Weapons Research, he tracks the Ersos to Lah'mu. His death troopers kill Lyra after she wounds him, and he takes Galen back to finish the superlaser while Jyn escapes.

~9 BBY - Construction Moves to Scarif

After Saw Gerrera gets close to discovering the station, construction moves from Geonosis to Scarif. Brierly Ronan becomes Krennic's devoted assistant director.

~4 BBY - Maltheen Divide Conference

Reveals the Empire's need for Ghorman's kalkite and gives Dedra Meero control of the Ghorman project, which will stir up rebellion to justify a takeover.

3 BBY - Sculdun's Investiture Party

Trades barbs with Senator Mon Mothma at Davo Sculdun's party on Coruscant.

2 BBY - Ghorman Massacre

The Ghorman project ends in a massacre and full Imperial occupation, and strip-mining for kalkite begins on Krennic's timetable.

1 BBY - Imperial Ball

Attends the Empire Day ball, where a drunk subordinate leaks his interest in Jedha's kyber to a reporter.

1 BBY - Sev Tok Conference

Aboard the Firedrake, gets Thrawn to bet his TIE Defender funding on solving Stardust's gralloc supply problem, and sends Ronan to watch him.

1 BBY - Destruction of Jedha City

After a pilot defects with a message about the station, Tarkin takes over the project and fires the superlaser at Jedha City at Krennic's urging. Tarkin then credits himself with the success.

1 BBY - Mustafar and Eadu

Asks Darth Vader for an audience with the Emperor but is warned instead. On Eadu, he has Galen's engineers executed, and Galen is killed in the rebel air raid that follows.

1 BBY - Death on Scarif

Fails to stop Rogue One from sending the Death Star plans and is wounded by Cassian Andor. He dies on Scarif when Tarkin fires the station on the Citadel.

Orson Krennic's Timeline

  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY
  • Andor, 5 BBY
  • Rogue One: A Star Wars Story, 1 BBY

Dryden Vos, born 50 BBY

Dryden Vos is best known as the refined, lethal face of the Crimson Dawn syndicate, a crime lord who hosted the galaxy's elite aboard his yacht First Light while secretly answering to Maul. He rose through the underworld during the Clone Wars as a Crimson Dawn leader within Maul's Shadow Collective, and in the war's last days he got Maul's warning to go into hiding before the Empire took power. In the early Imperial years he offered the hunted former Sith a way off Janix in exchange for Maul removing Crimson Dawn's boss Rintero, then led the extraction himself under stormtrooper fire. He took over the syndicate as its public figurehead, but Maul stayed in control behind him. Vos bought the young Qi'ra, branded her with the syndicate's mark, trained her in Teräs Käsi and made her his top lieutenant. In 10 BBY, after Tobias Beckett's crew lost a coaxium shipment on Vandor, Vos sent them to raid the Kessel mines instead, a job that brought Han Solo into his orbit. When the deal fell apart over Savareen, Vos turned on Han, and Qi'ra killed him in his own study. She then used his signet ring to report his death to Maul, blamed it on Beckett, and took his place.

Clone Wars - Rising Star of Crimson Dawn

Serves as a leader in Crimson Dawn under Boss Rintero after the syndicate joins Maul's Shadow Collective. He becomes a lieutenant in the alliance.

19 BBY - Ordered into Hiding

Maul contacts him by hologram, along with Marg Krim of the Pyke Syndicate and Ziton Moj of Black Sun. Maul foresees the end of the Clone Wars and tells the syndicate leaders to go into hiding.

~17–13 BBY - Bargain with Maul

Crimson Dawn has abandoned Maul, who is now trapped on Janix. After the assassination of Marg Krim, Vos reaches Maul through Looti Vario. He offers Maul safe passage off Janix if Maul kills Rintero and makes Vos head of Crimson Dawn.

~17–13 BBY - Extraction from Janix

Vos flies in on a Crimson Dawn shuttle and shoots down an Imperial gunship. He holds off stormtroopers at the landing ramp until Maul and his allies are aboard, then escapes into hyperspace.

~17–13 BBY - Figurehead of Crimson Dawn

Replaces Rintero as Crimson Dawn's public leader but remains subordinate to Maul, whom he contacts through a holographic signet ring. He runs the syndicate from his yacht, the First Light, which holds his collection of antiquities.

After 13 BBY - Acquiring Qi'ra

Buys Qi'ra from Sarkin Enneb and brands her with the Crimson Dawn mark. He trains her in martial arts, including Teräs Käsi, and makes her his top lieutenant.

~10 BBY - Beckett's Debt

Pays Tobias Beckett a bonus for killing Aurra Sing. Beckett later owes Vos a shipment of coaxium.

10 BBY - Failure on Vandor

Beckett's crew fails to steal coaxium from an Imperial conveyex because Enfys Nest's Cloud-Riders intervene. Beckett and his new recruit, Han Solo, come to the First Light to face Vos.

10 BBY - The Kessel Plan

Vos threatens Beckett but is persuaded by Han and Qi'ra to back a raid on the Kessel spice mines for unrefined coaxium, despite the risk of angering the Pykes. Qi'ra goes with the crew.

10 BBY - Death on Savareen

Warned by Beckett, Vos confronts Han on Savareen. Vos believes the coaxium is fake, but it is the real shipment. He fights Han and then Qi'ra with his petars, and Qi'ra kills him in his study. She uses his ring to tell Maul that Beckett killed Vos and takes over his position.

Dryden Vos's Timeline

  • Star Wars: Maul - Shadow Lord, 17 BBY
  • Solo: A Star Wars Story, 10 BBY

Tynnra Pamlo, born 50 BBY

Tynnra Pamlo lived a double life in the Imperial Senate. She sat openly for Taris while secretly serving as Minister of Education in the Rebel Alliance's Civil Government. In the first days of the Empire she joined Bail Organa and Riyo Chuchi in fighting the Imperial Defense Recruitment Bill, arguing that the galaxy had more urgent needs than a costly new army. That effort collapsed when Palpatine used Admiral Rampart's destruction of Tipoca City as a reason to endorse the bill himself. In the years that followed, Pamlo worked with Rebel Intelligence and Airen Cracken. She gathered reports of Imperial atrocities and spread counterpropaganda, and she took part in the Organas' strategy dinners on Alderaan, which were disguised as social evenings. Although she opposed Palpatine, she was one of the Alliance's most cautious voices and would not drag her homeworld into open war. When Jyn Erso told the Alliance High Command about the Death Star, Pamlo argued for disbanding the Alliance rather than attacking Scarif, dismissing the plan as nothing but hope. After Rogue One went ahead anyway and the Galactic Civil War began, she returned to Coruscant, publicly condemned the battle station and resigned her Senate seat.

~18 BBY - Opposes the Defense Recruitment Bill

As senator for Taris in the new Imperial Senate, Pamlo joins Bail Organa in opposing Admiral Rampart's plan to replace the clone army with conscripts. She argues that the war is over and that the galaxy has more pressing problems.

~18 BBY - The vote is tabled

Pamlo and Organa get Mas Amedda to table the vote again until provisions for retiring clones are clearly defined.

~18 BBY - The Clone Conspiracy exposed

Pamlo watches in shock as Riyo Chuchi reveals footage of Rampart destroying Tipoca City. Palpatine then appears, has Rampart arrested and uses the scandal to endorse the Defense Recruitment Bill, which marks the start of the stormtrooper era.

Imperial Era - Secret rebel minister

While still in the Senate, Pamlo becomes Minister of Education in the Alliance's Civil Government. She works with Rebel Intelligence and Airen Cracken, collecting reports of Imperial atrocities on Lasan, Geonosis and Ghorman and producing counterpropaganda.

3 BBY - Leia Organa's Day of Demand

Pamlo attends Leia's Day of Demand ceremony on Alderaan, where Queen Breha thanks her personally for coming.

~3 BBY - Strategy dinners on Alderaan

Pamlo attends the Organas' dinner parties, which are secretly rebel strategy meetings. At one of them Grand Moff Tarkin arrives unexpectedly, and Breha stages a drunken scene to hide what the guests are really discussing.

1 BBY - Operation Fracture

Pamlo is part of the Alliance leadership that plans to extract Galen Erso. She and Organa intend to bring him before the Senate to testify about the Empire's weapon.

1 BBY - Council on Yavin 4

After hearing from Jyn Erso and Cassian Andor, Pamlo asks what chance the rebels have against a planet-killing weapon. She calls for the Alliance to disband and scatter, and she rejects the raid on Scarif because it rests on nothing but hope.

1 BBY - Resignation from the Senate

After Rogue One's unsanctioned attack on Scarif starts the Galactic Civil War, Pamlo returns to Coruscant, publicly condemns the Death Star and resigns her Senate seat.

Tynnra Pamlo's Timeline

  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY
  • Andor, 5 BBY
  • Rogue One: A Star Wars Story, 1 BBY

Marrok, born 50 BBY

Marrok is best known as the silent, armored duelist who fought Ahsoka Tano twice during Morgan Elsbeth's plot to bring back Grand Admiral Thrawn. He was raised in the Jedi Order, but after the Jedi Purge he became an Inquisitor for the Empire under the title First Brother. He knelt before Darth Vader when the Inquisitorius was presented to its new master on Nur. A few years later, he and the Eleventh Brother hunted Maul and the fugitive Jedi Eeko-Dio Daki and Devon Izara across Janix. Using his rare gift of psychometry, he tracked his prey and met Maul blade to blade more than once, until Daki pushed him off a ruined structure into the jungle. He survived the fall of the Empire and became a mercenary for Elsbeth, working alongside Baylan Skoll and Shin Hati. In 9 ABY he held off Ahsoka at the Corellian shipyards while Elsbeth's forces stole a hyperdrive core. He then flew with Hati against Ahsoka's shuttle over Seatos. In the forests below, Ahsoka struck him down. His armor released a shrieking green mist and collapsed empty, which left his true nature a mystery. Decades later, historians could not agree whether the figure in the Corellian recordings had really been an Inquisitor.

Before 19 BBY - Member of the Jedi Order

Marrok is raised in the Jedi Order. The dates of his birth and his time as a Jedi are not recorded in canon.

19 BBY - Becomes the First Brother

After Order 66, Marrok is chosen for the Inquisitorius and given the title First Brother. His job is to hunt the Jedi who survived the Purge.

19 BBY - Presented to Darth Vader

In the Fortress Inquisitorius on Nur, he kneels with the Eleventh Brother, the Fourth Sister and Barriss Offee as the Grand Inquisitor presents them to their new master, Darth Vader.

~18 BBY - The Hunt for Maul on Janix

After Janix's Tactical Defense Force calls in the Empire, the First Brother arrives and interrogates Chief Klyce and Captain Brander Lawson. He then uses psychometry to read the aftermath of Maul's fight with two hidden Jedi.

~18 BBY - Clashes with Daki, Izara and Maul

He attacks Eeko-Dio Daki and Devon Izara at Lawson's home. He later chases Izara onto a hovertrain, where Maul joins the fight and the two cut him loose in a tunnel. He reports to Vader that Maul is more dangerous than expected.

~18 BBY - Duel with Maul Beside the Eleventh Brother

Joined by the Eleventh Brother, he duels Maul in the caverns of Janix, and the two Inquisitors kill the Nightbrother Scorn. Maul escapes by bringing the cave down on them, and they are pulled from the rubble.

~18 BBY - Chase Across Janix

He survives an ambush that goes wrong when the informant Rheena Sul turns on the Empire. He follows the fugitives across the city, destroys Maul's droid Spybot during a duel over a lake of acid, and warns Vader that the group is headed his way.

~18 BBY - Defeated by Eeko-Dio Daki

In jungle ruins, he and the Eleventh Brother corner Izara, then fight her and Daki together. Daki pushes the First Brother off the ruins into the jungle, but he survives.

9 ABY - Mercenary for Morgan Elsbeth

Now a mercenary, Marrok serves Morgan Elsbeth alongside Baylan Skoll and Shin Hati. He searches the ruins on Arcana with Skoll for the star map to Peridea.

9 ABY - Duel on Corellia

At the Santhe Shipyards, he duels Ahsoka Tano with the help of an HK-87 assassin droid while Elsbeth's forces steal a hyperdrive core for the Eye of Sion. Shin Hati picks him up once the transport has escaped.

9 ABY - Dogfight in the Denab System

Flying his Fiend fighter in Hati's squadron, he attacks Ahsoka's T-6 shuttle and chases it through a pod of purrgil down to Seatos.

9 ABY - Death on Seatos

Sent with Hati to delay Ahsoka and Sabine Wren in the forest, Marrok duels Ahsoka a final time and is cut down. Green mist bursts from his armor, which collapses empty.

35 ABY - An Uncertain Legacy

In The Rise and Fall of the Galactic Empire, the historian Beaumont Kin studies the Corellian security recordings and cannot confirm whether the armored figure was a real Inquisitor.

Marrok's Timeline

  • Star Wars: Maul - Shadow Lord, 17 BBY
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY

Asajj Ventress, born 50 BBY

Asajj Ventress is best known as Count Dooku's twin-bladed assassin during the Clone Wars, and later as one of the war's most unlikely redemption stories. Given away as an infant by her Nightsister clan, she grew up a slave on Rattatak until the stranded Jedi Knight Ky Narec rescued her and trained her as his Padawan. When pirates killed him, she turned to the dark side, took the planet from its warlords, and caught Dooku's eye. As his Sith acolyte she led Separatist forces, framed Obi-Wan Kenobi on Cato Neimoidia, killed Jedi Master Tholme, kidnapped Jabba's son Rotta, and fought Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker again and again. Dooku abandoned her on Darth Sidious's orders. She went home to Dathomir, helped Mother Talzin plot against him and trained Savage Opress, then watched Grievous slaughter her sisters. After that she drifted into bounty hunting and made uneasy alliances: she saved Kenobi from Maul and helped Ahsoka Tano clear her name. In 19 BBY she and Quinlan Vos tried to assassinate Dooku and fell in love along the way. When Vos slid to the dark side, she gave her life on Christophsis to save him, and that sacrifice brought him back. Talzin's magic later returned her to life. In the Imperial era she went back to bounty hunting, helped the clone Omega on Pabu, and then left her old life behind until the young Jedi Lyco Strata pulled her into new adventures.

50 BBY - Birth on Dathomir

Born into Mother Talzin's Nightsister clan.

~50 BBY - Given to Hal'Sted

To protect the clan, Talzin has Asajj's mother surrender the infant to the Siniteen criminal Hal'Sted, who takes her as a slave to Rattatak.

44 BBY - Found by Ky Narec

Hal'Sted dies during a pirate uprising. Asajj, now six, shows her Force power by hurling away a Weequay attacker, and the stranded Jedi Knight Ky Narec takes her on as his Padawan.

~34 BBY - Death of Ky Narec

After about ten years defending Rattatak together, Narec is shot dead by pirates. Asajj kills his attackers with his lightsaber and turns to the dark side.

~34 BBY - Conquest of Rattatak

She wages a war of vengeance against the warlords and takes the planet for herself, but her attempt on Osika Kirske fails and she is forced to fight as a gladiator.

23 BBY - Acolyte of Count Dooku

Dooku kills Kirske and recruits her. She pledges herself to him on Serenno, receives her curved red lightsabers, and becomes a Separatist commander.

~23 BBY - Killing of Jenza

On Dooku's orders she tracks down his sister Jenza, kills the Republic Intelligence officer Raz Fellidrone, and then kills Jenza herself.

22 BBY - Cato Neimoidia

Sent as the Separatist emissary, she frames Obi-Wan Kenobi for a bombing, manipulates the young guard Ketar Nor, and duels Anakin Skywalker while trying to lure away the initiate Mill Alibeth.

22 BBY - Execution of Tholme

She defeats Jedi Master Tholme in battle and executes him against Dooku's orders, then brings his lightsaber to the count as a trophy.

22 BBY - Battle of Christophsis

She commands the Separatist invasion, turns clone Sergeant Slick into a traitor, and duels Kenobi and Skywalker to cover the arrival of droid reinforcements.

22 BBY - Kidnapping of Rotta

She abducts Jabba's son and holds him on Teth to frame the Jedi, then loses to Skywalker at the monastery and again on Tatooine.

~22 BBY - Rescue of Nute Gunray

She frees the captured Viceroy from Republic custody aboard the Tranquility, fighting Luminara Unduli and Ahsoka Tano.

~21 BBY - Infiltration of Kamino

During the Separatist assault on the clone homeworld, she steals DNA data and fights Kenobi and Skywalker.

~21 BBY - Betrayed by Dooku

On Darth Sidious's orders, Dooku abandons her to die after a failed mission. She survives and goes back to the Nightsisters on Dathomir.

~21 BBY - Plots against Dooku

With Mother Talzin she tries to assassinate Dooku, then trains Savage Opress and plants him with Dooku as a Nightsister pawn.

~20 BBY - Massacre of the Nightsisters

Grievous's army wipes out her clan. Ventress survives and leaves Dathomir to wander the galaxy.

~20 BBY - Bounty hunter

She works as a bounty hunter, including a job with Boba Fett's crew. She also saves Kenobi from Maul and Savage Opress above Raydonia.

19 BBY - Helping Ahsoka Tano

She helps the fugitive Ahsoka Tano hunt for the real Jedi Temple bomber.

19 BBY - Quinlan Vos

The Jedi Order sends Quinlan Vos to work with her on killing Dooku. She teaches him the dark side and they fall in love, but the attempt on Raxus Secundus fails.

19 BBY - Rescue of Vos

She teams up with Kenobi and the Jedi Council to rescue Vos from Dooku, then sees that he has fallen to the dark side.

19 BBY - Death on Christophsis

During the second battle of Christophsis she takes a lightning attack from Dooku that was meant for Vos. She dies having embraced the light, and her death brings Vos back from the dark side.

19 BBY - Resurrection

Vos and Kenobi return her body to Dathomir. In the spirit world, Talzin's voice offers her life again at the cost of her heart's desire, and she accepts.

~18 BBY - Encounter on Pabu

Back to bounty hunting under the Empire, she briefly helps the clone Omega on Pabu and teaches her about the Force.

Imperial era - Security guard

She leaves her old life for a quiet job as a security guard on a remote asteroid.

Imperial era - Allied with Lyco Strata

She helps the young Jedi Lyco Strata escape stormtroopers and an Inquisitor, then goes on further missions with him.

Asajj Ventress's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (28 years old)
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY (31 years old)

Babu Frik, born 50 BBY

Babu Frik is known for the droid job that helped decide the war against the Sith: he could reprogram almost any droid, however well protected. A tiny black-market droidsmith, he ran a workshop for the Spice Runners of Kijimi for decades. There he repaired the gang's droids, including EV-6B6 for a young Poe Dameron and Zorii Bliss, and he helped Poe slip away when Poe left the gang to join the New Republic. Babu lived through the First Order occupation of Kijimi. In 35 ABY Poe brought the Resistance heroes back to him, and Babu got past C-3PO's programming so the droid could read out the Sith inscription on the Blade of Ochi. The procedure wiped C-3PO's memory. Babu then alerted Jannah's band on Kef Bir to help the heroes, and he escaped with Zorii just before a Sith Eternal Star Destroyer destroyed Kijimi. The two joined Lando Calrissian's Citizens' Fleet and flew a Y-wing at the Battle of Exegol, where they helped destroy a Xyston-class Star Destroyer. Afterward they celebrated the victory on Ajan Kloss, and Babu formally joined the Resistance.

~50 BBY - Birth

Babu Frik is born around this year. Canon gives only an approximate date.

~18 ABY - Droidsmith for the Spice Runners

Babu works as the Spice Runners of Kijimi's droidsmith. Zorii Bliss asks after him during the gang's mission to rescue Tomasso on Quintil.

~18 ABY - Repairing EV-6B6

Once Poe Dameron and Zorii Bliss join the gang, Zorii brings Poe and their damaged droid EV-6B6 to Babu, and he fixes it.

~18 ABY - Helping Poe escape

When Poe leaves the Spice Runners to join the New Republic, Babu lets him take a holographic disguise matrix so he can get offworld unnoticed.

~34 ABY - Occupation of Kijimi

At some point after the Battle of Crait, the First Order occupies Kijimi, making life harder for Babu and the spice runners.

35 ABY - Translating the Sith dagger

Poe brings Rey, Finn, BB-8 and C-3PO to Babu's workshop. Babu gets around C-3PO's programming so he can read out the Sith inscription on the Blade of Ochi, which erases the droid's memory.

35 ABY - Contacting Kef Bir

After the Resistance group leaves, Babu contacts Jannah and Company 77 on Kef Bir so they can help the heroes find the Emperor's wayfinder.

35 ABY - Destruction of Kijimi

Babu escapes with Zorii Bliss, leaving behind a battle droid he was working on, just before a Xyston-class Star Destroyer destroys Kijimi with its superlaser.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Babu and Zorii join Lando Calrissian's Citizens' Fleet and fly a Y-wing against the Sith Eternal, helping destroy one of the Xyston-class Star Destroyers.

35 ABY - Victory and joining the Resistance

Babu celebrates the Emperor's defeat on Ajan Kloss and later joins the Resistance.

Babu Frik's Timeline

  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (85 years old)

Beru Lars, born 48 BBY

Beru Whitesun Lars is best remembered as the warm, steady aunt who raised Luke Skywalker as her own son on a remote moisture farm. As a young woman she dreamed of opening a café in Anchorhead, helped locate and deprogram the tracking chips of freed slaves, and introduced Shmi Skywalker to Cliegg Lars, whose son Owen she later married. She served blue milk at Shmi's funeral during Anakin's visit on the eve of the Clone Wars. When Obi-Wan Kenobi brought the infant Luke to the homestead at the end of the war, Beru insisted they raise him. Over the next nineteen years she often took Luke's side against her overprotective husband. In 9 BBY she took up a blaster rifle to defend the farm from the Inquisitor Reva Sevander, holding her off long enough for Luke to escape. As Luke grew restless, Beru pressed Owen to let him leave for the Academy, reminding him that the boy had too much of his father in him. In 1 BBY the family bought the droids R2-D2 and C-3PO, and stormtroopers tracing the stolen Death Star plans killed Beru and Owen and burned their home. Their deaths pushed Luke to leave Tatooine with Kenobi, and he went on to destroy the Death Star.

Date unknown - Early Life on Tatooine

Beru Whitesun grows up on Tatooine. A cooking instructor praises her blue-milk cheese, and she starts dreaming of opening her own café in Anchorhead.

Before 22 BBY - Meeting Shmi Skywalker

While getting her portable nav computer repaired at Watto's shop in Mos Espa, Beru meets Shmi Skywalker, who is enslaved there. She later introduces Shmi to Cliegg Lars, who frees and marries her.

Before 22 BBY - Working with the White Suns

Beru helps Shmi find and deprogram the chips in freed slaves using her scanner, and she supports others on Tatooine who work to free enslaved people.

Before 22 BBY - Courting Owen Lars

Beru falls in love with Cliegg's son, Owen Lars, and the two begin dating.

22 BBY - Anakin Comes to the Homestead

Tusken Raiders abduct Shmi. Beru is at the Lars farm when Anakin Skywalker and Padmé Amidala arrive, and she serves blue milk and cheese at Shmi's funeral.

Between 22 and 19 BBY - Marriage to Owen

Beru marries Owen and takes the Lars name. After Cliegg dies, the couple struggles with infertility while trying to start a family.

19 BBY - Receiving Luke

At the end of the Clone Wars, Obi-Wan Kenobi brings the infant Luke Skywalker to the homestead. Beru declares that she and Owen will raise him as their own.

9 BBY - Watching Over Young Luke

Beru laughs when she finds a young Luke on the homestead roof pretending to podrace. From a distance, Kenobi watches the family.

9 BBY - Defending the Homestead from Reva

Beru refuses to abandon her home when the former Inquisitor Reva Sevander hunts for Luke. She arms herself and Owen with blaster rifles and holds Reva off long enough for Luke to flee. Afterward she sees Reva carry the boy back unharmed.

Between 9 and 2 BBY - Owen's Abduction

A year after the Great Drought, the bounty hunter Krrsantan seizes Owen over water taxes owed to Jabba the Hutt. Beru goes after her husband with a rifle and tells Luke to stay safe inside.

2 BBY - Kenobi's Distant Vigil

After his clash with Maul, Kenobi watches from afar as Beru calls a seventeen-year-old Luke home at sunset.

1 BBY - Arguing for Luke's Future

Beru persuades Owen to let Luke apply to the Academy after one more season. Over dinner she warns her husband that Luke is not a farmer and has too much of his father in him.

1 BBY - Death at the Homestead

Stormtroopers trace R2-D2 and C-3PO, who carry the stolen Death Star plans, to the Lars farm. They kill Beru and Owen and burn the homestead, and their deaths lead Luke to leave Tatooine with Kenobi.

0 ABY - Legacy

Luke finally grieves for his aunt and uncle after Nakari Kelen dies. Darth Vader and Doctor Aphra later visit the ruined homestead while tracking the pilot who destroyed the Death Star.

Beru Lars's Timeline

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY
  • Obi-Wan Kenobi, 9 BBY
  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY

Morgan Elsbeth, born 48 BBY

Morgan Elsbeth turned grief into an empire-building career and spent her life trying to bring back Grand Admiral Thrawn. She was a young Nightsister in 20 BBY when General Grievous massacred her coven and killed her mother, Selena. The Mountain Clan took her in, but its Matron cast her out after Elsbeth's push to arm the clan got the Matron's daughter Nali killed. Believing she was the last of her kind, she looked to the Empire for power and revenge. She made herself magistrate of Calodan on Corvus, industrialized the forest world and stripped planets of resources to feed the Imperial Navy. When Moff Isdain rejected her TIE Defender design, Thrawn took notice. He tested her in a staged fight and accepted her into his service, and she became one of his most devoted followers, running shipyards on Corellia. Long after the Empire fell, she kept Corvus under her rule. In 9 ABY she killed a New Republic envoy and burned the planet's forests. Around that time she hired Din Djarin to kill Ahsoka Tano, but the two teamed up against her. Tano beat her in a duel and forced her to reveal where the map to Thrawn was hidden. Baylan Skoll and Shin Hati freed her from New Republic custody. She took the map, finished the hyperspace ring Eye of Sion and jumped to Peridea, where she was reunited with Thrawn and the Great Mothers. There she received the Gift of Shadow and the Blade of Talzin, stayed behind to hold off the Jedi, and died at Tano's hands. Her sacrifice let Thrawn and the Great Mothers escape back to the galaxy.

Before 20 BBY - Birth on Dathomir

Born to the Nightsister Selena and raised among the witches of Dathomir. Canon gives no birth year.

20 BBY - Massacre of the Nightsisters

General Grievous's droid army wipes out the Nightsisters. Elsbeth sees her mother killed in a duel with Grievous, destroys a commando droid with a knife, and escapes before collapsing.

20 BBY - Taken in by the Mountain Clan

Mountain Clan members find her unconscious and nurse her back to health. Their Matron shows her the burned Nightsister lands, and Elsbeth decides she is the last of her people.

20 BBY - Exiled by the Matron

Elsbeth leads a group of clan youths to a hidden Nightsister weapons cache, where she takes a pair of sickles. Separatist droids attack them and Nali, the Matron's daughter, is killed. The Matron casts Elsbeth out.

Imperial Era (before 9 BBY) - Magistrate of Calodan

Elsbeth wins control of Calodan on Corvus by promising the people wealth. She fortifies the city, industrializes the planet, and strips worlds of resources to supply the Imperial Navy.

Imperial Era - The TIE Defender Pitch

She presents her TIE Defender design on Coruscant. Moff Isdain rejects it as too expensive and seizes Corvus's factories, and the people of Calodan turn on her.

Imperial Era - Joining Thrawn

She defeats Rukh in a fight that Admiral Thrawn and Gilad Pellaeon have staged to test her. She admits she is after power and revenge, and Thrawn takes her into his service, stationing part of his 96th Task Force over Corvus.

Imperial Era - Industrialist of the Empire

As one of Thrawn's most loyal followers, she becomes a powerful starship builder with shipyards on Corellia, while Calodan suffers under her harsh rule.

1 BBY - Thrawn Lost

Purrgil carry Thrawn and the Chimaera away from Lothal and into exile.

After 5 ABY - Visions of the Great Mothers

After the Empire collapses, Elsbeth keeps her subjects in the dark and starts receiving visions from the Great Mothers. She begins working to bring Thrawn back.

~9 ABY - Burning of Corvus

She refuses New Republic envoy Nadura's call to stand trial, kills Nadura and her escort, and orders the forests around Calodan burned.

9 ABY - Confronting Ahsoka Tano

Ahsoka Tano arrives in Calodan demanding to know where Thrawn is. Elsbeth takes civilians hostage and refuses.

9 ABY - Hiring the Mandalorian

She offers Din Djarin her pure beskar spear to kill Tano. Instead, he joins forces with Tano.

9 ABY - Defeat at Calodan

Djarin wipes out her army and kills Lang, and Tano beats Elsbeth in a duel. Elsbeth reveals that the map to Thrawn is hidden on Arcana. Wing takes over Calodan and Djarin keeps the spear.

9 ABY - Imprisonment and Rescue

The New Republic arrests her as an Imperial sympathizer. Baylan Skoll and Shin Hati free her from the cruiser Vesper while she is being taken to trial.

9 ABY - Arcana and Seatos

She finds the ruined Nightsister stronghold on Arcana and sends Hati after the map. With the map in hand, she uses magick to unlock the route to Peridea at the reflex point on Seatos.

9 ABY - Launch of the Eye of Sion

Her Corellian operation is exposed, but the last hyperdrive is installed. With Sabine Wren as her prisoner, she makes the jump to the far galaxy just ahead of Hera Syndulla's fighters.

9 ABY - Reunion on Peridea

The Great Mothers welcome her on Peridea, and Thrawn arrives to praise her efforts.

9 ABY - Gift of Shadow

Elsbeth offers to stay behind and hold off the Jedi. She pledges herself to the sisterhood and receives the Gift of Shadow and the Blade of Talzin.

9 ABY - Death on Peridea

In a rematch with Tano she destroys one of Tano's lightsabers but is cut down. Her stand gives Thrawn and the Great Mothers time to escape Peridea.

Morgan Elsbeth's Timeline

  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY
  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY

Mon Mothma, born 48 BBY

Mon Mothma is the political architect of the Rebellion: a senator who spent nearly two decades inside the Imperial system while secretly building the movement that would bring it down. She was one of the youngest senators in Republic history, and during the Clone Wars she led the loyalist opposition to the war. Alongside Bail Organa, Padmé Amidala and Onaconda Farr, she fought expanded military spending and the Chancellor's growing emergency powers, and she watched Farr die of poison in the middle of that fight. After Palpatine declared the Empire she survived the arrests of dissenting senators and tried to limit his rule by law with the Imperial Rebirth Act. When that failed, she and Organa quietly met Saw Gerrera and set up the network that became the Alliance. For years she led a double life, moving money to Luthen Rael's rebels behind the front of a society hostess. Her marriage grew cold, and she paid for her cover by promising her daughter to a gangster's son. Her lifelong friend Tay Kolma was killed to protect that secret. After the Ghorman Massacre she denounced Palpatine as a genocidal monster on the Senate floor and fled. Her resignation led to the formal founding of the Alliance to Restore the Republic, with Mothma as its Chancellor. She approved the mission that stole the Death Star plans, helped plan the attack at Endor, and after victory became the first Chancellor of the New Republic. There she signed the Galactic Concordance and pushed to disarm the galaxy, a policy tested by the threat of Thrawn's return.

~48 BBY - Birth on Chandrila

Born into a political family. Her father is a Republic arbiter-general and her mother, Tanis, is a governor on Chandrila.

~33 BBY - A Teenage Rebellion and Marriage

At fifteen she briefly turns away from politics to become a historian and tours the ruins of Phothrell. Following Chandrilan custom, she marries Perrin Fertha.

~32 BBY - Youngest Senator

At sixteen she joins the Galactic Senate as Chandrila's senator, one of the youngest ever, and moves to Coruscant with Perrin.

28 BBY - Meeting Padmé Amidala

She speaks at the first Senate session Padmé Amidala attends. At first she doubts the new senator, but later becomes her ally and mentor alongside Bail Organa and Onaconda Farr.

~22 BBY - Loyalist Committee

As a member of Palpatine's Loyalist Committee, she works to stop the Separatist Crisis from turning into war. War breaks out anyway.

22–19 BBY - Voice for Peace in the Clone Wars

She opposes bank deregulation and the bill for more clone troopers, and speaks against wider emergency powers. She attends the Alderaan refugee conference and represents the Republic at peace talks on Mandalore.

~21 BBY - Murder of Onaconda Farr

Farr is poisoned at a gathering of the anti-war senators, and Mothma is present. The military spending bill passes despite their efforts.

~19 BBY - Trial of Ahsoka Tano

She attends the funeral for the victims of the Jedi Temple bombing and sits in on Ahsoka Tano's trial, where Barriss Offee confesses.

19 BBY - Seeds of Rebellion

She meets in secret with Organa and other concerned senators. They agree to take a petition to the Chancellor rather than turn to the Jedi.

19 BBY - Rise of the Empire

Palpatine declares the Galactic Empire. Mothma attends Padmé's funeral on Naboo. She is arrested with other dissenting senators and freed after publicly declaring her loyalty.

19 BBY - The Imperial Rebirth Act

She drafts a bill to limit the Emperor's power. To stop an assassination plot that would have ruined it, she sets off Soujen Vak-Nhalis's hidden self-destruct device and is wounded herself. Palpatine vetoes the bill's key limits.

19 BBY - Pact on Phothrell

With Organa, she meets Saw Gerrera in the ruins of Phothrell. She agrees to be the political heart of their underground resistance.

18 BBY - Building "the Alliance"

She begins gathering military and intelligence assets into her own network.

5 BBY - A Double Life

Still a senator in the Imperial Senate, she secretly funds Luthen Rael's rebel network. She hides the money with help from her childhood friend Tay Kolma, while her marriage and her relationship with her daughter Leida fall apart.

~4 BBY - Leida's Wedding

To cover her rebel finances, she arranges Leida's marriage to Stekan Sculdun. During the wedding festivities, Tay Kolma is killed to protect her secret.

2 BBY - Denouncing Palpatine

After the Ghorman Massacre, she condemns the Emperor on the Senate floor and escapes Coruscant as a fugitive.

2 BBY - Founding of the Rebel Alliance

Her break with the Senate unites the rebel cells as the Alliance to Restore the Republic, and she leads it as Chancellor.

1 BBY - The Death Star Plans

She approves recruiting Jyn Erso. The Rogue One mission then steals the Death Star plans at Scarif.

0 BBY–4 ABY - Leading the Galactic Civil War

She leads the Alliance through the years after the Battle of Yavin and mentors Leia Organa.

4 ABY - Briefing for Endor

She briefs the rebel fleet on the second Death Star and the plan to attack it at Endor.

4–5 ABY - Chancellor of the New Republic

She is elected the first Chancellor of the New Republic, with its Senate seated on Chandrila. She plans to give up wartime powers and to cut the fleet by ninety percent.

5 ABY - Galactic Concordance

She signs the peace treaty with the Empire after the Battle of Jakku. The Military Disarmament Act follows.

9 ABY - The Thrawn Question

She presides over the Senate while Hera Syndulla is questioned over her warnings that Thrawn is returning.

Mon Mothma's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (26 years old)
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY (29 years old)
  • Andor, 5 BBY (43 years old)

  • Rogue One: A Star Wars Story, 1 BBY (47 years old)

Padmé Amidala Naberrie, born 46 BBY

"

Padmé Amidala is best known as the teenage queen who stood up to the Trade Federation's invasion of Naboo, and later as a senator who argued for democracy and peace while the Republic fell apart around her. She grew up in a modest family, went on relief missions as a child, and entered public service young. Elected monarch at fourteen, she took the regnal name Amidala and often posed as one of her own handmaidens for protection. When the Trade Federation occupied her world, she escaped the blockade with two Jedi and met a slave boy named Anakin Skywalker on Tatooine. She then called for a vote of no confidence in Chancellor Valorum, which helped Palpatine take power. After that she returned home, won the Gungans over as allies, and personally captured Viceroy Nute Gunray to free Naboo. She served two terms as queen and then became Naboo's senator, where she led the opposition to the Military Creation Act and survived repeated attempts on her life. During the Separatist Crisis she fell in love with Anakin, her Jedi bodyguard, and married him in secret after the Battle of Geonosis. Through the Clone Wars she kept working for a negotiated peace. Her pregnancy fed Anakin's fear of losing her, and that fear helped drive him to the dark side. She saw the Republic become an Empire and confronted her husband on Mustafar, where he choked her. She died on Polis Massa after giving birth to the twins Luke and Leia, still insisting there was good in him, and her children would one day bring that good back out.

"

"

46 BBY - Birth on Naboo

Born Padmé Naberrie, the younger daughter of Ruwee and Jobal Naberrie and the sister of Sola.

~39 BBY - Relief Mission to Shadda-Bi-Boran

At age seven she goes with her father and Senator Onaconda Farr on a relief mission to relocate the Shadda-Bi-Boran. The effort fails, and it shapes her lifelong commitment to helping others.

~38 BBY - Apprentice Legislature

At eight she joins Naboo's Apprentice Legislature. She later works as a junior senatorial adviser and studies at the Theed Royal Academy.

~34 BBY - Legislative Youth Program and Supervisor of Theed

At twelve she takes part in the Legislative Youth Program, where she has a brief romance with Palo Jemabie. The same year she begins a two-year term as supervisor of Theed.

32 BBY - Elected Queen of Naboo

She campaigns anonymously against the sitting queen, Sanandrassa, wins the election, and is crowned under the regnal name Amidala. She also recruits her handmaidens, led by her decoy Sabé.

32 BBY - Chommell Sector Summit

She hosts a summit of neighboring worlds to end Naboo's isolationism.

32 BBY - Blockade and Invasion of Naboo

She refuses to sign the Trade Federation's treaty. After droid armies occupy Theed, Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan Kenobi rescue her.

32 BBY - Stranded on Tatooine

Disguised as the handmaiden Padmé, she meets the young slave Anakin Skywalker, whose Podrace win pays for the parts to repair her ship.

32 BBY - Vote of No Confidence

On Palpatine's advice she moves for a vote of no confidence against Chancellor Finis Valorum before the Senate. The move helps Palpatine get elected chancellor.

32 BBY - Battle of Naboo

She reveals her true identity to Boss Nass to win the Gungans as allies. She then storms the Theed palace and captures Viceroy Nute Gunray, and later presents the orb of peace at the victory celebration.

32–28 BBY - Rebuilding Naboo

She leads Naboo's recovery and the debate over planetary defense, which causes a lasting rift with Captain Panaka. She serves two terms as queen.

28 BBY - Steps Down and Becomes Senator

She refuses a proposed constitutional change that would give her a third term. Queen Réillata then appoints her senator for Naboo and the Chommell sector. On Coruscant she survives a Trade Federation plot against her and finds allies in Bail Organa, Mon Mothma and Mina Bonteri.

22 BBY - Assassination Attempts on Coruscant

She returns to Coruscant to oppose the Military Creation Act, and her decoy Cordé is killed in a starship bombing. After another attempt on her life, the Jedi assign Anakin Skywalker to protect her.

22 BBY - Retreat to Naboo and Tatooine

In hiding on Naboo she falls in love with Anakin. She goes with him to Tatooine, where his mother dies.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

She is captured trying to rescue Obi-Wan Kenobi, survives the execution arena, and fights alongside the Jedi as the Clone Wars begin.

22 BBY - Secret Wedding

She marries Anakin in a secret ceremony on Naboo, with C-3PO and R2-D2 as witnesses.

~22–19 BBY - Senator in the Clone Wars

She pursues peace talks, including with her old mentor Mina Bonteri. She uncovers corruption and survives kidnappings and plots, including the Banking Clan affair with Rush Clovis, all while keeping her marriage hidden.

19 BBY - Pregnancy

After Anakin returns from the Outer Rim sieges, she tells him she is pregnant. His visions of her dying in childbirth push him toward Palpatine.

19 BBY - Petition of the 2,000 and Birth of the Empire

She joins the Delegation of 2,000, which challenges the chancellor's emergency powers. She is in the Senate when Palpatine declares himself Emperor.

19 BBY - Confrontation on Mustafar

She flies to Mustafar to beg Anakin to flee with her. He accuses her of betrayal and chokes her.

19 BBY - Death on Polis Massa

She gives birth to Luke and Leia and dies soon after, telling Obi-Wan that there is still good in Anakin.

19 BBY - Funeral on Naboo

She is laid to rest in Theed, apparently still pregnant, to hide that the twins survived.

"
