Kit Fisto, born 45 BBY

Kit Fisto was one of the Jedi Order's finest swordsmen, remembered as much for his easy grin in battle as for his skill with a blade. Already a seasoned Jedi Master before the Clone Wars, he trained the Padawan Nahdar Vebb, took part in Mace Windu's rescue of kidnapped children on Oosalon, and joined missions such as the troubled expedition to an underwater research station on Tordus. He survived the arena massacre at Geonosis and then led clone troops in the first battle of the war. As a Jedi General and member of the Jedi High Council, he fought alongside Windu on Hissrich and was sent after the fugitive Nute Gunray. That hunt led him into General Grievous's fortress, where Vebb was killed but Fisto came close to beating the cyborg. He later oversaw the quarantine of the brain-worm-infested ship carrying Ahsoka Tano and Barriss Offee, flew in the starfighter strike that got the rescue team off Lola Sayu, and fought underwater on Mon Cala, where he was captured and tortured before helping restore the planet's alliance and crown Prince Lee-Char. In the war's last days he went with Windu, Agen Kolar and Saesee Tiin to arrest Chancellor Palpatine. Palpatine revealed himself as Darth Sidious and killed him within moments.

Unknown - Birth on Glee Anselm

Kit Fisto is born on the ocean world of Glee Anselm. Canon gives no birth year. He is later taken into the Jedi Order.

34 BBY - Rescue on Oosalon

Now a Jedi Master, Fisto is one of the Jedi who wait in reserve while Mace Windu lets himself be captured by the warlord Guattako, who kidnapped children to use as soldiers. By the time Fisto's group gets through the tunnels, Guattako is already dead.

~33 BBY - Shut Out of the Glee Anselm Peace Talks

An Anselmi official objects to Jedi involvement in the Republic-mediated peace talks after the Hope of Glee Anselm statue is stolen. He argues that a Nautolan like Fisto makes the Order biased, so the Republic hires bounty hunters instead.

Before 22 BBY - Training Nahdar Vebb

Fisto takes the Mon Calamari Nahdar Vebb as his Padawan. Together with Mace Windu and Shaak Ti, they investigate the Axil Research Station on Tordus and fight to keep the sinking facility from being lost to the ocean depths.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

Fisto is part of the Jedi assault team that storms the Petranaki Arena. He is one of the few survivors rescued by the new clone army, then leads clone troopers and Saber-class tanks for the rest of the battle.

22 BBY - Jedi General and Council Member

With the Clone Wars under way, Fisto becomes a Jedi General with his own clone unit and fleet, and takes a seat on the Jedi High Council. Vebb passes his trials and becomes a Jedi Knight while Fisto is away at war.

22 BBY - Operation on Hissrich

Fisto serves in Mace Windu's squad on Hissrich. He protects the native population from Separatist drilling, saves Windu from the droid commander AD-W4, and fights his way into the droid base.

22 BBY - Lair of General Grievous

Fisto tracks the escaped Nute Gunray to the Vassek system, only to find the trail was a trap set by Dooku that leads into Grievous's fortress. Grievous kills Vebb and Commander Fil. Fisto cuts off one of the General's hands, recovers Vebb's lightsaber and escapes, bringing back the location of Grievous's lair.

22 BBY - Attack on the Jedi Temple

Fisto is seen entering the Holocron Vault during Cad Bane's raid on the Temple. He is also among the Council members present when Todo 360 bursts into the communications center with a bomb.

~21 BBY - Brain Worm Crisis

From the Ord Cestus medical station, Fisto guides the infested ship carrying Ahsoka Tano and Barriss Offee in to dock. He then boards it once the Geonosian brain worms have been frozen, and finds both Padawans alive.

~21 BBY - Battle of Lola Sayu

Fisto flies alongside Saesee Tiin and Adi Gallia in the starfighter assault that breaks through the Separatist defenses. The attack lets Plo Koon's gunship reach the survivors of the Citadel rescue and get them off the planet.

~21 BBY - Battle of Mon Cala

Fisto leads clone SCUBA troopers against the Separatist-backed Quarren and protects Prince Lee-Char. Riff Tamson captures and tortures him. He is freed in time to join the final battle and watch Lee-Char crowned king.

20 BBY - Protecting the Chancellor

Fisto joins the Council's planning to guard Chancellor Palpatine at the Festival of Light on Naboo. As the war drags on, he takes a more active role on the Council.

19 BBY - Death on Coruscant

After learning that Palpatine is a Sith Lord, Mace Windu brings Fisto, Agen Kolar and Saesee Tiin to arrest him. Darth Sidious cuts down Kolar and Tiin, and Fisto dies only moments later.

Kit Fisto's Timeline

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY

Breha Organa, born 45 BBY

Breha Organa ruled Alderaan as its queen through the last years of the Republic and the rise of the Empire, and helped found the Rebel Alliance. As a teenager she nearly died during the Challenge of the Body on her Day of Demand. Her heart and lungs were replaced with pulmonodes, and she chose to leave them visible as a reminder that she had survived. Her injuries led her and her husband, Bail, to decide against having biological children. At the end of the Clone Wars they adopted the newborn daughter of Padmé Amidala and Anakin Skywalker, raised her as Princess Leia, and never told her who her birth parents were. Breha was also Alderaan's minister of education. Under the Empire she mentored young Alderaanians in their world's heritage, took in refugees, and used palace banquets as cover for strategy meetings that helped unite scattered resistance cells into the Alliance. She oversaw Leia's Day of Demand and later admitted to her daughter that she and Bail were part of the rebellion. When an Inquisitor had Leia kidnapped to draw out Obi-Wan Kenobi, she lived through the fear of losing her. Breha died in 1 BBY, alongside Bail, when the Death Star destroyed Alderaan, and the galaxy remembered them as martyrs of the rebellion.

~45 BBY - Birth on Alderaan

Born into the Elder Houses as heir to the Alderaanian throne and raised in the Royal Palace at Aldera. Canon gives no exact year.

~29 BBY - Day of Demand and the Accident

At sixteen she claims the throne through her Day of Demand. A fall during her Challenge of the Body nearly kills her. Her heart and lungs are replaced with pulmonodes, which she leaves visible, and she is invested as crown princess.

Between 40 and 28 BBY - Queen and Marriage

She takes the throne of Alderaan and marries Bail Prestor Organa. Because of her injuries, the couple decide to adopt an heir rather than conceive one.

28 BBY - Hosting Padmé Amidala

She invites Senator Padmé Amidala to Alderaan for a week. The two talk about motherhood, monarchy and Naboo's defenses.

22–19 BBY - Clone Wars Humanitarian Efforts

She sends doctors, teachers, builders and supplies to worlds devastated by the war. Alderaan hosts a conference to help war refugees.

19 BBY - Adoption of Leia

After Padmé's death, Bail brings the infant Leia home, and Breha takes her in as her daughter. Leia is presented as Princess of Alderaan on her Name Day, and her true parentage is kept secret.

~18–10 BBY - Raising the Princess and Mentoring Alderaanians

She raises Leia, tells her stories of Padmé Amidala, and teaches her orchid care, piloting and leadership. She also mentors young Alderaanians such as Evaan Verlaine in their world's heritage.

9 BBY - Leia's Kidnapping

Leia is abducted during a family visit on orders from the Third Sister, who wants to lure out Obi-Wan Kenobi. Breha and Bail ask Kenobi for help. After he rescues Leia, Breha thanks him on Alderaan.

3 BBY - Leia's Day of Demand

She presides over Leia's Day of Demand and gives her the Rhindon Sword. Meanwhile she hosts banquets that double as secret strategy meetings for the growing rebellion.

3 BBY - Refugees from Wobani

She welcomes the refugees Leia brings back from Wobani and gives each of them a stipend. Afterwards she scolds Leia for derailing Bail's relocation negotiations.

~3–2 BBY - Revealing the Rebellion to Leia

Once Leia uncovers her parents' rebel activity, Breha and Bail admit it but keep the details from her to protect her.

2 BBY - Founding of the Rebel Alliance

After Mon Mothma breaks with the Empire, the resistance networks that Breha and Bail helped build are formally united as the Alliance to Restore the Republic.

1 BBY - Death in the Destruction of Alderaan

Days after the Battle of Scarif, the Death Star destroys Alderaan, killing Breha and Bail.

Breha Organa's Timeline

  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY
  • Obi-Wan Kenobi, 9 BBY

R2-D2, born 45 BBY

R2-D2 is the astromech who keeps turning up at the turning points of galactic history. He was never fully memory-wiped, and decades of unbroken memory made him stubborn, resourceful and fiercely loyal. He first proved himself in 32 BBY by repairing Queen Amidala's starship under fire during the Naboo blockade. That same year he flew beside a young Anakin Skywalker when Anakin destroyed the Droid Control Ship. He watched Anakin and Padmé marry, then served as Anakin's partner through the Clone Wars, helping to rescue Jabba's son, surviving capture by General Grievous and carrying a distress call from Vanqor that saved two Jedi. When the Republic fell he passed into Bail Organa's care. Years later Princess Leia hid the stolen Death Star plans inside him, and the mission that followed brought him to Luke Skywalker and led to the first Death Star's destruction. He stayed with Luke through Hoth, Cloud City, Jabba's palace and Endor, and later through Luke's failed attempt to rebuild the Jedi Order. After Luke vanished, R2-D2 went into a long low-power sleep with the Resistance. He woke after the Battle of Starkiller Base carrying the last piece of the map to Luke. He went on to guide Rey to Ahch-To and flew as Poe Dameron's copilot at the Battle of Exegol.

~32 BBY - Built by Industrial Automaton

The R2-series astromech is built by 32 BBY at the latest and joins the Royal House of Naboo, serving aboard Queen Amidala's royal starship.

32 BBY - Running the Blockade

He is the only one of four astromechs to survive a hull repair under fire. He restores the ship's shields so it can escape the Trade Federation blockade.

32 BBY - Meeting Anakin and C-3PO

On Tatooine he meets Anakin Skywalker and C-3PO, helps work on Anakin's podracer, and cheers him on at the Boonta Eve Classic.

32 BBY - Battle of Naboo

He rides in the N-1 starfighter that young Anakin launches by accident, and helps him destroy the Droid Control Ship.

28 BBY - Senator's Aide

He follows Padmé to Coruscant when she becomes a senator and works as her unobtrusive recorder and assistant.

22 BBY - Geonosis and a Secret Wedding

He protects Padmé on Naboo and Geonosis, saves her in the droid foundry, and puts C-3PO back together. With C-3PO he is the only witness to Anakin and Padmé's wedding, after which he passes into Anakin's service.

22 BBY - Christophsis and Teth

He exposes the clone traitor Slick on Christophsis, then helps Anakin and Ahsoka rescue Jabba's son Rotta.

22 BBY - Hunting the Malevolence

He helps Anakin and Obi-Wan board Grievous's flagship to rescue Padmé, and the ship is destroyed.

22 BBY - Captured by Grievous

Lost after the fighting at Bothawui, he is sold to General Grievous. Anakin rescues him from Skytop Station, and he defeats the spy droid R3-S6.

~21 BBY - Clone Wars Missions

He serves on Orto Plutonia, in the Blue Shadow Virus crisis, and in the liberation of Ryloth. He also helps hunt Cad Bane, who kidnaps him and wipes his memory to plan the Senate hostage crisis.

~21 BBY - The Vanqor Trap

When Boba Fett's trap leaves Anakin and Mace Windu pinned under wreckage, he fights off gundarks and bounty hunters and gets their distress call to Coruscant.

~21 BBY - Infiltrating the Citadel

He leads a squad of reprogrammed battle droids that carries the frozen strike team to Lola Sayu to rescue Even Piell.

19 BBY - Fall of the Republic

He flies with Anakin in the rescue of Chancellor Palpatine and is present as Anakin falls to the dark side. Afterward he is placed with Bail Organa, and C-3PO's memory is wiped while his own is left intact.

~5 BBY - Aiding the Rebellion

Now serving Bail Organa, he and C-3PO briefly cross paths with the Ghost crew on a mission to Lothal.

1 BBY - Carrier of the Death Star Plans

Aboard the Tantive IV, Princess Leia hides the stolen Death Star plans and her plea to Obi-Wan Kenobi inside him. He escapes to Tatooine, where he is sold to the Lars family and finds Luke Skywalker.

0 BBY - Battle of Yavin

He delivers the plans to the Rebel Alliance and flies as Luke's astromech in the attack that destroys the Death Star.

3 ABY - Hoth and Dagobah

He escapes the Battle of Hoth with Luke, goes with him to Dagobah to train with Yoda, and repairs the Millennium Falcon's hyperdrive as it flees Cloud City.

4 ABY - Jabba's Palace and Endor

He smuggles Luke's lightsaber into Jabba's sail barge, helps free Han Solo, and fights alongside the Ewoks at the Battle of Endor while the second Death Star is destroyed.

9 ABY - Arrival of Luke Skywalker

He goes with Luke to rescue Grogu from Moff Gideon's cruiser, then to Luke's Jedi school, where Grogu begins training.

28 ABY - Fall of Luke's Jedi Temple

Luke's new Jedi Order is destroyed. After Luke disappears, R2-D2 powers down into a long low-power sleep with the Resistance.

34 ABY - Reawakening

After the Battle of Starkiller Base he wakes up and completes the map to Luke. He then travels with Rey and Chewbacca to Ahch-To and reunites with Luke there.

34 ABY - Rekindling Luke's Hope

He plays Luke the old recording of Leia's plea, which helps persuade Luke to help Rey.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

He restores C-3PO's wiped memory and flies as Poe Dameron's copilot in the decisive battle against the Final Order.

Greedo, born 44 BBY

Greedo is best remembered for the way he died: cornering Han Solo in the Mos Eisley cantina on Jabba the Hutt's behalf, only to be gunned down across the table before he could collect. His life leading up to that moment was a string of bad luck and overconfidence. As a hot-tempered youngster in Mos Espa he picked a fight with Anakin Skywalker, accusing him of cheating in the Boonta Eve Classic. During the Clone Wars he took a Trade Federation contract to kidnap the daughters of Pantora's Chairman Papanoida, but a smear of his own blood led the chairman to him, and Jabba forced Greedo to lead the Pantorans to the hostage. As one of Jabba's mid-level hired guns he ran scams such as freeing bounties so he could recapture them, joined Solo and Chewbacca on a doomed heist for a verillix urn on Corellia, shot Solo during the double-crosses that followed, and later botched a job on Mygeeto that nearly got him fed to a rancor. Sent after Solo as his last chance with the Hutt, he finally had the smuggler at blasterpoint in 1 BBY and gloated long enough to be shot dead. Even then his death earned him more fame than anything he did while alive.

44 BBY - Birth

Greedo is born a Rodian and later moves to Tatooine as a youth, growing up in Mos Espa.

32 BBY - Scuffle with Anakin Skywalker

After the Boonta Eve Classic, Greedo accuses young Anakin Skywalker of cheating to beat Sebulba and starts a fight. His friend Wald warns him that picking fights will lead to a bad end.

~21 BBY - Kidnapping of the Papanoida Daughters

Now working for Jabba the Hutt, Greedo is hired by the Trade Federation to abduct Chairman Papanoida's daughters to pressure Pantora into joining the Separatists. Che Amanwe strikes him with an idol and leaves his blood on it.

~21 BBY - Exposed at Jabba's Palace

Papanoida and his son Ion trace the blood to Greedo and present the evidence to Jabba. The Hutt forces Greedo to lead them to the Mos Eisley cantina, where the kidnappers are killed in a firefight. Greedo slips away.

Imperial Era - Trouble in Mos Eisley

While Obi-Wan Kenobi is in exile on Tatooine, a blaster-wielding Sullustan confronts Greedo in the back alley behind Chalmun's Cantina.

Imperial Era - Capturing Figrin D'an

Greedo delivers the indebted bandleader Figrin D'an to Jabba. While they serve the Hutt, the Modal Nodes learn that Greedo has been releasing small-time bounties so he can recapture them. They later tell Jabba, and a grudge forms.

Imperial Era - The Urn Heist on Corellia

Jabba pairs Greedo with Han Solo and Chewbacca to steal a verillix urn holding the ashes of Krestrel D'Naran. The break-in at Augustus Graves' penthouse fails, and Solo leaves Greedo stranded on Corellia.

Imperial Era - Betraying Solo

Picked up by Khel Tanna's crew, Greedo helps chase the impostor Corbus Tyra and shoots Solo in the chest on Paqualis III. He then helps recover the Millennium Falcon from an Imperial impound on Iakar and cheats his way to ownership of the ship at sabacc.

Imperial Era - Collecting on a Fake

Solo interrogates Greedo and knocks him out to retake the Falcon. Greedo then teams up with Tyra, passes burnt ronto meat off to Jabba as D'Naran's ashes, and collects the reward.

Imperial Era - Failure on Mygeeto

Sent by Jabba to kill IGBC Vice Chairman Anolo and seize a Givin codebreaker, Greedo kills Anolo and takes Ki-Adi-Mundi's old lightsaber, but loses the codebreaker. Jabba threatens to feed him to a rancor until Dok-Ondar buys the lightsaber.

1 BBY - The Hunt for Han Solo

Jabba gives Greedo one last chance: collect the bounty on Solo for dumping a Hutt cargo. Greedo hires Ponda Baba and Doctor Evazan as backup and leans on the Bith musician Lirin Car'n over a debt.

1 BBY - Death in Chalmun's Cantina

After his backup is lost to Obi-Wan Kenobi's lightsaber, Greedo corners Solo alone and demands the money or the Millennium Falcon. He gloats for too long, and Solo shoots him dead from under the table. Wuher drags his body out of the cantina.

1 BBY - Aftermath

Rodians on Tatooine swear revenge on Solo, but he has already left the planet. Greedo's career is soon forgotten, remembered only for how it ended.

Greedo's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (22 years old)
  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY (43 years old)

Garazeb Orrelios, born 44 BBY

Zeb Orrelios is the hot-tempered, bo-rifle-wielding muscle of the Ghost crew. He was once a captain of the Lasan High Honor Guard and fought to hold the royal palace when the Empire devastated his homeworld with banned T-7 ion disruptors. Kanan Jarrus found him afterwards, adrift and believing himself one of the last of his kind, and he joined Hera Syndulla's Spectres. On Lothal he spent years humiliating stormtroopers, trading barbs with Ezra Bridger and Chopper, and fighting Agent Alexsandr Kallus, the officer who had overseen the slaughter on Lasan. His outlook changed when he guided the Ghost through a collapsed star cluster to Lira San, the ancestral Lasat world, and found that his people had survived. Stranded with Kallus on an icy moon, he formed a grudging bond with his old enemy, and that bond helped turn Kallus into a rebel spy. Zeb forgave him. Zeb stayed with the Spectres through the founding of the Rebel Alliance, the disaster at Atollon and the liberation of Lothal. After the war he brought Kallus to Lira San. He later served with the New Republic's Adelphi Rangers, helping Din Djarin hunt Imperial warlords and joining the assault on Nal Hutta to rescue him.

~44 BBY - Birth on Lasan

Born on Lasan. His birth year comes only from his stated age of thirty-nine at the start of Star Wars Rebels.

Before 19 BBY - Lasan High Honor Guard

Joins the Honor Guard, earns his bo-rifle and rises to captain, charged with protecting the royal family and the Lasat people.

Early Imperial Era - Fall of Lasan

The Empire devastates Lasan with T-7 ion disruptors. Zeb defends the palace until a bomb knocks him out, then wakes to find his people almost wiped out.

Early Imperial Era - Found by Kanan Jarrus

Kanan Jarrus finds the despairing survivor, and Zeb joins Hera Syndulla's crew aboard the Ghost.

5 BBY - Meeting Ezra Bridger

On Lothal the Spectres run into Ezra Bridger. Zeb helps free Wookiee prisoners from Kessel and grudgingly accepts Ezra as a crewmate.

5 BBY - The T-7 Disruptors

Steals a shipment of T-7 disruptors on Garel and duels Agent Kallus, who boasts of his part in the genocide on Lasan. Ezra saves Zeb, and the weapons are destroyed.

5 BBY - Raids on Lothal

Steals a TIE fighter with Ezra, escapes the Grand Inquisitor at the Spire on Stygeon Prime and helps extract Ezra from the Imperial Academy.

5 BBY - Joining the Wider Rebellion

After the rescue of Kanan from Grand Moff Tarkin and the burning of Lothal's skies, the Spectres join the larger rebel network under Ahsoka Tano and Commander Sato.

4 BBY - Legends of the Lasat

Meets the surviving Lasat Chava and Gron and uses his bo-rifle to guide the Ghost through a collapsed star cluster to Lira San, where he finds his people alive.

3 BBY - Stranded with Kallus

Trapped with Agent Kallus on the ice moon Bahryn, Zeb forms an unlikely truce with him that sets Kallus on the path to defection.

3 BBY - Kallus Defects

Kallus, now the rebel spy Fulcrum, is exposed by Thrawn. Zeb welcomes his old enemy into the Rebellion and forgives him for Lasan.

2 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Survives Thrawn's assault on Chopper Base as the Phoenix Cell rebels escape Atollon.

1 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

Fights in the final campaign to free Lothal, which drives out the Empire and destroys the Imperial Dome with Thrawn's Star Destroyer carried off by the purrgil.

After 4 ABY - Return to Lira San

After the Galactic Civil War, Zeb takes Kallus to Lira San to show him that the Lasat live on.

~9 ABY - Adelphi Rangers

Now a member of the New Republic's Adelphi Rangers, he is stationed on Adelphi as Din Djarin and Carson Teva deal with pirate attacks on Nevarro.

After 9 ABY - Hunting Imperial Warlords

Helps Din Djarin track down Imperial warlords, including Barro and Janu Coin.

After 9 ABY - Attack on Nal Hutta

Joins the rest of the Adelphi Rangers in an attack on Nal Hutta to rescue Din Djarin.

Garazeb Orrelios's Timeline

  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (39 years old)
  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY (53 years old)
  • The Mandalorian and Grogu, 10 ABY (54 years old)

Lando Calrissian, born 43 BBY

Lando Calrissian is the galaxy's most charming scoundrel: a card sharp, smuggler and self-styled "galactic entrepreneur" who once owned the Millennium Falcon and who, against every instinct for self-preservation, became one of the heroes of the Rebellion. As a young gambler flying with his droid partner L3-37, he joined the coaxium heist on Kessel, lost L3 in the raid, and later lost the Falcon itself to Han Solo in a rematch at sabacc. He then went legitimate, or close to it, with a mining venture on Lothal where he crossed paths with the Ghost's rebel crew, and he rose to become Baron Administrator of Cloud City on Bespin. When Darth Vader used the city to trap Luke Skywalker, Lando was forced to hand over his old friend Han, then broke the deal, evacuated his people, and set out to undo the damage. He helped free Han from Jabba's palace, took a general's commission, and flew the Falcon into the core of the second Death Star at Endor to deliver the killing shot. In the years of peace he became a father, only for the First Order to steal his infant daughter; after a fruitless hunt with Luke for the Sith assassin Ochi, he withdrew into solitude on Pasaana. Called back by the Resistance in 35 ABY, he rallied a vast fleet of ordinary citizens to turn the tide at the Battle of Exegol.

~43 BBY - Birth on Socorro

Landonis Balthazar Calrissian is born on Socorro into the Calrissian family.

Before 10 BBY - Gambler and smuggler

Lando makes his name at sabacc, wins the Millennium Falcon in a game at Bespin, wins a moon in the Oseon Belt, and flies with his self-built droid partner L3-37.

Before 10 BBY - Debts and escapades

He runs weapons, pays off a debt to Dryden Vos's Crimson Dawn, and helps arm Petrusian slaves in an uprising against an Imperial outpost on Kullgroon before the Falcon is impounded on Vandor.

10 BBY - The Kessel heist

On Vandor, Han Solo stakes his claim to the Falcon at sabacc and Lando wins by cheating. He joins Qi'ra, Beckett, Han and Chewbacca to steal coaxium from Kessel, where L3-37 is destroyed and her mind is merged with the Falcon's navicomputer during the escape through the Maelstrom.

10 BBY - Losing the Falcon

After abandoning the crew on Savareen, Lando is tracked to Numidian Prime, where Han steals his spare card and wins the Falcon for good.

~5 BBY - Galactic entrepreneur on Lothal

Lando buys land on Lothal to mine minerals, wins Chopper from Zeb Orrelios at sabacc, and tricks the Ghost's rebel crew into helping him acquire a puffer pig from Azmorigan.

4 BBY - Smuggling the Spectres off Lothal

With Darth Vader hunting the rebels on Lothal, Lando lends them his droid and masking transponders in exchange for shield generators, allowing them to escape.

Before 3 ABY - Baron Administrator of Cloud City

Lando takes charge of Cloud City, a Tibanna gas mining colony on Bespin, running it with his aide Lobot while staying out of the Empire's way.

3 ABY - Betrayal on Bespin

Coerced by Darth Vader, Lando hands over Han, Leia and Chewbacca. When Vader keeps altering the deal, Lando frees his captives, orders the city evacuated, and flees with the rebels aboard the Falcon after failing to stop Boba Fett.

3 ABY - Joining the Rebellion

Lando throws in with the Rebel Alliance and begins the search for the carbonite-frozen Han, crossing paths with bounty hunters and Crimson Dawn along the way.

4 ABY - Rescue of Han Solo

Disguised as a guard in Jabba's palace on Tatooine, Lando helps free Han and survives the fight at the Great Pit of Carkoon.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Promoted to general, Lando leads the fighter assault on the second Death Star, flying the Falcon into its core with Nien Nunb and firing the shot that destroys the station.

4 ABY - Liberation campaigns

In the weeks after Endor, Lando helps drive Imperial forces from worlds including Cloud City as the Empire begins to collapse.

After 4 ABY - Life in the New Republic

Lando returns to business and adventures with Han, including a hunt for the missing Phylanx transmitter, and forms a relationship with Kaasha Bateen.

Before 35 ABY - A daughter taken

Lando becomes father to Kadara, who is kidnapped by the First Order while still an infant.

Before 35 ABY - The search for Ochi

Some six years later, Lando and Luke Skywalker track the Sith assassin Ochi of Bestoon to Pasaana while investigating the growing darkness, but the trail goes cold.

Before 35 ABY - The Hermit of Pasaana

Lando settles alone on Pasaana, where the Aki-Aki know him as the Hermit, while staying in touch with the Solo family.

35 ABY - Rejoining the fight

Lando helps Rey, Finn, Poe and Chewbacca on Pasaana, meets Jannah, and returns to the Resistance base on Ajan Kloss.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Flying the Falcon with Chewbacca, Lando leads the Citizens' Fleet to Exegol to reinforce the Resistance, helping bring down the Final Order and end the war.

Lando Calrissian's Timeline

  • Solo: A Star Wars Story, 10 BBY (33 years old)
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (38 years old)
  • Episode V: The Empire Strikes Back, 3 ABY (46 years old)
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY (47 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (78 years old)

Saw Gerrera, born 42 BBY

Saw Gerrera is best known as the Rebellion's most uncompromising fighter, a guerrilla leader whose methods made even the Alliance keep him at arm's length. He first took up arms with his sister Steela during the Clone Wars, when a Separatist-backed coup put a puppet king on Onderon's throne. Trained by Anakin Skywalker, Ahsoka Tano, Obi-Wan Kenobi and Captain Rex, their rebels helped restore King Ramsis Dendup, but Saw was tortured by the droid general Kalani and lost Steela in the final battle, a death he blamed on himself for the rest of his life. When the Republic became the Empire, he turned his fight against the new regime, formed the Partisans and hid the Erso family on Lah'mu. After Galen Erso was taken, he raised young Jyn as his own and trained her as a soldier before leaving her at sixteen to protect her. His bombings and willingness to spend lives made him a feared figure. Worn down by years of combat and dependent on a breathing apparatus, he broke with Mon Mothma and Bail Organa and set up his final base on Jedha, drawn by the Empire's kyber mining there. There he finally received Galen's message about the Death Star and showed it to Jyn. Then he chose to stay behind and died when the battle station's test shot destroyed Jedha City.

~42 BBY - Birth on Onderon

Born on Onderon in the last years of the Republic and raised in the capital city of Iziz alongside his sister Steela. Records of his birth were later destroyed.

~22 BBY - Coup on Onderon

After the Separatists put King Sanjay Rash on Onderon's throne in place of Ramsis Dendup, Saw and Steela are banished from Iziz for defying him. They start gathering fighters for a resistance.

Pre-20 BBY - Rhydonium work camp

Held prisoner in a jungle rhydonium labor camp as a youth, where he develops his strange attachment to the volatile substance.

20 BBY - Jedi advisors arrive

With Lux Bonteri, Saw asks the Jedi Council for help. Anakin Skywalker, Obi-Wan Kenobi, Ahsoka Tano and Captain Rex come to train the Onderon rebels in guerrilla tactics.

20 BBY - Guerrilla war in Iziz

The rebels move into the capital and knock out the city's power generator with a hijacked tank. Steela, not Saw, is elected leader of the movement.

20 BBY - Capture and torture

A solo attempt to rescue King Dendup ends with Saw's capture and torture by the super tactical droid Kalani. His words persuade General Tandin to defect, and Tandin helps the rebels free him and the king.

20 BBY - Death of Steela

During the final battle in the Onderon highlands, the gunship Saw shoots down crashes near Steela, and she falls to her death. The Separatists withdraw and Dendup is restored, but Saw blames himself for her death.

19 BBY - Resisting the Empire

After the Empire is proclaimed, Saw's Onderonian fighters are branded insurgents and keep fighting. On Onderon he crosses paths with Clone Force 99.

~13 BBY - Takes in Jyn Erso

After hiding the Erso family on Lah'mu, Saw rescues young Jyn when Orson Krennic seizes Galen and kills Lyra. He raises Jyn as his own and trains her as a Partisan.

14 BBY - Kashyyyk

Leads Partisan operations against the Imperial occupation of Kashyyyk and fights alongside Cal Kestis.

~6 BBY - Leaves Jyn behind

Leaves the sixteen-year-old Jyn to fend for herself after her identity is put at risk.

5 BBY - Segra Milo

Meets Luthen Rael, disputes his fellow rebel cells' agendas and refuses to join a coordinated effort.

~2 BBY - Mission to Jalindi

Works with the Ghost crew's Ezra Bridger to sabotage an Imperial relay station and discovers an Imperial kyber shipment, but his ruthlessness drives the Spectres away.

~2 BBY - Break with the Alliance

Formally cut off and censured by Mon Mothma and Bail Organa's Alliance as an extremist.

1 BBY - Holdout on Jedha

Now dependent on a respirator and armored suit, Saw leads a Partisan insurgency against the Imperial occupation of Jedha City.

1 BBY - Reunion with Jyn and death

Saw receives defector Bodhi Rook and plays Galen Erso's message about the Death Star for Jyn. When the station's test shot destroys Jedha City, he stays in his base and dies in the blast.

Saw Gerrera's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY
  • Andor, 5 BBY
  • Rogue One: A Star Wars Story, 1 BBY

Carson Teva, born 42 BBY

Carson Teva is the New Republic X-wing captain who saw the Imperial resurgence coming while his superiors on Coruscant refused to believe it. He fought for the Rebel Alliance as early as the destruction of Alderaan, serving alongside the astromech R5-D4. After the war he became a captain in the Adelphi Rangers, patrolling the Outer Rim with too few ships to cover it. In 9 ABY he and Trapper Wolf chased Din Djarin's Razor Crest onto Maldo Kreis. When they found out the Mandalorian had helped New Republic officers aboard the Bothan-5, they rescued him from ice spiders and let him go. Teva later looked into the destroyed Imperial base on Nevarro, where he tried to recruit Cara Dune, and he let Djarin off with a warning above Tatooine. When pirates took Nevarro, he flew to Coruscant to ask for help and was turned away by Colonel Tuttle and Amnesty officer Elia Kane. He then took Greef Karga's plea to the Mandalorian covert himself, which led to the planet's liberation. He also found the wrecked prison shuttle that proved Moff Gideon never reached trial, and he agreed to hire Djarin as an unofficial contractor. Later he flew with Hera Syndulla on her unauthorized mission to Seatos, stood up for her at the hearing that followed, and helped rescue Djarin, Grogu and Rotta from Nal Hutta.

Unknown - Birth on Torolis

Carson Teva is born on Torolis. Canon gives no birth year.

1 BBY - Rebel Service at Alderaan

Teva is already serving in the Rebel Alliance when the Death Star destroys Alderaan. During his time with the Rebellion he serves alongside the astromech R5-D4.

~5 ABY - Joins the New Republic Starfighter Corps

After the Empire falls, Teva stays on with the New Republic. He becomes a captain flying a T-65B X-wing with the Adelphi Rangers out of Adelphi Base.

9 ABY - Pursuit of the Razor Crest

On Spinward patrol with Trapper Wolf, Teva stops the Razor Crest because its transponder is off. When the pilots tie it to the Bothan-5 breakout, Din Djarin flees and they chase him into the canyons of Maldo Kreis.

9 ABY - Rescue on Maldo Kreis

The Bothan-5 records show that Djarin helped the New Republic officers. Teva and Wolf save him from a swarm of ice spiders and let him go, telling him to fix his transponder.

9 ABY - Investigation on Nevarro

Teva questions Greef Karga about the destroyed Imperial base and the Razor Crest. He offers Cara Dune a place in the New Republic, and when she turns him down he leaves her a rank plaque.

~9 ABY - Warning Above Tatooine

Teva and Lieutenant Reed pull over Djarin's N-1 starfighter. Teva recognizes the Mandalorian's voice and lets him off with a warning. Djarin escapes before Teva can ask about Nevarro.

~9 ABY - Plea to Coruscant

Greef Karga sends word that Gorian Shard's pirates have taken Nevarro. Teva flies to Coruscant to ask for help, but Colonel Tuttle and Amnesty officer Elia Kane turn him down.

~9 ABY - Recruiting the Mandalorians

Teva follows R5-D4's signal to the Mandalorian covert and gives Djarin Karga's message. The Mandalorians go on to free Nevarro from the pirates.

~9 ABY - Gideon's Wrecked Transport

Teva finds the breached prison shuttle that was carrying Moff Gideon. The dead guards and a fragment of beskar suggest Gideon was broken out before he could reach trial.

~9 ABY - Djarin Signs On

After Mandalore is liberated, Teva agrees to take on Din Djarin as an unofficial New Republic contractor. As an advance payment, he supplies parts to rebuild IG-11.

~11 ABY - Mission to Seatos

Teva leads fighters escorting Hera Syndulla's Ghost on her unauthorized mission. He survives the shockwave when the Eye of Sion jumps to hyperspace, and he helps search for Ahsoka Tano and pull her from the sea.

~11 ABY - Stalling the Fleet

Teva holds off Captain Girard's fleet while Syndulla and Tano leave to follow the purrgil. Then he watches the purrgil jump away.

~11 ABY - Syndulla's Hearing

At Hera Syndulla's hearing on Coruscant, Teva challenges Senator Hamato Xiono's claim that the Imperial remnants pose no threat. Leia Organa's authorization ends the proceedings.

~9-12 ABY - Rescue on Nal Hutta

Teva flies with the Adelphi Rangers under Colonel Ward to rescue Din Djarin, Grogu and Rotta from Nal Hutta. The Rangers fight off Starhoppers and destroy the Hutt hideout.

Carson Teva's Timeline

  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY
  • The Book of Boba Fett, 9 ABY
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY

Anakin Skywalker, born 41 BBY

The prophesied Chosen One who was meant to bring balance to the Force, Anakin Skywalker went from enslaved boy to celebrated Jedi hero, then became the Sith Lord Darth Vader, and was finally redeemed by his son. Born a slave to Shmi Skywalker with no father, he had an unmatched midi-chlorian count. Qui-Gon Jinn found him at age nine, and he won his freedom in the Boonta Eve podrace. Later that year he destroyed the Trade Federation's droid control ship at the Battle of Naboo. The Jedi Council had doubts about him, but he trained under Obi-Wan Kenobi and secretly married Padmé Amidala at the start of the Clone Wars. During the war he earned the name "Hero With No Fear" commanding the 501st Legion, and he trained Ahsoka Tano as his Padawan. Palpatine played on his fear of losing Padmé, and in 19 BBY he turned to the dark side, led the attack on the Jedi Temple during Order 66, and was left maimed after his duel with Kenobi on Mustafar. Rebuilt as an armored cyborg, Vader spent two decades hunting Jedi and enforcing the Emperor's rule. He killed Kenobi aboard the Death Star, discovered that Luke was his son, and tried to turn him. At the Battle of Endor in 4 ABY, seeing Luke tortured brought Anakin back. He threw Palpatine to his death, fulfilled the prophecy at the cost of his own life, and became one with the Force.

41 BBY - Birth

Born to the slave Shmi Skywalker, who conceived him without a father. Sources disagree on whether he was born on Tatooine or brought there as an infant.

~38 BBY - Sold to Watto

At about age three, Gardulla the Hutt loses Anakin and his mother to the Toydarian junk dealer Watto in a podracing bet.

~33 BBY - Builds C-3PO and a Podracer

While working in Watto's shop in Mos Espa, he builds the protocol droid C-3PO from scrap to help his mother and assembles his own podracer.

32 BBY - Discovered by Qui-Gon Jinn

Meets Qui-Gon Jinn and Padmé Amidala, who is disguised as a handmaiden. A blood test shows a midi-chlorian count higher than Yoda's.

32 BBY - Wins the Boonta Eve Classic

Wins the podrace, which pays for the Queen's ship repairs and earns him his freedom. He leaves his mother behind to go with the Jedi.

32 BBY - Tested by the Jedi Council

The Council judges him too old and too fearful to be trained, but lets him go with Qui-Gon to Naboo.

32 BBY - Battle of Naboo

Flying an N-1 starfighter, he destroys the Trade Federation's droid control ship. Qui-Gon dies, and Obi-Wan Kenobi takes Anakin as his Padawan.

22 BBY - Guardian of Padmé

Assigned to protect Senator Amidala after attempts on her life, he goes into hiding with her on Naboo and falls in love with her.

22 BBY - Death of Shmi and the Tusken Massacre

Troubled by visions, he returns to Tatooine and finds his mother dying in a Tusken Raider camp. In his rage he slaughters the entire tribe.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

Loses his right arm fighting Count Dooku as the Clone Wars begin, and is fitted with a cybernetic replacement.

22 BBY - Secret Wedding

Marries Padmé Amidala in secret on Naboo, breaking the Jedi Code.

22 BBY - Knighthood and Ahsoka Tano

Now a Jedi Knight and General of the 501st Legion, he is given Ahsoka Tano as his Padawan during the Battle of Christophsis and the rescue of Jabba's son.

~21 BBY - Mortis

On Mortis, the Father tests him as the Chosen One. He glimpses his future as Darth Vader, but the memory is erased.

19 BBY - Ahsoka Leaves the Order

Ahsoka is falsely accused of bombing the Jedi Temple. Even after she is cleared, she walks away from the Order, and Anakin's faith in the Jedi is badly shaken.

19 BBY - Battle of Coruscant

Rescues Chancellor Palpatine and executes Count Dooku at Palpatine's urging. He is then seated on the Jedi Council but denied the rank of Master.

19 BBY - Becomes Darth Vader

Learns that Palpatine is Darth Sidious and helps him strike down Mace Windu, hoping Sidious can save Padmé from the deaths he has foreseen. He pledges himself to the Sith as Darth Vader.

19 BBY - Order 66

Leads the 501st in the attack on the Jedi Temple and kills its inhabitants, younglings included. He then wipes out the Separatist Council on Mustafar.

19 BBY - Duel on Mustafar

Chokes Padmé in a fit of rage and loses a duel to Obi-Wan, who leaves him maimed and burning. Palpatine has him rebuilt in a life-support suit. Padmé dies after giving birth to Luke and Leia.

~18 BBY - Jedi Hunter

As the Emperor's enforcer, he hunts down surviving Jedi and oversees the Inquisitorius.

9 BBY - Hunting Kenobi

Draws Obi-Wan out of hiding and fights him twice. In their second duel Kenobi damages his helmet and leaves him behind.

2 BBY - Confronts the Ghost Crew

Moves against the growing rebel movement at Lothal and in the Siege of Lothal. Ahsoka Tano learns that her former master has become Vader.

2 BBY - Duel on Malachor

Duels Ahsoka inside the Sith temple on Malachor and nearly dies in its collapse.

1 BBY - Battle of Scarif

Boards the rebel flagship Profundity and slaughters its crew, but fails to recover the stolen Death Star plans.

1 BBY - Kills Obi-Wan Kenobi

Captures Princess Leia, his own daughter though neither knows it, and strikes down Kenobi aboard the Death Star.

0 BBY - Battle of Yavin

Survives the Death Star's destruction and learns that the pilot who destroyed it was a Skywalker, his son.

3 ABY - Battle of Hoth and Cloud City

Leads the attack on Echo Base and pursues Luke to Bespin. There he cuts off Luke's hand, reveals that he is Luke's father, and offers to rule the galaxy with him.

4 ABY - Redemption and Death at Endor

Brings Luke before the Emperor aboard the second Death Star. Watching Sidious torture his son, he turns on his master and throws him down a reactor shaft, taking mortal wounds from the lightning. He dies unmasked beside Luke and becomes one with the Force.

4 ABY - Appears as a Force Spirit

Appears as a Force spirit on Endor alongside Obi-Wan and Yoda during the victory celebration.

~9 ABY - Teaches Ahsoka in the World Between Worlds

Appears to Ahsoka Tano in the World Between Worlds for a last lesson, making her face the choice between life and death.

35 ABY - Voice at Exegol

He is one of the Jedi voices that urge Rey to bring back the balance during the Battle of Exegol.

Anakin Skywalker's Timeline

Grogu, born 41 BBY

Grogu is the small, Force-gifted foundling whose rescue turned a hardened bounty hunter into a father. As a youngling he was raised at the Jedi Temple on Coruscant and taught by several Masters. When Order 66 struck, Kelleran Beq carried him out of the burning Temple, and for decades afterward he survived by hiding his powers from the Empire. His species ages slowly, so he was about fifty but still looked like an infant when Moff Gideon's Imperial remnant hired Din Djarin to bring him in. Djarin handed him over, then changed his mind and took him back, and the Mandalorian covert on Nevarro declared the pair a clan of two. On their travels Grogu lifted a mudhorn, healed Greef Karga and turned a flamethrower's fire back on an Imperial trooper. On Tython he called out through the Force, was captured by Gideon, and was freed when Luke Skywalker arrived to take him for Jedi training. At Luke's academy he was offered a choice between Yoda's lightsaber and Mandalorian chain mail, and he chose Djarin. He helped retake Mandalore, was formally adopted as Din Grogu, and began training as a Mandalorian. He later worked with his father as a New Republic ally, hunting Imperial warlords and rescuing Rotta the Hutt.

~41 BBY - Birth

Grogu is born to Yoda's species during the Republic Era. He is later raised at the Jedi Temple on Coruscant as a youngling.

~41 BBY-19 BBY - Youngling at the Jedi Temple

Several Jedi Masters train him over the years. During this time he meets a group of dust bunnies, who give him a flower.

19 BBY - Escape from Order 66

During the attack on the Jedi Temple, Jedi defenders die getting him to safety. Kelleran Beq then flees with him by BARC speeder and escapes Coruscant aboard a Naboo yacht.

19 BBY-9 ABY - Hidden through the Imperial Era

Grogu goes into hiding and conceals his Force abilities, which keeps him from being detected by the Inquisitors. His memories of this period are dark and lonely.

9 ABY - Found on Arvala-7

Din Djarin and IG-11 storm a Nikto camp to claim the bounty on him. Djarin shoots IG-11 before the droid can kill him.

9 ABY - The Mudhorn

Grogu uses the Force to lift a charging mudhorn, which lets Djarin kill it. The effort leaves him unconscious.

9 ABY - Rescued on Nevarro

Djarin delivers Grogu to the Client and Dr. Pershing, then changes his mind and breaks him out. The Mandalorian covert helps them fight off the Bounty Hunters' Guild.

9 ABY - Sorgan and Tatooine

Grogu hides out with Djarin in a krill-farming village on Sorgan. On Tatooine, Peli Motto looks after him, and Toro Calican briefly takes him hostage.

9 ABY - Prison ship job

He rides along on Ranzar Malk's mercenary mission, and Q9-0 nearly kills him before Djarin destroys the droid.

9 ABY - Battle for Nevarro

Grogu heals Greef Karga's wounds and turns an incinerator trooper's flames back on him. The Armorer declares him Djarin's foundling, forming Clan Mudhorn.

9 ABY - Searching for the Jedi

He travels with Djarin to Mos Pelgo, Trask and Nevarro. On Corvus, Ahsoka Tano learns his name and past through the Force but declines to train him.

9 ABY - Captured on Tython

Grogu reaches out through the Force from the seeing stone on Tython. Dark troopers seize him for Moff Gideon.

9 ABY - Rescue by Luke Skywalker

Djarin rescues him from Gideon's cruiser. Luke Skywalker arrives, destroys the dark troopers and takes Grogu away for Jedi training.

9 ABY - Training under Luke

He trains at Luke's new academy site on Ossus. When he has to choose between Yoda's lightsaber and Djarin's gift of beskar chain mail, he chooses the mail.

9 ABY - Return to Din Djarin

Grogu reunites with Djarin on Tatooine during Boba Fett's war against the Pyke Syndicate. He uses the Force to calm a rampaging rancor.

~9 ABY - Liberation of Mandalore

Grogu fights alongside the reunited Mandalorians to defeat Moff Gideon and retake Mandalore. Afterward the Armorer formally makes him Din Grogu, Djarin's son and Mandalorian apprentice.

After 9 ABY - Hunting Imperials for the New Republic

Grogu helps Djarin hunt the warlord Barro, rescues Rotta the Hutt and saves his poisoned father with an antidote. The adventure ends in the destruction of the Hutt Palace on Nal Hutta.

Grogu's Timeline

  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY (50 years old)
  • The Book of Boba Fett, 9 ABY (50 years old)
  • The Mandalorian and Grogu, 10 ABY (51 years old)

Fennec Shand, born 39 BBY

Fennec Shand is an elite mercenary and Master Assassin who went from a young newcomer in the bounty-hunting trade to Boba Fett's most trusted partner. She broke into the business just after the Clone Wars. One of her first big jobs was a secret commission from the Kaminoan scientist Nala Se to recover the clone girl Omega. That hunt pitted her against Clone Force 99 on Pantora and against the veteran hunter Cad Bane at Bora Vio, where she outfought him. Over the Imperial years she built a reputation as one of the most feared and expensive guns for hire in the underworld, working for syndicates including the Hutts. After the Empire fell, the New Republic jailed many of her employers and she went on the run with a price on her head. In 9 ABY, Din Djarin and the rookie Toro Calican caught her in the Tatooine desert, and Calican shot her and left her to die. Boba Fett found her, paid to have her rebuilt with cybernetic organs, and won her loyalty. She helped him reclaim Slave I, recover his armor on Tython, and rescue Grogu from Moff Gideon's cruiser. She then stood beside him as he took Jabba's old palace and became daimyo of Mos Espa, acting as his enforcer, strategist and right hand through the war against the Pyke Syndicate.

19 BBY - Enters the Bounty Hunting Trade

With the Clone Wars just over, Shand begins working as a bounty hunter and gun for hire. She quickly earns a reputation for cunning and ruthlessness, and even Cad Bane takes notice.

19 BBY - Hired to Capture Omega

The Kaminoan scientist Nala Se secretly commissions Shand to bring in the clone girl Omega, who has fled Kamino with Clone Force 99, so that Omega stays out of Lama Su's hands.

19 BBY - Chase on Pantora

Acting on a tip from Raspar Six, Shand tracks Omega to Ro Station, wins the girl's trust and then fights Hunter and Wrecker. A skyway chase ends with her stolen airspeeder destroyed and Omega getting away.

19 BBY - Showdown with Cad Bane at Bora Vio

Shand kills Taun We to stop Bane's handoff of Omega. She fights Bane through the abandoned cloning facility, knocks him over a balcony and sabotages his ship, while the Bad Batch rescues Omega. Nala Se pays her for the job.

~18 BBY - Hunt for Sylar Saris

In exchange for information, Hunter and Wrecker help Shand pursue the fugitive bounty hunter Sylar Saris through the swamps of Veelo.

Imperial Era - Master Assassin

Shand becomes one of the galaxy's most feared and expensive mercenaries, working for the top crime syndicates, including the Hutt Clan, and becomes wanted by the Imperial Security Bureau.

3 ABY - Too Expensive to Hire

After the Battle of Hoth, the bondsman Syphacc hires the pit fighter Tasu Leech instead of Shand to hunt Beilert Valance, because she costs more.

~5 ABY - On the Run

After the Empire's defeat at Jakku, the New Republic imprisons many of her employers. Shand becomes a fugitive with a 10,000-credit bounty on her head.

9 ABY - Captured Beyond the Dune Sea

Hiding on Tatooine, Shand kills a pursuing hunter, then holds off Din Djarin and Toro Calican until a night assault forces her to surrender.

9 ABY - Shot by Toro Calican

Shand talks Calican into turning on Djarin, but the rookie shoots her in the stomach and leaves her for dead.

9 ABY - Saved by Boba Fett

Boba Fett finds her dying, has a Mos Eisley mod artist replace her ruined organs with cybernetics, and brings her back to health. Shand pledges herself to his service.

9 ABY - Reclaiming Slave I

Shand helps Fett infiltrate Jabba's palace to take back Slave I from Bib Fortuna. They then search the Sarlacc pit for his armor, and she kills the Sarlacc with a seismic charge.

9 ABY - Confrontation on Tython

With Fett, Shand confronts Din Djarin at the Force-henge to reclaim Fett's armor. The three fight off Moff Gideon's stormtroopers, but Dark Troopers carry Grogu away.

9 ABY - Raid on Morak

Shand provides sniper cover alongside Cara Dune while Djarin and Migs Mayfeld infiltrate the Imperial refinery to find Gideon's cruiser.

9 ABY - Rescue of Grogu

Shand joins Bo-Katan Kryze, Koska Reeves and Cara Dune in storming Gideon's light cruiser and taking the bridge. Luke Skywalker arrives and leaves with Grogu.

9 ABY - Taking Jabba's Palace

Back on Tatooine, Shand stands beside Fett as he kills Bib Fortuna and claims the throne. She becomes his chief lieutenant as he takes over as daimyo of Mos Espa.

9 ABY - Defending the Daimyo

Shand fights off the Night Wind assassins who ambush Fett in Mos Espa. She advises him through his dealings with the Hutt twins, Black Krrsantan and Mayor Mok Shaiz.

9 ABY - Recruiting the Mods

Shand brings a gang of cybernetically modified youths into Fett's service as enforcers.

9 ABY - War with the Pyke Syndicate

During the fight for Mos Espa, Shand kills the mayor and the crime bosses who betrayed Fett, then fights alongside Fett, Din Djarin and the Mods until the Pykes are defeated.

Fennec Shand's Timeline

  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY
  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY
  • The Book of Boba Fett, 9 ABY

Ahsoka Tano, born 36 BBY

Best known as Anakin Skywalker's headstrong Padawan during the Clone Wars and later as the rebel spymaster "Fulcrum," Ahsoka Tano grew from an overeager teenager into one of the galaxy's most principled warriors. Found by Plo Koon as a toddler and raised at the Jedi Temple, she was placed with Skywalker at fourteen. Anakin nicknamed her "Snips," and she fought beside him and Captain Rex of the 501st from Christophsis and Teth to Ryloth, Geonosis, and Onderon. On Mortis she died and was brought back to life by the Daughter. When she was framed for the bombing of the Temple hangar, the Order expelled her. Anakin exposed Barriss Offee as the real bomber, but Ahsoka declined to return. She came back to lead the Siege of Mandalore against Maul, survived Order 66 with Rex, and faked her death. Under the Empire she lived in hiding and then ran Bail Organa's intelligence network, guiding cells like the Ghost crew. She faced her former master, now Darth Vader, on Malachor and was pulled to safety through the World Between Worlds by Ezra Bridger. After the Empire fell, she searched for Ezra with Sabine Wren, helped Din Djarin free Calodan, and went after Grand Admiral Thrawn. Decades later, her voice was among the Jedi who helped Rey defeat Sidious.

36 BBY - Birth on Shili

Born to Pav-ti and Nak-il Tano. As a small child she rides a raxshir, and the elder Gantika sees that she is Force-sensitive.

33 BBY - Discovered by Plo Koon

At age three she escapes a false Jedi who tries to take her. Jedi Master Plo Koon finds her and brings her to the Jedi Temple on Coruscant.

~30 BBY - Gathering on Ilum

As an initiate she finds her kyber crystal in the Ilum cave by following Yoda's advice, then builds her lightsaber under Huyang's guidance.

22 BBY - Padawan to Anakin Skywalker

Yoda assigns the fourteen-year-old to Anakin Skywalker. She joins him at the Battle of Christophsis, where he nicknames her "Snips," and meets Captain Rex.

22 BBY - Rescue of Rotta

With Anakin she rescues Jabba's son from Teth, fights Asajj Ventress, and brings the Huttlet home, which secures the Republic's treaty with the Hutts.

22 BBY - The Malevolence Campaign

She rescues Plo Koon's survivors and serves as Anakin's gunner in the attack that cripples Grievous's flagship.

22 BBY - Hunt for R2-D2

She duels General Grievous at the Separatist listening post near Ruusan while Anakin recovers R2-D2.

22 BBY - Battle of Ryloth

In her first command she disobeys orders and loses Blue Squadron, then recovers to help win the battle.

21 BBY - Second Battle of Geonosis

With Barriss Offee she destroys the droid foundry and survives being trapped in the rubble.

20 BBY - Death and Resurrection on Mortis

The Son kills her on Mortis, and the Daughter gives up her own life to revive her.

20 BBY - Wasskah and Kadavo

Captured by Trandoshan hunters, she leads the captive younglings in a revolt. She then helps free a Togruta colony from the Zygerrian slavers.

20 BBY - Onderon Rebellion

She advises Lux Bonteri and the Onderon rebels as they free their world from the Separatists.

19 BBY - Framed and Leaves the Order

Accused of bombing the Temple hangar, she escapes into the Underworld and gets help from Ventress, but she is caught and expelled. Anakin exposes Barriss Offee as the bomber, and Ahsoka turns down the Council's offer to take her back.

19 BBY - Siege of Mandalore

She returns to lead the 332nd alongside Bo-Katan and captures Maul.

19 BBY - Order 66

She frees Rex from his inhibitor chip, escapes the Tribunal as it crashes, and fakes her death.

~18 BBY - Hiding as Ashla

Under the name Ashla she lives on Thabeska and Raada. She helps the Raada uprising and defeats the Sixth Brother.

~18 BBY - Becomes Fulcrum

She joins Bail Organa's resistance and runs its intelligence network under the codename Fulcrum.

4 BBY - Meets the Ghost Crew and Vader

After revealing herself to the Spectres, she senses that Darth Vader is Anakin and fights the Inquisitors.

3 BBY - Duel on Malachor

She reunites with Rex, then faces Vader in the Sith temple and is believed to have died there.

Unknown - World Between Worlds

A future Ezra Bridger pulls her into the World Between Worlds. She helps him escape Sidious and returns to her own time.

~5 ABY - Search for Ezra

After the Empire falls she joins Sabine Wren to look for the missing Ezra and trains Sabine for a time.

9 ABY - Liberation of Calodan

With Din Djarin she frees Calodan from Morgan Elsbeth. She declines to train Grogu and sends Djarin to Tython.

9 ABY - Visits Luke's Academy

She stops at Luke Skywalker's new temple and watches Grogu's training.

~11 ABY - Pursuit of Thrawn

She brings Sabine back into the search, follows Elsbeth to Peridea, and helps free Ezra, although she and Sabine are left stranded there.

35 ABY - Voice at Exegol

Hers is one of the Jedi voices that strengthen Rey against Sidious.

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (14 years old)
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY (45 years old)

Bo-Katan Kryze, born 36 BBY

Bo-Katan Kryze spent decades fighting to free Mandalore, and she did it first as a terrorist, then as a rebel, and finally as its ruler. She was born into the royal House Kryze and grew up in the shadow of her pacifist sister, Duchess Satine. Rejecting Satine's New Mandalorian ideals, she joined Death Watch and became Pre Vizsla's trusted lieutenant and commander of the elite Nite Owls. She helped Vizsla and Maul's Shadow Collective overthrow her sister's government, then broke with Maul when he killed Vizsla and seized the throne. She rescued Satine too late to save her, freed Obi-Wan Kenobi, and led the resistance in the civil war that followed. Working with Ahsoka Tano, she brought the Republic's forces to Mandalore, captured Maul during the Siege of Mandalore and was named regent. She lost the position when she refused to serve the new Emperor and Clan Saxon betrayed her. Years later she helped Sabine Wren destroy the Duchess weapon and accepted the Darksaber to lead the clans against the Empire. Her rule ended in the Night of a Thousand Tears, when Moff Gideon took the blade from her. After the Empire fell she raided Imperial shipments, helped Din Djarin rescue Grogu and eventually won back the Darksaber. She then united the scattered Mandalorian coverts, retook Mandalore from Gideon's remnant forces and relit the Great Forge to begin rebuilding her homeworld.

Unknown - Birth into House Kryze

Born on Mandalore into Clan Kryze, the royal house of Mandalore, as the younger sister of Satine Kryze. She grows up flying over the hills of Kalevala.

Unknown - Taking the Creed

As a child she recites the Mandalorian Creed in the Living Waters beneath Mandalore while her father, Adonai Kryze, watches. Later she dismisses the ceremony as theater for the royal family's subjects.

Unknown - Joining Death Watch

After her father dies and Satine becomes Duchess of the pacifist New Mandalorians, Bo-Katan joins Death Watch. She becomes Pre Vizsla's lieutenant and commander of the Nite Owls.

~21 BBY - Exile to Carlac

After Death Watch's failed alliance with Count Dooku, the group is exiled from the Mandalore system and sets up camp on Carlac.

20 BBY - Conflict on Carlac

She meets Lux Bonteri and his disguised companion, Ahsoka Tano, and fights Tano and a group of rebuilt battle droids when the Jedi's identity comes out.

19 BBY - Discovery of Maul

While Death Watch is based on Zanbar, she and Vizsla find Maul and Savage Opress drifting in an escape pod. Maul Force-chokes her when she questions an alliance with the Sith.

19 BBY - Forming the Shadow Collective

She joins the missions to Mustafar, Nal Hutta and Tatooine that bring Black Sun, the Pykes and the Hutts into Maul's Shadow Collective, and she leads the attack on Jabba's Palace.

19 BBY - Takeover of Mandalore

She helps stage Death Watch's fake rescue of Sundari, topples Satine's government, introduces Vizsla as Prime Minister and takes Maul prisoner.

19 BBY - Break with Maul

After Maul kills Vizsla in a duel for the Darksaber, she refuses to accept an outsider as ruler and escapes the palace with her loyal Nite Owls.

19 BBY - Rescue of Satine and Kenobi

With her nephew Korkie, she breaks Satine out of the royal prison, but Satine is recaptured. After Maul kills Satine, Bo-Katan frees Obi-Wan Kenobi and asks him to bring the Republic to Mandalore.

19 BBY - Hunting Maul

During the civil war she and the Nite Owls track Maul for months. She recruits Ahsoka Tano on Coruscant, and together they ask Kenobi and Anakin Skywalker for Republic help.

19 BBY - Siege of Mandalore

She fights alongside Tano and Rex's clone troopers, captures Prime Minister Almec and duels Gar Saxon. She helps take Maul prisoner and seals him in a Mandalorian vault.

19 BBY - Regent of Mandalore

The Jedi name her regent of Mandalore. When she refuses to obey the new Emperor, Clan Saxon betrays her and removes her from power during the Imperial occupation.

1 BBY - Destroying the Duchess

She allies with Clan Wren and the Spectres and helps Sabine Wren destroy the Arc Pulse Generator aboard Tiber Saxon's Star Destroyer.

1 BBY - Taking the Darksaber

With the backing of several clans, she accepts the Darksaber from Sabine and leads the Mandalorian resistance. The Children of the Watch reject her claim because she did not win the blade in combat.

Imperial Era - Night of a Thousand Tears

The Empire devastates Mandalore in the Great Purge. She surrenders to Moff Gideon, who betrays the ceasefire and takes the Darksaber from her.

9 ABY - Raid on Trask

With Koska Reeves and Axe Woves, she rescues Din Djarin and Grogu, then seizes an Imperial Gozanti cruiser loaded with weapons.

9 ABY - Assault on Gideon's Cruiser

She helps Djarin rescue Grogu and takes the bridge of Gideon's light cruiser. Djarin wins the Darksaber from Gideon, and she refuses to accept it from him as a gift.

9 ABY - Loss of her Fleet

Without the Darksaber, she loses the support of her followers. Axe Woves takes her fleet, and she withdraws alone to Kalevala.

9 ABY - Return to Mandalore

She guides Djarin through the ruins of Mandalore, saves him from a cyborg captor and sees a living mythosaur while bathing in the Living Waters.

9 ABY - Reclaiming the Darksaber

She joins the Armorer's covert of the Children of the Watch, wins back her Nite Owl fleet in battle and receives the Darksaber from Djarin.

9 ABY - Liberation of Mandalore

She leads the united Mandalorians in retaking the planet from Moff Gideon's Imperial remnant and relights the Great Forge to begin the reconstruction.

Bo-Katan Kryze's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY
  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY

R4-P17, born 35 BBY

Obi-Wan Kenobi's loyal astromech through the last years of the Republic, R4-P17 was a cautious but quick-reacting droid with feminine programming, recognizable by the red replacement dome she wore on her white body. She rode in the socket of Kenobi's starfighter when he investigated the plot against Senator Padmé Amidala, which took them to Kamino and a dogfight with Slave I before they reached Geonosis. She stayed with him once the Clone Wars broke out. She fended off scavenging Jawas on Tatooine with her shock prod, flew his fighter by herself above Teth after he leapt into the battle below, and went with him on missions to Rodia and Mandalore. She was also with him when Asajj Ventress shot them down at Sullust. Her service ended in the war's final days during the Battle of Coruscant, when buzz droids swarmed Kenobi's interceptor near General Grievous's flagship and sawed off her dome before his warning could help. R4-G9 took her place for the mission to Utapau.

Before 22 BBY - Manufactured by Industrial Automaton

Built as an R4-P model astromech with feminine programming and later assigned to Jedi Master Obi-Wan Kenobi. She wears a red dome taken from another astromech.

22 BBY - Mission to Kamino

Rides in the droid socket of Kenobi's Delta-7 starfighter while he traces the assassin who tried to kill Padmé Amidala, and helps him evade Jango Fett's Slave I.

22 BBY - Geonosis

Goes with Kenobi to Geonosis, where he uncovers the Separatist droid army just before the Clone Wars begin.

~22 BBY - Tatooine and Jabba's Palace

While guarding Kenobi's starfighter during talks with Jabba the Hutt, she shocks a Jawa with her prod and drives off the scavengers.

~22 BBY - Battle of Teth

Pilots Kenobi's starfighter on her own after he jumps from it to join the fight on the ground during the rescue of Jabba's son.

~21 BBY - Rodia

Travels with Kenobi to Rodia to stop Cad Bane from kidnapping Force-sensitive children.

~21 BBY - Mandalore

Goes with Kenobi to Mandalore, where he meets Duchess Satine Kryze. She stays behind while he goes to the Sundari Royal Palace.

~20 BBY - Battle of Sullust

Asajj Ventress shoots her and Kenobi down. They escape as the Separatist command ship is destroyed.

~19 BBY - Jedi Temple

Rolls through the Temple corridors alongside R4-M1 while Kenobi and Mace Windu discuss Yoda.

19 BBY - Destroyed at the Battle of Coruscant

Buzz droids land on Kenobi's Eta-2 interceptor as he approaches the Invisible Hand to rescue Chancellor Palpatine, and they saw off her dome before his warning can help.

19 BBY - Replaced by R4-G9

R4-G9 takes over as Kenobi's astromech for his mission to Utapau.

R4-P17's Timeline

  • Episode II: Attack of the Clones, 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • Episode III: Revenge of the Sith, 19 BBY

C-3PO, born 34 BBY

C-3PO is the anxious, fussy protocol droid who spent more than half a century close to the most important moments in galactic history, usually complaining about it. He speaks over six million forms of communication, later more than seven million. A slave boy named Anakin Skywalker rebuilt him from scrap in Mos Espa to help his mother Shmi, and he first met his lifelong partner R2-D2 around the Boonta Eve race. He stayed with Shmi on the Lars moisture farm, then followed Anakin into the first battle of the Clone Wars on Geonosis and was there when Anakin married Padmé Amidala. Given gold plating, he served Padmé as her aide through the war. After the Republic fell, his memory was wiped and he joined Bail Organa's household aboard the Tantive IV, while R2-D2 kept the secrets he had lost. Nineteen years later, Leia Organa's mission with the Death Star plans pulled the two droids into the rebellion. They ended up with Luke Skywalker and Han Solo, and 3PO survived the Death Star and Cloud City, where he was blasted apart and rebuilt. On Endor the Ewoks took him for a god, and he talked them into joining the fight. Decades later he was still at Leia's side in the Resistance. To find the path to Exegol he let his memory be wiped so he could translate a forbidden Sith inscription, and R2-D2's backup later brought his memories back once Palpatine was defeated.

Unknown - Early Service

In an earlier life as a 3PO-series unit, he serves as protocol droid to the chief negotiator of the Manakron system. He is later badly damaged, and his parts end up on a Tatooine junk pile.

~34 BBY - Rebuilt by Anakin Skywalker

Young slave Anakin Skywalker starts rebuilding the droid from scrap in Mos Espa to help his mother Shmi. He has no plating, so his wiring is exposed.

32 BBY - Meeting R2-D2

Anakin fits him with a photoreceptor, and he sees for the first time. He meets R2-D2 and Padmé Amidala, and the two droids help prepare Anakin's podracer.

32 BBY - Boonta Eve Classic

He carries Anakin's flag in the opening parade and watches him win his freedom. Anakin leaves Tatooine and says goodbye to him, the job still unfinished.

~32 BBY - The Lars Homestead

He goes with Shmi when Cliegg Lars frees and marries her. Shmi gives him grey plating, and he works on the moisture farm.

22 BBY - Reunion with Anakin

He greets Anakin and Padmé when they arrive looking for the kidnapped Shmi. He attends her funeral and leaves Tatooine with them.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

In the droid foundry his head ends up on a battle droid's body, and he is dragged into the opening battle of the Clone Wars. R2-D2 later reattaches his head to his own body.

22 BBY - Anakin and Padmé's Wedding

He witnesses Anakin and Padmé's secret wedding in Naboo's Lake Country.

22 BBY - Aide to Senator Amidala

With new gold plating, he serves Padmé through the Clone Wars. That includes calling in the Coruscant Guard to rescue her from Ziro the Hutt and escaping the Malevolence.

22–21 BBY - Clone Wars Missions

He survives the Separatist ambush on Rodia and helps negotiate the Pantoran–Talz truce on Orto Plutonia. On Naboo he questions a tactical droid during the Blue Shadow Virus crisis.

~21 BBY - Senate Hostage Crisis

Cad Bane kidnaps him, scans his memory and wipes it. He is later held hostage with Padmé when Bane takes over the Senate Building.

~21–20 BBY - Adventures with R2-D2

He calms the earthquakes on Aleen, lives through the Zillo Beast's rampage on Coruscant, and wanders between Patitite Pattuna, Balnab and a Weequay pirate ship before Plo Koon's fleet rescues him.

19 BBY - Fall of the Republic

He goes with Padmé to Mustafar and is there when she dies. Bail Organa has his memory wiped, and he joins the crew of the Tantive IV.

9 BBY - Alderaan

He works at the Organa court while young Leia is growing up.

~5 BBY - Mission with the Ghost Crew

On a mission for Bail Organa, he and R2-D2 cross paths with the rebels of the Ghost.

1 BBY - Battle of Scarif

He is aboard the Tantive IV when the stolen Death Star plans come to Leia at Scarif.

1 BBY - The Death Star Plans

He escapes to Tatooine with R2-D2 and is sold to the Lars family. He meets Luke Skywalker and Obi-Wan Kenobi, then helps rescue Leia from the Death Star.

0 ABY - Destroyed and Rebuilt

He is destroyed on Vrogas Vas during a rebel mission and then rebuilt.

3 ABY - Hoth and Cloud City

He escapes Hoth on the Millennium Falcon. On Bespin, stormtroopers blast him apart, and Chewbacca later puts him back together.

4 ABY - Jabba's Palace

He is given to Jabba the Hutt as part of the rescue of Han Solo and is forced to serve as the Hutt's translator.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

The Ewoks worship him as a golden god, and he convinces the Bright Tree tribe to fight alongside the Rebel Alliance.

~9 ABY - New Republic Service

As an aide to Senator Leia Organa, he brings the message that saves Hera Syndulla from a court-martial.

~34 ABY - Red Arm

After a crash, he wears a red replacement arm for a short time.

34 ABY - The Resistance

He serves General Leia Organa's Resistance, running its spy droid network, and is at the Battle of Crait.

35 ABY - Translating the Sith Dagger

On Kijimi he lets his memory be wiped so he can translate a Sith inscription that leads to Exegol.

35 ABY - Memory Restored

After the Battle of Exegol, R2-D2 uses his backup to restore C-3PO's memories.
