Kanan Jarrus, born 33 BBY

Kanan Jarrus is best known as the Jedi who survived Order 66 as a teenager and later helped build the rebellion that grew into the Rebel Alliance. He was raised in the Jedi Temple as Caleb Dume and became Depa Billaba's Padawan late in the Clone Wars. He fought beside her clone battalion on Kardoa, Mygeeto and Kaller until the clones turned on her and she died so he could escape. The smuggler Janus Kasmir taught him how to lie, steal and survive, and after that he went by a new name and drifted across the Outer Rim with his lightsaber hidden away. During the Gorse Conflict he met Hera Syndulla, joined her crew aboard the Ghost and became her second-in-command and her partner. Together they formed the Spectres cell on Lothal. Although he had never finished his own training, he took Ezra Bridger as his apprentice, defeated the Grand Inquisitor, and carried on after Maul blinded him on Malachor, learning from the Bendu to see through the Force. He also taught Sabine Wren to wield the Darksaber. In 1 BBY, during the fight to free Lothal, he held back a fuel explosion so that Hera and his friends could escape, and it killed him. He lived on through the Force as the loth-wolf Dume and through his son Jacen, and his voice was among those that helped Rey at Exegol.

33 BBY - Birth

Born Caleb Dume. He is taken to the Jedi Temple on Coruscant so young that he never knows his parents.

~22 BBY - Youngling at the Temple

Trains with fellow initiates Tai Uzuma and Sammo Quid under Yoda. He builds his first lightsaber with help from Huyang, and with Depa Billaba he watches Ahsoka Tano's training session.

~20 BBY - Bombing at the Jedi Temple

Passes the Initiate Trials. He helps Depa Billaba stop the Separatist bomber Rackham Sear, and afterwards she takes him as her Padawan, which makes him a Jedi Commander in the Grand Army.

~20 BBY - Skirmish on Kardoa

On his first mission he is wounded in a droid ambush and saved by the clone trooper CT-1157, who earns the nickname "Stance" for it.

~19 BBY - Third Battle of Mygeeto

Skull Squadron covers him and his unit. Stance is killed, and Caleb defeats Colonel Coburn Sear, his first kill.

19 BBY - Battle of Kaller

At 14 he fights under Billaba in the last days of the Clone Wars. She gives him a holocron, and he fights alongside Clone Force 99.

19 BBY - Order 66

Billaba's clones turn on her, and she dies telling him to run. Hunter of the Bad Batch lets him escape, and he hides out alone in Plateau City.

19 BBY - Partnership with Janus Kasmir

The smuggler Janus Kasmir takes him in. He cuts off his Padawan braid, hides his lightsaber and learns to live as a thief while the clones Grey and Styles hunt him. Later he flees and takes the name Kanan Jarrus.

~18–11 BBY - Years adrift

Moves from world to world as a pilot, bounty hunter and ranger, keeping his Force abilities hidden and staying out of the Empire's sight.

11 BBY - Gorse Conflict

While hauling cargo on Gorse he meets Hera Syndulla. Together they defeat Count Denetrius Vidian, and he joins her crew aboard the Ghost.

~11–5 BBY - Forming the Spectres

Becomes Hera's second-in-command and builds the crew with Zeb Orrelios, Sabine Wren and Chopper, running raids against the Empire around Lothal.

5 BBY - Taking Ezra Bridger as his Padawan

Meets Ezra Bridger on Lothal and reveals himself as a Jedi while rescuing Wookiee slaves on Kessel. He then takes Ezra as his apprentice.

5 BBY - Capture and rescue at Mustafar

Captured by the Empire and interrogated by the Grand Inquisitor. His crew rescues him above Mustafar, where he defeats the Inquisitor in a duel. The Spectres then join Phoenix Cell.

4 BBY - Knighted on Lothal

A Jedi Temple Guard formerly known as the Grand Inquisitor names him a Jedi Knight in the Lothal Jedi Temple.

3 BBY - Blinded on Malachor

Maul blinds him during the mission to the Sith temple on Malachor.

2 BBY - Meeting the Bendu

On Atollon the Bendu teaches him to see through the Force and helps him repair his bond with Ezra.

2 BBY - Training Sabine with the Darksaber

Teaches Sabine Wren to use the Darksaber and helps win Mandalorian support for the rebellion.

2 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Survives Grand Admiral Thrawn's attack on Chopper Base with the rest of Phoenix Cell.

1 BBY - Death on Lothal

Rescues Hera from Governor Pryce's forces in Capital City. When a fuel pod is set alight, he holds back the blast with the Force so that his friends can escape, and he dies in the explosion.

1 BBY - Dume the loth-wolf

His presence in the Force appears to Ezra as a giant loth-wolf named Dume. Hera later gives birth to their son, Jacen.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

His voice is among the Jedi who encourage Rey in her fight against Darth Sidious.

Kanan Jarrus's Timeline

  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY (14 years old)
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (28 years old)

Cassian Andor, born 33 BBY

"

Cassian Andor spent his whole short life fighting, first as a boy fighter on a stripped mining world, then as one of the Rebellion's most trusted spies and killers, and finally as the man who got the Death Star plans off Scarif. Scavengers Clem and Maarva Andor took him from the wreck of a crashed Republic ship, renamed the boy Kassa, gave him papers saying he was born on Fest, and raised him on Ferrix. There he watched the Empire hang his adoptive father. He grew into a drifter and petty thief who only cared about getting by, until an accidental double killing on Morlana One put him in the path of Luthen Rael. For the money, he flew the escape ship in the Aldhani payroll heist. Soon after he was swept up by the Empire's harsher sentencing laws and sent to the Narkina 5 prison factory, where he helped lead the uprising that let the inmates break out. Maarva's funeral set off a riot on Ferrix, and after it he offered himself to Rael's cause. Over the next years he stole a prototype TIE Avenger, took the reprogrammed security droid K-2SO as his partner, witnessed the Empire's crimes on Ghorman, and helped Mon Mothma escape Coruscant. By then he had become a captain in Alliance Intelligence. Sent to kill Galen Erso on Eadu, he could not pull the trigger. He then gathered volunteers to back Jyn Erso's unauthorized raid on Scarif. The team, called Rogue One, sent the Death Star plans to the rebel fleet, and he died alongside Jyn when the battle station destroyed the base.

"

"

33 BBY - Birth on Kenari

Born on Kenari as Kassa. He loses his mother while still young and grows up among a tribe of children left behind after mining poisoned the planet.

~27 BBY - A Fighter at Six

By his own account, he starts fighting as a revolutionary at the age of six.

~24 BBY - Rescued from Kenari

A Republic transport crashes near his village, and a scouting trip to the wreck ends with the tribe's leader dead. Maarva and Clem Andor, scavenging the ship with their droid B2EMO, find Kassa and take him off Kenari. His little sister Kerri is left behind.

~24 BBY - A New Name on Ferrix

He settles on Ferrix as Cassian Jeron Andor. Forged records say he was born on Fest.

19 BBY - Clem Andor's Execution

Clone stormtroopers arrive to bring Ferrix under Imperial control. Clem is wrongly taken for a protester and hanged on Rix Road, and the boy's attack on the troopers who did it begins a long record of trouble with the authorities.

~20 BBY - Youth Detention on Sipo

Starting at thirteen, he spends three years in a youth center on Sipo, one of several detention centers he passes through as a boy.

~17 BBY - Brief Service on Mimban

He later claims he was made to fight for the Empire on Mimban after prison. Luthen Rael says he was actually a cook there for six months and then deserted.

5 BBY - Killings on Morlana One

Looking for his sister on Morlana One, he is shaken down by two Pre-Mor security officers and kills both of them. Corporate investigators follow the trail back to Ferrix.

5 BBY - Meeting Luthen Rael

He tries to sell a stolen Imperial Starpath unit to Luthen Rael. Pre-Mor forces attack the meeting, and Rael gets him off Ferrix.

5 BBY - The Aldhani Heist

Using his adoptive father's name, Clem, he joins Vel Sartha's crew and flies the escape freighter through the Eye of Aldhani with an Imperial sector's payroll aboard. Afterward he shoots Arvel Skeen when Skeen proposes betraying the rest of the crew.

5 BBY - Arrest on Niamos

He hides out on Niamos as a tourist named Keef Girgo, but is picked up on a false charge and given six years under the Public Order Resentencing Directive.

5 BBY - Narkina 5 Escape

He works in the Narkina 5 prison factory until he and floor manager Kino Loy lead a mass uprising. He escapes with Ruescott Melshi.

5 BBY - Maarva's Funeral and the Ferrix Riot

He learns that Maarva has died and goes back to Ferrix, where he frees Bix Caleen from Imperial custody while her funeral turns into a riot. Then he goes to Luthen Rael and offers either to die or to join the fight.

4 BBY - Stealing the TIE Avenger

For Rael's network, he steals a prototype TIE Avenger from Sienar Test Facility 73. On Yavin 4 he is taken prisoner by the warring Maya Pei Brigade and escapes in the fighter.

4 BBY - Mina-Rau

He hurries to Mina-Rau as the Empire moves in, rescues Bix and his Ferrix friends, and gets them off the planet.

~2 BBY - Ghorman and K-2SO

He takes the reprogrammed Imperial security droid K-2SO as his partner. On Ghorman he witnesses the Empire's crackdown, including the massacre.

~2 BBY - Mon Mothma's Escape

After Mon Mothma denounces the Emperor in the Senate, he gets her off Coruscant and she joins the Rebellion openly.

1 BBY - Losing Luthen and Bix

After Luthen Rael dies, he helps Kleya Marki escape and brings back Rael's intelligence on the Empire's superweapon. Bix leaves him for his own safety, carrying their child, though he never knows about it.

1 BBY - Operation Fracture

Now a captain in Alliance Intelligence, he and K-2SO free Jyn Erso and take her to Jedha to find Saw Gerrera. They get away as the Death Star destroys Jedha City.

1 BBY - Mission to Eadu

General Draven orders him to kill Galen Erso, but he does not fire. Galen dies anyway when rebel ships bomb the facility.

1 BBY - Forming Rogue One

After Alliance High Command turns down Jyn's plan, he gathers volunteers to raid the Imperial archive on Scarif without authorization.

1 BBY - Death on Scarif

He and Jyn take the Death Star plans from the Citadel Tower and transmit them to the rebel fleet. The two die together on the beach when the Death Star destroys the base.

"

Cassian Andor's Timeline

  • Andor, 5 BBY (28 years old)
  • Rogue One: A Star Wars Story, 1 BBY (32 years old)

Tech, born 32 BBY

Tech was the brains of Clone Force 99, the Bad Batch: a clone whose deliberate mutations gave him an extraordinary intellect, making him the squad's slicer, engineer, pilot of the Marauder and constant source of statistics nobody asked for. Late in the Clone Wars he helped uncover the Separatists' theft of Rex's battle algorithm on Anaxes, rescued Echo from the Techno Union on Skako Minor and helped turn the tide of the Republic's campaign. When Order 66 came, his inhibitor chip failed to activate. He was the first to work out why the regular clones had turned on the Jedi. Along with Hunter, Wrecker, Echo and the young clone Omega, he deserted the new Empire and left Crosshair behind. As fugitives and mercenaries working for Cid on Ord Mantell, he forged chain codes, raced riot racers, survived the destruction of Tipoca City and helped expose the Empire's secret clone program. Beneath his literal manner lay real loyalty, and he told Omega that thinking differently did not mean he felt any less. During a raid on the Imperial summit at Raven's Peak on Eriadu, Tech stayed behind on a failing tram line so his brothers could escape, and he fell to his death.

~32 BBY - Created on Kamino

Grown in Tipoca City as clone CT-9902 from Jango Fett's template. In Nala Se's private laboratory his mutations are enhanced, giving him an exceptional intellect.

~22-20 BBY - Clone Force 99

Serves as the computer, technology and weapons specialist of the experimental Bad Batch alongside Hunter, Wrecker and Crosshair, on missions across the war including Felucia.

19 BBY - Insurrection on Yalbec Prime

Helps the squad put down an insurrection before being redeployed to the struggling Anaxes campaign.

19 BBY - Battle of Anaxes

With Rex, Jesse and Kix, infiltrates a Separatist Cyber Center. There he discovers that Rex's strategy algorithm is being fed to the enemy from Skako Minor.

19 BBY - Rescue of Echo on Skako Minor

Talks to the Poletecs in their own language, tracks Echo's signal to Purkoll and disconnects him from the Techno Union's machinery. He then summons keeradaks so the group can escape.

19 BBY - Victory at Anaxes

Disguises Echo's transmissions aboard Admiral Trench's dreadnought, helping trick the Separatist fleet into defeat. He turns down the medals offered, and Echo joins the squad.

19 BBY - Kaller and Order 66

Fights alongside Depa Billaba and Caleb Dume on Kaller. When Order 66 is issued, his inhibitor chip fails to activate. He relays the order's meaning to his squad.

19 BBY - Rise of the Empire on Kamino

Explains to the squad how the regular clones' programming differs from theirs. He meets Omega and determines from her DNA that she is also an enhanced clone.

19 BBY - Onderon and desertion

Refuses Tarkin's order to kill Saw Gerrera's civilians. He breaks out of the Kamino brig with his squad and Omega, then flies the Marauder away while Crosshair stays loyal to the Empire.

19 BBY - Fugitives on Saleucami

Forges Imperial chain codes so Cut Lawquane's family can escape off-world. Omega chooses to stay with the Batch.

19 BBY - Ordo Moon and Pantora

Starts building an inhibitor chip scanner, makes repairs while an Ordo Moon dragon prowls outside, and scrambles the Marauder's signature on Pantora while Fennec Shand hunts Omega.

19 BBY - Mercenaries for Cid

Becomes a mercenary for Cid on Ord Mantell. He takes jobs across the galaxy, including piloting a riot racer to earn a coaxium payout.

19 BBY - Fall of Tipoca City

Returns to Kamino to rescue Hunter and survives the Empire's destruction of Tipoca City. The squad escapes with Nala Se's clone data.

~19-18 BBY - Life on the run

Keeps taking missions for Cid, and on one of them grows close to the pirate Phee Genoa. He finds the island world of Pabu as a possible refuge and helps Senator Riyo Chuchi investigate the Empire's treatment of clones.

18 BBY - Death on Eriadu

Infiltrates Raven's Peak during the Imperial summit to find Dr. Royce Hemlock and Crosshair. As the squad's tram fails, he cuts himself loose so his brothers can escape, and falls into the chasm below.

Tech's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY

Wrecker, born 32 BBY

Wrecker is the muscle and demolitions specialist of Clone Force 99, the "Bad Batch" of genetically deviant clones whose mutations made them better at particular jobs. His gave him enormous size and strength and an eager love of explosions. He came out of the Kaminoan cloning program with a scarred, clouded left eye from an early mission. He fought through the late Clone Wars alongside Hunter, Tech and Crosshair. At the Battle of Anaxes he lifted a burning gunship off Commander Cody. He helped rescue Echo from the Techno Union on Skako Minor, and Anakin Skywalker let him detonate a Separatist dreadnought. His squad's mutations shielded them from Order 66, and after refusing to follow the new Empire's orders they deserted Kamino with the young clone Omega. Wrecker became her protective big brother, even after his inhibitor chip briefly turned him against her before it was removed. Through years as fugitives and mercenaries he survived the destruction of Tipoca City, lost Tech on Eriadu, and helped Hunter storm Mount Tantiss to free Omega and the captive clone children. He then settled on Pabu, one of the few clones to find a peaceful life after the war.

Date unknown - Created on Kamino

Grown from Jango Fett's template as CT-9903. A cloning deviation gives him extraordinary size and strength, and he is placed in the experimental unit Clone Force 99 with Hunter, Tech and Crosshair.

Clone Wars - Eye injury

An early mission leaves his left eye scarred and clouded, but the injury barely slows him down.

19 BBY - Insurrection on Yalbec Prime

While the squad puts down an insurrection, Wrecker cuts off a Yalbec queen's stinger, and the male Yalbec swarm the team.

19 BBY - Battle of Anaxes

After their gunship is shot down, he lifts the wreck off a trapped Commander Cody. He then helps Captain Rex's team raid a Separatist Cyber Center and trace Admiral Trench's intelligence to a live signal.

19 BBY - Rescue of Echo on Skako Minor

He overcomes his fear of heights to storm Wat Tambor's tower and destroys the lab where Echo was held. He also holds back a tri-droid to protect the Poletec villagers.

19 BBY - Destruction of Trench's fleet

Anakin Skywalker hands him a detonator taken from the Invulnerable, and Wrecker destroys the Separatist fleet over Anaxes. Echo then joins the squad.

19 BBY - Order 66 on Kaller

After the squad rescues Depa Billaba's forces, he watches the clones turn on the Jedi. Unaffected by the order, he stalls troopers pursuing Caleb Dume.

19 BBY - Return to an Imperial Kamino

He meets Omega, starts a mess-hall brawl, and is tested with live fire in a combat simulation run for Tarkin.

19 BBY - Desertion

After refusing to kill Saw Gerrera's followers on Onderon, the squad is imprisoned. Wrecker breaks the cell wall and escapes with Omega, but a reprogrammed Crosshair shoots him in the shoulder during the escape.

19 BBY - Life as fugitives

He reunites with the Lawquanes on Saleucami, tears the Marauder free of its impound clamps, and survives a crash on the Ordo Moon. On Pantora, Fennec Shand knocks him out. The squad starts taking jobs for Cid on Ord Mantell.

19 BBY - Inhibitor chip activates

His chip, weakened by his head injuries, takes over during a mission, and he tries to kill Omega. Once he is subdued, the chip is removed on Kamino.

19 BBY - Fall of Tipoca City

While rescuing Hunter, he survives the Empire's bombardment of Tipoca City and escapes the sinking ruins with his squad.

~18 BBY - Loss of Tech

During a raid on an Imperial vault on Eriadu, Tech falls to his apparent death and Omega is captured by the Empire.

~18 BBY - Search for Omega

He and Hunter hunt for Omega while hiding on Pabu and dodging Imperial pursuit, eventually working alongside a changed Crosshair.

~18 BBY - Raid on Mount Tantiss

He joins the assault on Dr. Hemlock's facility on Wayland, which frees Omega and the imprisoned clone children.

Years later - Life on Pabu

Now living peacefully on Pabu with Hunter and Crosshair, he sees a grown Omega off as she leaves to join the rebellion.

Wrecker's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY

Crosshair, born 32 BBY

Crosshair is the razor-eyed sniper of Clone Force 99, the "Bad Batch," whose mutated eyesight made him one of the deadliest marksmen in the Grand Army of the Republic. After fighting alongside his squad in the late Clone Wars, including the rescue of Echo on Skako Minor and the Battle of Anaxes, he alone among his brothers obeyed Order 66 on Kaller. Tarkin had his inhibitor chip strengthened, and he turned on his squad as they fled Kamino with the young clone Omega. He then led an Imperial Elite Squad against them on Onderon, Bracca and Ryloth, and kept serving the Empire even after his chip was removed. He killed his own squad to try to win his brothers back, but refused to leave with them when Tipoca City was destroyed. His faith in the Empire wore down over the next year. He watched clones like Cody desert, and after the Empire let his comrade Mayday die on Barton IV, he killed the officer responsible. That act sent him to a cell at Mount Tantiss, where Doctor Hemlock held him for months and questioned him about his old squad. He escaped with Omega, fought his way back into his brothers' trust despite a worsening tremor in his shooting hand, and helped destroy Tantiss. He finally settled on Pabu with the family he had once hunted.

~32 BBY - Created on Kamino

Grown on Kamino as CT-9904, a clone of Jango Fett with mutations that gave him exceptional eyesight. He is assigned to the experimental unit Clone Force 99 as its sniper.

Clone Wars - Service with the Bad Batch

Fights in the Clone Wars with Hunter, Tech and Wrecker, looking down on regular "regs." The squad builds a near-perfect mission record and at some point meets Cut Lawquane's family on Saleucami.

19 BBY - Battle of Anaxes and Skako Minor

Deployed under Anakin Skywalker to Anaxes, where he clashes with Rex. He helps rescue Echo from the Techno Union on Skako Minor, then helps break the Separatist hold on Anaxes by boarding Admiral Trench's dreadnought.

19 BBY - Order 66 on Kaller

Alone among his squad, he tries to carry out Order 66, firing on the Padawan Caleb Dume until Dume knocks him out.

19 BBY - Rise of the Empire

Back on Kamino, he watches the Empire's proclamation, meets Omega and is tested by Tarkin. When he tries to massacre civilians on Onderon, his squad stops him.

19 BBY - Turns on Clone Force 99

Tarkin has his inhibitor chip strengthened. He fires on his former squad as they escape Kamino with Omega, wounding Wrecker.

19 BBY - Commander of the Elite Squad

Takes command of a squad of non-clone Imperial recruits. On Onderon he executes surviving insurgents and kills ES-01 for questioning his orders.

19 BBY - Burned on Bracca

Corners the Bad Batch in a wrecked Venator at the Bracca scrapyards, but is badly burned when they blow out an ion engine. The injury likely damages his inhibitor chip, which is later removed.

19 BBY - Occupation of Ryloth

Serves Vice Admiral Rampart on Ryloth. He shoots Orn Free Taa to frame Cham Syndulla, but fails to stop the Bad Batch from rescuing the Syndullas.

19 BBY - Destruction of Tipoca City

Lures his old squad to Kamino using the captured Hunter, then kills his own Elite Squad and reveals that his chip is gone. He saves Omega and AZI as Rampart sinks the city, but refuses to leave with his brothers and waits 32 rotations to be recovered.

~18 BBY - Mission to Desix

Once medically cleared, he deploys with Commander Cody and executes the Separatist holdout leader Tawni Ames. Cody's doubts, and his later desertion, stay with Crosshair.

18 BBY - Mayday's Death on Barton IV

Forms a bond with the neglected Clone Commander Mayday. When Lieutenant Nolan leaves Mayday to die, Crosshair kills Nolan and is taken into custody.

18 BBY - Prisoner of Tantiss

Held at Mount Tantiss on Wayland, where Doctor Royce Hemlock and Emerie Karr question him about the Bad Batch. Around this time a tremor develops in his shooting hand.

18 BBY - Escape with Omega

After Omega is captured and brought to Tantiss, the two escape together. They survive in the wilds and on the run before reuniting with Hunter and Wrecker, who reluctantly let him back into the squad.

18 BBY - Return to Tantiss

Helps free Omega and the captive clone children and bring down Hemlock's operation at Mount Tantiss. He plays a key part in the final battle despite his faltering hand.

After 18 BBY - Life on Pabu

Settles on Pabu with Hunter, Wrecker and Omega. Years later he is there to see a grown Omega leave to join the fight against the Empire.

Crosshair's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY

Boba Fett, born 32 BBY

Boba Fett is known as the most feared bounty hunter of the Imperial age. He later became something rarer: a hunter who went legitimate and ruled a city. He began as "Alpha," the one unaltered clone that Jango Fett asked for as payment for serving as the clone army's template, and Jango raised him as a son. Jango trained him in combat, piloting and the bounty trade, and Boba flew Slave I as a boy. At ten he watched Mace Windu kill his father in the Geonosis arena. His first attempt at revenge, sabotaging the Republic cruiser Endurance with Aurra Sing's crew, ended with him in a Coruscant prison. After his release he led the Krayt's Claw syndicate and learned from hunters such as Cad Bane. He then rebuilt his father's armor and built a reputation that made him the first choice of Jabba the Hutt and Darth Vader. His best-known job was capturing Han Solo in carbonite at Cloud City, a prize fought over in the War of the Bounty Hunters. At Solo's rescue a damaged jetpack dropped him into the sarlacc. He clawed his way out, lived for a time with a Tusken tribe, and took back his armor from Din Djarin. He then helped rescue Grogu from Moff Gideon, killed Bib Fortuna and claimed Jabba's old throne. With Fennec Shand beside him, he fought off the Pyke Syndicate, the Hutt Twins and his old mentor Cad Bane to earn Mos Espa's loyalty as its Daimyo.

32 BBY - Created on Kamino

Grown in Tipoca City as "Alpha," the unaltered clone Jango Fett asked for as part of his payment for being the clone army's template. He has no growth acceleration and no behavioral conditioning. Jango raises him as his son.

~32–23 BBY - Childhood training

Raised by Jango on Kamino, he learns marksmanship, survival skills and how to fly Slave I, sometimes going along on his father's hunts.

23 BBY - First mission on Ord Mantell

Joins Jango and a hired crew to recover a runaway Twi'lek. When the crew turns on them, Boba kills two of the traitors, his first taste of the trade.

22 BBY - Flight from Kamino

After Obi-Wan Kenobi finds the Fetts, Boba fires Slave I's cannons at the Jedi during his duel with Jango, then helps the two of them escape to Geonosis.

22 BBY - Death of Jango Fett

Watches Mace Windu behead his father in the Geonosis arena. He picks up Jango's helmet, flees in Slave I and swears revenge.

~21 BBY - Sabotage of the Endurance

Disguised as a clone cadet, he plants a bomb meant for Windu and wrecks the Republic cruiser Endurance, working with Aurra Sing, Bossk and Castas.

~21 BBY - Capture on Florrum

Upset by Sing's cruelty to their hostages, he is captured by Plo Koon and Ahsoka Tano. On Hondo Ohnaka's advice he reveals where the hostages are, and he is sent to prison on Coruscant.

~20 BBY - Prison riot

Paid by Cad Bane to start a fight with "Rako Hardeen," who is really Kenobi in disguise, as a cover for Bane's escape from the Republic detention center.

~20 BBY - Krayt's Claw

Released from prison, he forms a bounty hunter syndicate based on Tatooine. On a job on Quarzite, Asajj Ventress double-crosses him and leaves him tied up in a cargo chest.

~19 BBY - Raid on Serenno

Hired by Ventress to rescue Quinlan Vos. Count Dooku easily beats Krayt's Claw, and the crew retreats.

~19–11 BBY - Assuming his father's mantle

Rebuilds Jango's armor, takes back Slave I, adopts the EE-3 carbine and becomes known as the best bounty hunter in the galaxy.

~10 BBY - The Corellia job

A contract with Nakano Lash's crew against the Unbroken Clan falls apart. Boba's shot meant for Lash kills T'ongor instead, starting lasting grudges with Beilert Valance and Bossk.

9 BBY - Collecting Caij Vanda

Captures the bounty hunter Caij Vanda on Koboh after a tense standoff with the Jedi Cal Kestis.

1 BBY - Docking Bay 94

Is with Jabba the Hutt when the gangster confronts Han Solo on Tatooine.

3 ABY - Capture of Han Solo

Hired by Darth Vader, he tracks the Millennium Falcon to Cloud City and claims Solo, now frozen in carbonite, to deliver to Jabba.

3 ABY - War of the Bounty Hunters

Loses Solo on Nar Shaddaa when Crimson Dawn steals him. He fights through hunters, syndicates and the Empire to get him back and finally hands him to Jabba.

4 ABY - Fall into the sarlacc

During the Rebels' rescue of Solo above the Great Pit of Carkoon, a blow to his jetpack sends him into the sarlacc. He cuts his way out, but Jawas strip his armor.

~4–9 ABY - Life among the Tuskens

Taken captive by a Tusken tribe, he earns their respect, learns their ways and fights beside them until the Nikto gang working for the Pykes massacres the tribe.

9 ABY - Reclaiming his armor

Confronts Din Djarin on Tython, gets his armor back and helps rescue Grogu from Moff Gideon's cruiser.

9 ABY - Daimyo of Mos Espa

Kills Bib Fortuna and takes Jabba's old throne, with Fennec Shand as his partner.

9 ABY - Battle of Mos Espa

With Djarin, Grogu, Krrsantan and allies from the city, he defeats the Pyke Syndicate. He kills his old mentor Cad Bane and wins the loyalty of Mos Espa's people.

Rex, born 32 BBY

Captain Rex is the best-known clone trooper of the Clone Wars. He led the 501st Legion at Anakin Skywalker's side and was a steady mentor and close friend to Ahsoka Tano. His fighting took him from Christophsis and Teth to Ryloth, Geonosis and Saleucami. On Saleucami he let the deserter Cut Lawquane stay with his family, the first sign that Rex would think for himself. Serving under the treacherous Pong Krell on Umbara shook his blind trust in orders, and the death of his friend Fives, who had uncovered Order 66, led Rex to find the control chip in his own head. When the order came during the Siege of Mandalore, Ahsoka removed his chip and the two escaped the purge together. Rex then worked in secret to free other clones, retired to Seelos with Wolffe and Gregor, and returned to the fight to help the Spectres and the Rebel Alliance. He trained Ezra Bridger, fought on Lothal and at Atollon, and as a rebel commander lived to see the Empire beaten at Endor.

32 BBY - Born on Kamino

Rex is grown as CT-7567, one of the first generation of clones made from Jango Fett's genetic template.

~22 BBY - Command and ARC training

He is singled out for leadership, receives command and ARC training, is made a clone captain and is given Torrent Company of the 501st.

22 BBY - First Battle of Geonosis

Rex fights in the battle that opens the Clone Wars. Afterward the 501st is expanded into a legion under Jedi General Anakin Skywalker.

22 BBY - Battle of Christophsis

Rex and Commander Cody catch the clone traitor Slick. Rex meets Skywalker's new Padawan, Ahsoka Tano, and is told to show her the ropes.

22 BBY - Battle of Teth

He holds the monastery against Asajj Ventress to protect Skywalker and Tano while they rescue Jabba the Hutt's son.

22 BBY - Rishi Moon

With Cody, Rex retakes Rishi Station from droid commandos. He brings the surviving rookies, Fives and Echo, into the 501st.

22 BBY - Bothawui and Skytop Station

Rex helps ambush General Grievous's fleet and destroy the Separatist listening post.

22 BBY - Stranded on Maridun

After crashing with Skywalker, Tano, Aayla Secura and Bly, Rex helps defend the Lurmen village against Lok Durd's defoliator.

22 BBY - Blue Shadow Virus and Ryloth

He is infected during the raid on Nuvo Vindi's lab on Naboo and survives. He then serves with Tano when she breaks the blockade of Ryloth.

~21 BBY - Felucia, Devaron and Malastare

Rex fights on Felucia, against Cad Bane above Devaron, and on Malastare and Coruscant during the Zillo Beast crisis.

~21 BBY - Second Battle of Geonosis

He supports the attack on Poggle the Lesser's droid foundry.

~21 BBY - Battle of Saleucami

Rex is wounded while hunting Grievous. He recovers on the farm of the deserter Cut Lawquane and decides to let him stay out of the war.

~20 BBY - Battle of Umbara

Rex serves under Jedi General Pong Krell, whose reckless orders kill many clones. He finally turns on Krell, who is revealed as a traitor.

~20 BBY - Onderon

He trains Saw Gerrera and the Onderon rebels in their fight against the Separatist-backed king.

19 BBY - Death of Fives

Fives uncovers the inhibitor chips and Order 66 and is killed before anyone will listen. His death leaves Rex doubting the Republic.

19 BBY - Ahsoka leaves the Order

Rex sees Tano framed for the Jedi Temple bombing and then walk away from the Jedi Order.

19 BBY - Siege of Mandalore

Rex, now a commander, leads 501st troops under Tano against Maul. When Order 66 is given, Tano removes his chip. They fake their deaths, bury the fallen clones, and part ways.

~19–18 BBY - Clone underground

Rex secretly helps other clones escape the Empire, working with Clone Force 99, Echo and Gregor.

~5 BBY - Retirement on Seelos

With his chip removed, Rex lives on a converted AT-TE on Seelos with fellow clones Wolffe and Gregor.

~4 BBY - Joins the rebellion

The Spectres find him, and Rex joins the Phoenix Cell, where he is reunited with Ahsoka Tano. He also makes peace with Kanan Jarrus.

~4 BBY - Mentor to Ezra Bridger

Rex trains Ezra Bridger in combat and becomes a regular ally of the Ghost crew.

2 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Rex, Wolffe and Gregor help defend Chopper Base against Grand Admiral Thrawn's forces.

1 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

Rex fights alongside the Spectres in the battle that frees Lothal from the Empire.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Now a commander in the Alliance, Rex fights at Endor, where the Emperor falls and the second Death Star is destroyed.

Rex's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY (10 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (10 years old)
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY (13 years old)
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (27 years old)

Hunter, born 32 BBY

Hunter led Clone Force 99, the genetically modified commando squad the rest of the clone army called the Bad Batch, and his defining choice was to protect his squad and a lost child instead of obeying the Empire. His mutation gave him heightened senses, letting him track movement and electromagnetic signals no ordinary trooper could detect. As a sergeant in the last year of the Clone Wars, he and his men helped Captain Rex and Anakin Skywalker retake Anaxes and rescue the long-lost ARC trooper Echo from the Techno Union on Skako Minor, and Echo then joined the squad. Because of their mutations, most of the Batch were unaffected by the inhibitor chips, and when Order 66 came on Kaller, Hunter let the Padawan Caleb Dume escape. Back on Kamino, he refused an Imperial order to wipe out civilians on Onderon and rescued the young clone Omega, which led the squad to desert. Crosshair, whose chip still worked, stayed loyal to the Empire. Hunter spent the next years on the run with Omega, taking mercenary work from Cid, losing Tech, and finally storming the secret Imperial facility on Mount Tantiss to free her and the other captive clones. Afterward he settled on the island of Pabu as Omega's father figure, and he lived to see her grow up and leave to join the rebellion.

~32 BBY - Created on Kamino

Grown in Tipoca City from Jango Fett's genetic template and designated CT-9901. As part of the experimental unit Clone Force 99 he was given a deliberate mutation that heightened his senses.

22–19 BBY - Leading the Bad Batch

Serves as sergeant of Clone Force 99 alongside Wrecker, Tech and Crosshair. The squad keeps a perfect mission record but has a reputation for unorthodox methods, which includes the mission against Doctor Krail's modified droids on Hylanth.

19 BBY - Battle of Anaxes

Commander Cody recalls the squad from an insurrection on Yalbec Prime. Hunter leads the raid on the Separatist Cyber Center with Captain Rex, and the raid reveals that Echo is alive and being used by the Separatists.

19 BBY - Rescue of Echo on Skako Minor

Hunter joins Rex and Anakin Skywalker, wins over the Poletecs and helps free Echo from Wat Tambor's tower in Purkoll.

19 BBY - Anaxes liberated

The squad infiltrates Admiral Trench's flagship so that Echo can shut down the droid army. Afterward Hunter invites Echo to join the Bad Batch.

19 BBY - Order 66 on Kaller

The squad reinforces Depa Billaba's troops. When the clones turn on the Jedi, Hunter is unaffected, lets Caleb Dume escape and tells Crosshair the Padawan fell into a ravine.

19 BBY - Rise of the Empire

On Kamino, Hunter witnesses the Proclamation of the New Order and meets Omega. After refusing to attack civilians on Onderon, he returns to rescue her. The squad escapes Imperial custody without Crosshair, and the Batch deserts.

19 BBY - Mercenaries for Cid

From their base on Ord Mantell, Hunter leads the squad on jobs for the broker Cid while evading the Empire and Crosshair. That year Tipoca City is destroyed, and Hunter and Crosshair nearly die with it.

18 BBY - Loss of Tech

During a raid on an Imperial train over Eriadu, Tech is killed and Omega is captured and taken to Mount Tantiss.

18 BBY - Refuge on Pabu

Hunter and Wrecker hide on Pabu and search for Omega. He is captured, and the Empire's clone experiments come to light.

18 BBY - Assault on Mount Tantiss

Reunited with Crosshair and Omega, Hunter helps free the captive clones and children and brings down the facility.

After 18 BBY - Life on Pabu

Hunter settles on the island with Wrecker, Crosshair and Omega, where he raises her as a father figure and his hair turns grey.

Before 4 ABY - Omega departs

An older Hunter sees the grown Omega off when she leaves Pabu to join the rebellion.

Hunter's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY
  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY

Han Solo, born 32 BBY

Han Solo is the galaxy's most famous smuggler-turned-hero, the cocky pilot of the Millennium Falcon who went from street thief to Rebel general. Raised as a scrumrat for the White Worms gang on Corellia, he escaped by enlisting in the Imperial Navy, where a recruiting officer gave him the surname Solo. He washed out of the Carida flight academy for insubordination and was sent to the mud of Mimban. There he met Chewbacca and deserted with Tobias Beckett's crew. The coaxium heist that followed cost him his mentor and his first love, Qi'ra, but won him the Falcon from Lando Calrissian and the record Kessel Run in twelve parsecs. Years of smuggling for Jabba the Hutt left him deep in debt, and then a charter job for Luke Skywalker and Obi-Wan Kenobi pulled him into the Rebellion. He rescued Princess Leia, saved Luke at the Battle of Yavin, and was frozen in carbonite at Cloud City before his friends freed him. He then led the strike team that destroyed the second Death Star's shield generator on Endor. He married Leia and had a son, Ben, but when Ben fell to the dark side as Kylo Ren, Han drifted back to smuggling. Rey and Finn brought him back into the fight, and during the Resistance assault on Starkiller Base he tried to bring his son home and was killed by him. A year later, Han's memory helped Ben turn back to the light.

32 BBY - Birth on Corellia

Han is born in a poor district of Corellia. His father, Ovan, is a shipyard worker at Corellian Engineering Corporation who tells him he is meant to fly ships, not build them, and later abandons him.

~24 BBY - Scrumrat of the White Worms

Han joins Lady Proxima's White Worms gang as a street thief and meets fellow scrumrat Qi'ra, who later becomes his first love.

13 BBY - Escape from Corellia

Han steals a vial of coaxium, defies Proxima, and flees with Qi'ra. She is recaptured at the spaceport, and Han enlists with the Empire to get offworld. The recruiting officer gives him the surname Solo.

13 BBY - Cadet at Carida

As Cadet 124-329, Han shows real talent as a pilot but keeps disobeying orders. He crashes a stolen TIE fighter and helps his fellow cadets rescue Beilert Valance on Qhulosk.

~10 BBY - Expelled and Sent to Mimban

A court-martial expels Han from the flight academy. He is sent to the 224th Imperial Armored Division as a mudtrooper corporal.

10 BBY - Meeting Chewbacca

On Mimban, Han is thrown into a pit with Chewbacca, a Wookiee held prisoner by the Empire. The two escape together and desert with Tobias Beckett's gang.

10 BBY - The Vandor Heist

The raid on a coaxium conveyex fails. Rio Durant and Val are killed, and the crew now owes a debt to Dryden Vos of Crimson Dawn.

10 BBY - The Kessel Run

Han reunites with Qi'ra and teams up with Lando Calrissian. He pilots the Millennium Falcon through the Kessel Run in twelve parsecs with stolen unrefined coaxium.

10 BBY - Showdown on Savareen

After Beckett betrays the crew, Han shoots him. Qi'ra kills Vos, takes his place in Crimson Dawn, and leaves Han behind. Han then wins the Falcon from Lando in a game of sabacc.

~10–1 BBY - Smuggler for Jabba

With Chewbacca as his co-pilot, Han smuggles for Jabba the Hutt. When he dumps a spice shipment to avoid Imperials, he ends up with a bounty on his head.

1 BBY - Charter to Alderaan

At Mos Eisley, Han agrees to fly Luke Skywalker, Obi-Wan Kenobi and the droids to Alderaan. He finds the planet destroyed and the Falcon captured by the Death Star.

1 BBY - Rescue of Princess Leia

Han helps free Leia Organa from the Death Star and delivers the stolen plans to the Rebel base on Yavin 4.

0 BBY - Battle of Yavin

Han comes back at the last moment and drives Darth Vader off Luke's tail, which lets Luke destroy the Death Star.

0–3 ABY - Rebel Operative

Han flies missions for the Rebel Alliance while still trying to settle his debt to Jabba.

3 ABY - Battle of Hoth and Flight to Bespin

Han escapes Echo Base with Leia and falls in love with her. At Cloud City, Lando's hand is forced and Darth Vader captures the group.

3 ABY - Frozen in Carbonite

Vader tests the carbon-freezing chamber on Han. Boba Fett then takes him to Jabba on Tatooine.

4 ABY - Rescue from Jabba's Palace

Leia frees Han from the carbonite. His friends defeat Jabba at the Great Pit of Carkoon.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Now a general, Han leads the Pathfinders with Ewok help to destroy the shield generator protecting the second Death Star.

4 ABY - Marriage to Leia

Han and Leia marry on Endor soon after the battle. Han keeps leading operations against the Empire's remnants.

5 ABY - Birth of Ben Solo

Leia gives birth to their son, Ben, around the time of the Battle of Jakku.

~5–28 ABY - Racer and Pilot

Han spends the New Republic years in civilian life, flying and racing. Ben's growing darkness puts a strain on the family.

28 ABY - Loss of His Son

Ben falls to the dark side under Snoke and destroys Luke's Jedi temple. Han and Leia separate, and Han goes back to smuggling. The Falcon is later stolen from him.

34 ABY - Reclaiming the Falcon

Han and Chewbacca find the Falcon with Rey and Finn and help take BB-8 to the Resistance.

34 ABY - Death on Starkiller Base

During the Resistance attack, Han confronts his son, Kylo Ren, and begs him to come home. Kylo stabs him with his lightsaber, and Han falls into the weapon's core.

35 ABY - Memory on Kef Bir

A vision of Han appears to Ben Solo and helps him give up the name Kylo Ren and return to the light.

Han Solo's Timeline

  • Solo: A Star Wars Story, 10 BBY (22 years old)
  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY (31 years old)
  • Episode V: The Empire Strikes Back, 3 ABY (35 years old)
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY (36 years old)
  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (66 years old)

Omega, born 32 BBY

Omega is best known as the youngest and most determined member of Clone Force 99, the renegade squad nicknamed the Bad Batch. She was one of only two unaltered copies of Jango Fett's genetic template, the other being Boba Fett, and she grew up quietly in Tipoca City as a medical assistant to the Kaminoan scientist Nala Se. When the Clone Wars ended and the Empire rose in 19 BBY, she befriended the Bad Batch and escaped Kamino with them, leaving behind the only home she had known. As she travelled with Hunter, Wrecker, Tech and Echo, she learned to fight with a Zygerrian energy bow, took on jobs for the information broker Cid, and was chased by bounty hunters including Fennec Shand and Cad Bane. She saw Tipoca City destroyed, grieved when Tech was killed on Eriadu, and was later taken to Mount Tantiss because her unusually high M-count made her vital to Doctor Royce Hemlock's cloning research. She escaped with Crosshair and brought him back into the family. After she gave herself up to protect the people of Pabu, she helped free the Force-sensitive children held at Tantiss before her brothers rescued her and Hemlock was killed. She then spent peaceful years on Pabu before leaving as a young adult to fly for the Rebel Alliance.

Date unknown - Created on Kamino

Nala Se creates Omega in her private laboratory in Tipoca City as an unmodified female copy of Jango Fett's template. Only Omega and Boba Fett are pure copies of that template.

Clone Wars era - Nala Se's assistant

Omega works as Nala Se's medical assistant. She is present when Clone Force 99 is created, then follows the squad's wartime exploits from Kamino without ever meeting them.

19 BBY - Meets the Bad Batch

After Order 66 and the proclamation of the Empire, Omega introduces herself to the Bad Batch and stands up for them in the mess hall. She also warns Hunter that Tarkin does not trust clones.

19 BBY - Escape from Kamino

Omega is imprisoned for snooping in the squad's barracks. She helps the Bad Batch break out, fires a blaster for the first time against Crosshair, who is now loyal to the Empire, and flees Kamino aboard the Marauder.

19 BBY - First steps offworld on Saleucami

Omega sees dirt and sunlight for the first time at Cut Lawquane's farm. She delivers forged chain codes so the family can escape, but refuses to leave with them and returns to the squad instead.

19 BBY - Ordo Moon

Omega crawls into a moon dragon's den to recover the Marauder's stolen power capacitor. Wrecker gives her a room of her own, and Hunter tells her she is part of the squad.

19 BBY - Hunted by Fennec Shand

During a supply run on Pantora, bounty hunter Fennec Shand, hired by Nala Se, tries to take Omega. The squad rescues her.

19 BBY - Working for Cid

On Ord Mantell, Omega frees her captured squad by releasing the rancor Muchi and picks up a Zygerrian energy bow. On Corellia, the squad teams up with Trace and Rafa Martez to steal a tactical droid head.

19 BBY - Bracca and capture by Cad Bane

Omega goes with Rex and the squad to Bracca, where the clones' inhibitor chips are removed. Cad Bane then kidnaps her, but she escapes him at the Bora Vio cloning facility and the Bad Batch rescues her.

19 BBY - Fall of Tipoca City

Omega helps rescue Hunter on Kamino as Imperial Star Destroyers level Tipoca City. She leads the group to safety through the tube system and nearly drowns trying to save AZI-3, but Crosshair pulls her out. Crosshair then chooses to stay with the Empire.

19–18 BBY - Adventures across the galaxy

Omega goes on jobs and missions with the squad on Serenno, Serolonis, Skara Nal, Kashyyyk and other worlds.

18 BBY - Coruscant and Echo's departure

The squad helps Senator Riyo Chuchi expose the Empire's destruction of Tipoca City. Echo then leaves to join Rex's Clone Underground, and Omega says a tearful goodbye.

18 BBY - Pabu

After breaking with Cid, the squad works with the pirate Phee Genoa and finds the island haven of Pabu, where Hunter hopes Omega can have a peaceful life.

18 BBY - Loss of Tech

The squad's attempt on Eriadu to find Crosshair through Doctor Hemlock goes wrong, and Tech dies making sure the others escape.

18 BBY - Captured and taken to Tantiss

Cid betrays the Bad Batch, and Omega is taken to Mount Tantiss. There she is made to work alongside Emerie Karr and Nala Se, and her blood tests reveal a high M-count that Project Necromancer needs.

18 BBY - Escape with Crosshair

With help from Nala Se, Omega frees the imprisoned Crosshair. The two escape Tantiss together and rejoin the Bad Batch.

18 BBY - Hiding on Pabu

Omega learns that CX troopers are hunting her because of her M-count and lies low on Pabu. Asajj Ventress tests whether she can use the Force, but the results are inconclusive.

18 BBY - Surrender to protect Pabu

When Imperial forces raid Pabu, Omega gives herself up to CX-2. She is taken back to Tantiss and meets the Force-sensitive children held in the vault.

18 BBY - Liberation of Tantiss

Omega leads the children and imprisoned clones in a breakout while her brothers attack the base. Hemlock is killed as Hunter and Crosshair rescue her.

After 18 BBY - Life on Pabu

Omega and the surviving members of Clone Force 99 settle on Pabu and live there for many years.

Date unknown - Joins the Rebel Alliance

As a young adult, Omega leaves Pabu to become a pilot for the Alliance to Restore the Republic.

Omega's Timeline

  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY

Rotta the Hutt, born 32 BBY

Jabba the Hutt's only son, Rotta is best remembered as the sickly Huttlet whose kidnapping early in the Clone Wars became a Separatist plot to turn the Hutts against the Republic. Count Dooku arranged the abduction with Rotta's great-uncle Ziro, and Asajj Ventress carried it out, holding the infant on Teth so the Jedi could be framed for his death. Anakin Skywalker and his new Padawan Ahsoka Tano fought their way into the B'omarr Monastery to rescue him. Ahsoka, who nicknamed him "Stinky," nursed him through a dangerous fever and carried him across the Tatooine desert while Anakin led Dooku away. Returning Rotta safely won Jabba's alliance and secured Republic access to Hutt hyperspace routes. Rotta stayed at his father's palace for the rest of the war. Decades later, after Jabba's death left him heir to the family's criminal empire, he turned his back on that legacy. He grew into a huge, muscular gladiator and became a crowd favorite in the fighting pits of Shakari while working off a debt to the Imperial warlord Janu Coin. When his scheming relatives, the Twins, sent Din Djarin and Grogu to bring him in, Rotta first fought the Mandalorian. Once Coin tried to have him killed, the two became allies and escaped the arena together.

~32 BBY - Birth

Rotta is born to Jabba the Hutt of the Desilijic kajidic. Jabba tells the young bounty hunter Greedo that he has recently become a father.

~32 BBY - A Wookiee Toymaker

Hoping to impress Jabba, Greedo brings him a captured Wookiee named Brewhada. Jabba makes Brewhada the infant Rotta's personal toymaker, and he crafts toys for the Huttlet, including a plush Wookiee.

22 BBY - Kidnapped by Ventress

During the Clone Wars, Asajj Ventress snatches Rotta from Jabba's sail barge, the Khetanna, with a Trident-class assault ship. The plot is Count Dooku's, aided by Rotta's great-uncle Ziro, and Rotta is locked in a cell at the B'omarr Monastery on Teth.

22 BBY - Rescue on Teth

After the Battle of Christophsis, Anakin Skywalker, Ahsoka Tano and Torrent Company storm the monastery and find the sick Huttlet. The droid 4-A7 secretly records the Jedi so the rescue can be passed off as a kidnapping.

22 BBY - Escape from the Monastery

Trapped by Ventress and Separatist droids, Rotta points the Jedi to a distant landing platform. From there they escape Teth aboard the freighter Twilight.

22 BBY - Fever Broken

With the Republic cruiser's hangar destroyed, the Jedi head straight for Tatooine. On the way, Ahsoka gives Rotta a medical booster that breaks his life-threatening fever.

22 BBY - Returned to Jabba

After the Twilight crash-lands on Tatooine, Anakin draws Dooku away with a decoy backpack while Ahsoka fights MagnaGuards and delivers Rotta to his father. Padmé Amidala exposes Ziro's role, and Jabba allies with the Republic.

21 BBY - Papanoida at the Palace

Rotta is at Jabba's palace when Baron Papanoida and his son Ion come looking for Greedo, who has kidnapped the Baron's daughters. Rotta smashes a slug-beetle and eats it.

4 ABY - Heir to the Empire

Jabba dies during the rescue of Han Solo, leaving Rotta sole heir to his criminal empire. This angers his relatives, the Hutt crime lords known as the Twins, and Rotta rejects the inheritance to become his own man.

New Republic Era - Champion of Shakari

Now fully grown and heavily muscled, Rotta owes a debt to the Imperial warlord Janu Coin and must fight in Coin's gladiatorial pits on Shakari. Crowds first jeer Jabba's son, but they come to cheer him as he keeps winning.

New Republic Era - Hunted by the Mandalorian

The Twins make a deal with the New Republic to have Din Djarin and Grogu find Rotta, secretly planning to kill him. Rotta turns the Mandalorian away, insisting he has no interest in leaving Shakari or taking over the family business.

New Republic Era - Freedom in the Arena

Coin sets Rotta against Djarin, who lets the Hutt win. Coin then unleashes dejarik beasts to kill Rotta, and the two fight side by side. Rotta short-circuits the arena's electric fence, the beasts pour into the stands, and the chaos lets them escape.

Rotta the Hutt's Timeline

  • The Clone Wars (TV series), 22 BBY (10 years old)
  • The Clone Wars (movie), 22 BBY (10 years old)
  • The Mandalorian and Grogu, 10 ABY (42 years old)

Reva, born 31 BBY

Reva Sevander, known as the Third Sister, survived the massacre at the Jedi Temple and turned Darth Vader's own Inquisitorius into her means of revenge. She trained as a Jedi initiate under Yoda. During Order 66 she watched Anakin Skywalker cut down her clanmates, whom she thought had come to be saved by him. He stabbed her, and she lived by playing dead among their bodies. Years later she joined the Inquisitors, and she was ruthless and reckless enough to anger both the Grand Inquisitor and the Fifth Brother. Her plan was to deliver Obi-Wan Kenobi to Vader and strike while the Sith Lord was distracted. In 9 BBY she lured Kenobi out of hiding by arranging the kidnapping of young Leia Organa. She stabbed the Grand Inquisitor on Daiyu, captured Leia again on Mapuzo, and tracked the Hidden Path to Jabiim, which earned her the title of Grand Inquisitor. Her attempt on Vader's life there failed badly. He ran her through, stripped her of rank and left her for dead. She then learned about Luke Skywalker from a message meant for Kenobi and went to Tatooine to kill the boy. At the last moment she saw that she was becoming the man she hated. She returned Luke to his family, laid down her lightsaber on Kenobi's advice and walked away into an unknown future.

~31 BBY - Birth

Reva Sevander is born. She is Force-sensitive and is taken into the Jedi Order as a child.

~22 BBY - Jedi Initiate

Reva trains as an initiate in the Jedi Temple on Coruscant, taught by Yoda, and comes to see her clan of younglings as her family.

19 BBY - Survives Order 66

During Operation: Knightfall, Master Minas Velti leads Reva's clan away from the clone troopers until she is killed. Anakin Skywalker, now Darth Vader, slaughters the younglings and stabs Reva. She survives by hiding among the bodies and later needs cybernetic repairs.

~19 BBY - Turns to the Dark Side

Reva escapes the Temple, consumed by survivor's guilt and a thirst for revenge. She joins the Inquisitorius as the Third Sister so she can get close to Vader.

~19–9 BBY - Hunting Jedi

As an Inquisitor she hunts Jedi and Force-sensitives on worlds including Pantora, Valo, Cerea, Boz Pity and Alsakan, while fixating on finding Obi-Wan Kenobi.

9 BBY - Hunt for Nari on Tatooine

Reva goes to Tatooine with the Grand Inquisitor and the Fifth Brother. In Anchorhead she maims a local and threatens Owen Lars and his family to find Kenobi, and her fellow Inquisitors rein her in.

9 BBY - Kidnapping of Leia Organa

Defying her superiors, she hires bounty hunters to abduct Princess Leia Organa and bring her to Daiyu as bait for Kenobi.

9 BBY - Betrayal on Daiyu

Reva corners Kenobi and tells him Anakin is alive. When the Grand Inquisitor tries to take over, she stabs him and leaves him for dead, and Kenobi escapes with Leia. Vader promises her the Grand Inquisitor's post if she catches Kenobi.

9 BBY - Battle on Mapuzo

She finds a Hidden Path safehouse and its escape tunnel, kills the waiting pilot and recaptures Leia.

9 BBY - Fortress Inquisitorius

Kenobi and Tala Durith rescue Leia from the fortress on Nur. Vader chokes Reva for her failure, but spares her when she reveals she planted a tracker in Leia's droid, L0-LA59.

9 BBY - Named Grand Inquisitor

The tracker leads the Empire to the Hidden Path's base on Jabiim, and Vader promotes Reva to Grand Inquisitor aboard the Devastator.

9 BBY - Attack on Jabiim

Reva leads the siege. Kenobi realizes she survived the Temple massacre and wants revenge. When she tries to kill Vader, he defeats her, runs her through and strips her of rank, and the surviving Grand Inquisitor is at his side. Lying wounded, she plays a message from Bail Organa that reveals Luke Skywalker's existence.

9 BBY - Hunt for Luke Skywalker

Reva attacks the Lars homestead on Tatooine and chases Luke into Beggar's Canyon. On the point of killing him, she sees herself becoming Vader and spares him.

9 BBY - A New Path

She carries Luke back to Owen and Beru. Kenobi tells her she has not failed and is free to choose her own path, and she lays down her lightsaber and leaves to start a new life.

1 BBY - Fate Unknown

The Inquisitorius is gone by the time of the Battle of Scarif. What became of Reva is never revealed.

Reva's Timeline

  • Obi-Wan Kenobi, 9 BBY

Qi'ra, born 31 BBY

Qi'ra is best known as Han Solo's first love, who clawed her way from the gutters of Coronet City to the head of the Crimson Dawn syndicate. As a scrumrat of Lady Proxima's White Worms she grew close to Han and rose to Head Girl, but their attempt to escape Corellia together failed at the spaceport gate. Han got through and she was dragged back. Proxima sold her to the slaver Sarkin Enneb, and she ended up with Dryden Vos. He trained her in Teräs Käsi and made her his top lieutenant once she had killed Enneb. In 10 BBY she met Han again and joined the Kessel coaxium heist, then killed Vos to protect Han. Instead of leaving with him, she took Vos's place as the public face of Crimson Dawn under its true master, Maul. Maul taught her that Palpatine and Vader were Sith and that the Empire was their instrument, and that knowledge shaped the rest of her life. After Maul's death she rebuilt the syndicate in secret. Around 3 ABY she stole the carbon-frozen Han from Boba Fett and auctioned him off to draw the galaxy's powers into conflict. She dueled Darth Vader, supplied the Rebel Alliance, and started a Syndicate War. Her campaign against the Sith ended when the Knights of Ren betrayed her at the Amaxine station trap and Crimson Dawn fell apart. Qi'ra left what remained of its resources to the young Cadeliah and disappeared, watching the Empire's end from hiding.

31 BBY - Birth on Corellia

Qi'ra is born on Corellia and grows up in the Bottoms of Coronet City.

~20s BBY - Taken in by the White Worms

She is caught trying to pickpocket Moloch, brought before Lady Proxima, and made a scrumrat of the White Worms. There she meets Han, and they go from rivals to friends and then to lovers.

~13 BBY - Head Girl

At about eighteen, Proxima names her Head Girl of the White Worms.

~13 BBY - Failed escape from Corellia

Han steals a vial of coaxium and the two flee Proxima together. At the Coronet Spaceport gate the White Worms recapture Qi'ra while Han gets through, promising to come back for her.

~13 BBY - Sold into slavery

Proxima sells her to the slaver Sarkin Enneb, who later sells her on to Dryden Vos of Crimson Dawn.

~12 BBY - Joins Crimson Dawn

After a year of failed escape attempts, Vos offers her a place in Crimson Dawn in exchange for lifelong loyalty. She kills Enneb, is branded with the syndicate's mark, and is trained by Vos in Teräs Käsi.

~12–10 BBY - Vos's lieutenant

She becomes Vos's enforcer and trusted lieutenant. On one assignment on Ord Mantell she turns IG-88 and Hondo Ohnaka against each other and captures them both.

10 BBY - Reunion with Han Solo

On the yacht First Light she meets Han again, now working with Tobias Beckett. Vos sends her with the crew on a plan to steal unrefined coaxium from Kessel.

10 BBY - The Kessel Run

She brings Lando Calrissian and the Millennium Falcon into the job and rekindles her romance with Han. On Kessel she poses as a slaver to get the crew inside the Pyke mines.

10 BBY - Kills Dryden Vos

On Savareen, Vos uncovers Han's double-cross and Qi'ra kills Vos to save Han. She then contacts Maul, Crimson Dawn's true leader, and goes to Dathomir instead of leaving with Han.

~10 BBY onward - Maul's pupil

She becomes the public face of Crimson Dawn. Maul teaches her the truth about the Sith, Palpatine and Vader, and trains her to fight them.

~2 BBY - Crimson Dawn in decline

After Maul dies on Tatooine, most of the galaxy believes Crimson Dawn is destroyed. Qi'ra quietly takes control of what remains.

~1 BBY–3 ABY - Preparing a war on the Sith

She builds the Dawn Fleet and recruits the Archivist Madelin Sun, Ochi of Bestoon, Deathstick and the Knights of Ren. She also sends Sun to search for Yoda.

3 ABY - Claims the frozen Han Solo

Her agents take the carbon-frozen Han from Boba Fett on Nar Shaddaa. She then announces Crimson Dawn's return to the galaxy's crime lords.

3 ABY - Auction on the Vermillion

At Jekara she auctions Han to the syndicates. When Darth Vader arrives to claim him, she duels Vader, and the auction ends in open conflict between the Empire and the Hutts.

3 ABY - Meets Leia Organa

She stops the Millennium Falcon, tells Leia that Han is alive at Jabba's Palace, and offers the Rebellion her help.

3–4 ABY - Crimson Reign

She starts a Syndicate War among the crime families. She reveals to her followers that her goal is to kill Sidious and Vader, and she arranges the assassination of the Emperor's Royal Guard.

3–4 ABY - Aids the Rebel Alliance

She gives Leia the location of the Rebels' last surviving fleet division and hands over Ellian Zahra's hunter fleet. She also offers to manage the Alliance's supply chain.

3–4 ABY - Takes Cadeliah

Deathstick brings her the child Cadeliah, heir to the Mourner's Wail Syndicate and the Unbroken Clan, and Qi'ra takes her in.

4 ABY - Hidden Empire and the fall of Crimson Dawn

She lures Palpatine and Vader into a trap built around the Fermata Cage at the Amaxine Space Station. The Knights of Ren betray her and free the Sith Lords, and Crimson Dawn collapses.

4 ABY - Into hiding

She leaves her remaining resources to Cadeliah, goes into hiding, and watches the Empire fall from obscurity.

Qi'ra's Timeline

  • Solo: A Star Wars Story, 10 BBY (21 years old)

IG-11, born 30 BBY

IG-11 is the killer-turned-caretaker of The Mandalorian. He was a Bounty Hunters' Guild assassin droid with a built-in self-destruct charge, and in 9 ABY he teamed up with Din Djarin to storm a Nikto encampment on Arvala-7. When they found their target, the infant Grogu, IG-11 moved to carry out his order to terminate him, and Djarin shot him down. The Ugnaught farmer Kuiil salvaged the wreck and patiently retrained it into a gentle nurse droid whose new purpose was to nurse and protect. After Kuiil was killed on Nevarro, IG-11 rescued Grogu from Imperial scout troopers and rode into Nevarro City to break his companions out of a siege. He also treated Djarin's wounds, becoming the first to see the Mandalorian's face without breaking his creed. He then walked into a lava river and self-destructed to wipe out the stormtrooper platoon waiting in ambush, and Nevarro honored him with a statue. Djarin's first attempt to revive him failed when the droid's old assassin programming came back. The Anzellan droidsmiths turned his remains into IG-12, a mech that Grogu piloted in the battle to reclaim Mandalore. Afterward Djarin traded work for the New Republic for a replacement IG head, and IG-11 was rebuilt once more and installed as Nevarro's new marshal.

Before or during the Galactic Civil War - Built by Holowan Mechanicals

IG-11 is built as an IG-series assassin droid in a Holowan Mechanicals factory, with a self-destruct mechanism to protect the design from capture.

Before 9 ABY - Joins the Bounty Hunters' Guild

He becomes a Guild bounty hunter and assassin, programmed to follow Guild protocols.

9 ABY - Hunt for the Asset on Arvala-7

IG-11 takes a contract to terminate a Force-sensitive infant, attacks a Nikto encampment and reluctantly teams up with Din Djarin to take the compound.

9 ABY - Shot Down by Din Djarin

When IG-11 raises his blaster to kill Grogu, Djarin shoots him in the head and takes the child.

9 ABY - Rebuilt by Kuiil

Kuiil salvages the droid and slowly retrains him. With his memory wiped, IG-11 learns to tend blurrgs, cook and serve tea, and takes on a caring new personality.

9 ABY - Journey to Nevarro

Djarin reluctantly lets IG-11 come along on the Razor Crest for the mission to Nevarro. The droid stays aboard the ship to guard it.

9 ABY - Rescue of Grogu

After Kuiil is killed, IG-11 overpowers the scout troopers who took Grogu, steals a speeder bike and fights his way through Nevarro City to the besieged cantina.

9 ABY - Saving the Mandalorian

He cuts an escape route into the sewers, refuses Djarin's request for a warrior's death, and treats his head wound with bacta. As a droid, he can remove Djarin's helmet without breaking his creed.

9 ABY - Sacrifice at the Lava River

Facing a stormtrooper ambush at the end of the lava river, IG-11 says goodbye to Grogu, wades through the lava and self-destructs, wiping out the platoon.

9 ABY - Memorial Statue

The people of Nevarro City build a statue in the market square to honor him, partly from his recovered parts.

9 ABY - Failed Revival

Djarin takes the remains from the statue and powers them up, but IG-11 reverts to his assassin programming and goes after Grogu. CU-28 destroys him again with a bust of Greef Karga.

9 ABY - Anzellan Workshop

The Anzellan droidsmiths cannot restore him without a new IG memory circuit, and none can be found. Djarin takes R5-D4 instead.

9 ABY - Repurposed as IG-12

The Anzellans strip the body to its basic motor functions and turn it into IG-12, a mech Grogu pilots. IG-12 is destroyed during the battle to reclaim Mandalore.

9 ABY - Marshal of Nevarro

Using an IG head that Djarin gets from Carson Teva in return for working with the New Republic, IG-11 is rebuilt from the IG-12 remains and introduced to cheering crowds as Nevarro's new marshal.

IG-11's Timeline

  • The Mandalorian, 9 ABY

Mando, born 30 BBY

Known across the Outer Rim simply as "the Mandalorian," Din Djarin was a feared bounty hunter who threw away his livelihood to save the child he had been hired to capture. Orphaned as a boy when Separatist droids destroyed his village during the Clone Wars, he was carried off by a Death Watch warrior and raised as a foundling on Concordia. He took the creed of the Children of the Watch and swore never to remove his helmet in front of another living being. In the years after the Empire fell he became one of the Bounty Hunters' Guild's best hunters, working out of Nevarro in his gunship, the Razor Crest. In 9 ABY an Imperial remnant hired him to retrieve a mysterious asset. It turned out to be Grogu, a Force-sensitive infant of Yoda's species, and Djarin rescued him from his Imperial clients instead of handing him over. That turned the Guild and Moff Gideon against him. With allies like Greef Karga, Cara Dune, Kuiil and IG-11 he fought his way free. The Armorer then named him and Grogu a clan of two and charged him with finding the child's own kind. That search brought him to Bo-Katan Kryze, Ahsoka Tano and Boba Fett. It ended with Djarin beating Gideon, taking the Darksaber, and taking off his helmet to say goodbye as Luke Skywalker took Grogu away. The Armorer cast him out as an apostate. He fought beside Boba Fett at Mos Espa and got Grogu back. He then bathed in the Living Waters beneath Mandalore to redeem himself, and helped Bo-Katan unite the Mandalorians and retake their homeworld, where Gideon was killed. After that he formally adopted Grogu as his son and apprentice and settled on Nevarro, taking contracts from the New Republic to hunt down Imperial warlords. Those jobs later pitted him against the warlord Barro, the Hutt Twins and the Imperial commander Janu Coin.

~30 BBY - Birth on Aq Vetina

Din Djarin is born on Aq Vetina, late in the life of the Galactic Republic. Canon only says he was born before 19 BBY.

~20 BBY - Orphaned in the Clone Wars

Separatist battle droids attack his village. His parents hide him in a hatch and are then killed. A super battle droid finds him, but a Death Watch warrior destroys it and flies the boy to safety.

~20 BBY - Raised as a Foundling

The Mandalorians take him in as a foundling. He grows up on Concordia, trains in the Fighting Corps and becomes part of Death Watch.

Before 9 ABY - Joining the Tribe

After the Great Purge of Mandalore he joins the Tribe, a Children of the Watch covert hidden on Nevarro. He receives his armor and swears never to show his face to anyone.

Before 9 ABY - Mercenary Work with Ranzar Malk

He works alongside Ranzar Malk and the Twi'lek siblings Xi'an and Qin, and has a relationship with Xi'an. The crew falls apart after Qin is captured.

Before 9 ABY - Guild Bounty Hunter

Flying the Razor Crest, he becomes one of the Bounty Hunters' Guild's top hunters, taking pucks from Greef Karga on Nevarro.

9 ABY - The Mythrol Bounty

He tracks down a Mythrol fugitive on the ice world Pagodon, freezes him in carbonite and collects the payment on Nevarro.

9 ABY - The Client's Commission

An Imperial remnant officer known as the Client hires him to bring in an unnamed asset, paying a beskar ingot up front. The Armorer forges it into a pauldron.

9 ABY - Finding the Child on Arvala-7

With help from the Ugnaught Kuiil and the droid IG-11, he storms a Nikto camp and finds the asset: an infant of Yoda's species. He shoots IG-11 to keep it from killing the child.

9 ABY - The Mudhorn

To buy back his ship's parts from the Jawas, he fights a mudhorn for its egg. The child saves him by lifting the beast with the Force.

9 ABY - Rescuing the Child

He hands the child over for a camtono of beskar and a full armor set, then turns around, raids the Imperial compound and takes the child back. The Tribe comes out of hiding to cover his escape from the Guild.

9 ABY - Sorgan

While lying low on Sorgan, he and Cara Dune train villagers who then destroy a raider gang's AT-ST.

9 ABY - The Prison Ship Job

He joins Ranzar Malk's crew to free Qin from a New Republic prison ship. When the crew betrays him, he leaves them locked in cells.

9 ABY - Battle of Nevarro

Karga lays a trap, and he fights Moff Gideon's forces alongside Karga, Dune, Kuiil and a reprogrammed IG-11. Kuiil and IG-11 are killed. The Armorer names him and the child a clan of two, Clan Mudhorn, and tells him to find the child's own kind.

9 ABY - Tatooine and the Krayt Dragon

He meets Marshal Cobb Vanth in Mos Pelgo, and together with the Tusken Raiders they kill a krayt dragon. Vanth gives him Boba Fett's armor in return.

9 ABY - Meeting Bo-Katan Kryze

While escorting the Frog Lady to Trask, he meets Bo-Katan Kryze and learns the Children of the Watch are a fringe sect. He helps her raid an Imperial freighter, and she tells him to look for Ahsoka Tano on Corvus.

9 ABY - Ahsoka Tano on Corvus

He helps Ahsoka free Calodan from Magistrate Morgan Elsbeth. Ahsoka learns the child's name is Grogu, but she refuses to train him.

9 ABY - Grogu Taken on Tython

At the seeing stone on Tython he meets Boba Fett and Fennec Shand. Dark troopers kidnap Grogu, and Gideon's forces destroy the Razor Crest.

9 ABY - Morak

With Migs Mayfeld, he infiltrates an Imperial refinery to get Gideon's coordinates, taking off his helmet to pass a facial scan.

9 ABY - Rescue of Grogu

He boards Gideon's light cruiser with Bo-Katan, Koska Reeves, Dune and Fennec. He defeats Gideon and wins the Darksaber. Luke Skywalker arrives, and Djarin takes off his helmet to say goodbye before Grogu leaves with him.

9 ABY - Declared an Apostate

Back with the surviving covert, he admits he removed his helmet, and the Armorer casts him out. He replaces the Razor Crest with a modified N-1 starfighter.

9 ABY - Battle of Mos Espa

He fights for Boba Fett against the Pyke Syndicate on Tatooine. Grogu, choosing him over Jedi training, joins him there.

9 ABY - Bathing in the Living Waters

He goes into the mines of Mandalore and bathes in the Living Waters, which restores his standing with the Armorer. Bo-Katan saves him, and she gains the Darksaber when she defeats the creature that had disarmed him.

9 ABY - Plazir-15 and the Pirate Attack

He fights alongside the Armorer's covert to repel Gorian Shard's pirates from Nevarro and serves the Duchess of Plazir-15.

9 ABY - Liberation of Mandalore

With Bo-Katan and the reunited Mandalorians, he retakes Mandalore from the Imperial Remnant, and Moff Gideon is killed.

9 ABY - Din Grogu

He formally adopts Grogu as his son and apprentice, and they settle in a cabin on Nevarro. He takes work as an independent contractor for the New Republic.

After 9 ABY - Hunting Barro

Years later, working with Garazeb Orrelios, he hunts the Imperial warlord Barro on Phostera and kills him. The New Republic rewards him with a new Razor Crest.

After 9 ABY - Mission to Shakari

Colonel Ward sends him after Rotta the Hutt, who has become a gladiator on Shakari. There he discovers that crime lord Janu is really Imperial Commander Coin and captures him with Zeb's help.

After 9 ABY - Battle of Nal Hutta

Embo captures him and hands him to the Hutt Twins. Grogu and Minch free him, and he leads the assault on the Hutt palace to rescue Rotta while New Republic forces attack from orbit. Afterward he and Grogu fly off again in the new Razor Crest.

Mando's Timeline
