Chopper, born 30 BBY

Chopper is the rebellion's most ill-tempered astromech. He is a battered, patched-together droid who has run for decades past his expiration date and is fiercely loyal to Hera Syndulla. He began as a navigation droid in the Republic Navy during the Clone Wars. When his Y-wing was shot down over Ryloth, a young Hera pulled him from the wreck and rebuilt him, and he never flew in a Y-wing again. He stayed with her through the Imperial occupation of her homeworld and followed her into space. There he became the only droid who could work the Ghost's systems, which he had rewired himself. As a member of the Spectres he pranked his crewmates, argued with everyone, and saved them again and again. He posed as Imperial droids to get into an academy and a communications ship, found out where Kanan Jarrus was being held, and gunned down TIE fighters when no one else could. He also befriended the defecting droid AP-5, and together they found the rebels a new base on Atollon, which was named Chopper Base after him. He fought through the Liberation of Lothal and stayed with Hera through the Galactic Civil War, including the rebel strikes at Scarif. He then served with her in the New Republic, where he helped in the search for Ezra Bridger and was there when Ezra finally came home.

Unknown - Manufactured on Lasan

Industrial Automaton builds the C1-series astromech C1-10P on Lasan. He later keeps running for decades past his expiration date.

Clone Wars - Service in the Republic Navy

He serves as a navigation astromech in the Republic Navy and takes part in the fight for Ryloth.

Clone Wars - Shot down over Ryloth

His Y-wing is shot down. The young Hera Syndulla pulls him from the wreck and repairs him, and he refuses to ride in a Y-wing ever again.

~19 BBY - Spying on the Empire

With Hera, he watches the Imperial forces occupying Ryloth after the Republic becomes the Empire.

~19 BBY - Rescue on Ryloth

He helps Hera and Clone Force 99 rescue her parents, Cham and Eleni Syndulla, from Imperial custody.

~Early Imperial era - Leaving Ryloth

He follows Hera when she leaves Ryloth to fight the Empire. He becomes the Ghost's maintenance droid and rewires the ship so thoroughly that only he can work its computer.

11 BBY - Gorse Conflict

He waits on the Ghost while Hera meets Kanan Jarrus, who then joins the crew.

5 BBY - Convoy ambush

When the crew's attack on an Imperial convoy turns out to be a trap, he takes over the Phantom's guns and destroys the last TIE fighter.

5 BBY - Ezra joins the crew

He meets Ezra Bridger with open hostility and plays pranks on him and Zeb aboard the Ghost.

5 BBY - Encounter with R2-D2 and C-3PO

During the theft of T-7 ion disruptors, he clashes with Bail Organa's droids and helps overload the weapons before they can reach the Empire.

5 BBY - Lost in a sabacc game

Zeb bets him in a sabacc game and loses him to Lando Calrissian. The crew earns him back by helping Lando with a smuggling run.

5 BBY - Infiltrating the Imperial Academy

With black paint as a disguise, he gets into Lothal's Academy for Young Imperials to support Ezra and contact Zare Leonis.

5 BBY - Lothal communications tower

He uploads a data spike into the tower's computer so Ezra can broadcast a message of rebellion. Kanan is captured during the escape.

5 BBY - Posing as a courier droid

Disguised as the Imperial courier droid 264, he gets aboard an Imperial communications ship and learns that Kanan is being held on Tarkin's Star Destroyer.

5 BBY - Rescue at Mustafar

He brings a rebel fleet out of hyperspace to wipe out the TIE fighters chasing the Spectres after they rescue Kanan.

4 BBY - Joining Phoenix Cell

The Spectres join the wider rebellion. He escapes the Siege of Lothal and Darth Vader's attack on the Phoenix fleet.

3 BBY - Friendship with AP-5

He befriends the Imperial protocol droid AP-5. Together they find the new rebel base on Atollon, which is named Chopper Base in his honor.

2 BBY - Briefly reprogrammed

Imperials take control of him and turn him against his crew for a short time before he is restored.

2 BBY - Battle of Atollon

He escapes with the rebels when the Empire destroys Chopper Base.

1 BBY - Battle of Scarif

He goes with Hera and the Ghost as part of the Alliance fleet at Scarif.

1 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

He fights with the Spectres to free Lothal from the Empire.

Galactic Civil War - At Hera's side

He stays with General Hera Syndulla for the rest of the war against the Empire.

~9 ABY - Quest to find Ezra Bridger

Now serving the New Republic, he helps Hera and Ahsoka Tano search for the missing Ezra. He is there when Ezra returns home.

Chopper's Timeline

  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY
  • Rogue One: A Star Wars Story, 1 BBY
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY

Hera Syndulla, born 29 BBY

Hera Syndulla was the steady hand at the controls of the Ghost and one of the people who made the Rebel Alliance possible. As a child during the Clone Wars she watched Republic gunships over her home, pulled the astromech Chopper from a crashed Y-wing, and decided she would fly. In the early Imperial years her father Cham's Free Ryloth Movement and her mother Eleni's death left Hera at odds with her father's cause, so she left home to fight the Empire across the galaxy instead. During the Gorse Conflict she met Kanan Jarrus, a Jedi hiding after Order 66, and together they founded the Spectres. Sabine Wren, Garazeb Orrelios and later Ezra Bridger joined the crew. Their insurgency on Lothal brought them into Phoenix Cell, where Hera rose to Phoenix Leader and helped build a base on Atollon until Grand Admiral Thrawn destroyed it. After Kanan died saving her, she helped liberate Lothal and then gave birth to their son, Jacen. As a general she fought at Scarif, Hoth and Endor. In the New Republic she commanded forces that included Alphabet Squadron, fought at Jakku, and later went against her superiors to pursue Thrawn's return.

29 BBY - Birth on Ryloth

Born to Twi'lek resistance leader Cham Syndulla and his wife Eleni.

~22 BBY - Clone Wars on Ryloth

Hides underground with her mother while Separatist forces occupy Ryloth. Seeing Republic ships overhead makes her want to be a pilot, and she rescues the astromech Chopper from a crashed Y-wing.

~19 BBY - Rescue on Ryloth

Captured by the Empire after a supply run with Gobi Glie. After her parents are arrested, she calls in Clone Force 99 and flies a stolen shuttle, her first solo flight, to help free them.

~14 BBY - Relief mission to Ryloth

Flies the freighter Eclipse past an Imperial blockade to bring gattis-root medicine to her people. Free Ryloth leaders praise her leadership.

~11 BBY - The Gorse Conflict

While tracking Count Vidian in the Gorse system, meets Kanan Jarrus. Together they stop Vidian's plan to destroy the moon Cynda, and Kanan joins her aboard the Ghost.

~11 BBY to 5 BBY - Forming the Spectres

Builds a rebel cell with Kanan, Chopper, Garazeb Orrelios and Sabine Wren, working as an agent in Fulcrum's network and with Bail Organa.

5 BBY - Ezra Bridger joins the crew

Meets Ezra Bridger on Lothal. The Spectres rescue Wookiee slaves from Kessel, destroy T-7 disruptors, and free Kanan from Grand Moff Tarkin.

4 BBY - Joining Phoenix Cell

Brings the Spectres into Phoenix Cell and later becomes Phoenix Leader. Reconciles with her father during a mission on Ryloth.

3 BBY - Chopper Base on Atollon

Helps set up the rebel base on Atollon while Grand Admiral Thrawn hunts the rebels.

2 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Thrawn finds and attacks the base. Hera helps lead the survivors' escape, and her cell becomes part of the newly formed Rebel Alliance.

1 BBY - Attack on Lothal and Kanan's death

Leads a failed strike on Lothal's Imperial Armory Complex and is captured. Kanan dies saving her during the Spectres' rescue.

1 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

Helps free Lothal from the Empire. Ezra vanishes with Thrawn during the battle.

1 BBY - Battle of Scarif

Takes part in the Alliance's attack on Scarif as General Syndulla, flying the Ghost.

~0 ABY - Birth of Jacen Syndulla

Gives birth to Jacen, her son with Kanan.

3 ABY - Mako-Ta and Hoth

Serves at the Battle of Mako-Ta and during the Alliance's time on Hoth.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Fights at Endor, the battle in which Emperor Palpatine dies.

~4 ABY - 5 ABY - Barma Battle Group

As a New Republic general, commands the Barma Battle Group, including Alphabet Squadron, against the remnants of the Empire.

5 ABY - Battle of Jakku

Takes part in the Empire's final defeat at Jakku.

~9 ABY - Hunt for Thrawn

Commands New Republic forces, looks into signs of Thrawn's return, and takes the Ghost to Seatos without authorization to back Ahsoka Tano. She faces a hearing before the Senate for it.

~9 ABY - Battle of Lothal's Remnant

Fights the Imperial forces tied to Thrawn over Lothal and welcomes Ezra Bridger home.

Hera Syndulla's Timeline

  • The Bad Batch, 19 BBY (10 years old)
  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (24 years old)
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY (38 years old)

R5-D4, born 25 BBY

Nicknamed "Red" by Luke Skywalker, R5-D4 is the astromech whose sudden breakdown at the Lars homestead changed galactic history. The breakdown was deliberate. Held by Jawas after an earlier stint with the rebellion ended in a memory wipe, the droid was persuaded by R2-D2 that the fate of the galaxy depended on R2 being sold. When Owen Lars chose R5 instead, the droid blew its own head plate, so the Lars family bought R2-D2 and the Death Star plans reached Luke and Obi-Wan Kenobi. R5 then played dead while stormtroopers slaughtered its Jawa owners and set off to find the Rebellion. The historian Voren Na'al later bought and repaired it, and it served with Carson Teva's X-wing squadron until the Empire fell. After another memory wipe it drifted back to Mos Eisley, trained under EV-9D9 at Chalmun's Cantina, and ended up in Peli Motto's hangar, where it helped Din Djarin and was later reunited with R2-D2. Peli eventually sold the reluctant droid to Djarin for his quest to Mandalore. There R5 confirmed the planet's air was breathable, fetched Bo-Katan Kryze to rescue Djarin, and helped retake the homeworld by shutting down the shields of Moff Gideon's base. Afterward it joined the New Republic's Adelphi Rangers.

22 BBY - Rolling Through Mos Espa

Serving a master in Mos Espa, R5-D4 is out in the streets when Anakin Skywalker returns to Tatooine to search for his mother.

~4 BBY - Memory Wiped by Jawas

After working with the early rebellion against the Empire, R5-D4 ends up with a Jawa clan. The Jawas wipe its memory, and it spends years stored in their sandcrawler hoping to be sold.

1 BBY - Meeting R2-D2

The Jawas capture R2-D2 and put him in the same sandcrawler. R2 starts sabotaging R5 to remove the competition, then stops and explains that the Rebellion needs him to be sold.

1 BBY - Sacrifice at the Lars Homestead

Owen Lars picks R5-D4 over R2-D2. R5 deliberately blows its head plate, faking a bad motivator, so Owen buys R2-D2 instead and the Death Star plans reach Luke Skywalker.

1 BBY - Surviving the Sandcrawler Massacre

R5 plays dead while stormtroopers kill the Jawas, then sets off along their trade route to look for a new owner and the Rebellion.

~0 ABY - Recruited by Voren Na'al

While researching Luke Skywalker's origins, the Alliance historian Voren Na'al finds R5-D4 on Tatooine, buys it cheaply, repairs its motivator and records its account of the droid sale.

~0–5 ABY - Service with the Rebellion

R5 is assigned to Carson Teva's X-wing squadron and takes part in several classified missions during the Galactic Civil War.

~5 ABY - Returned to Tatooine

After the Battle of Jakku, Na'al wipes R5's memory and returns it to Tatooine, where it wanders the dunes before reaching Mos Eisley.

~5–9 ABY - Trainee at Chalmun's Cantina

EV-9D9 finds R5 by the cantina's garbage masher and takes it on as a trainee bartender. When a Trandoshan patron kicks R5, EV-9D9 poisons his drink and leaves with the droid.

~9 ABY - Joining Peli Motto's Hangar

EV-9D9 brings R5 to Hangar 3-5, and Peli Motto takes it in. R5 projects a map of Tatooine that shows Din Djarin where Mos Pelgo is.

~9 ABY - Reunion with R2-D2

R5 is at Peli's hangar when R2-D2 arrives with Grogu in Luke Skywalker's X-wing.

~9 ABY - Sold to Din Djarin

Peli sells a reluctant R5 to Din Djarin at half price, and it joins his mission to the Living Waters of Mandalore.

~9 ABY - Mines of Mandalore

R5 goes into the ruins to take an air sample and confirms the atmosphere is breathable. After Djarin is captured, R5 and Grogu bring Bo-Katan Kryze from Kalevala to rescue him.

~9 ABY - Pirate Campaign and the Mandalorian Fleet

R5 passes the covert's location to Carson Teva, flies as co-pilot against Gorian Shard's pirates, and is with Bo-Katan when she finds the Mandalorian fleet on Plazir-15.

~9 ABY - Liberation of Mandalore

At Moff Gideon's base, R5 gets past its droids, sends the schematics to Djarin and shuts down four shields, then escapes on its rocket boosters before the base is destroyed.

~9 ABY - Joining the Adelphi Rangers

After Mandalore is liberated, R5 enters New Republic service at Adelphi Base.

  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY

Jyn Erso, born 21 BBY

Jyn Erso is best remembered as the leader of Rogue One, the small band of rebels who stole the Death Star plans at the Battle of Scarif and so made the Rebel victory at Yavin possible. She was the daughter of scientist Galen Erso and his wife Lyra. Her early childhood was spent on the move: she was born in a Separatist prison, spent the last days of the Clone Wars on Coruscant and Lokori, and then lived inside the Empire's kyber research program until her parents fled to the remote farm world of Lah'mu. When she was eight, Orson Krennic found the family there. He killed her mother and took her father back to work on the Death Star. Saw Gerrera rescued Jyn and raised her as a child soldier among his Partisans, where she learned to fight and forge codes. At sixteen he abandoned her on Tamsye Prime to protect her identity. She briefly found a new family with the Pontas on Skuhl, but lost them to an Imperial crackdown. After that she survived for years as a forger, smuggler and petty criminal under a string of aliases, until she was sent to the Wobani labor camp. The Rebel Alliance freed her so she could reach Saw on Jedha. There she learned that her father had secretly built a fatal flaw into the Death Star, and she then watched him die on Eadu. When the Alliance Council refused to act, she and Cassian Andor took a volunteer squad to Scarif. Jyn transmitted the plans to the Rebel fleet, and she and Cassian died together when the Death Star's blast engulfed the base.

21 BBY - Birth on Vallt

Born to Galen and Lyra Erso inside the Separatist Tambolor prison on Vallt, where her father was being held for refusing to help the Confederacy.

~20 BBY - Rescue to Coruscant

About six months old when Lieutenant Commander Orson Krennic of the Republic extracts the family and brings them to the Institute of Applied Science on Coruscant.

~20 BBY - Move to Lokori

The family relocates to Lokori after Krennic arranges for Galen to take a position with Helical HyperCom.

19 BBY - Battle on Lokori

Trapped with her parents as Separatist droids overrun the Helical HyperCom facility, the family survives only because the droids shut down when the Clone Wars end.

~19 BBY - Life inside Project Celestial Power

Grows up in the project complex on Coruscant, mostly in the care of the nanny droid Mac-Vee, while her father's kyber research is secretly turned toward a superweapon.

~18 BBY - Trip to Alpinn

At age three, travels with her mother, Has Obitt and Nari Sable to an archaeological dig on Alpinn.

~17 BBY - Flight from Coruscant

At four, escapes the Empire with her parents aboard Saw Gerrera's ship and helps choose Lah'mu as their hiding place.

~17–13 BBY - Childhood on Lah'mu

Grows up on an isolated skycorn farm, taught at home by her mother and drilled in the family's emergency plans.

13 BBY - Loss of her parents

Krennic and his death troopers arrive at the homestead. Lyra is killed, Galen is taken away, and Jyn hides in the family's bunker until Saw Gerrera comes to rescue her.

~13 BBY - Taken in by Saw Gerrera

Brought to Saw's hidden outpost on Wrea and trained there in blasters, truncheons, hand-to-hand combat and forging codes.

~10 BBY - Meeting Enfys Nest

At eleven, goes with Saw to meet Enfys Nest and the Cloud-Riders, who offer him coaxium for the fight against the Empire.

~9 BBY - Meeting Arhul Nemo

At twelve, meets Arhul Nemo of the Commenor Underground.

~9–5 BBY - Partisan missions

Takes part in Partisan operations, including her first solo mission to Horuz and the massacre on Inusagi, where her friend Maia is killed.

~5 BBY - Abandoned on Tamsye Prime

At sixteen, after Reece Tallent betrays a mission, Saw leaves her hidden in a bunker and never returns. She escapes the planet on her own.

~5 BBY - Life on Skuhl

Taken in by freighter captain Akshaya Ponta. On Skuhl she begins a relationship with Akshaya's son, Hadder.

~4 BBY - Escape from Skuhl

Stormtroopers raid the Ponta home and the family flees. Hadder and Akshaya die when their freighter is destroyed, and Jyn reaches Five Points station alone.

~4 BBY - Forger for Lucka Solange

On Five Points, she is pressed into forging casino credits for Imperial Commander Lucka Solange.

~4–1 BBY - Years as a criminal

Drifts across the galaxy as a forger, smuggler and petty criminal under aliases such as Liana Hallik and Tanith Ponta. On Garel she rescues a girl's tooka-cat from stormtroopers.

1 BBY - Imprisoned on Wobani

Arrested as "Liana Hallik" and sentenced to twenty years at the Wobani labor camp. She helps Dunning Tredup's crew escape but gives herself up to save Theera.

1 BBY - Freed by the Rebellion

Freed during a prisoner transfer by a Rebel team including K-2SO, and recruited by Mon Mothma and Cassian Andor to find Saw Gerrera.

1 BBY - Jedha

Reunites with Saw on Jedha and sees her father's message about the Death Star's hidden flaw, just before the Death Star destroys Jedha City.

1 BBY - Eadu

Reaches her father on Eadu only to see him mortally wounded in a Rebel airstrike. He dies in her arms.

1 BBY - Rogue One

After the Alliance Council refuses to act, she and Cassian lead a volunteer squad to Scarif under the call sign Rogue One.

1 BBY - Death on Scarif

Steals the Death Star plans from the Citadel and transmits them to the Rebel fleet. She dies beside Cassian Andor when the Death Star fires on Scarif.

Jyn Erso's Timeline

  • Rogue One: A Star Wars Story, 1 BBY (20 years old)

Sabine Wren, born 21 BBY

Sabine Wren is the graffiti-slinging demolitions expert of the Ghost crew, whose painted starbird became the emblem of the Rebel Alliance. Raised in Clan Wren during the Empire's grip on Mandalore, she built the Arc Pulse Generator as an Imperial cadet. When she saw the weapon turned against her own people, she deserted, and the break left her estranged from her family. After a stint as a bounty hunter with Ketsu Onyo, she joined Hera Syndulla and Kanan Jarrus's rebel cell and harried the Empire across Lothal and beyond. In 2 BBY she found the Darksaber on Dathomir and learned to wield it. She used it to reconcile with her mother, Ursa, helped bring down Viceroy Gar Saxon, and rallied the clans against the Empire before giving the blade to Bo-Katan Kryze and destroying her own weapon for good. She fought in the liberation of Lothal, where she lost Kanan and saw Ezra Bridger vanish with Thrawn, then stayed behind to guard the planet. She later trained under Ahsoka Tano, but they drifted apart after the Empire's purge of Mandalore killed her family. In 9 ABY she crossed into another galaxy, found Ezra on Peridea, and was left stranded there as Thrawn escaped. On Peridea she began to reach the Force.

21 BBY - Birth on Mandalore

Born into Clan Wren of House Vizsla near the end of the Clone Wars, while Mandalore is in turmoil. Her mother, Ursa, belongs to Death Watch. The family's ancestral home is Krownest.

~7 BBY - Imperial Academy of Mandalore

Enrolls as a cadet at the Imperial Academy on occupied Mandalore and designs the Arc Pulse Generator, nicknamed "the Duchess," which turns beskar armor against the person wearing it.

~6 BBY - Desertion from the Empire

Horrified to see her weapon used against Mandalorians, she speaks out, destroys the prototype and flees with Ketsu Onyo. The Empire and her own clan brand her a traitor.

~6 BBY - Bounty hunter

Works as a bounty hunter with Ketsu Onyo until Ketsu leaves her for dead and the partnership ends badly.

~5 BBY - Joins the Spectres

At about 16, she is recruited into Hera Syndulla and Kanan Jarrus's rebel cell aboard the Ghost and takes the call sign Spectre 5.

5 BBY - The Artist of Lothal

Paints the starbird on a TIE fighter at a Capital City airfield and escapes behind a paint bomb. She helps free enslaved Wookiees from Kessel and welcomes Ezra Bridger to the crew.

5 BBY - Missions across Lothal

Takes part in the Stygeon Prime rescue attempt, patches up her rift with Hera at Fort Anaxes, frees slaves on Oon, and joins the Empire Day sabotage and the operation to get Tseebo off Lothal.

5 BBY - Siege of Lothal and rebel fleet

Helps rescue Kanan from Mustafar. After the Siege of Lothal, the Spectres join Phoenix Squadron and the wider rebel movement.

~4 BBY - Mandalorian encounters

Reunites with Ketsu Onyo and helps her break from the Empire. She also clashes with the Mandalorian Protectors of Concord Dawn under Fenn Rau.

3 BBY - Imperial Super Commandos

Fights Gar Saxon's Imperial Super Commandos on Concord Dawn and learns her brother Tristan serves among them.

2 BBY - The Darksaber

Finds the Darksaber in Maul's lair on Dathomir. Kanan and Fenn Rau train her to use it.

2 BBY - Return to Krownest

Goes home to Krownest and wins over her family. She defeats Gar Saxon in a duel but spares him, and her mother kills him when he tries to shoot Sabine in the back.

2 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Leads Mandalorian reinforcements to help the rebels at Chopper Base during the Imperial assault.

1 BBY - Mandalorian Civil War

Rescues her father, frees Mandalore from Imperial control, destroys the Duchess weapon and all her research, and gives the Darksaber to Bo-Katan Kryze.

1 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

Returns to Lothal with the Ghost crew. She fights through Kanan's death and Grand Admiral Thrawn's occupation, and the planet is freed after Ezra disappears into hyperspace with the Chimaera.

After 1 BBY - Guardian of Lothal

Stays on Lothal to protect it, living in Ezra's old comm tower.

~4 ABY - Apprentice to Ahsoka Tano

After the Empire falls, she sets out with Ahsoka Tano to search for Ezra and trains as her apprentice. The two later part ways after Mandalore's purge kills Sabine's family.

9 ABY - Search for Ezra Bridger

Ahsoka recruits her again to decode a star map. Sabine is badly wounded by Shin Hati and then gives the map to Baylan Skoll in exchange for a chance to find Ezra.

9 ABY - Peridea

Travels to another galaxy with Morgan Elsbeth's forces, reunites with Ezra, and is left stranded on Peridea with Ahsoka when Thrawn escapes. There she begins to use the Force.

Sabine Wren's Timeline

  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (16 years old)
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY (30 years old)

Wedge Antilles, born 21 BBY

Wedge Antilles is one of the Rebellion's great survivor pilots. He flew against both Death Stars and lived through both battles, and later helped train the next generation of Resistance flyers. He grew up flying and fixing ships on Corellia and first trained as a TIE fighter cadet at the Imperial Skystrike Academy. The Empire had hurt his family, friends and the girl he loved, and after an Imperial massacre of surrendering rebels he reached out to Fulcrum. In 2 BBY Sabine Wren got him and Hobbie Klivian out of the academy. He flew with Phoenix Squadron through the Battle of Atollon, then as Red Two at Yavin, where he and Luke Skywalker were the only members of Red Squadron to survive. At Hoth, flying as Rogue Three with gunner Wes Janson, he brought down the first AT-AT of the battle. He then rebuilt the Alliance's shattered fighter group as its commander and led Red Squadron at Endor, where he and Lando Calrissian destroyed the second Death Star. For the New Republic he was captured while scouting Akiva, formed Phantom Squadron to help liberate Kashyyyk, and fought at Jakku, the battle that ended the Empire. He then became a flight instructor, married Norra Wexley and settled on Akiva. Decades later he came out of retirement to fly with the Resistance at Exegol, but he arrived too late to save his stepson Temmin.

Unknown - Youth on Corellia

Grows up on Corellia working at a fuel depot and on farms, learning to fly and repair ships. As a youth he chases down a pirate gunboat in a borrowed starfighter and destroys it.

Before 2 BBY - Imperial Cadet at Skystrike Academy

Recruited by the Empire while hauling cargo, he trains as a TIE pilot at Skystrike Academy on Montross, where he befriends Hobbie Klivian and Rake Gahree.

2 BBY - Defection to the Rebellion

After the Teralov massacre he contacts Fulcrum about defecting. Sabine Wren infiltrates the academy, and with Agent Kallus's help Wedge and Hobbie escape in a TIE bomber and join Phoenix Squadron at Chopper Base on Atollon.

2 BBY - Visit to Gahree's Family

Wedge and Hobbie try to honor their fallen friend's family, but Instructor Goran ambushes them there. They escape and realize they can never go home.

~1 BBY - Mission to Killun 71

Now an Alliance lieutenant, he poses as a TIE pilot to steal Imperial codes with AP-5 and Chopper. On the way back, Imperial slicers take control of Chopper.

1 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Flies an A-wing against Grand Admiral Thrawn's assault on Chopper Base and escapes with the survivors to Yavin 4.

1 BBY - Battle of Scarif Announcement

While grounded by an injury on Yavin 4, he tells rebel pilots over the base announcement system to redeploy to Scarif.

0 BBY - Battle of Yavin

Flies as Red Two, saves Luke Skywalker from a TIE fighter, and is forced out of the trench run by damage. He and Luke are the only Red Squadron survivors.

~0 ABY - Missions with Red Squadron

Continues flying under Arhul Narra, including the Giju evacuation and the hijacking of the Star Destroyer Harbinger to break the blockade of Tureen VII.

~2 ABY - Mercy Mission to Oulanne

As a lieutenant commander he leads a relief mission and recruits the former Imperial pilot Thane Kyrell into the Rebellion.

3 ABY - Battle of Hoth

As Rogue Three, he and gunner Wes Janson use a tow cable to bring down the first AT-AT of the battle, then help cover the evacuation.

3 ABY - Rebuilding Red Squadron

Promoted to commander, he gathers new pilots into a revived Red Squadron and wipes out a pirate base that threatens the fleet's rendezvous point.

~3 ABY - Operation Starlight

Flies with Shara Bey's Starlight Squadron to warn scattered fleet divisions that Alliance codes have been compromised.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Leads Red Squadron into the second Death Star's superstructure and, with Lando Calrissian, destroys its main reactor.

4 ABY - Captured on Akiva

Captured by Imperials while scouting their gathering on Akiva alone. He is freed during the uprising there alongside Norra Wexley and her allies.

5 ABY - Phantom Squadron and Kashyyyk

Forms Phantom Squadron and joins the unauthorized strike to liberate Kashyyyk, after which the squadron is disbanded.

5 ABY - Battle of Jakku

Leads the reformed Phantom Squadron at Jakku, the battle that finally defeats the Empire.

After 5 ABY - Flight Instructor and Marriage

Retires to teach at the New Republic's flight academy on Hosnian Prime, later marries Norra Wexley and settles on Akiva.

34 ABY - Recruited by the Resistance

Visited on Akiva by Poe Dameron, Temmin Wexley and Karé Kun, he agrees to help the struggling Resistance.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Flies with Lando Calrissian and Chewbacca's Citizens' Fleet against the Final Order at Exegol. He arrives too late to save his stepson Temmin.

Wedge Antilles's Timeline

  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY
  • Episode IV: A New Hope, 1 BBY
  • Episode V: The Empire Strikes Back, 3 ABY
  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY

Max Rebo, born 20 BBY

Max Rebo is the bandleader of the Max Rebo Band, famous for playing his red ball jett organ with his feet while the group entertained Jabba the Hutt and the crowd at his Tatooine palace. His brother Azool Phantelle was a swindler. Once, in Mos Eisley, angry locals mistook Max for Azool and chased him. Max ended the chase by throwing Azool's stolen money back to the crowd, and Jabba then put Azool to work as a waiter on his sail barge. In 4 ABY, Rebo was playing in the throne room when Oola was fed to the rancor and when Leia Organa arrived disguised as Boushh. He then rode the Khetanna out to the Great Pit of Carkoon, where he was drawn into a plot to poison Jabba. He was caught, but Luke Skywalker's rescue of Han Solo distracted the Hutt. Rebo escaped when the barge was destroyed. After Jabba's death he kept playing, and by 9 ABY he performed on a newer organ at Garsa Fwip's Sanctuary in Mos Espa, where Boba Fett visited more than once. When the Pyke Syndicate bombed the cantina, Garsa Fwip and his bandmates were killed, but Rebo survived a second explosion.

Date unknown - Birth on Orto

Maximilian Rebo is born on Orto, a cold world, to Jeph Rebo and his wife. His brother is Azool Phantelle.

Date unknown - Forms the Max Rebo Band

Rebo starts his own jizz band and becomes known for playing the red ball jett organ with his feet. Posters advertise "Max Reebo" as early as the Clone Wars.

~22 BBY - Name on the posters

Posters for "Max Reebo" are seen around the galaxy during the Clone Wars and later during the Imperial era, which shows his act was already well known.

~4 ABY - Mistaken for his brother

In Mos Eisley, people Azool had swindled mistake Max for him and chase him. Azool pulls him onto a speeder bike to get away. Max ends the chase by throwing the stolen money back to the crowd, and Jabba makes Azool a waiter on the Khetanna as punishment.

4 ABY - Playing in Jabba's palace

The band is playing for Jabba when Oola is dropped into the rancor pit, and is still there when Leia Organa arrives disguised as the bounty hunter Boushh.

4 ABY - Plot against Jabba

On board the Khetanna at the Great Pit of Carkoon, Rebo is drawn into a plot to poison Jabba. He is caught, but Luke Skywalker's rescue of Han Solo distracts the Hutt.

4 ABY - Escapes the sail barge

Rebo gets out alive before the Khetanna explodes over the Dune Sea.

9 ABY - Playing at Garsa's Sanctuary

Rebo now plays a newer organ at Garsa Fwip's Sanctuary in Mos Espa, backed by a Bith guitarist and the drummer droid R0-1N6. Boba Fett visits the cantina more than once.

9 ABY - Survives the Sanctuary bombing

The Pyke Syndicate bombs the Sanctuary during business hours. Garsa Fwip and the band are killed, but Rebo survives.

Max Rebo's Timeline

  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY
  • The Book of Boba Fett, 9 ABY

Amilyn Holdo, born 19 BBY

Amilyn Holdo is remembered for the Holdo maneuver, the lightspeed ramming attack that tore through the First Order fleet above Crait and let the last of the Resistance escape. She started out as a flamboyant, astrology-loving teenage delegate to the Imperial Apprentice Legislature. There she spoke out against Imperial policy and became Leia Organa's closest friend. She helped Leia warn Bail Organa of the Imperial strike on the rebel fleet at Paucris, and she was with Leia when Kier Domadi died. Once the Galactic Civil War broke out in earnest, Holdo joined the Rebel Alliance as a civil minister. She proved herself a commander when she took over the stricken blockade runner Candor and broke it free of a Star Destroyer's tractor beam, which earned her a captain's commission. After that she secured fuel and supplies through underworld contacts, including Crimson Dawn. Decades later Leia recruited her into the Resistance as a vice admiral. She commanded the cruiser Ninka on covert missions and bomber campaigns during the cold war with the First Order. After the Resistance leadership was wiped out in 34 ABY, she took command of the fleet as it fled. Her secretive escape plan clashed with Poe Dameron, and when it was betrayed she stayed aboard the Raddus and jumped it into the Supremacy. She gave her life to save the Resistance.

~19 BBY - Birth on Gatalenta

Born on Gatalenta around the start of the Empire's reign. As a child she took to spelunking, tobogganing, skyfaring, astrology and philosophy, and she pushed back against her homeworld's traditions of quiet simplicity.

3 BBY - Pathfinding on Alderaan

Takes part in Queen Breha Organa's pathfinding class in the Istabith range. She turns her emergency tent into a toboggan so her group can bring an injured Harp Allor back before nightfall, and meets Leia Organa and Kier Domadi.

3 BBY - Apprentice Legislator

Represents Gatalenta in the Apprentice Legislature on Coruscant. She is horrified when the Arreyel vote leads to its people being displaced, and she argues against sanctions on Lolet, which wins her Leia's trust.

3 BBY - Stowaways to Ocahont

On Pamarthe, she and Leia overhear Senator Winmey Lenz's secret quadanium deal and stow away on a freighter to Ocahont. Leia tells her about the rebellion, and they meet Mon Mothma at a rebel outpost.

3 BBY - Warning at Paucris

Gives up her chartered ship so she and Leia can warn Bail Organa about an Imperial attack on the rebel fleet at Paucris. She watches Kier Domadi die, flies her grieving friend home to Alderaan, and bluffs past Imperial patrols with her astrology knowledge.

~3 BBY - Leia's Investiture and the Lost Museum

Attends Leia's investiture as crown princess and begins working for the rebellion in secret. Later, she and Leia go deep into Coruscant's underworld to find a lost museum on Level 4.

1 BBY - Rebel Minister

Leaves Imperial politics when open war breaks out and becomes a minister in the Alliance Civil Government, known for her anti-Imperial speeches.

~1 BBY–3 ABY - Commanding the Candor

After a Star Destroyer attack kills the Candor's captain, she takes command and fires through the enemy hangar to break free of its tractor beam. The feat earns her a captain's commission in the Alliance Fleet.

3 ABY - Crimson Dawn Intelligence

While working the underworld for supplies, she learns that Crimson Dawn has returned and is auctioning Han Solo on Jekara. She sends Leia the information through a dead drop, then coordinates supply lines with Qi'ra during the Syndicate War.

~3–4 ABY - Auction on Spira

Brings Leia, Luke, Chewbacca and Lando to Spira to bid on a Nihil strikeship whose Path engine could lead to the lost Kezarat convoy's fuel. After losing the bid, the group steals the engine and flees in her ship Skyfarer, which is pulled into No-Space.

5 ABY - Galactic Concordance

The Alliance wins the Galactic Civil War, and the New Republic is founded.

~28 ABY - Joining the Resistance

Leia recruits her into the Resistance. She becomes a vice admiral of the fleet and takes command of the cruiser Ninka.

Before 31 ABY - Brooksdion Station

Leads a covert team that steals Admiral Mathieson's protocol droid head as evidence of New Republic inaction. She guides her pursuer, Poe Dameron, through the Lost Souls asteroid field without revealing who she is.

34 ABY - Battle of Sheh Soahi

Leads Cobalt and Crimson bomber squadrons against pirates acting as a First Order proxy in the Cassander sector, confirming that the First Order was arming them.

34 ABY - Atterra Campaign

Helps plan the covert relief bombing runs to Atterra Bravo with Leia and Casca Panzoro, and recruits Rose Tico to teach the Ninka's crew her baffler technology.

34 ABY - Evacuation of D'Qar

After the Hosnian Cataclysm, she brings the Ninka and the bomber force to cover the evacuation of D'Qar and escapes with the fleet after the Fulminatrix is destroyed.

34 ABY - Acting Commander of the Resistance

With Leia badly injured and Admiral Ackbar killed, she takes command of the fleet as it is tracked through hyperspace and runs low on fuel. She clashes with Poe Dameron over her secret plan to evacuate to Crait on cloaked transports.

34 ABY - The Holdo Maneuver and Death

After Poe's mutiny fails and her plan is betrayed to the First Order, she stays alone on the Raddus. She jumps it to lightspeed into the Supremacy, splitting the flagship and wrecking much of the fleet. She dies in the collision, and the survivors escape to Crait.

Amilyn Holdo's Timeline

  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY

Luke Skywalker, born 19 BBY

Luke Skywalker is the farm boy who became the galaxy's most famous Jedi, the hero whose single torpedo shot destroyed the first Death Star and whose faith in his father brought Darth Vader back to the light. Born in secret in 19 BBY to Anakin Skywalker and Padmé Amidala and separated from his twin sister Leia at birth, he grew up on his aunt and uncle's moisture farm, watched from a distance by Obi-Wan Kenobi. When two droids carrying stolen Death Star plans came into his life, he lost his family to the Empire, joined the Rebel Alliance, and began training in the Force, first under Kenobi and then with Yoda on Dagobah. On Cloud City he lost his hand and learned that Vader was his father. At Endor he refused to kill him, and Anakin turned on the Emperor to save his son. After the war he searched the galaxy for lost Jedi knowledge, trained Leia, briefly taught the foundling Grogu, and founded a new academy. It ended in disaster when his nephew Ben Solo fell to the dark side and destroyed his temple. Consumed by guilt, Luke went into exile on Ahch-To until Rey found him. After giving her a reluctant final lesson, he projected himself across the galaxy to face Kylo Ren on Crait, bought the Resistance time to escape, and became one with the Force. As a spirit, he later urged Rey to confront the resurrected Palpatine.

19 BBY - Birth on Polis Massa

Born minutes before his twin sister Leia, two days after the Empire is proclaimed. His dying mother Padmé names him before she dies.

19 BBY - Hidden on Tatooine

Obi-Wan Kenobi carries the infant to Owen and Beru Lars, who raise him on their moisture farm and keep his father's identity from him.

16 BBY - Marauders at the Homestead

At age three, Luke throws his toy starfighter to distract a Gamorrean raider while Kenobi and Owen fight off an attack on the farm.

9 BBY - Hunted by Reva

The former Inquisitor Reva Sevander tracks Luke to Tatooine and chases him into the desert, but she spares him. Afterward, Owen lets Kenobi meet the boy.

7 BBY - Lost in a Sandstorm

At twelve, Luke and his friend Windy are caught in a sandstorm in the desert. Kenobi rescues them and introduces himself as an old friend of Luke's father.

~5 BBY - Rescuing Uncle Owen

Luke repairs his grounded T-16 skyhopper and catches Owen in midair after the bounty hunter Krrsantan throws him from a cliff.

1 BBY - The Droids and the Message

Owen buys R2-D2 and C-3PO. Luke finds Princess Leia's plea for help and follows R2 to Ben Kenobi, who gives him his father's lightsaber.

1 BBY - Leaving Tatooine

Stormtroopers murder Owen and Beru. Luke leaves with Kenobi and hires Han Solo and Chewbacca in Mos Eisley.

1 BBY - Rescue from the Death Star

Luke helps free Leia from the battle station and watches Kenobi fall to Darth Vader.

0 BBY - Battle of Yavin

Flying as Red Five, Luke trusts the Force, destroys the Death Star with one shot, and is awarded the Medal of Bravery.

3 ABY - Battle of Hoth

Luke survives a wampa attack, leads Rogue Group's snowspeeders against Imperial walkers, and escapes the planet.

3 ABY - Training on Dagobah

Guided by Kenobi's spirit, Luke finds Yoda and begins his training. He leaves early to help his friends.

3 ABY - Duel on Cloud City

Luke loses his right hand to Darth Vader, learns that Vader is his father, and is rescued by Leia.

4 ABY - Rescue from Jabba's Palace

Luke, now a Jedi Knight in all but name, frees Han Solo and defeats Jabba's forces at the Great Pit of Carkoon.

4 ABY - Yoda's Death

The dying Yoda confirms Vader is Luke's father. Kenobi's spirit reveals that Leia is his twin.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

Luke surrenders to Vader and refuses to kill him before the Emperor. Anakin turns on Palpatine to save his son and dies redeemed.

4 ABY - Training Leia

Luke teaches his sister in the Jedi arts on Ajan Kloss. She later sets her training aside.

5 ABY - Battle of Jakku

Luke helps the New Republic in its final campaigns against the Imperial holdouts, including self-proclaimed Grand Moff Adelhard.

~6 ABY - Liberation of the Nagai

Luke works to free the Nagai people and their homeworld from their enslavers.

9 ABY - Rescuing Grogu

Luke answers Grogu's call through the Force and destroys Moff Gideon's dark troopers aboard his cruiser.

9 ABY - Grogu's Choice

While training Grogu at his new temple site, Luke hosts Ahsoka Tano. He lets the child choose, and Grogu goes back to Din Djarin instead of taking Yoda's lightsaber.

~15 ABY - Journey to Elphrona

Luke, his apprentice and nephew Ben Solo, and Lor San Tekka search for Jedi lore and first encounter the Knights of Ren.

21 ABY - Quest for Exegol

Troubled by dark visions, Luke searches for the Sith world. He faces wraiths on Tython with Anakin's spirit at his side, but the trail goes cold when he fails to track down the assassin Ochi.

28 ABY - Fall of the Jedi Temple

Luke briefly considers killing Ben Solo to stop the darkness he senses. Ben turns, destroys the temple, and kills his students.

28 ABY - Exile on Ahch-To

Convinced the Jedi should end, Luke hides on the first Jedi Temple's island and cuts himself off from the Force.

34 ABY - Rey Arrives

Rey brings Luke his old lightsaber. He reluctantly gives her three lessons, and Yoda's spirit helps him let go of his failure.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait and Death

Luke projects himself across the galaxy to face Kylo Ren, buys the Resistance time to escape, and becomes one with the Force on Ahch-To.

35 ABY - Spirit Counsel

Luke's spirit stops Rey from exiling herself and urges her to face Palpatine. He later watches over her on Tatooine.

Luke Skywalker's Timeline

Leia Organa, born 19 BBY

The adopted princess of Alderaan who became one of the Rebellion's most important leaders, Leia spent her whole life fighting tyranny, from the Imperial Senate to the command bunkers of the Resistance. She and her twin, Luke, were the secret children of Anakin Skywalker and Padmé Amidala. They were separated at birth, and Leia was raised by Bail and Breha Organa as heir to Alderaan's throne. At ten she was kidnapped on an Inquisitor's orders and rescued by Obi-Wan Kenobi. As a teenager she became the youngest member of the Imperial Senate while quietly working for her father's rebel network. After she smuggled the Death Star plans out of Scarif, she was captured and tortured by Darth Vader and watched Alderaan's destruction. Once rescued, she helped bring about the Rebel victories at Yavin and Endor, where she learned that Vader was her father. She married Han Solo and briefly trained as a Jedi under her brother, but she set the lightsaber aside when she foresaw her son's fate. After Ben Solo was born, she became a senator of the New Republic. When the Senate learned whose daughter she was, her political career collapsed, and she founded the Resistance to oppose the rising First Order. She lost Han to their son, now Kylo Ren, and saw the New Republic destroyed, yet she kept the Resistance going through Crait and trained Rey. In 35 ABY she spent her last strength reaching out to Ben, a sacrifice that helped turn him back to the light.

19 BBY - Birth on Polis Massa

Born as Leia Amidala Skywalker to Padmé Amidala two days after the Empire was proclaimed. Her twin brother, Luke, is born minutes earlier. Padmé dies shortly afterward, and the twins are separated to hide them from Vader and the Emperor.

19 BBY - Adopted by the House of Organa

Bail Organa and Queen Breha adopt her, and she is raised on Alderaan as Princess Leia Organa, heir to the throne.

10 BBY - Runs away from the palace

At nine, fearing an arranged marriage, she hides in the woods for a week and teaches herself to survive before the royal guards find her.

9 BBY - Kidnapped from Alderaan

Mercenaries hired by the Third Sister, Reva, abduct her to lure Obi-Wan Kenobi out of hiding. She is held on Daiyu.

9 BBY - Rescued by Obi-Wan Kenobi

Kenobi frees her on Daiyu. On the run through Mapuzo, she is recaptured by Reva and resists interrogation in the Fortress Inquisitorius until Kenobi and the Path rescue her.

9 BBY - Escape from Jabiim and return home

She crawls through the vents to restore the Path's escape ship and returns to Alderaan. Kenobi tells her he knew her birth parents.

~3 BBY - Challenges and the Apprentice Legislature

At sixteen she takes on the Challenges of the Mind, Body and Heart to be named heir, joins the Apprentice Legislature, and discovers her parents' secret involvement in the rebellion.

~2 BBY - Youngest Imperial Senator

She succeeds Bail as Alderaan's representative in the Imperial Senate. Under the cover of humanitarian missions, she supplies rebel cells, including delivering ships to the Lothal rebels.

1 BBY - The Death Star plans

After the Battle of Scarif, she escapes aboard the Tantive IV with the stolen plans. She hides them in R2-D2 before she is captured by Darth Vader.

1 BBY - Destruction of Alderaan

Grand Moff Tarkin destroys Alderaan in front of her after she is interrogated aboard the Death Star. Luke Skywalker, Han Solo and Obi-Wan Kenobi rescue her.

0 ABY - Battle of Yavin

The plans she protected let the Rebellion destroy the Death Star. She presents medals to the heroes of Yavin.

3 ABY - Hoth and Cloud City

She commands the evacuation of Echo Base and flees with Han Solo to Bespin, where she confesses her love before Han is frozen in carbonite.

4 ABY - Rescue of Han Solo

Disguised as the bounty hunter Boushh, she infiltrates Jabba's palace. After she is enslaved, she strangles Jabba during the escape at the Great Pit of Carkoon.

4 ABY - Battle of Endor

She fights alongside the Ewoks on Endor's forest moon and learns that Luke is her twin brother and that Vader is their father. The Emperor dies and the second Death Star is destroyed.

~4 ABY - Marries Han Solo

She weds Han Solo after the victory at Endor and begins Jedi training under Luke.

5 ABY - Birth of Ben Solo

Her son is born on the day the Empire signs the Galactic Concordance after the Battle of Jakku. She has already stepped away from her Jedi training after foreseeing her son's death.

~5 ABY - Senator of the New Republic

She helps build the New Republic and serves in its Senate, pushing for a stronger galactic defence.

28 ABY - Exposed as Vader's daughter

Her political rivals reveal her parentage in the Senate, which ends her career. She begins organizing the Resistance against the First Order.

~29 ABY - Ben falls to the dark side

Ben destroys Luke's Jedi temple and becomes Kylo Ren. Leia and Han grow apart.

34 ABY - Hosnian Cataclysm and Starkiller Base

The First Order destroys the New Republic's capital. Her Resistance destroys Starkiller Base, but Han is killed by their son.

34 ABY - Evacuation of D'Qar and Battle of Crait

She survives being thrown into space when her bridge is destroyed, saving herself with the Force. She then leads the remnant of the Resistance to Crait, where Luke dies buying them time.

35 ABY - Trains Rey on Ajan Kloss

She rebuilds the Resistance and continues Rey's Jedi training.

35 ABY - Death on Ajan Kloss

She uses the last of her strength to reach Ben through the Force and dies, becoming one with the Force. Her sacrifice helps turn Ben back to the light before the Battle of Exegol.

  • Obi-Wan Kenobi, 9 BBY (10 years old)
