  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (53 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (54 years old)

Ezra Bridger, born 19 BBY

Ezra Bridger is remembered as the street-smart orphan who became a Jedi Padawan and one of the early rebellion's most important young leaders. He was born on the first Empire Day. His parents were arrested for their anti-Imperial broadcasts, and from about the age of seven he lived alone as a thief and con artist. In 5 BBY he fell in with the rebel crew of the Ghost, and Kanan Jarrus took him on as an apprentice. He built his own lightsaber from a kyber crystal he received in the Lothal Jedi Temple, and his broadcast of defiance helped bring scattered rebel cells together. Learning that his parents had died in an Imperial prison pushed him toward the dark side, which peaked on Malachor, where he opened a Sith holocron alongside Maul and Kanan was blinded. He came back from the edge, went on to lead missions of his own, helped Rex's droid veterans end their war, and set out to find Obi-Wan Kenobi. After Kanan died, Ezra led the liberation of Lothal in 1 BBY. He called a pod of purrgil to drag Grand Admiral Thrawn's fleet into hyperspace, and he was carried off with it. For almost a decade he was stranded on Peridea in another galaxy. There Sabine Wren and Ahsoka Tano found him, and he got home aboard Thrawn's own flagship.

19 BBY - Birth on Empire Day

Born on Lothal to Ephraim and Mira Bridger on the day the Galactic Empire was proclaimed.

~18 BBY - The Graf Children

As an infant, meets Milo and Lina Graf, whom his parents shelter after the Empire abducts the Grafs' parents.

14 BBY - Parents Speak Out

His parents begin illegal public broadcasts criticizing Imperial rule on Lothal.

12 BBY - Orphaned

The Empire arrests Ephraim and Mira. The seven-year-old Ezra is left to survive alone on the streets as a thief and con artist, and eventually makes his home in an abandoned comm tower.

~5 BBY - Gladiator Night

Guides the bounty hunter Bossk through Capital City and helps expose a corrupt Imperial officer after his mentor, the pawnbroker Ferpil Wallaway, is killed.

5 BBY - Joins the Spectres

Runs into the crew of the Ghost during a heist in Capital City and helps free Wookiee slaves from Kessel. He then accepts Kanan Jarrus's offer to become his Padawan.

5 BBY - Stygeon Prime

Joins Kanan's attempt to rescue Luminara Unduli and learns it was a trap set by the Grand Inquisitor.

5 BBY - Undercover at the Imperial Academy

Infiltrates Lothal's Academy for Young Imperials as cadet "Dev Morgan," steals a decoder, and helps Jai Kell escape the Inquisitor with Zare Leonis's help.

5 BBY - Empire Day

On his fifteenth birthday, helps rescue his parents' friend Tseebo. At Fort Anaxes he briefly taps the dark side to command a giant fyrnock against the Inquisitor.

5 BBY - Lothal Jedi Temple

Passes a trial in the Jedi Temple on Lothal, where Yoda guides him and gives him a kyber crystal. He uses it to build his own lightsaber.

5 BBY - A Message of Hope

Broadcasts a call for resistance across the Lothal sector, which helps unite rebel cells.

5 BBY - Mustafar

Helps rescue Kanan from Grand Moff Tarkin and sees the Grand Inquisitor die. Afterwards the Spectres join Phoenix Cell.

4 BBY - Parents' Fate

Learns that his parents died leading a prison revolt they had been inspired to start by his own broadcast.

4 BBY - Malachor

Allies with Maul in a Sith temple and uses the dark side to open a Sith holocron. Kanan is blinded and Ahsoka Tano faces Darth Vader.

3 BBY - Under Maul's Shadow

Struggles with the holocron's influence while fighting Grand Admiral Thrawn. He destroys the holocron with Kanan's help and Maul's attempts to claim him fail.

2 BBY - Agamar

Helps Rex and a band of leftover Separatist battle droids make peace, giving them closure on the Clone Wars.

2 BBY - Twin Suns

Goes to Tatooine to find Obi-Wan Kenobi and witnesses Kenobi's final duel with Maul.

2 BBY - Battle of Atollon

Survives Thrawn's attack on Chopper Base with the help of Kanan and the Force-wielder known as the Bendu.

1 BBY - Return to Lothal

Rejoins the Lothal resistance with Ryder Azadi and uncovers the Empire's TIE Defender program. Kanan sacrifices himself to save the crew.

1 BBY - The World Between Worlds

Guided by the white Loth-wolf, enters a realm outside time through the Lothal Jedi Temple and pulls Ahsoka Tano out of her duel with Darth Vader. He refuses Emperor Palpatine's temptation to bring Kanan back.

1 BBY - Liberation of Lothal

Calls a pod of purrgil to drag Thrawn's fleet into hyperspace, taking himself and Thrawn with it. His friends destroy the Imperial dome and free Lothal.

~5 ABY - The Search Begins

After the Empire falls, Sabine Wren and Ahsoka Tano set out to find him.

9 ABY - Exile on Peridea

After almost a decade stranded on the planet Peridea in another galaxy, he is reunited with Sabine and Ahsoka.

9 ABY - Homecoming

When Thrawn escapes Peridea aboard the Chimaera, Ezra stows away on the Star Destroyer and returns to the main galaxy, leaving Sabine and Ahsoka behind.

Ezra Bridger's Timeline

  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (14 years old)
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY (28 years old)

8D8, born 16 BBY

8D8 is best known as the gaunt enforcer of Jabba the Hutt's droid torture chamber, the one who pressed hot irons to the feet of a power droid while EV-9D9 sorted newly arrived R2-D2 and C-3PO into the Hutt's service. Built by the Roche Hive as an industrial smelter droid for ore-extraction work, he was lobotomized and reprogrammed for cruelty. He devoted himself to EV-9D9 and carried out her orders, keeping the palace's droid pool terrified and submissive. After Jabba's death shortly before the Battle of Endor, the palace droids were shut down, but 8D8 got a second career when Boba Fett took the throne from Bib Fortuna around 9 ABY. Reactivated as the new Daimyo's majordomo, he announced visitors paying tribute, briefed Fett on the history and uneasy alliances of the Hutt territories, identified a captured Night Wind assassin, translated for Gamorrean guards and the Aqualish crime boss Garfalaquox, and passed on news from the evasive mayor of Mos Espa. He kept pushing old Tatooine habits, such as urging Fett to torture prisoners to project strength, and Fett kept turning him down. He stayed at Fett's side through the war against the Pyke Syndicate and was with the Daimyo's party in the Sanctuary ruins when Cad Bane arrived.

Unknown - Built as a smelter droid

Manufactured by the Roche Hive as an 8D-series smelter droid for simple labor in ore-extraction facilities.

Unknown - Reprogrammed for Jabba's palace

Lobotomized and reprogrammed for sadism, then put to work in the droid torture chamber of Jabba the Hutt's palace on Tatooine, where he served EV-9D9, the chief of Cyborg Operations.

1 BBY - Torturing droids during Bossk's visit

Tortured a power droid while EV-9D9 questioned the bounty hunter Bossk about 261, a rogue Imperial astromech droid that had escaped the palace droid pool.

4 ABY - Arrival of R2-D2 and C-3PO

Branded a GNK power droid's feet with a hot iron while EV-9D9 assigned R2-D2 and C-3PO their new duties in Jabba's service.

4 ABY - Deactivated after Jabba's death

After Jabba was killed at the Great Pit of Carkoon, the palace droid pool was shut down, 8D8 included.

~9 ABY - Majordomo to Boba Fett

Reactivated after Boba Fett and Fennec Shand overthrew Bib Fortuna, and put to work as the new Daimyo's majordomo and advisor.

~9 ABY - Tribute from Mos Espa

Announced guests and collected gifts from Garfalaquox and Dokk Strassi. Watched the mayor's majordomo demand tribute instead of offering it, then urged Fett to torture two captured Gamorreans. Fett spared the Gamorreans in exchange for their loyalty.

~9 ABY - Night Wind interrogation

Identified a captured assassin as a member of the Order of the Night Wind and insisted he would never talk. Shand's rancor-pit bluff made him name Mayor Mok Shaiz.

~9 ABY - Briefing the Daimyo and Lortha Peel's petition

Briefed Fett on the territories and alliances of Jabba and Fortuna. Translated for a Gamorrean guard and sharply cut off the water-monger Lortha Peel, whose complaint about the Mods led Fett to recruit that gang.

~9 ABY - The Hutt Twins and the mayor's evasion

Announced the Hutt Twins' arrival with a peace offering. He later interrupted Fett's rancor training to report that the mayor would be unavailable for twenty days, and Fett left at once for Mos Espa city hall.

~9 ABY - Translating at the crime families' banquet

Translated for Garfalaquox at Fett's banquet, where the Mos Espa crime families agreed to stay neutral in the conflict with the Pyke Syndicate.

~9 ABY - Battle for Mos Espa

Stood watch at the ruins of the Sanctuary with Fett, Shand, Din Djarin and the Twi'lek majordomo, announced Cad Bane's arrival, and watched the majordomo go out to negotiate with the Pyke forces. His fate after the battle is not recorded.

8D8's Timeline

  • Episode VI: Return of the Jedi, 4 ABY
  • The Book of Boba Fett, 9 ABY

Snoke, born 12 BBY

Supreme Leader of the First Order and the dark side master who seduced Ben Solo into becoming Kylo Ren, Snoke believed he was a self-made seeker of forbidden Force lore. In reality he had been grown in the Sith Eternal's laboratories on Exegol, an early attempt at a vessel for Darth Sidious's spirit. Sidious rejected the body as unworthy, but after his resurrection he used Snoke as an unwitting puppet. Snoke gathered ancient knowledge in the Unknown Regions and, with the help of his navigators, guided the Imperial remnant that fled there after the Empire's collapse. He then pushed aside its old leadership and rebuilt it as the First Order, ruling from the mobile Star Dreadnought Supremacy. For years he whispered to the young Ben Solo, feeding his resentment toward his family and his uncle Luke Skywalker. After the fall of Luke's Jedi temple in 28 ABY, Snoke took Ben as his apprentice and sent him to take command of the Knights of Ren. In 34 ABY he approved the Starkiller Base strike that destroyed the New Republic's capital and began the First Order–Resistance War. Months later he was betrayed and cut in half by Kylo Ren in his throne room. His death was the final step of Sidious's plan to shape Ren into a Sith-worthy successor.

By 12 BBY - Created on Exegol

The Sith Eternal grows Snoke in the Sith Citadel's laboratory as one of its earliest attempts at a body to hold Darth Sidious's spirit. Sidious rejects him as an unworthy vessel but keeps him as a potential pawn. Several of his clone "brothers" are kept dormant in vats.

Imperial Era - Seeker of Lost Lore

Snoke becomes a master of the dark side and searches the Unknown Regions for forgotten Force knowledge, including the prophecy of the Force dyad. He visits Darth Vader's castle on Mustafar, where he finds the gold ring he later wears.

4 ABY - Witnesses the Emperor's Fall

From afar, Snoke watches the Battle of Endor, where Vader betrays Sidious to save his son. He later blames the Empire's defeat on Vader's sentiment.

~4 ABY - Becomes Sidious's Proxy

Sidious returns in a failing clone body on Exegol and begins steering Snoke from the shadows without Snoke knowing.

~5 ABY - Guides the Imperial Exodus

After the Empire's collapse, Snoke's navigators lead the Imperial remnant into the Unknown Regions. There he helps it survive and reorganize.

~5–15 ABY - Seizes the First Order

Snoke sidelines, co-opts or kills the old Imperial leadership, takes power as Supreme Leader and launches a large rearmament program.

15 ABY - Whispers to Ben Solo

As Ben Solo begins Jedi training under Luke Skywalker, Snoke starts speaking to him in his mind and wins the boy's trust.

19 ABY - Elphrona

During Ben's mission to Elphrona with Luke, Snoke encourages his grievances against his uncle and the Jedi.

28 ABY - Fall of the Jedi Temple

Snoke uses the public revelation of Vader's parentage and Luke's moment of doubt to push Ben into destroying Luke's temple. He then welcomes Ben at the Amaxine space station.

~28 ABY - Birth of Kylo Ren

Snoke vouches for Ben with the Knights of Ren. After Ben kills Ren, their leader, and takes command of the Knights, he swears loyalty to Snoke as his apprentice, Kylo Ren.

34 ABY - The Hosnian Cataclysm

Snoke approves the Starkiller Base strike that destroys the New Republic's capital. He orders Kylo Ren to bring him the scavenger Rey after the base is destroyed.

34 ABY - Death Aboard the Supremacy

While chasing the Resistance, Snoke links the minds of Rey and Kylo Ren and then captures Rey. In his throne room Ren turns Luke's lightsaber on him and cuts him in half, taking the title of Supreme Leader.

Snoke's Timeline

  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (46 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (46 years old)

Temmin Wexley, born 11 BBY

Temmin "Snap" Wexley went from teenage junk dealer to one of the most dependable wingmen in the Resistance, and he fought in the battles that ended both the Empire and the First Order. His father was taken by the Empire and his mother Norra left to fly for the Rebellion, so Temmin grew up on his own in Myrra. He ran a scrap shop out of the family home, did business with local gangsters, and rebuilt a battle droid, Mister Bones, into his fiercely loyal bodyguard. When Norra came home after Endor, the two reconciled during the uprising that freed Akiva from Imperial control. Temmin then joined her team as they hunted Imperial war criminals, learned to fly under Wedge Antilles (who later became his stepfather), and helped free Chewbacca and other prisoners from Ashmead's Lock, where he found his father again. The reunion ended badly when Brentin, controlled by an Imperial implant, took part in the Liberation Day attack on Chandrila. Temmin fought at the Battle of Jakku and went on to serve as a New Republic pilot. Decades later he joined the Resistance, flew with Black Squadron, and became a captain and recon specialist in Blue Squadron at Takodana and Starkiller Base. He married fellow pilot Karé Kun and helped rebuild the Resistance after Crait. He was killed in the Battle of Exegol, and Poe Dameron remembered him as the best wingman he ever had.

11 BBY - Birth on Akiva

Born to Norra and Brentin Lore Wexley and raised in the city of Myrra.

~5 BBY - Father arrested

Imperial forces arrest Brentin for carrying messages for the Rebel Alliance, and Temmin loses his father while still a young child.

~1 ABY - Mother joins the Rebellion

At about twelve, Temmin is left with his aunt Esmelle and her wife Shirene when Norra leaves Akiva to become a Rebel pilot. He soon moves back into the family home to live on his own.

~2 ABY - Junk dealer and Mister Bones

Turns the family house into a spare-parts shop, gets involved with the gangster Surat Nuat, and rebuilds a B1 battle droid into his bodyguard, Mister Bones.

3 ABY - Meets Kay Vess

After Surat's gang robs him, Temmin makes a deal with the outlaw Kay Vess: he fits a hydro-repulsor to her speeder in exchange for the durasteel struts she recovers from the gang.

4 ABY - Reunion with Norra

After Endor, Norra comes back to Akiva. Temmin is still bitter that she left, but he is pulled into the fight when Surat's thugs capture him and Jas Emari and Sinjir Rath Velus rescue him.

4 ABY - Rebellion on Akiva

Helps seize an Imperial communications station and broadcast a call to revolt. He secretly tries to trade his companions to Surat for safe passage, is betrayed and thrown from the Satrap's palace, then redeems himself by freeing his mother and friends aboard the Golden Harp. Akiva is liberated and joins the New Republic.

4 ABY - Joins the hunt for Imperials

Reconciles with Norra on Chandrila, joins her team to track down Imperial war criminals, and begins flight training under Wedge Antilles.

5 ABY - Capture of Perwin Gedde

Flies the getaway ship Halo on Vorlag and shoots down two pursuing TIE fighters. Wedge offers him a future place in Phantom Squadron.

5 ABY - Rescue at Ashmead's Lock

Joins Han Solo to storm Golas Aram's compound on Irudiru, then helps free Chewbacca and the other prisoners from the automated prison on Kashyyyk. Among the freed prisoners is his father Brentin.

5 ABY - Attack on Chandrila

Uncovers the Imperial agent Windom Traducier but is stunned and hidden by his implant-controlled father. On Liberation Day he confronts Brentin during the assassination attempt on Mon Mothma and stops him from turning the gun on himself.

5 ABY - Hunt for Rae Sloane

Goes with Norra's team across the galaxy looking for Grand Admiral Sloane. After the capture of Mercurial Swift, the trail leads to Jakku.

5 ABY - Battle of Jakku

Fights in the battle that ends the Galactic Empire.

~5 ABY - New Republic pilot

Joins the New Republic Defense Fleet as a starfighter pilot and later serves in the New Republic's precision air team.

~34 ABY - Joins the Resistance

Is recruited into the Resistance and flies in Poe Dameron's Black Squadron alongside Karé Kun.

34 ABY - Battle of Takodana

As a captain in Blue Squadron, flies in the Resistance strike that relieves Takodana.

34 ABY - Destruction of Starkiller Base

Briefs the Resistance on Starkiller Base's defenses and flies in the attack that destroys it.

34 ABY - Rebuilding the Resistance

Is away on a mission during the evacuation of D'Qar and Crait. Afterwards he helps rebuild the Resistance, including recruiting on Corellia.

35 ABY - Kijimi

Takes part in the Resistance operation that rescues the heroes' allies on Kijimi.

35 ABY - Death at the Battle of Exegol

Is killed while flying in the Resistance fleet against the Sith Eternal at Exegol. Poe Dameron later delivers his eulogy.

Temmin Wexley's Timeline

  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (45 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (46 years old)

Jacen Syndulla, born 1 BBY

Jacen Syndulla is the son Kanan Jarrus never met. The Jedi died saving Hera Syndulla and the Spectres shortly before Lothal was liberated, and Jacen was born later that same year. He grew up during the last years of the Galactic Civil War. By the fall of the Empire he was already part of the Ghost's crew, with the callsign Spectre 7 and the ornery astromech Chopper as his constant companion and partner in mischief. He was a natural pilot like his mother and Force-sensitive like his father, and he openly wanted to become a Jedi, just as his "Aunt Sabine" was doing under Ahsoka Tano. In the New Republic era he flew with his mother to Seatos to help Sabine and Ahsoka, and pulled the Ghost's hyperdrive lever himself on the way. There his connection to the Force let him sense Ahsoka, adrift in the World Between Worlds and dueling the memory of her old master. Jacen led the rescuers to her, then watched in wonder as she and Huyang left with a pod of purrgil.

1 BBY - Death of His Father

Before Jacen is born, the Jedi Knight Kanan Jarrus dies on Lothal saving Hera Syndulla and the Spectres from the Empire, shortly before the planet is liberated.

1 BBY - Birth on Lothal

Jacen is born on Lothal to Rebel general Hera Syndulla. He is the son of Kanan Jarrus and the grandson of the Twi'lek freedom fighter Cham Syndulla.

~4 ABY - Spectre 7

By the time the Empire is broken at Endor, the young Jacen is counted as a member of the Ghost's crew with the callsign Spectre 7. The droid Chopper becomes his close and constant companion.

5 ABY - Apart from His Mother

Jacen and Hera are separated while she fights in the last campaigns of the Galactic Civil War. At the Battle of Jakku, facing what she believes is her death, Hera thinks of her son.

~9 ABY - Wanting to Be a Jedi

Jacen goes with his mother to her hearing before a New Republic Senate committee. Chopper tells him that Sabine Wren is training under Ahsoka Tano, and afterward Jacen tells Hera he wants to be a Jedi too.

~9 ABY - Flight to Seatos

Huyang calls for help, and Jacen joins Hera and Chopper aboard the Ghost. He pulls the hyperdrive lever to send her small task force to Seatos.

~9 ABY - Finding Ahsoka

On Seatos, Jacen senses Ahsoka through the Force and hears lightsabers clashing while she is lost in the World Between Worlds. He helps Hera and Huyang pull her from the sea.

~9 ABY - Reunion and Departure

Jacen shares a warm reunion with the recovered Ahsoka. From the Ghost, he watches in awe as she and Huyang leave with a pod of purrgil to chase the Eye of Sion.

Jacen Syndulla's Timeline

  • Star Wars Rebels, 5 BBY (-4 years old)
  • Ahsoka, 9 ABY (10 years old)

Armitage Hux, born 1 BBY

Armitage Hux is best known as the young, venomous First Order general who ordered Starkiller Base to fire on the Hosnian system, wiping out the New Republic's capital and fleet in a single shot. He was born illegitimate to the Imperial academy commandant Brendol Hux and grew up scorned and beaten by his father. As the Empire collapsed he was carried into the Unknown Regions alongside Grand Admiral Rae Sloane and a band of child soldiers placed under his command, and he came of age inside the Imperial remnant that became the First Order. Driven by ambition and resentment, he quietly had his father poisoned with Captain Phasma's help, took over the program that raised stormtroopers from infancy, and became the youngest general in the First Order's High Command. His bitter rivalry with Kylo Ren shaped his career: he commanded the Finalizer during the hunt for Luke Skywalker, oversaw the destruction of the Hosnian worlds and the loss of Starkiller, then pushed the hyperspace tracking that let the fleet chase the Resistance from D'Qar toward Crait aboard the Supremacy. After Snoke's death, Ren became Supreme Leader and Hux lost most of his standing. Sidelined under Allegiant General Enric Pryde, he secretly began feeding intelligence to the Resistance, less out of conscience than a wish to see Ren lose. He even helped captured Resistance fighters escape from the Steadfast over Kijimi, until Pryde exposed him and shot him for treason.

1 BBY - Birth on Arkanis

Born on Arkanis as the illegitimate son of Commandant Brendol Hux and a kitchen worker. He never knew his mother and grew up friendless, mocked by Imperial officers and abused by his father.

5 ABY - Flight from Arkanis

As the New Republic besieges Arkanis, the bounty hunter Mercurial Swift, hired at Gallius Rax's request, pulls Armitage and Brendol off the planet before they can be captured.

5 ABY - Rax's Gift on Jakku

On Jakku, Counselor Gallius Rax places the child soldiers trained by Brendol under the boy's command and names him his father's future successor.

5 ABY - Escape into the Unknown Regions

After Rae Sloane kills Rax during the Battle of Jakku, Hux, his father and Sloane flee into uncharted space aboard the Imperialis. Sloane shields the boy from Brendol's abuse and orders his father to educate him as a proper heir.

New Republic Era - Rise to General

Grows up within the Imperial remnant that becomes the First Order and becomes the youngest general in its High Command, carrying on his father's methods of training soldiers from birth.

Cold War - Retrieval from Parnassos

Answers his father's distress call from Parnassos and brings Brendol, Phasma and Frey back to the First Order. On the way to the Absolution, he watches Brendol bombard the planet from orbit.

Cold War - Death of Brendol Hux

Conspires with Phasma to kill his father. She uses a Parnassos beetle toxin that can't be traced. Hux then takes Brendol's place, pushes aside Brendol's loyalist Captain Cardinal, and leads Project Resurrection with Phasma.

Cold War - Marooned with Kylo Ren

Survives a sabotaged shuttle crash on a forest world with his rival Kylo Ren. He tricks the old Alderaanian guard Bylsma into calling for a rescue, then marks the planet for Starkiller target practice.

Cold War - Execution of Admiral Brooks

Holds Admiral Brooks responsible for the sabotage and shoots him, paying back a lifetime of humiliation and admitting that he had his own father killed.

34 ABY - Hunt for the Map to Skywalker

Commands the Finalizer during the attack on Tuanul and the search for BB-8. Poe Dameron and the defecting FN-2187 escape from his ship.

34 ABY - Hosnian Cataclysm

Gives a fiery speech to the assembled troops on Starkiller Base and orders the superweapon to fire, destroying the Hosnian system and the New Republic's leadership.

34 ABY - Fall of Starkiller Base

Evacuates as the Resistance destroys Starkiller Base and follows Snoke's order to bring back the wounded Kylo Ren.

34 ABY - Battle of D'Qar

Leads the fleet against the Resistance base on D'Qar but loses a Siege Dreadnought to Poe Dameron and the Resistance bombers. Snoke punishes him through the Force, and Hux answers with his plan for hyperspace tracking.

34 ABY - Pursuit of the Resistance Fleet

From the Supremacy, directs the slow chase of the Resistance fleet and captures Finn and Rose Tico. The Holdo maneuver cripples the Supremacy, and Snoke is found dead.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

Bows to Kylo Ren as the new Supreme Leader and follows him into the attack on the Resistance outpost on Crait, from which the survivors escape.

35 ABY - Supreme Council and Demotion

Sits on the First Order Supreme Council but loses influence and serves under Allegiant General Enric Pryde aboard the Steadfast.

35 ABY - Resistance Spy

Secretly leaks intelligence to the Resistance, including word of the Sith Eternal's fleet on Exegol, hoping to see Kylo Ren lose.

35 ABY - Execution Aboard the Steadfast

After helping Finn, Poe Dameron and Chewbacca escape from the Steadfast above Kijimi, he reveals that he was the spy. Pryde shoots him dead for treason.

Armitage Hux's Timeline

  • Star Wars Resistance, 34 ABY (35 years old)
  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (35 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (35 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (36 years old)

Zorii Bliss, born 0 BBY

Hidden behind a bronzium-finished helmet and a voice-masking vocoder, Zorii Bliss was the feared leader of the Spice Runners of Kijimi and one of the few people who knew Poe Dameron's past as a criminal. She grew up in the smuggling outfit her mother Zeva ran and fell for Poe when he joined as a teenager. Their relationship fell apart at the Kijimi summit, when he turned down her offer to take the gang over together and walked away. Zorii went on to take control of the Spice Runners herself. Years later, with the First Order occupying Kijimi, she was saving up to escape on a Y-wing she named the Comeuppance. When Poe came back with Rey, Finn, Chewbacca and C-3PO in search of the way to Exegol, she met him at blaster point. Rey's fighting skill won her over, and she took the group to the droidsmith Babu Frik and gave Poe a captain's medallion so he could board a Star Destroyer. She turned down his offer to join the Resistance, but she escaped Kijimi before a Xyston-class Star Destroyer destroyed the planet. She then flew the Comeuppance with Babu Frik at the Battle of Exegol and celebrated the victory on Ajan Kloss, sharing a knowing look with Poe across the crowd.

~0 BBY - Birth on Kijimi

Zorii is born into the Bliss family, sometime between 1 BBY and 1 ABY. Her mother Zeva leads the Spice Runners of Kijimi, taking over from Zarah Bliss.

~5 ABY - Joins the Spice Runners

While still young, Zorii joins her mother's spice-running crew on Kijimi.

~18 ABY - Meets Poe Dameron

Teenage pilot Poe Dameron joins the Spice Runners. He and Zorii start a romance.

~18 ABY - The Kijimi Summit

Zeva plans to wipe out rival families at a fake summit. Zorii, who also opposes her mother, asks Poe to take over the gang with her. He says no and leaves Kijimi, and her feelings for him turn to fury. She tells him never to come back.

Before 34 ABY - Leader of the Spice Runners

Zorii takes command of the Spice Runners and becomes feared by her enemies and respected by her crew.

~34 ABY - First Order Occupation

After the Battle of Crait, the First Order takes over Kijimi. Tired of the suffering she sees everywhere, Zorii buys a BTA-NR2 Y-wing for about 40,000 credits and names it the Comeuppance, planning to leave.

35 ABY - Reunion with Poe

Poe comes back to Kijimi with Resistance agents looking for Exegol. Zorii and her goons confront him, but Rey's skill impresses her and she agrees to help.

35 ABY - Babu Frik's Workshop

Zorii takes the group to the droidsmith Babu Frik, who unlocks the forbidden Sith translation in C-3PO's memory. On the rooftop she talks with Poe about the occupation, encourages him to keep fighting and briefly lifts part of her helmet.

35 ABY - The Captain's Medallion

Zorii warns the group that First Order troops are coming and gives Poe a captain's medallion, at great cost to herself, so he can get aboard the Steadfast. She turns down his offer to come with them and stays on Kijimi.

35 ABY - Escape from Kijimi

Darth Sidious orders Kijimi destroyed by a Xyston-class Star Destroyer's axial superlaser. Zorii gets away before the planet is obliterated.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Zorii flies the Comeuppance with Babu Frik alongside the Resistance and the Citizens' Fleet against the Sith Eternal's Final Order fleet.

35 ABY - Victory on Ajan Kloss

Zorii joins the Resistance's victory celebration and shares a look with Poe across the crowd.

Zorii Bliss's Timeline

  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY

Poe Dameron, born 2 ABY

"

Poe Dameron is best known as the Resistance's top starfighter pilot. He flew the black X-wing Black One and eventually took over from Leia Organa as the movement's commander. He was born just after the Battle of Yavin to two Rebel veterans, the A-wing pilot Shara Bey and the Pathfinder Kes Dameron. His mother taught him to fly in her lap and died when he was eight. As a restless teenager he ran off with the Spice Runners of Kijimi, where he met Zorii Bliss, but he walked away from the criminal life after its violence nearly killed him. He later became a New Republic pilot, trained under Wedge Antilles and led Rapier Squadron. Frustrated that the Republic ignored the First Order's growing aggression, he joined Leia Organa's Resistance and became one of her most trusted agents. He founded Black Squadron and went looking for Lor San Tekka's clue to where Luke Skywalker was. On Jakku he was captured and tortured by Kylo Ren, then escaped with the defecting stormtrooper he named Finn. He fired the shots that destroyed Starkiller Base's weapon. His recklessness at D'Qar got him demoted, and his mutiny against Vice Admiral Holdo backfired, but he learned from both and led the survivors out of Crait. After Leia died he became acting general alongside Finn. At Exegol he rallied a galaxy-wide citizens' fleet that broke the Sith Eternal armada and helped end the First Order.

"

"

1 BBY - A Voice in the World Between Worlds

Years before he is born, Poe's voice is among the echoes Ezra Bridger hears in the World Between Worlds.

2 ABY - Birth on Yavin 4

Born to Rebel pilot Shara Bey and Pathfinder Kes Dameron. While his parents fight the Empire, his maternal grandfather looks after him.

~5 ABY - Family Reunited

Six months after Endor, his parents leave Rebel service and settle on Yavin 4. They plant a Force-sensitive tree next to their homestead.

8 ABY - First Flights

At age six, Poe learns to fly sitting in his mother's lap in her old RZ-1 A-wing.

~10 ABY - Death of Shara Bey

His mother dies unexpectedly when he is eight. Family friend L'ulo L'ampar helps raise him.

18 ABY - Joining the Spice Runners

After wrecking his mother's A-wing on a joyride and arguing with his father, Poe agrees to fly for Zorii Wynn's crew. They turn out to be the Spice Runners of Kijimi. He learns hyperspace skipping from Tomasso.

~19 ABY - Breaking with the Spice Runners

Poe wrecks Zeva Bliss's plot at the Kijimi summit and is badly wounded. He turns down Zorii's offer to rule the gang with her and makes his way home.

~20s ABY - New Republic Pilot

Joins the New Republic Defense Fleet, trains under Wedge Antilles, becomes a commander and leads Rapier Squadron with BB-8.

~32 ABY - Suraz Engagement

Rapier Squadron answers a distress call from the Yissira Zyde and finds First Order TIE fighters attacking it. Poe then scouts a First Order staging point on his own.

~32 ABY - Joining the Resistance

Leia Organa recruits Poe, Karé Kun and Iolo Arana into the Resistance. He goes on to lead its starfighter squadrons as Black Leader in Black One.

~33 ABY - Operation: Sabre Strike

Steals navicomputer data from Senator Erudo Ro-Kiintor's ship. The data reveals that the First Order is hunting Lor San Tekka.

34 ABY - Black Squadron and the Hunt for Tekka

Forms Black Squadron and trades blows with Agent Terex on Ovanis, Megalox Beta and Kaddak. He eventually exposes Oddy Muva as the squadron's mole.

34 ABY - Assignments with Kazuda Xiono

Recruits Kazuda Xiono as a Resistance spy on the Colossus and checks in on his missions.

34 ABY - Captured on Jakku

Receives part of the map to Luke Skywalker from Lor San Tekka and hides it in BB-8. Kylo Ren captures him and pulls the information from his mind.

34 ABY - Escape with Finn

Escapes the Finalizer with a defecting stormtrooper he names Finn. They crash on Jakku, and Poe makes his own way back to D'Qar.

34 ABY - Takodana and Starkiller Base

Leads the Resistance air strike at Takodana. At Starkiller Base he fires the shots that destroy the superweapon.

34 ABY - Battle of D'Qar and Demotion

Disobeys orders to destroy the dreadnought Fulminatrix. The victory costs the bomber fleet, and Leia demotes him to captain.

34 ABY - Mutiny Against Holdo

Sends Finn and Rose Tico to disable the hyperspace tracker and leads a mutiny against Vice Admiral Holdo. Leia stuns him to end it.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

Leads the failed ski-speeder counterattack. He then realizes Luke is buying them time and guides the survivors to the Millennium Falcon.

34 ABY - Restored to Commander

Promoted back to commander. He rebuilds the Resistance and recruits new allies across the galaxy.

35 ABY - Hunt for the Wayfinder

Brings word of Palpatine's return. He searches for the Sith wayfinder with Rey, Finn and Chewbacca on Pasaana, Kijimi, the Steadfast and Kef Bir, and reunites with Zorii Bliss along the way.

35 ABY - Acting General

After Leia dies he becomes acting general of the Resistance, with Leia's blessing, and names Finn co-general.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Leads the attack on the Final Order. Lando Calrissian arrives with the Citizens' Fleet, and together they destroy the Sith fleet and spark a galaxy-wide uprising against the First Order.

35 ABY - Victory Celebration

Celebrates the victory on Ajan Kloss with Finn, Rey, Zorii and the surviving Resistance.

"

Poe Dameron's Timeline

Ben Solo, born 5 ABY

Heir to both the Skywalker legacy and the Solo name, Ben Solo is best known as Kylo Ren, the masked "Jedi Killer" who turned against his family and rose to rule the First Order. The son of Han Solo and Leia Organa, he was born on the day the Galactic Civil War formally ended. He grew up lonely while his parents were busy with their careers, and from childhood Snoke whispered to him in his mind, a voice that was secretly a tool of the resurrected Darth Sidious. At about ten he became his uncle Luke Skywalker's first and most gifted Padawan, and Luke expected him to help lead a new Jedi Order. In 28 ABY he learned from a public news broadcast that his grandfather was Darth Vader. Soon after, Luke briefly considered killing him in his sleep. Ben brought the temple down, abandoned the Jedi, killed the leader of the Knights of Ren to take his place, and became Snoke's enforcer. When the First Order went to war in 34 ABY, he killed his father on Starkiller Base, formed a Force bond with Rey, murdered Snoke to become Supreme Leader, and faced Luke on Crait. A year later Sidious offered to make him the new Emperor if he killed Rey. Instead, his mother's final act and the memory of his father drew him back to the light. As Ben Solo he fought beside Rey on Exegol and gave his life to bring her back, ending the Skywalker bloodline and becoming one with the Force.

5 ABY - Birth on Chandrila

Born in Hanna City to Leia Organa and Han Solo, one year and four days after the Battle of Endor, on the same day the Galactic Concordance ended the Galactic Civil War. Han named him after "Ben" Kenobi.

~7 ABY - A Near Miss in the Nursery

When he was a toddler, the family's culinary droid BX-778 was corrupted by a virus and nearly killed him. Han stopped it by destroying the Phylanx Redux Transmitter.

~5–15 ABY - A Troubled Childhood

His Force powers surfaced early, often in bursts of anger. With his parents frequently away he grew lonely, and Snoke's voice, secretly an instrument of Darth Sidious, began whispering to him.

15 ABY - Padawan of Luke Skywalker

Shortly before his tenth birthday he began Jedi training under his uncle Luke and became the first student of the new Jedi Order.

~16 ABY - The New Jedi Temple

Other students, including Voe, Hennix and Tai, joined him at Luke's temple on Ossus. Ben outpaced them all in the Force and with the lightsaber, and he built a blue-bladed lightsaber of his own.

~19 ABY - Mission to Elphrona

He went with Luke and Lor San Tekka to an ancient Jedi outpost, where they clashed with the Knights of Ren. Their leader, Ren, sensed the darkness in Ben and invited him to seek the Knights out.

21 ABY - Senior Student

By now one of Luke's senior pupils, he supervised the younger students' lightsaber training while Luke travelled with Lor San Tekka.

28 ABY - The Vader Revelation

At twenty-three he learned from a public broadcast that Darth Vader was his grandfather. He felt betrayed by his family's secrecy, and Snoke used that anger against him.

28 ABY - Fall of the Jedi Temple

Sensing Snoke's hold on his nephew, Luke briefly ignited his lightsaber over Ben as he slept. Ben fought back and brought the temple down, then fled with several students as the temple was destroyed.

~28 ABY - Becoming Kylo Ren

He killed Ren on Mimban to take command of the Knights of Ren, took the name Kylo Ren, and became Snoke's apprentice and the First Order's enforcer.

34 ABY - Massacre at Tuanul

He hunted for the map to Luke Skywalker on Jakku, killed Lor San Tekka, and captured Poe Dameron.

34 ABY - Takodana and Starkiller Base

He captured Rey at Maz Kanata's castle and killed his father, Han Solo, on Starkiller Base. Rey then defeated and scarred him in a lightsaber duel.

34 ABY - The Force Bond

Snoke opened a Force connection between Ben and Rey. Through it, Ben learned the truth about the night Luke's temple fell.

34 ABY - Supreme Leader

Aboard the Supremacy he cut Snoke in half and fought beside Rey against the Praetorian Guard. He then took the title of Supreme Leader of the First Order.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

He led the assault on the Resistance's last base and dueled Luke, only to discover he was fighting a Force projection while the Resistance escaped.

35 ABY - Summoned to Exegol

Hunting the source of the Emperor's broadcast, he found Sidious's wayfinder on Mustafar and travelled to Exegol. Sidious promised him an empire if he killed Rey. He had his broken helmet reforged and hunted her across Pasaana and Kijimi.

35 ABY - Redemption on Kef Bir

He dueled Rey in the wreckage of the second Death Star. Leia reached him with her last strength through the Force, and Rey healed his wound. A memory of his father then led him to cast his lightsaber away and become Ben Solo again.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol and Death

With Luke's lightsaber he fought beside Rey against the Knights of Ren and Sidious. After Sidious was destroyed, he gave his life force to bring Rey back to life, shared a kiss with her, and became one with the Force.

Ben Solo's Timeline

  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (29 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (29 years old)
  • Galaxy's Edge, 34 ABY (29 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (30 years old)

Phasma, born 6 ABY

Phasma was the chrome-armored enforcer of the First Order's stormtrooper corps, a survivor who treated loyalty as a tool and betrayal as a way to climb. She grew up on a ruined, radiation-scarred world and, as a teenager, became war leader of the Scyre clan alongside her brother Keldo, whom she had already crippled while arranging the deaths of their parents. When General Brendol Hux crash-landed near her people, she saw her way off-planet. She guided him across the wastes, abandoned her clan, and killed Keldo when he came after her. In the First Order she rose quickly to captain and took over training its adult recruits. She forged her armor from the plating of Palpatine's old yacht. Working with Armitage Hux, she poisoned her patron Brendol, silenced the young recruit Frey, and left her rival Cardinal for dead, which secured her a place beside Hux and Kylo Ren at the top of the Order. She led the massacre at Tuanul on Jakku that drove FN-2187 to defect. When he returned to Starkiller Base with Han Solo and Chewbacca, they forced her at gunpoint to lower the base's shields. She survived its destruction and framed another officer for the breach. Months later, aboard the crippled Supremacy, she faced the renegade again, now called Finn. He beat her, and she fell into the flames consuming the ship.

6 ABY - Birth on Parnassos

Born into a small, struggling clan living around the Nautilus on the devastated world of Parnassos, with her older brother Keldo.

~22 ABY - Betrayal of Her Family

Conspires with the rival Scyre clan to have her parents and family killed. She stabs Keldo to keep him out of the fight, and the two siblings are taken into the Scyre.

~22 ABY - Co-Leader of the Scyre

At sixteen, defeats the usurper Porr in combat after the Scyre leader Egil is mortally wounded. She becomes the clan's military leader, while Keldo leads it politically.

~24 ABY - War with the Claws

Drives off a night raid by Balder's Claws. Keldo then pushes through a truce against her wishes, and the siblings become enemies.

~24 ABY - Meeting Brendol Hux

Finds the stranded First Order general Brendol Hux among the Claws, kills Balder, and offers to guide Hux back to his downed ship.

~24 ABY - Trek Across Parnassos

Steals her clan's supplies and crosses the wastes with Hux, his stormtroopers and her warriors. On the way they survive venomous beetles, forced labor at Terpsichore Station and the arena of the Arratu, where she kills the Arratu and his jailer Vrod.

~24 ABY - Killing Keldo

At Hux's ship in the irradiated dead lands, fights off Keldo's combined Scyre and Claw force, then shoots her brother dead. She takes the child Frey with her and leaves Siv behind.

~24 ABY - Joining the First Order

Picked up by Armitage Hux. From orbit, Brendol destroys her former home to show the First Order's power. She begins training aboard the Star Destroyer Absolution as Brendol's new protégé and bodyguard.

~25 ABY - Captain and Chrome Armor

Makes captain within a year and returns to Parnassos to strip chromium plating from Palpatine's old yacht, forging her signature armor at Cleo Station.

~34 ABY - Murder of Brendol Hux

Poisons her patron with a Parnassos beetle at Armitage Hux's direction. She also arranges the death of the cadet Frey to bury her past.

34 ABY - Duel with Cardinal

Her rival Cardinal learns her history from the captured spy Vi Moradi and attacks her. She defeats him and leaves him for dead, taking full control of stormtrooper training.

34 ABY - Handling Agent Terex

Directs Agent Terex's hunt for Poe Dameron and struggles to rein him in when he goes rogue.

34 ABY - Massacre at Tuanul

Leads stormtroopers in the attack on the Jakku village of Tuanul while searching for the map to Luke Skywalker. She notices FN-2187's hesitation.

34 ABY - Hosnian Cataclysm

Commands the troops at Starkiller Base as the superweapon destroys the Hosnian system.

34 ABY - Captured at Starkiller Base

Finn, Han Solo and Chewbacca ambush her and force her to lower the base's shields. She is dumped in a trash compactor but survives the base's destruction.

34 ABY - Framing Sol Rivas

Pins the shield failure on Lieutenant Sol Rivas and leads the hunt for him after he discovers the truth.

34 ABY - Death Aboard the Supremacy

After the Holdo maneuver wrecks the Supremacy, she duels Finn in the hangar. He defeats her, and she falls to her death in the fires consuming the ship.

Phasma's Timeline

  • Star Wars Resistance, 34 ABY (28 years old)
  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (28 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (28 years old)

Rose Tico, born 11 ABY

Rose Tico is best known as the Resistance mechanic who teamed up with Finn to try to stop the First Order from tracking the fleeing Resistance through hyperspace, and for her line that the war would be won by saving what they love, not fighting what they hate. She and her older sister Paige fought the First Order's exploitation of their mining homeworld and joined the Resistance after it. The First Order later wiped out their people. Rose worked on the ground crew as a quiet, book-smart engineer while Paige flew as a bomber gunner. After Paige died destroying a Dreadnought during the evacuation of D'Qar, Rose caught Finn trying to leave the Raddus, then went with him and BB-8 on a mission to Canto Bight to find a codebreaker. The slicer DJ betrayed them aboard the Supremacy, and they barely escaped execution. At Crait she wrecked her own ski speeder to stop Finn's suicide run, and was badly hurt doing it. Over the next year she helped rebuild the Resistance, including missions to Mon Cala and Minfar. Leia Organa promoted her to commander of the Engineering Corps, where she worked on ways to beat hyperspace tracking. During the final campaign against the returned Emperor she studied Star Destroyer specifications and joined the ground team that boarded the Steadfast at Exegol, and she was at the victory celebration on Ajan Kloss afterward.

~11 ABY - Birth on Hays Minor

Rose is born to Hue and Thanya Tico on Hays Minor, a poor mining world in the Otomok system, a few years after her sister Paige.

~20 ABY - Childhood with Paige

The sisters grow up flying their grandmother Etta's Z-95 simulator and watching fathier races. Their mother tells them that whatever their world lacks, they will always have the sky.

~33 ABY - Resisting the First Order on Hays Minor

The First Order takes over the planet's mines. Rose blows up twelve OreDiggers and stops mining for three days. Her parents urge both daughters to go and find the Resistance.

~33 ABY - Joining the Resistance

Admirals Ackbar and Holdo welcome Rose and Paige into the Resistance, and they join Cobalt Squadron, Rose as a mechanic and Paige as a gunner. When they learn the First Order has wiped out their people, Leia Organa offers her condolences and the sisters commit themselves to the cause.

~34 ABY - Rose's trucks on D'Qar

Rose builds wheeled trucks from the D'Qar junk heap. On a test drive she rescues Paige from a ravine, and together they find valuable durasteel ore.

34 ABY - Atterra mission

Rose and Paige fly aboard the bomber Cobalt Hammer on a fact-finding mission to learn what the First Order is doing to the settlements in the Atterra system.

34 ABY - Freeing the Raddus hangar

After studying the Raddus's manuals, Rose finds a Calamarian reboot code that opens a jammed hangar door, so X-wings can launch to help Poe Dameron.

34 ABY - Battle of D'Qar and Paige's death

While the Resistance evacuates D'Qar, Paige gives her life to destroy the Dreadnought Fulminatrix. Rose is devastated.

34 ABY - Meeting Finn

While guarding the escape pods during the chase through the Oetchi system, Rose stuns Finn as he tries to leave. Together they work out a plan to disable the First Order's hyperspace tracker aboard the Supremacy.

34 ABY - Mission to Canto Bight

Rose, Finn and BB-8 look for the Master Codebreaker, are arrested, and escape with the help of the slicer DJ. They free the abused fathiers, and Rose gives her Resistance ring to the stable boy Temiri Blagg.

34 ABY - Captured aboard the Supremacy

DJ betrays the group once they are inside the flagship. Rose bites General Hux, and she and Finn are saved from execution when Holdo rams the fleet. They escape to Crait in a stolen shuttle.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

Rose flies a ski speeder against the siege cannon and crashes into Finn's speeder to stop his suicide run. She kisses him before passing out, and escapes unconscious aboard the Millennium Falcon.

34 ABY - Recovery and the Attack on Ryloth

Rose recovers aboard the Falcon, and she and Finn agree to stay friends. When the Resistance's hideout on Ryloth is betrayed, she helps coordinate the escape.

34 ABY - Mission to Mon Cala

Rose repairs the Falcon with Leia and goes with her to Mon Cala. She intervenes in Rey's rite of challenge, chases down a Quarren saboteur, and sees the Mon Calamari join the fight.

34 ABY - Mission to Minfar

Rose names the Falcon's porgs. After a crash landing on Minfar, she helps free the Zixon from the First Order's Echo Horn.

~35 ABY - Commander of the Engineering Corps

Leia promotes Rose to lead the Engineering Corps, where she develops technology to counter hyperspace tracking and First Order jamming.

35 ABY - News of Palpatine's return

On Ajan Kloss, Rose hears the decoded spy message about Darth Sidious and the Final Order. She stays behind to study the specifications of old Imperial Star Destroyers.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Rose serves on the landing team that boards the Steadfast, and reluctantly leaves Finn and Jannah behind to take down the ship's navigation transmitter.

35 ABY - Victory celebration

Rose celebrates the Resistance's victory on Ajan Kloss and gets a big hug from Chewbacca.

Rose Tico's Timeline

  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (23 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (24 years old)

Finn, born 11 ABY

Finn is the stormtrooper who walked away. Taken from his family as a toddler and raised as FN-2187, a model cadet in Captain Phasma's FN Corps, he broke on his first combat mission when he refused to fire during the massacre at Tuanul on Jakku. He freed the captured pilot Poe Dameron, who gave him the name Finn, and escaped. Stranded on Jakku, he fell in with Rey and BB-8, fled aboard the Millennium Falcon and ended up at the side of Han Solo and Chewbacca. For a while he wanted only to run from the war. Even so, his inside knowledge helped the Resistance destroy Starkiller Base, and he took up the Skywalker lightsaber against Kylo Ren there and nearly died. After waking from a coma, he joined Rose Tico on a desperate mission to Canto Bight and the Supremacy, where he defeated Phasma. By the Battle of Crait he was ready to give his life for the cause. A year later he joined Rey's search for the Sith wayfinder. He began to feel the Force, led a rising of ex-stormtroopers on Kef Bir, and, newly promoted to general alongside Poe after Leia's death, led the ground assault at the Battle of Exegol that helped end the Final Order.

1 BBY - An Echo Beyond Time

Years before Finn is born, Ezra Bridger hears Rey's voice in the World Between Worlds promising Finn that they will meet again.

11 ABY - Birth

The boy who will become Finn is born during the New Republic era. He never learns who his family were.

~14 ABY - Taken by the First Order

At about three years old he is taken from his family, given the designation FN-2187 and raised to be a stormtrooper.

~14–34 ABY - Cadet in the FN Corps

He trains with Batch Eight and then the FN Corps under Captain Phasma alongside Nines, Zeroes and Slip. He scores top marks, but officers notice he keeps going back for weaker squadmates. He also pulls sanitation duty on Starkiller Base and aboard Star Destroyers.

34 ABY - Pressy's Tumble

Sent to break a miners' strike in the Pressylla system, he cannot bring himself to execute a captive negotiator. Afterwards his unit is made full stormtroopers.

34 ABY - Massacre at Tuanul

On his first combat mission on Jakku, he watches his friend Slip die and refuses Kylo Ren's order to fire on the villagers.

34 ABY - Desertion and a New Name

Facing reconditioning, he frees Poe Dameron and they escape the Finalizer in a stolen TIE fighter. Poe names him Finn before they crash in the Goazon Badlands.

34 ABY - Rey, BB-8 and the Falcon

At Niima Outpost he meets Rey and BB-8, pretends to be with the Resistance, and escapes Jakku with them aboard the Millennium Falcon. Han Solo and Chewbacca then take them aboard, and they survive the rathtars on the Eravana.

34 ABY - Battle of Takodana

At Maz Kanata's castle he confesses his past and plans to run with Sidon Ithano's pirates. Then he sees the Hosnian Cataclysm, picks up the Skywalker lightsaber, fights his former squadmate Nines, and watches Kylo Ren take Rey away.

34 ABY - Battle of Starkiller Base

He helps plan the attack on the base, forces Phasma to lower its shields, and reunites with Rey. Then he sees Han killed and duels Kylo Ren with the lightsaber, suffering terrible wounds that leave him in a coma.

34 ABY - Awakening on the Raddus

He wakes during the evacuation of D'Qar and tries to slip away to protect Rey, but Rose Tico catches him. Together they plan to disable the First Order's hyperspace tracker.

34 ABY - Mission to Canto Bight

On Cantonica he sees how war profiteers live and is changed by it. He and Rose free the fathiers and recruit DJ to get them aboard the Supremacy.

34 ABY - Duel with Phasma

Betrayed by DJ and nearly executed, he escapes when the Supremacy is rammed, then defeats Captain Phasma in the hangar.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

He leads the ski speeder attack on the siege cannon and tries to sacrifice himself, but Rose saves him. He escapes with the Resistance survivors on the Falcon.

35 ABY - Quest for the Wayfinder

After Palpatine's return is announced, he joins Rey, Poe, Chewbacca, BB-8 and C-3PO in the search for the Sith wayfinder across Pasaana, Kijimi and the Death Star wreck on Kef Bir. He starts sensing the Force.

35 ABY - Meeting Jannah

On Kef Bir he meets Jannah and her band of ex-stormtroopers, who, like him, deserted rather than kill.

35 ABY - Promoted to General

After Leia's death, Poe makes Finn a general alongside himself.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

He leads the ground forces against the Sith fleet's navigation tower, destroys a Xyston-class Star Destroyer from its deck, and helps secure victory over the Final Order.

35 ABY - Victory Celebration

He reunites with Rey, Poe and their allies on Ajan Kloss as the galaxy rises up against the First Order.

Finn's Timeline

  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (23 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (23 years old)
  • Galaxy's Edge, 34 ABY (23 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (24 years old)

Kaydel Ko Connix, born 13 ABY

Kaydel Ko Connix is best known as one of the steady voices in the Resistance command center, relaying orders between Leia Organa's staff and the pilots in the thick of the First Order–Resistance War. She served as an operations controller in Fleet Command at the D'Qar base. After coordinating communications during the attack on Starkiller Base, she was promoted to lieutenant. Her first job at that rank was running the evacuation of D'Qar, and she got off the planet on one of the last shuttles before the Dreadnought Fulminatrix destroyed the base. During the Resistance fleet's flight, she helped Poe Dameron hide Finn and Rose Tico's secret departure and then joined his mutiny against Vice Admiral Holdo, until Leia stunned Poe and Connix surrendered. She survived the near destruction of the Resistance on Crait and escaped with the last survivors aboard the Millennium Falcon. Leia forgave her and let her keep her rank. Connix went on to help build the new base on Ajan Kloss. At the Battle of Exegol she joined the boarding team that struck the Star Destroyer Steadfast's navigation tower, where she was wounded in the leg, and she lived to celebrate the First Order's defeat.

~13 ABY - Birth on Dulathia

Kaydel Ko Connix is born on Dulathia during the New Republic era.

~28–34 ABY - Joins the Resistance

She joins the naval branch of Leia Organa's Resistance and is assigned to Fleet Command at the D'Qar base.

34 ABY - Operations controller on D'Qar

She serves as an operations controller directly under General Organa as tensions with the First Order boil over.

34 ABY - Battle of Starkiller Base

From the D'Qar command center, she coordinates communications between the Resistance pilots and their commanders during the attack that destroys Starkiller Base.

34 ABY - Promotion to lieutenant

She is promoted to lieutenant for her service during the Starkiller crisis.

34 ABY - Evacuation of D'Qar

She oversees the evacuation of the base, putting supplies first and organizing the retreat. She escapes on one of the last shuttles before the Dreadnought Fulminatrix destroys the base.

34 ABY - Covering the Canto Bight mission

Aboard the Raddus, she helps Poe Dameron keep Finn and Rose Tico's unauthorized departure hidden from Vice Admiral Holdo.

34 ABY - Mutiny against Holdo

She joins Dameron's mutiny and helps seize the Raddus's bridge. Leia Organa stuns Dameron, and Connix surrenders.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

She fights with the Resistance at the old Rebel outpost on Crait and escapes with the few survivors aboard the Millennium Falcon.

34–35 ABY - Regaining Leia's trust

Leia forgives her and she keeps her rank. At the Resistance camp on Anoat, she reports the destruction of Tah'Nuhna and later warns General Jonato that a First Order armada is heading for Mon Cala.

35 ABY - Building the Ajan Kloss base

She helps Rose Tico and Beaumont Kin set up the Resistance's new jungle base on Ajan Kloss.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

She joins Tico and Kin in the assault on the Steadfast's navigation tower. She is wounded in the leg, and Kin helps her back to the Fortitude.

35 ABY - Victory celebration

She celebrates the First Order's defeat with Tico and Kin at the Ajan Kloss base.

Kaydel Ko Connix's Timeline

  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (21 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (21 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (22 years old)

Rey Skywalker, born 15 ABY

Rey rose from a lonely scavenger's life in the Starship Graveyard to become the Jedi who ended Palpatine's return. Left behind as a child by parents who died protecting her from the Sith, she grew up salvaging wrecks for Unkar Plutt and waiting for a family that never came back. That changed in 34 ABY, when she rescued the droid BB-8 and fled aboard the Millennium Falcon with the defector Finn. She joined Han Solo and Chewbacca, awakened to the Force, and beat Kylo Ren in a duel on Starkiller Base. She then found Luke Skywalker on Ahch-To and pushed the reluctant exile to train her. Through a Force bond with Ben Solo she learned the two of them were a dyad. She killed Snoke alongside him, but after he refused to turn back to the light, she helped the last of the Resistance escape Crait and carried on her training under Leia Organa. A year later she learned she was Palpatine's granddaughter. On Exegol, with Ben's help and the voices of past Jedi, she destroyed the Emperor but died in the effort, and Ben gave his own life to revive her. Afterward she buried the lightsabers of Luke and Leia on Tatooine and took the Skywalker name as her own.

1 BBY - A Voice Across Time

Years before she is born, Ezra Bridger hears Rey's voice in the World Between Worlds.

15 ABY - Birth on Hyperkarn

Born to Dathan, a Strand-Cast clone of Darth Sidious, and his wife Miramir. Her parents go into hiding as junk traders to keep her from the Emperor's agents.

~21 ABY - Left on Jakku

Her parents leave her with Unkar Plutt at Niima Outpost. Ochi, the Sith assassin, catches and kills them. Rey grows up alone, still waiting for them to return.

~21 ABY - The Scavenger of Niima Outpost

Rey salvages parts from the Starship Graveyard in exchange for food rations and makes her home inside the fallen AT-AT Hellhound Two.

~25 ABY - The Rebel Pilot's Helmet

At about ten, she finds Dosmit Ræh's Rebel flight helmet and makes up stories about the pilot's adventures. She also teaches herself to fly on a salvaged simulator.

28 ABY - A Chill in the Force

Rey feels a cold sensation as Ben Solo falls to the dark side, without understanding what it is.

28–34 ABY - Life on Jakku

She repairs a Ghtroc 690 freighter, only for Devi and Strunk to steal it. She also rescues Unkar Plutt from Zool Zendiat's gang and keeps working as Niima Outpost's best scavenger.

34 ABY - Meeting BB-8 and Finn

She rescues BB-8 from Teedo and turns down Plutt's offer to buy the droid. With Finn, she escapes the First Order aboard the Millennium Falcon.

34 ABY - Han Solo and Chewbacca

Han Solo and Chewbacca take in the fugitives. Rey helps them get free of the Guavian Death Gang and Kanjiklub, and Han offers her a job on the Falcon.

34 ABY - Maz Kanata's Castle

On Takodana, Luke Skywalker's old lightsaber calls to Rey and gives her a Force vision. Kylo Ren captures her during the First Order attack.

34 ABY - Starkiller Base

She resists Kylo Ren's mind probe, uses a Jedi mind trick to escape, and sees Han die. She then defeats Kylo Ren in a lightsaber duel as the base collapses.

34 ABY - Finding Luke Skywalker

Using the completed map, Rey flies with Chewbacca and R2-D2 to Ahch-To and offers Luke his lightsaber.

34 ABY - Training on Ahch-To

Luke reluctantly teaches her about the Force. A Force bond forms between Rey and Kylo Ren, and she is drawn into the dark side cave.

34 ABY - Snoke's Throne Room

Rey goes to the Supremacy to turn Ben Solo back to the light. They kill Snoke and his Praetorian Guards together, but Ben will not come with her, and Luke's lightsaber is broken in two.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

Rey clears the rocks blocking the survivors' escape and saves the last of the Resistance. She takes the Jedi texts with her.

34–35 ABY - Apprentice to Leia

Leia Organa trains Rey as a Jedi while the Resistance rebuilds.

35 ABY - Search for Exegol

When Palpatine returns, Rey follows Ochi's trail to Pasaana, Kijimi, and Kef Bir. There she learns she is Palpatine's granddaughter and nearly kills Kylo Ren before healing him.

35 ABY - Exile on Ahch-To

Afraid of her heritage, Rey tries to exile herself. Luke's spirit convinces her to face Palpatine, and she takes Luke's X-wing to Exegol.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

Rey and a redeemed Ben Solo face Palpatine together. With strength from past Jedi, she turns his lightning back on him and destroys him, dying in the process.

35 ABY - Resurrection

Ben Solo gives his life force to revive Rey. They kiss before he becomes one with the Force.

35 ABY - Rey Skywalker

On Tatooine, Rey buries Luke's and Leia's lightsabers at the Lars homestead, shows her own yellow-bladed saber, and takes the name Skywalker.

After 35 ABY - Jedi Master

At some later point, Rey becomes a Jedi Master.

Rey Skywalker's Timeline

  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (19 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (19 years old)
  • Galaxy's Edge, 34 ABY (19 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (20 years old)

BB-8, born 29 ABY

BB-8 is the rolling astromech whose flight across the Jakku desert, carrying a fragment of the map to Luke Skywalker, set the war between the Resistance and the First Order in motion. Built in 29 ABY, he became Poe Dameron's copilot and friend in Rapier Squadron, then followed Poe into Leia Organa's Resistance, flying with Black Squadron in the hunt for Lor San Tekka and repeatedly outsmarting the First Order agent Terex. Poe lent him to Kazuda Xiono during Kaz's spy work on the Colossus, where BB-8 kept the inexperienced pilot alive through pirate raids and First Order schemes. After the attack on Tuanul, he escaped alone with the map, met Rey and Finn, and helped bring it to the Resistance, which led to the destruction of Starkiller Base. During the evacuation of D'Qar he flew with Poe, then went with Finn and Rose Tico to Canto Bight and rescued them aboard the Supremacy by commandeering an AT-ST walker. He stayed with the survivors from Crait as they rebuilt, joined the search for the Sith wayfinder, and was at the final battle on Exegol, where the Sith Eternal were defeated.

29 ABY - Built

BB-8 is built as a BB-series astromech droid and later becomes Poe Dameron's companion.

~33 ABY - Suraz Engagement

BB-8 flies with Poe and Rapier Squadron when they answer the hijacked freighter Yissira Zyde's distress call, and they fail to stop the First Order from taking it.

~33 ABY - Skirmish in OR-Kappa-2722

Against orders, BB-8 and Poe track the freighter to a hidden First Order fleet. Back at base, Poe is disciplined, and BB-8 follows him when he leaves the New Republic to join the Resistance.

34 ABY - Black Squadron's Hunt for Lor San Tekka

BB-8 flies with Black Squadron to Ovanis and finds a First Order tracker on Poe's X-wing. He stuns Agent Terex and, on Megalox Beta, disables the prison's gravity field so the squadron can escape with Grakkus.

34 ABY - Mission to Kaddak

BB-8 helps Poe and C-3PO recover the spy droid N1-ZX. On a desert world he stops Terex from executing Poe, and Terex is handed over to the First Order.

34 ABY - Lent to Kazuda Xiono

About six months before the Battle of Starkiller Base, Poe assigns BB-8 to help Kaz spy on the Colossus refueling station on Castilon.

34 ABY - Adventures on the Colossus

BB-8 saves Kaz during pirate raids, helps him infiltrate Doza Tower twice, and joins Poe and Kaz on the mission to Station Theta Black, where he uncovers the First Order's weapons build-up.

34 ABY - Mission to the Dassal System

BB-8 helps discover that the First Order destroyed the Dassal system, then goes back to Poe while CB-23 stays with Kaz.

34 ABY - Attack on Tuanul

After Poe is captured on Jakku, BB-8 escapes with the map fragment that points to Luke Skywalker.

34 ABY - Meeting Rey

Rey frees BB-8 from Teedo's net. The two escape bandits and Unkar Plutt's thugs, meet Finn, and flee Jakku aboard the Millennium Falcon.

34 ABY - Takodana and D'Qar

BB-8 survives the First Order attack on Maz Kanata's castle and delivers the map to the Resistance on D'Qar, where he is reunited with Poe.

34 ABY - Battle of Starkiller Base

BB-8 flies with Poe in the attack that destroys Starkiller Base. When R2-D2 reawakens, the full map to Luke Skywalker is assembled.

34 ABY - Evacuation of D'Qar

BB-8 flies with Poe during the attack on the First Order dreadnought and repairs Black One's systems in the middle of the fight.

34 ABY - Canto Bight and the Supremacy

BB-8 goes with Finn and Rose Tico to find the master codebreaker. Aboard the Supremacy, he rescues them by taking control of an AT-ST walker.

34 ABY - Battle of Crait

BB-8 is with the Resistance survivors who escape Crait aboard the Millennium Falcon.

35 ABY - Rebuilding the Resistance

BB-8 goes on missions with Poe and his friends as the Resistance looks for new allies and resources after Crait.

35 ABY - Search for the Wayfinder

BB-8 goes with Rey, Finn, Poe and Chewbacca to Pasaana, Kijimi and Kef Bir in search of the Sith wayfinder, and meets the droid D-O.

35 ABY - Battle of Exegol

BB-8 takes part in the final battle against the Sith Eternal and Palpatine's Final Order, then celebrates the victory with the Resistance on Ajan Kloss.

BB-8's Timeline

  • Star Wars Resistance, 34 ABY (5 years old)
  • Episode VII: The Force Awakens, 34 ABY (5 years old)
  • Episode VIII: The Last Jedi, 34 ABY (5 years old)
  • Galaxy's Edge, 34 ABY (5 years old)
  • Episode IX: The Rise of Skywalker, 35 ABY (6 years old)
